moheñco-tarō allatu cintuveḷi nākarikam — moheñco-tarō allatu cintuveḷi nākarikam


					ṭākṭar. mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār





1941





12 jūṉ, 2024 aṉṟu vikkimūlattil iruntu pativiṟakkappaṭṭatu





﻿ulakaḷāviya potuk kaḷa urimam (CC0 1.0)

patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

***

itu, ulakat tamiḻ vikkiyūṭakac camūkamum ( https://ta.wikisource.org ), tamiḻ iṇiyak kalvik kaḻakamum ( http://tamilvu.org ) iṇinta kūṭṭumuyaṟciyil, pativēṟṟiya nūlkaḷil oṉṟu. ikkūṭṭu muyaṟciyip paṟṟi, https://ta.wikisource.org/s/4kx eṉṟa mukavariyil virivākak kāṇalām.

﻿patippuri



vaḷattukkum vāṇikattukkum intiyā eppōtum ulaka makkaḷiṉ kaṇṇil niḻalāṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟatu; appaṭiyē kali, nākarikam mutaliya pala taliyāṉa ārāyccikaḷukkum ulaka aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷatti eṉṟum atu kavarntu koṇṭirukkiṉṟatu. intiya nāṭṭiṉ elli, intiya makkaḷiṉ paṉṉuṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa cāti camaya moḻip pirivukaḷ, avvavaṟṟiṉ paḻimi perumi, vaḷarcci taḷarccikaḷ mutaliya karuttukkaḷik kuṟittu mēlināṭṭavarum kīḻināṭṭavarum pala kālamākavē ārāyntu varukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭalāḻaṅ kāṇpatum intiy nākarika āḻaṅ kāṇpatum oṉṟu.

cila kālattukkumuṉ variyil ārāycciyāḷar intiya nilikaḷip paṟṟip paṟpala karuttuṭiyarāyiruntaṉar. ‘intiya makkaḷil āriya reṉpavarē nākarikamuṭiyavar; avaratu moḻiyē vaṭamoḻi yeṉpatu; atuvē tamiḻ uṭpaṭa ellā intiya moḻikaḷukkun tāy; āriya nākarikamē ulaka nākarikaṅkaṭku aṭippaṭi’ eṉpaṉa akkaruttukkaḷiṟ cila. ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṇukku vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷē intiyāviṉ tali nūlkaḷ eṉṟu appōtu kāṇappaṭṭu vantamiyē ataṟkuk kāraṇam.

piṉpu, paṇṭik kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa, tamiḻ nūlkaḷ cila veḷippaṭalāyiṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ paḻimiyum cevviyum ārāyappaṭṭu, itaṟku āriyamoḻi tāy aṉṟu eṉpatum, immoḻiyāḷariṉ ﻿nākarikam āriya nākarikattiṉum vēṟu eṉpatum, uyarntatu eṉpatum, vēṟu cilavum viḷaṅkiṉa.

itaṟkuḷ nūlārāycciyē yallāmal, ñāla ārāycci, kalveṭṭu ārāycci, akapporu ḷārāycci mutalāṉa palavaki ārāyccikaḷ vaḷarccipeṟalāyiṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉāka, oru kālattil intiyāviṉ vaṭapakutiyum, imayamaliyum kaṭaluḷ iruntaṉa; appōtu intiyāviṉ kumarimuṉikkut teṟkē intumāk kaṭalil kumarik kaṇṭam eṉṟu oru periya nilapparappu iruntatu; ulakattilēyē makkaṭ piṟaviyiṉ tōṟṟam mutalmutal ak kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē uṇṭāyiṟṟu; piṉpu, akkaṇṭam ciṟitu ciṟitākak kaṭaluḷ. āḻntu vaṭakkē imayamali eḻuntapōtu, ak kaṇṭattilirunta makkaḷ vaṭakkē vantu ulakeṅkum paraviṉar. ippōtu teṉṉintiyāvāka uḷḷa tirāviṭa nilappakuti eññāṉṟum aḻiyāta mikappaḻiya nilamātaliṉ, vaṭakkilum ulakattilum paraviya makkaḷ ittirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrē yākiṉṟaṉar; imaya malikku vaṭakkēyum vaṭamēṟkēyum ceṉṟa am muṉṉōrē piṉṉorukāl vēṟṟumoḻiyāḷarpōla māṟi mīṇṭum intiyāvukkuḷ pukuntapōtu, avarkaḷ aṅkaṅkumiruntu vantavarākak kūṟa iṭamāyiṟṟēyaṉṟi vēṟilli; ākavē tirāviṭa nākarikamē ulaka nākarikattukku aṭippaṭi eṉṉum uṇmikaḷellām tiṭṭamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa; tamiḻarkaḷiṉ nalviṉippayaṉāl 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala ikkālaṅkaḷil mēlumēlum veḷivantu, avaṟṟiṉ āḻnta ariya ārāyccikaḷālum mēluṇmikaḷ naṉṟāka valuppeṟalāyiṉa; kumarikkaṇṭa nākarikam ikkāla ﻿nākarikattiṉum uyarntateṉpatu, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ perumi akkālattilēyē iṉṉum mikuntiruntateṉpatum, tamiḻvaḷartta tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷil mutaṟcaṅkam kumarik kaṇṭattiliruntatu eṉpatum, itupōṉṟa pala aruṅkuṟippukaḷellāmum caṅka nūlārāyccikaḷiliruntu veḷippaṭṭaṉa.

eṉiṉum, kāṇṭal, karutal, uriyaḷavikaḷil, ārāycci eṉpatu karutalaḷaviyilum caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ uriyaḷaviyilum aṭaṅkutaliṉ kāṇṭalaḷaviyiluntakka cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkumāyiṉ, intiyā muḻuvatumē oru kālattil tirāviṭa nāṭu; tirāviṭa makkaḷē piṟkālattiṟ palvēṟu intiyak kiḷiyiṉarāyiṉar tirāviṭamoḻiyē tirintu tirintu palvēṟu intiya moḻikaḷāyiṟṟu: tirāviṭa nākarikamē ulaka nākarikattiṟku aṭippaṭi iṉṉum tirāviṭa nākarikamē uyarntatāka uḷḷatu’ eṉṉum ivvaḷavu periya puti poruḷuṇmikaḷi mikat teḷivākat tuṇintu koḷvataṟku aiyuṟavillāta nilippu ēṟpaṭum. oru karuttit tuṇivataṟku iṅṅaṉam mūvakiyaḷavikaḷum mutaṉmiyāṉavi.

‘tamiḻ’ eṉpatip piṟar ‘tirāviṭam’ eṉṟaṉar; tamiḻ makkaḷ aṅkaṅkum pōkkuvaraviṉṟit taṅkiyakālattil avarkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyē palvēṟu vakiyāṉa intiya moḻikaḷākat tirintu niṉṟaṉa; oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kalantu piṉṉum palavāyiṉa. mikutiyākat tirintuviṭṭavi vaṅkāḷam mutaliya vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ; oraḷavil tirintavi teluṅku mutaliya tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ; moḻitiriyavē tamiḻmakkaḷum avvam moḻik kuriyavarāy ﻿vaṭa intiyarum teṉṉintiyarumākat tirintu pala cātiyiṉarāyiṉar; itaṉāl intiyar yār eṉpatē terintu koḷvatu aritāyiṟṟu.

ittakiya uṟṟa nērattilētāṉ, intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkē cintu āṟṟiṉ karimaruṅku iṟṟikku 5000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ ciṟanta tirāviṭa nākarikattōṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntu piṉ maṇmūṭuṇṭa ‘moheñco-tarō’ ‘harappā’ mutaliya peru nakaraṅkaḷ, akaḻntu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. takka nākarikac cāṉṟukaḷ avaṟṟiliruntu nēriṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuc ciṟanta ārāycci yaṟiñarkaḷāl naṉku cōtaṉi ceyyappeṟṟaṉa: cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikamē eṉpatu kāṇṭalaḷaviyālum maliyilakkākat tulaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. iṟṟikku 4500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ intiyā muḻumiyum tirāviṭamē yeṉṟu viḷakki, nilappaṭap puttakattiṟ (Atlas) paṭamum eḻutappeṟṟirukkiṟatu.

tirāviṭar ippōtu enniliyi lirukkiṉṟaṉar. tam muṉ nilikaḷic ciṟitēṉum eṇṇip pārppārāyiṉ, intiyāvil cātiyālum moḻiyālum camayattālum pala piriviṉarāy oṟṟumiyiṉṟi eḻucciyiṉṟi mēlumirukka oruppaṭuvarō! tirāviṭar tam toṉmiyiyum vaṉmiyiyum ulakaṟiya vittu vāḻavaṉṟō muṟpaṭuvar! iv vuṇarcciyum muyaṟciyum uṇṭākum poruṭṭē im mohañco-tarō ārāyccikaḷi viḷakkamākat tamiḻmoḻiyil eḻutuvittu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟōm. iv veḷiyīṭu ak karuttic cevvaṉam niṟivēṟṟumeṉṟu nampukiṟōm. ﻿

nāṅkaḷ virumpiyataṟku ēṟpa, innūlip pala talippukaḷil takutiyākap pākupāṭu ceytu paṉṉūl ārāyccikaḷiyum naṉṟākak kōviceytu porutti ēṟṟa mēṟkōṭ kuṟippukkaḷuṭaṉ mikavum teḷivāka mika eḷiya tamiḻ naṭiyil nalluḻippōṭu eḻutiyutaviya itaṉ āciriyar ṭākṭar. mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār, M.A., M.O.L., L.T., Ph.D. avarkaḷiṉ toṇṭu tamiḻakattārāṟ peritum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatākum. acciṭuṅ kālattil, innūli naṉku pārviyiṭṭutaviyavar tarumiyātīṉa vittuvāṉ, tiruvāḷar. kāḻi. civa. kaṇṇucāmip piḷḷi B.A. avarkaḷāvar. ivvaṟiñariruvarkkum eṅkaḷ naṉṟiyi aṉpuṭaṉ terivittuk koḷḷukiṟōm.

civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattār.





﻿uḷḷuṟi

1. putiporuḷ ārāycci pakkam 1 - 17

puti poruḷ ārāycci ārāycci avā uṇṭāvatēṉ? ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ - mālṭṭā - ekiptu - pālastīṉam - aciriyā - pāpilōṉiyā - mecoppoṭṭēmiyā - ēlam - innāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum naṭanta ārāycci ‘ur’ nakaril ārāycci - pāracīkam - intiyāvil ārāycci - cintuveḷi nākarikam.

2. cintuveḷiyiṟ putiyuṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷ 18- 34

cintu yāṟu - pañcāp maṇṭilam - cintu maṇṭilam - kīrtar malittoṭar - harappā - moheñco - tarō - stupattiyuṭiya maṇmēṭu - 1925-1934 vari naṭipeṟṟa ārāycci - kuṟippiṭattakka ceytikaḷ - cāṉha tarō - lohāñco - tarō - takañco - tarō - cakpūrpāṉi epiyāl - alimurāt - pāṇṭivāhi amri. kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉ nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷ.

3. piṟamaṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉ putiporuḷkaḷ 35 - 53

ārāycci iṭaṅkaḷiṟ pāyum āṟukaḷ kaṅki narumati tapati yāṟukaḷ - teṉṉāṭu kōtāvari kiruṣṇi - kāviri tāmiraparaṇi - periyāṟu -āṟṟuveḷikaḷil paṇṭi makkaḷ - aikkiya maṇṭilattup puti poruḷkaḷ - keḷacāmpi tōṉṟiya kālam - pīkār maṇṭilttup putiporuḷkaḷ - naṭu maṇṭilattu veḷḷit ﻿tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ - orissāvil iraṭṭik kōṭarikaḷ - ṭekkāṉ - ceṉṉi maṇṭilam - makiḻccikkuriya maṇmēṭu - putukkōṭṭiyil tāḻikaḷ - tiruvitāṅkūr - ṭekkāṉ pakuti taṉittiruntatā? - paṇṭit tamiḻ nakaraṅkaḷ caṅku cāṉṟu pakarum . mēṉāṭṭār muyaṟci nammavar kaṭami.

4. nakara amippum āṭci muṟiyum 54 - 65

maṟintāriṉ maṇ mēṭukaḷ - āṟṟōram aminta nakaram nakaram aminta iṭam - terukkaḷiṉ amippu - kālvāy amippu - cuvarukkuḷ kaḻinīrk kuḻi - mūṭappeṟṟa kālvāykaḷ iṭi iṭiyē perun toṭṭikaḷ - matakuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ - paṉmuṟi uyarttap peṟṟa kālvāykaḷ - malam kaḻikka otukkiṭam nakara āṭci muṟi avaciyamē ഷിക്കവ vaḷarppatu piṟa nāṭukaḷil illāta aṟputa nakara amippu.

5. kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ 66 - 80

kaṭṭiṭa amippu muṟi aṇi aṇiyāṉa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ - parutta cuvarkaḷ neṭuñ cuvarkaḷ - palavakik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ - eḷiyavar illaṅkaḷ periya muṟṟamuṭiya illaṅkaḷ pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ vāḻnta vīṭukaḷ celvar tam ‘ māṭa māḷikikaḷ - aracaṉatu araṇmaṉiyō! - tiyalārkkut taṉi aṟikaḷ - nīrāṭum aṟikaḷ - camiyal aṟikaḷ aṅkāṭiyō? ampalamō? kaḷḷukkaṭiyō? taṇṇirp pantalō? uṇṭic cāliyō? - vēṭkōvar viṭutikaḷ! - vīṭṭuvāyilkaḷ - tūṇkaḷ- harappāvil kaṭṭiṭa amippu iruvaki illaṅkaḷ - peruṅ ﻿kaḷañciyam - toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ - vīṭṭiṟku uriya vīṭukaḷē.

6. kiṇaṟukaḷ - ceykuḷam - ceṅkaṟkaḷ 81 - 89

5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ iṉṟum curappuṭiya kiṇaṟukaḷ - māḷikikaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ . kayiṟum uruḷikaḷum palamuṟi uyarttappaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ - aḻakiya ceykuḷam kuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭamum uṭcuvarkaḷum - nilakkīl - kuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē - kuḷattiṟku nīr vacati ceṅkaṟkaḷ - paṇṭi nāṭukaḷ - ceṅkaṟkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭa muṟikaḷ ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ - ceṅkaṟkaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷ.

7. vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷum viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷum 90 - 104

vīṭṭukkuriya poruḷkaḷ - maṭpāṇṭa māṇpu palavaki maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ - kaḷimaṇ kalavi vēṭkōvar. uruḷikaḷ kāḷavāy merukiṭum karuvikaḷ pala niṟap pāṇṭaṅkaḷ niṟaṅkaḷip pūcuvāṉēṉ? - oviyam koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ - eṅkum illā eḻiluṟu oviyam vēṟu pala oviyaṅkaḷ maṭpāṇṭa vakikaḷ pūcikkuriya maṭpāṇṭam kaṉal caṭṭi - kumiḻikaḷ koṇṭa tāḻi - veṇkaṟ pāṉikaḷ kippiṭi koṇṭa kalaṉkaḷ - mikkūṭukaḷ purimaṉikaḷ putikkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ - elippoṟikaḷ pīṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟi ammi, entiram, ural palavaki viḷakkukaḷ - piṟa poruḷkaḷ - viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ iṉṟum ūtum ūtukuḻal tali acikkum erutu - vaṇṭikaḷ - cokkaṭṭāṉ karuvikaḷ. ﻿

8. kaṇip poruḷkaḷ 105-116

payaṉpaṭṭa kaṇip poruḷkaḷ - poṉṉum veḷḷiyum - cempil īyak kalavi-cempil nikkal kalavi - cempu kalanta maṇ - veṇkalam - veḷḷiyam - kārīyam - makkaḷiṉ mati nuṭpam - vēli muṟi - cempu, veṇkalap :oruḷkaḷ īṭṭikaḷ - uṭivāḷkaḷ iṭi vāḷkaḷum kattikaḷum vēlkaḷ - ampu muṉikaḷ - irampam - uḷikaḷ - tōl cīvum uḷikaḷ - kōṭarikaḷ - vāycci - maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ - uḻu karuvikaḷ tūṇṭil muṭkaḷ piṟa karuvikaḷ - cāṇikkal eṇ iṭappaṭṭa karuvikaḷ.

9. vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum 117 - 123

maṉitaṉukku muṉ tōṉṟiya vilaṅkukaḷ kālattiṟ kēṟṟa māṟupāṭu - cintup piratēca vilaṅkukaḷ - yāṉi - erutukaḷ - nāykaḷ - pūṉikaḷ - paṉṟikaḷ - āṭukaḷ - kaḻutikaḷ - māṉkaḷ . erumikaḷ - oṭṭakam muyalkaḷ - āmi mutaliyaṉa - paṟavikaḷ - nākaṅkaḷ.

10. uṇavum uṭiyum 124 - 130

viḷiporuḷkaḷ - maruta nilamum nakar vaḷamum pulāl uṇṭa makkaḷ - piṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ camiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ - uṇavu - koṇṭa muṟi - uṭikaḷ aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ āṭicciṟappu - muṇṭācu kaṭṭiya makaḷirkāl caṭṭiyō - kūtta makaḷ.

11. aṇikalaṉkaḷ 131 - 151

aṇikalaṉkaḷ - putikkappaṭṭa nakikaḷ putiyuṇṭa veḷḷikkalaṉkaḷ - putiyuṇṭa cempukkalaṉkaḷ ﻿kāṭcik kiṉiya kaḻuttumāli maṟṟum iru kaḻuttu mālikaḷ - harappāvil kiṭitta kaḻuttu mālikaḷ - aḻakoḻukum iṭip paṭṭikaḷ - tuḷi iṭṭa pēraṟivu - āṟu caraṅkoṇṭa aḻakiya kiyaṇi - uḷḷē arakkiṭṭa kāppukaḷ palavakippaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ - oviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ - kāl kāppukaḷ - neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ kātaṇikaḷ - mūkkaṇikaḷ - mōtiraṅkaḷ pottāṉkaḷ - talināṭākkaḷ - koṇṭi ūcikaḷ - camayattoṭarpuḷḷa patakkam - palavakik kaṟkaḷ - maṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa vitam oviyam aminta maṇikaḷ - paṭṭi veṭṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ kaṟkaḷiṟ pulami - taṅkak kavacam koṇṭa maṇikaḷ - cippukaḷ - kaṇṇāṭikaḷ makaḷir kūntal oppaṉi - mintar kūntal oppaṉi - mīci illā āṭavar kaṇṇukku mi - mukattiṟkup poṭi.

12. vāṇipam 152 – 165

uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam - veḷināṭṭu vāṇipam nanti vaḻipāṭu ekiptiyaruṭaṉ vāṇipam - nīrvaḻi vāṇipam nilavaḻi vāṇipam paṇṭikkālap palucistāṉam cintuveḷi makkaṭkup piṉ niṟik kaṟkaḷ niṟi aḷavukaḷ - tarācukaḷ aḷavukkōl muttiri patittal.

13. viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ - toḻilkaḷ- kalikaḷ 166-187

palavaki viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ - vēṭṭiyāṭal - kōḻi, keḷatāric caṇṭikaḷ - kottu vēli - maṭpāṇṭat toḻil - kaltaccar toḻil marat taccar toḻil - kaṉṉāra vēli - araṇmaṉiyuḷ kāḷavāykaḷ - poṟkollar toḻil -irattiṉak kal cōtaṉi - cetukkuvēli ﻿caṅkut toḻil - mīṉ piṭittal - vaṇṭi oṭṭutal - nāvitat toḻil - tōṭṭi vēli - kāval toḻil - kappal toḻil - payirttoḻil - necavut toḻil - tiyalum piṉṉalum - tanta vēli - maṇi ceyyun toḻil pāy piṉṉutal - eḻutak kaṟṟavar - vāṇipat toḻil - ciṟpak kali - oviyak kali - toṭarntu varum toṉmi nākarikam - iciyum naṭaṉamum - kaṇitap pulami - maruttuvak kalivāṉanūṟ pulami uṭaṟpayiṟci nakara makkaḷ.

14. camaya nili 188 - 205

cāṉṟukaḷ - tarip peṇ vaṇakkam - kaviṉpeṟu kaṟpaṉi narapali uṇṭā? - taliyil viciṟip pāki - civa vaṇakkam liṅka vaḻipāṭu - puttar perumāṉā? kaṇṇapirāṉā? kompuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ - nāṉku kit teyvaṅkaḷ - camaṇa camayamum paṇṭiyatō? nanti vaḻipāṭu oṟṟik kompu erutu - āṟutali vilaṅku - katiravak kaṭavuḷ kalappu uruvaṅkaḷ piṟa vilaṅkukaḷ nāka vaṇakkam - puṟā vaṇakkam karuṭa vaṇakkam mara vaṇakkam. - mara tēvatikaḷ - āṟṟu vaṇakkam - pali iṭum paḻakkam - tāyittu aṇital - camayap patakkam - kaṭavuḷ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ūrvalam - nērttik kaṭaṉ - iciyum naṭaṉamum - paṭittal paḻakkam - kōvil vaḻipāṭu - cintu veḷic camayam yātu? - civattiṉ paḻimi.

15 iṭutalum cuṭutalum 206-211

cuṭutal - iṭutal - īrāṉiyar paḻakkam - tāḻikaḷ mītu oviyaṅkaḷ mayilkaḷ teyvīkat taṉmi peṟṟaviyā? ﻿paṟavimuka maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ - tāḻikaḷuṭ pala poruḷkaḷ - uṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam - muṭivu.

16. cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ 212-225

eḻuttu ārāycciyāḷar - paṭikka muṭiyāta eḻuttukaḷ eḻuttukaḷip peṟṟuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ - eḻutum muṟi eḻuttukaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa . rā.) eḻuttukaḷ - eḻuttukaḷ muṭivuri.

17. cintuveḷi makkaḷ yāvar? 226-261

paṇṭi intiya makkaḷ - nīkrōvar . āsṭrēliyar melaṉēviyar - mattiyatarik kaṭaliṉar . maṅkōviya . alpiṉar moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷ - kālam yātu im makkaḷ vāḻkki āriyar - āriyar allātavar (anāriyar) - āriyar - anāriyar pōrkaḷ - inta anāriyar cintuveḷi makkaḷē - cintuveḷi makkaḷ yāvar? - moḻi ārāycci kūṟum uṇmi - piṟa cāṉṟukaḷ - intiya makkaḷ paṟṟiya aṟikki neṭuṅkālam vāḻnta makkaḷ - muṭivuri.

itaṉkaṇ eṭuttāḷap peṟṟa mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ 262





﻿moheñco-tarō allatu

cintu veḷi nākarikam

1 . putiporuḷ ārāycci



putiporuḷ ārāycci

mikap paḻiya maṉitaṉ vīṭu kaṭṭa aṟiyātu mali muḻikaḷil vāḻntu vantāṉ. appōtu avaṉ iyalpākak kiṭitta kaṟkaḷiyē taṉ karuvikaḷākak koṇṭāṉ. avaṉ nāḷaṭivil ulaka iyalpi kūṟupāṭṭi aṟintu, cempu, veṇkalam, irumpu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷik kaṇṭāṉ: avaṟṟik koṇṭu taṉakkuvēṇṭiya palavakipporuḷkaḷic ceytu koṇṭāṉ.[1] āṟṟaṅkarikaḷilum camaveḷikaḷilum iyalpākak kiṭitta kaḷimaṇṇik koṇṭu vīṭukaḷi amittāṉ. āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil ōṅki vaḷarnta kōrip pullikkoṇṭu kūrikaḷi amittāṉ taṉ vāḻviṟkēṟṟa pāṇṭaṅkaḷi maṇṇālēyē ceytu koṇṭāṉ; taṉ. aṟivukku eṭṭiya aḷavil, tāṉ. pēcivanta moḻiyip pulappaṭuttac cittira eḻuttukkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ; av veḻuttukkaḷip paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷ mītum



* * *



﻿1. The Indus Valley Civilization. ↑ 2. ikkālam kaṟkālam eṉappaṭum. 3. ikkālaṅkaḷ muṟiyē cempuk kālam, veṇkalak kālam, irumpuk kālam eṉappaṭum.



maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītum cuṇṇāmpuk kaṟkaḷāl āṉa pāttiraṅkaḷ mītum piṟavaṟṟiṉmītum poṟittu vittāṉ; akkāla aṟivunilikku ēṟṟavāṟu kaṟkaḷikkoṇṭum pacci maṇṇik koṇṭum, piṉṉarc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭum. taṉ maṉattiṟkiṉiya kōvilkaḷi amittuk kaṭavuḷari uṇṭākkiṉāṉ;tāṉ vāḻnta maruta nilatti uḻutu payir ceyyalāṉāṉ; taṉakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷi vittukkoṇṭu eñciyavaṟṟi, avi kiṭikkappeṟāta mali nāṭukaḷilum pālivaṉaṅkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum irunta makkaṭku utavi, taṉṉiṭam illāmal avarkaḷiṭamē ciṟappāka irunta pal poruḷkaḷip paṇṭamāṟṟākap peṟṟu vāḻntu vantāṉ. ippaḻakkamē nāḷaṭivil vāṇipamāka vaḷarntōṅkiyatu. iṅṅaṉam oru pakutiyil vāḻum makkaḷ maṟṟap pakutikaḷil vāḻum mkkaḷōṭu vāṇipam ceyyuṅkāl irutiṟattārum avvappōtu kalantu uṟavāṭa vāyppukaḷ uṇṭātal iyalpu. akkūṭṭuṟaviṉāl oru cārāriṭam kāṇappaṭum nalliyalpukaḷ palavaṟṟippiṟitoru cārār kikkōṭal iyalpu:irucārārum nāḷaṭivil peṇ koḷvatilum koṭuppatilum īṭupaṭṭuk kalappuṟutalum uṇṭu. ikkalappiṉāl iruvēṟupaṭṭa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, kalikaḷ, mata uṇarccikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum kalap puṟutalum iyalpē. ikkalappu nākarikattiṉiṉṟum teḷivupeṟṟa putiyatōr nākarikam tōṟṟam eṭuttalum uṇṭu. iḵtu ulaka iyaṟki iṅṅaṉam ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷil makkaṭ kalappum nākarikak kalappum paṇṭikkālattilēyē uṇṭāyiṉa.

iṅṅaṉam ulakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷil vāḻnta paṇṭi makkaḷ, taṉittum kalantum palavaki nākarikaṅkaḷi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kaṟkāla makkaḷ cempip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiyatum, kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷip puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭaṉar; payaṉaṟṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi vilakkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam nīkkappaṭṭa apporuḷkaḷ, kavaṉippāraṟṟu nāḷaṭivil maṇṇuḷ putiyuṇṭaṉa.kaṟkāla makkaḷ maṇṇāl tāḻikaḷic ceytu iṟantavar uṭampukaḷi avaṟṟuḷ vittu ഥങ്ങജൂൺ putittu vantaṉar. teṉ intiyāvil ivvakit tā, kaḷ putukkōṭṭiyil ērāḷamākak kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭi makkaḷ nalliṭaṅkaḷ tēṭi aṭikkaṭi iṭam māṟit tirintaṉar. ātaliṉ, āṅkāṅkup paḻutuṟṟa poruḷkaḷip pōṭṭup pōyiṉar. apporuḷkaḷ nāḷaṭivil maṇṇuḷ maṟippuṇṭaṉa. paḻaṅkāla maṉṉar uṭalaṅkaḷ putikkap peṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kaṟkōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mītu akkālac cittira eḻuttukkaḷum pala cittiraṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷilum kaṭaṟkariyi aṭuttum oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷi amittuk koṇṭu vāḻnta cempu - veṇkalak kāla makkaḷ muṟiyē āṟṟu veḷḷattiṟkum kaṭaliṉ kontaḷippiṟkum añci annakaraṅ *տոպմ tam poruḷkaḷiyum viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiviṭṭamiyum uṇṭu. vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷ, vēṟṟu makkaḷ paṭiyeṭuppukku añcit tuṟakkappaṭṭirukkalām. cila nakaraṅkaḷ erimalikaḷiṉ cēṭṭiyāl aḻivuṟṟu irukkalām. cila nakaraṅkaḷ maṇ māriyāl aḻintatuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷāl appaṇṭi makkaḷ vāḻntu vanta kirāmaṅkaḷum nakaraṅkaḷum kiviṭappaṭṭu, nāḷaṭivil maṇ mūṭappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. pala nakaraṅkaḷ kaṭaluḷ āḻntaṉa. pala āṟṟaṅkarikaḷiṉ aṭiyil putiyuṇṭu viṭṭaṉa. pala camaveḷikaḷil maṇ mēṭiṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

iṅṅaṉam maṇ mēṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷit tōṇṭip pārttu, avviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum palatiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi. ārāyntu avaṟṟippaṟṟiya uṇmic ceytikaḷiyum, avaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiya paṇṭi makkaḷippaṟṟiya cuvi payakkum ceytikaḷiyum aṟiyum muyaṟciyē. putiporuḷ ārāycci eṉappaṭum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, palaporuḷkaḷic ceytu nākarikattit tōṟṟuvitta paḻaṅkāla makkaḷatu varalāṟṟik kaṇṭaṟivatē putiporuḷ ārāycciyākum.’[1] ivvārāyccikkup pakkapalamākac ciṟappuṟṟu iruppavi maṇṭiyōṭṭic cōtikkum kali, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpukaḷic cōtikkum kali, ‘nilanūl aṟivu’ eṉṉum mūṉṟākum.

ārāycci avā uṇṭāṉatēṉ?

mikka paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ taṅkaḷ varalāṟukaḷi eḻuti vittirāviṭiṉum, pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum camātikaḷ mītum kaṟkampaṅkaḷ mītum appaḻaṅkāla moḻikaḷil pala ceytikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷip paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ ceviyāṟāka vantu ulakattup paḻiya nūlkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭum piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya pipiḷ, rikvētam, purāṇaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ naṭattiya pōrkaḷ, kaṭal kerntaḷippu, pala nakaraṅkaḷ aḻintmi pōṉṟa cila kuṟippukaḷ piṟkāla makkaḷiṉ kavaṉatti īrttaṉ. kaṭal kontaḷippāl uṇṭāṉa tīmi pipiḷilum rikvētattilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum īḻa nāṭṭavar paḻiya nūlkaḷilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pipiḷ, rikvētam, purāṇaṅkaḷ mutaliya nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷ pala iṉṟu kāṇappaṭavilli. pala nakaraṅkaḷ teyvaṅkaḷatu cīṟṟattukkuṭpaṭṭu erikkappaṭṭum kaṭaluḷ āḻttappaṭṭum viṭṭaṉa eṉṉum kuṟippukkaḷip paṭikkum aṟivuḷḷa maṉitaṉ, avi iruntaṉavākak kūṟappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷit tēṭik kaṇṭaṟiya avāvutal iyalṭēyaṉṟō? ‘hōmar’ eṉṉum kirēkka kaviñar eḻutiyuḷḷa ‘iliyaṭ’ eṉṉum nūlil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa ṭrāy nakaram eṅkē? appoṉṉakarik kaṇṭu, ataṉ amippiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum aṟiyiṉ, appaḻaṅkāla nakarattil vāḻnta makkaḷippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷi aṟital kūṭumaṉṟō? eṉṟu aṟivuṭiyavar eṇṇutal iyalpēyākum. nācarēttūr aṭikaḷ vāḻntu pala aṟputac ceyalkaḷic ceyta iṭaṅkaḷ pipiḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avviṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟila. avi maṇmūṭi iruttal vēṇṭum. aṅkuc ceṉṟu maṇmēṭukaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, avaṟṟit tōṇṭi, uṇmiyi uṇartal vēṇṭum eṉṉum avā aṟiñar uḷḷattē vērūṉṟutal iyalpu tāṉē!

intiya nāṭṭup paḻiya nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa nālantā, takṣacīlam, kōcāmpi, pāṭalipuram, koṟki, kāyal, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, vañcimānakaram, uṟiyūr mutaliya varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭiya nakaraṅkaḷi akaḻntu kaṇṭu, nam muṉṉōrippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi aṟintu iṉpuṟa nām virumputal iyalpaṉṟō? “tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa ‘kōṇṭ’ eṉpatu mattiya mākāṇattilum ‘kūy’ eṉpatu ceṉṉi mākāṇattiṉ vaṭa kōṭiyilum, ‘kuruk’ eṉpatum ‘irāj mahāl’ eṉpatum vaṅkāḷa mākāṇattilum, pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷiyuṭiya ‘prāhi’ eṉpatu palucistāṉattilum iruttalik kāṇiṉ, vaṭa intiyāvil mikap paḻiya kālattil mūlat tirāviṭamoḻi pēcappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum. prāhiyil uḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷi nōkkuṅkāl, palucistāṉattiṟku aṇmiyil tirāviṭar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam tōṉṟukiṟatu”[2] eṉpatip paṭikkum poḻutu, tirāviṭa makkaḷākiya nām, nam muṉṉōr cintu veḷiyil vāḻntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟōm. uṭaṉē, avarkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷ akappaṭāvō? avi eṉṉa vāyiṉa? maṇṇuḷ maṟintaṉavō? avartam paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nammiṭam iṉṟu iruppavitāmā? avaṟṟi aṟiya vēṇṭumē eṉap palavāṟu puttuṇarcci peṟukiṉṟōm. ivvuṇarcci tōṉṟaṟku aṟiñarkaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ eḻuti vittuḷḷa ariya nūlkaḷē kāraṇamākum.

nām iṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ tiṭīreṉa vantirā. avaṟṟuḷ cila mikap paḻiya kālantoṭṭē vantiruttal vēṇṭum. oru vaki nākarikam tōṉṟum poḻutu aḵtu, ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa nākarikattiṉ oru pakutiyit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭē tōṟṟameṭukkum. inta uṇmi, varalāṟṟiṉ uyir nāṭiyākum. varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṟum toṭarpuṟṟu varum iyakkamākum. ‘ittuṭaṉ ellām muṭivaṭintuviṭṭaṉa; iṉinammiccārvaṉa eviyum illi’ eṉṟu enta iṭattilum enta nērattilum kūṟa, uṇmi varalāṟu iṭamtarātu. iṉṟiya airōppā, paḻiya urōmappēraraciliruntu tōṉṟiyatākum. anta urōmap pēraracum aleksāṇṭaratu peru veṟṟiyiliruntu piṟantatākum. aleksāṇṭaratu pēravā, pāracīka maṉṉarkaḷiṉ taṉiyaracu uṇarcciyiṉiṉṟum piṟantatākum. pāracīka vēntarkaḷ ataṉi āciriyarpāl kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam uḷḷa pala uṇmikaḷik kāṇum pōtu, varalāṟu eṉṟum toṭarpuṭiyatē eṉṉum uṇmic ceytiyi uḷḷavāṟu uṇaralām”.[3]

ivvuṇarcci nam uḷḷattē eḻumāyiṉ, namatu varalāṟṟut toṭarpik kaṇṭaṟiya nām avāvi niṟṟal iyalpē ākum. avā nāḷaṭivil ceyalil muṭikiṉṟatu. aḵtāvatu, putintu kiṭakkum iṭaṅkaḷit tōṇṭi maṇ mūṭuṇṭa nākarikatti aṟiya maṉam nāṭukiṉṟatu: ceyalāṟṟip pala uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu taḷarntuḷḷa varalāṟṟuc caṅkiliyi muṟukkuṭiyatāka ākkukiṉṟatu.

ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ

kaṟkāla makkaḷum cempu veṇkala irumpuk kāla makkaḷum kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷ ellām ārāyccikku uriyaṉavē ākum. ammakkaḷ perumpālum āṟṟup pāyccal uḷḷa camaveḷikaḷiṟṟāṉ vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. malippāṅkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil peritum kaṟkāla maṉitar nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷē mikkirukkum. camaveḷikaḷil piṟkāla makkaḷ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. ikkuṟippik koṇṭum, nām mēṟkūṟiya pipiḷ mutaliya paḻiya nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷik koṇṭum ārāyntāl, ovvoru kaṇṭattilum ārāyccikku uriya iṭaṅkaḷ iruttal uṇmi. avaṟṟi aṟiñar kaṇṭaṟintu varukiṉṟaṉar. īṇṭu nām eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukku uriyaṉavum ataṟkuc cārpāṉaviyumāka iruppavi mattiyatarik kaṭaliliruntu intiyā aṭaṅka uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ ākum. avi mālṭṭā, ekiptu, pālastīṉam, aciriyā, pāpilōṉiyā, ēlam, pāracīkam, cintup piratēcam, kaṅkic camaveḷi, ṭekkāṉ, teṉ intiyā eṉpaṉa ākum. ivi paṟṟiya iṉṟiyamiyāta kuṟippukkaḷi maṭṭum īṇṭuk kāṇpōm.

taṭitta eḻuttukkaḷ

mālṭṭā eṉpatu mattiya tarik kaṭalil iṭṭālikkum vaṭa āprikkak karikkum iṭiyil uḷḷa paḻantīvu. itaṉ talinakaram ‘lāveṭṭā’ eṉpatu. iḵtu, ‘araṇmaṉikaḷik koṇṭuḷḷa nakaram’ eṉappaṭum. itil uḷḷa pala māṭa māḷikikaḷ jeruslēttiṉ makāṉāṉa jāṉ eṉpāruṭiya vīrarkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavi. itu mikap paḻiya kāla mutalē ciṟappuṟṟa tīvākum. itaṉ oru puṟam ārāycci vēli naṭipeṟṟatu. ataṉ palaṉāka, ārāycciyāḷar karuttik kavarnta maṭkalaṅkaḷum palavaki maṇikaḷum veḷippaṭṭaṉa. ārāycci muṟṟuppeṟavilli. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu palavakiccittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷ uṭinta niliyil kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi, oṟṟik kippiṭiyum iraṭṭik kippiṭiyum uṭiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. cuṇṇāmpuk kallālāṉa liṅkam oṉṟu kiṭittatu.[4] mālṭṭā nākarikam veṇkalak kālattatu eṉṟu ārāycci aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

ekiptu

āprikkāviṉ vaṭa pakutiyil nīla āṟu pāyum marutanila veḷiyē ekiptu eṉpatu. ulakattil eṅkumē iruntirāta mika uyarnta nākarikam aṅku irunta teṉpataṟku uriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ mikap pala uṇṭu. āyiṉum, avi ēṟakkuṟiya nūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ vari kavaṉikkappaṭṭila. keyrō mutal ‘laksar’ variyuḷḷa 960 ki. mī. tolivuvari paḻutupaṭṭa paṇṭikkālattuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ paṇṭi aracar kallaṟikaḷ pala kōvilkaḷ pala kal mēṭukaḷ pala uṭinta cilikaḷ pala ki. mu. 4000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉum piṉṉum irunta ekiptiya aracarkaḷ taṅkaṭkeṉak kaṭṭiya kallaṟikaḷē piramiṭkōpuraṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa. avi cumār 80 ākum. avaṟṟuḷ mikap periyatu 6,37,600 ca.ce.mī parappum 14,400 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭiyatu. atil uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ 70 laṭcam ṭaṉ niṟiyuḷḷavi nāṉku allatu aintu aṭukkukaḷiyuṭiya 22 āyiram vīṭukaḷik koṇṭa nakaram oṉṟik kaṭṭi muṭikkap pōtumāṉavi; eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ivviyappūṭṭum piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭiya pēraracar tam nākarikatti eṉṉeṉpatu ārāycciyiṟ kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; pala niṟaṅkaḷ pūcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; pāṉikaḷum piṟavum perumpālum taṭṭiyāṉa aṭiyuṭiyaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum, ap paṇṭi makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya cittira eḻuttukkaḷum vilaṅku paṟavikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar avaṟṟip pala āṇṭukaḷ muyaṉṟu paṭittu muṭittaṉar; ataṉ piṉṉarē ekiptiṉ varalāṟṟi oḻuṅkāka eḻutalāyiṉar.

pālastīṉam

ippakuti, ulakattiluḷḷa kiṟistava camayattiṉar aṉivarum pōṟṟattakkatākum. ātaliṉ, ippakutiyil uḷḷa ārāyccikkuriya maṇmēṭukaḷit tōṇṭi uṇmic ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟi aṟiya avākkoṇṭa aṟiñar kuḻu ārāycci vēliyit toṭaṅkiyatu. akkuḻu piriṭṭiṣ aṟiñar cilariyum amerikka aṟiñar cilariyum koṇṭatākum. pipiḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittalē ivvaṟiñarkaḷiṉ mukkiya nōkkamākum. ivarkaḷ ārāycci naṭattiyatiṉ payaṉāyp pala nakaraṅkaḷ veḷippaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cotam (Sodum), komorāh (Gomorrah), jerikō (Jericho), mamri (Mamre), kirjāt cepar (Kiriath - Sepher) mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. pipiḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ innakaraṅkaḷil iruttalik kaṇṭu aṟiñarkaḷ viyappum makiḻcciyum eytiṉar. cila nakaraṅkaḷil irunta cavappeṭṭikaḷuḷ cippi ōṭukaḷ, maṇikaḷ, pala niṟaṅkoṇṭa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ, vili uyarnta kaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl āṉa mālikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟōṭu mālikaḷil iṇikkappaṭum patakkaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi muttuc cippi, elumpu, muttukkaḷ paṉṉiṟak kaṇṇāṭit tuṇṭukaḷ ivaṟṟik koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭavi ākum palavaki maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. kal entiraṅkaḷum, kalvaṅkaḷum kuḻavikaḷum, vēṟupala uruṇṭik kaṟkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.[5]

aciriyā

innilappakuti mecoppoṭṭēmiyāvukkuk kiḻakkilum kurtistāṉattukku mēṟkilum pāpilōṉiyāvukku vaṭakkilum armīṉiyāvukkut teṟkilum amintiruppatu itaṉ talinakaram acur eṉpatu. innāṭṭiṉ mukkiya nakaraṅkaḷ mōcul, niṉaveh, kalā, recaṉ mutaliyaṉavām. innāṭu mikka ceḻippuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kaḷimaṇ mikutiyākak kiṭittamiyāl, paṇṭi aciriyar, ceṅkaṟkaḷāl vīṭukaḷik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu uyarnta nākarikattil vāḻntu vantaṉar. ceṅkaṟkaḷilum karuṅkaṟkaḷilum innāṭu pāpilōṉiyāviviṭac ciṟappuṭiyatē ākum. mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷ oru kālattil mikac cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟu iruntu, piṉṉar maṇmēṭukaḷāy maṅkip pōṉavi ākum.

pāpilōṇā

innilappakuti yūprēṭis, ṭikris yāṟukaḷukku iṭiyil uḷḷa marutanilap pakutiyākum. iṅkuc ciṟanta kaḷimaṇ kiṭittamiyāl, iṅku vāḻnta paṇṭi makkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyvatilum ceṅkaṟkaḷ ceyvatilum ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉar. āṟṟōraṅkaḷil vaḷarnta kōri kūriyākavum kūṭiyākavumpāyākavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. iṅku viḷi poruḷkaḷ atikamākum. pāpilōṉiyar, ivi nīṅkalāka uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ, tēkkamaram, perṉ veḷḷi mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟic cuṟṟup pakkattu nāṭukaḷiliruntu varavaḻittuk koṇṭaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷ ciṟanta vāṇipattiṟami uṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. mecoppoṭṭēmiyā

innilappakuti skāṭlaṇṭ aḷavuṭiyatu; ṭikris yāṟṟukku mēṟkē amintiruppatu itaṉ perum pakuti ciṟu malit toṭarkaḷik koṇṭa pāli nilamākum. ‘kappūr, palīk’ eṉṉum yāṟukaḷ innāṭṭiṟ pāyntu yūprēṭis yāṟṟil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kariyōraṅkaḷil maṭṭumē payir ceyya vacati uṇṭu. itaṟku vaṭakiḻakkē koḷḷiyaṭikkum mali nāṭṭār vacittu vantaṉar. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, mecop poṭṭēmiyā ciṟitu vaḷamuṭiyatu - peritum vaḷamillātatu; ciṟitaḷavu payir uṭiyatu - peritaḷavu pāṟi uṭiyatu; cila iṭaṅkaḷil nīr vacati uṭiyatu pala iṭaṅkaḷil nīr vacati aṟṟatu.

ēlam

ēlam eṉpatu pāpilōṉiyāvukkuk kiḻakkē uḷḷa ciṟiya nilapparappākum; ciṟitaḷavu malippakuti uṭiyatu. itaṉ talinakaram cucā. maṟṟoru ciṟanta nakaram ‘aṉcaṉ’ eṉpatu. ip pakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ aṭikkaṭi vaṭakkē irunta aciriyarōṭu pōriṭṭu vantaṉar. aciriyar ki.mu.650-il ippakutiyil irunta makkaḷi veṉṟu aṭippaṭuttiṉar. aciriyaratu pēraracu vīḻnta piṟaku ēlam, pāracīkap pēraracil kalantu maṟintatu.

innāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum naṭanta ārāycci

in nāṉku nilap pakutikaḷilum paṟpala iṭṅkaḷil maṇmēṭukaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ mutaṉ mutalāka aciriyāvil uḷḷa mōcul eṉṉum iṭattiṟku arukil irunta ‘niṉaveh’ eṉṟa paḻiya nakarattiyum pāpilōṉiyāvil ‘hillah’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟku arukil irunta maṇmēṭṭiyum veṭṭi ārāycci naṭattiṉar. iv vārāycciyiṉāl aciriyarip paṟṟiyum pāpilōṉiyarip paṟṟiyum ivviruvarkkum muṟpaṭṭa cumēriyarip paṟṟiyum cemiṭṭiyarippaṟṟiyum pala ceytikaḷ veḷippōntaṉa. [6]aṅkuk kiṭitta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷic cōtittup pārtta eṭvarṭ hiṅks (Edward Hicks) eṉpavar, ‘pāpilōṉiyar kiyāṇṭa eḻuttukkaḷ avarkaḷuṭiyaṉa alla’ eṉṟu karutiṉār. avveḻuttukkaḷi naṉku ārāynta āpparṭ eṉpār. ‘ivi cumēriyaruṭiyaṉa’ eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟiṉār. mēlum ārāycci naṭattiyatil, pāpilōṉiyarum aciriyarum taṅkaṭku muṟpaṭṭa cumēriyariṭamiruntē kali, matam, moḻi mutaliya ellām peṟṟaṉar eṉpataṟku uriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittaṉa. 1855 il ṭeylar eṉpavar naṭattiya ārāycciyil pāpilōṉiyāvil eritu (Eridu) ur (Ur) eṉṉum cumēriya nakaraṅkaḷ veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. 1874 il nikaḻttappaṭṭa ārāycciyiṉ piṟakē cumēriyaruṭiya varalāṟu, kalikaḷ, moḻi mutaliyaṉa naṉku terintaṉa. ki.mu.2800 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ciṟpaṅkaḷum mikap paḻiya cācaṉaṅkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaku jermāṉiyar pala maṇmēṭukaḷit tōṇṭi ārāycci naṭattiṉar. acur nakaram tōṇṭappaṭṭuc cumēriyarip paṟṟiya pala uṇmikaḷ veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar amerikkar cila maṇmēṭukaḷi ārāyat toṭaṅkiṉar. 1889il nippūr tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. atil kāṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ kōvilum, pala kālaṅkaḷic cērnta 50,000 cācaṉaṅkaḷum kiṭittaṉa. ceṉṟa airōppiyap pōrukkup piṉṉar eritu, ur, kiṣ, tal, asmar, kapjē mutaliya cumēriyar nakaraṅkaḷ ārāyccikku uṭpaṭṭaṉa. avviṭaṅkaḷilirunta maṇmēṭukaḷil pala aṭukkukaḷiyuṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu.3000kku muṟpaṭṭa nākarikatti uṭiya cumēriyaruṭiya ciṟappukaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.

cumēriyar cittira eḻuttukkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. nīṇṭa uruṇṭi muttirikaḷi upayōkittaṉar; kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭiyākak karuṅkallip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. 70 aṭi uyarattil ceykuṉṟukaḷ amittu avaṟṟiṉmītu ciṟiyakōvilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉar; tari mītu periya kōvilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉar; avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil cittiraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. cumēriyar mikap paḻiya kālattil āṭṭut tōlāṭi uṭuttiyiruntaṉar; kalliṉ mītu cetukkum vēliyil ciṟantiruntaṉar. itiyum vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya ceyalkaḷiyum ceyyum poḻutu perumpālum āṭiyiṉṟi iruntaṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

cumēriyar vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ mitu cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭak kaṟṟiruntaṉar; erutukaḷum kaḻutikaḷum pūṭṭappeṟṟa vaṇṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; cempu mutaliya poruḷkaḷāl āṉa - āyutaṅkaḷi vittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ eṇṇuvatil oru kuṟippiṭṭa muṟiyik kiyāṇṭu vantaṉar;[7] orē kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭivamuḷḷa ceṅkaṟkaḷiyē ceytuvantaṉar;[8] iṟantavarkaḷit tāḻikaḷiṟ putittuvantaṉar. avarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vanta teyvaṅkaḷ palavākum. orē kōvilil payirkkaṭavuḷāṉa apu, akkaṭavuḷiṉ maṉiviyāṉa iṉaṉṉā, avartam makaṉ āka immūṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷum vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.[9] ak kōviliṉ aṭiyil cuṇṇāmpuk kallālum paḷiṅkālum āṉa 12 teyvaṅkaḷiṉ cilikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi or aṭi uyaram mutal iraṇṭari aṭivari vēṟupaṭṭiruntaṉa. iccumēriyartam moḻi turukki tamiḻ pōṉṟa oṭṭu[10] moḻiyākum.[11]

‘ur’ nakaril ārāycci

inta nakaram cumēriya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ mikac ciṟantatākum. innakarattil naṭipeṟṟa ārāycciyiṉ palaṉāka ariya kuṟippukaḷ pala veḷippaṭṭaṉa. innakaril tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ tam maṉiviyōṭu eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa kōvil oṉṟum nilamakaḷ kōvilum katiravaṉ kōyilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. nilamakaḷ kōvilil cempālāya erutum paṟaviyum kiṭittaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.mu.4300 ākum. tēr, vīṇi, irampaṅkaḷ, uḷikaḷ, malar ēntum cāṭikaḷ, poṉṉālāṉa ariya poruḷkaḷ, mutaliyaṉa eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ kallaṟiyum araciyiṉ kallaṟiyum ārāyccikku uṭpaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉip putitta iṭattil aṭutta piṟaviyil avaṉōṭu iruppataṟkeṉṟu āṭavar peṇṭir āka 60 pēr paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatum, cila erutukaḷ paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum aṅkirunta cittiraṅkaḷālum elumpukaḷālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.araciyiṉatu cavappeṭṭiyil taliyi maṟikkum poṉṉālāṉa taliyaṇi oṉṟum, kūntaliṉ mēṟpuṟam aṇiyat takka mālipōṉṟa aṇi oṉṟum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. aracaṉatu cavap peṭṭiyil kiṭantavāṟē iṅkum elumpuk kūṭukaḷ kiṭantaṉa. ēḻiyiṉ cavappeṭṭi oṉṟil maṇ pommikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

inta nakarattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ palavum.mēl varum pakkaṅkaḷil pala iṭaṅkaḷil cuṭṭappaṭum ātaliṉ, avipaṟṟi iṅku virivākak kūṟavēṇṭuvatilli. innakara ārāycci paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ palavākum. putiporuḷ ārāycciyiṉ puṇṇiya stalamāka viḷaṅkum - cumēriyar

nākarikattit teṟṟeṉa viḷakkum - innakarac ciṟappi virinta nūlkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu makiḻka.[12]

pāracīkam

yūprēṭis, ṭikris yāṟukaḷ pāyappeṟum nilap pakutikkuk kiḻakkē āpkāṉistāṉamvari uḷḷa nilapparappē pāracīkam eṉpatu. itaṉ perum pakuti pālivaṉam; atik kaṭantāl kaṇṇukkiṉiya kāṭcikaḷi nalkum kuṉṟukaḷum, malit toṭarkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṅkāṅku aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṅkup paḻiya kāla makkaṭkuriya ciṉṉaṅkaḷum eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi mutaṉ mutal 1885 il ārāycci naṭattiyavar karṉal sar heṉṟi irāliṉsaṉ eṉpavar. ivar pāracīkariṉ paṇṭi eḻuttukkaḷi aritiṉ muyaṉṟu paṭittār; pāracīkarippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷi aṟintu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār.[13]

intiyāvil ārāycci

nam nāṭṭil ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ evi? mekastaṉis, pāhiyaṉ. hiyūṉ - saṅ, pḷinī, tālami pōṉṟa ayalnāṭṭār intiyāvil iruntapōtum intiyāviṟku vanta pōtum kaṇṭaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭa paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ iṉṟu maṇṇuḷ maṟintum urumāṟiyum aḻintu uḷḷaṉa. puttar kālattil ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiya paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ iṉṟu eṅkē irukkiṉṟaṉa? tamiḻ aracar āṇṭa talinakaraṅkaḷ eṅkē? ivviṭaṅkaḷil ārāycci naṭatta araciyalār taṉikkuḻu oṉṟi amittaṉar.akkuḻu intiyap putiporuḷ ārāyccikkuḻu[14] eṉappaṭum.akkuḻuvil ciṟappuṟṟa ārāycciyāḷarāka iruntavar sar aleksāṇṭar kaṉṉiṅhām eṉpavar āvar. avar vaṭintayāvil pala iṭaṅkaḷil ārāycci nikaḻttiṉār. avaratu uḻippiṉ payaṉāy nālantā, takṣacīlam, pāṭalipuram, kōcāmpi mutaliya paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ cilavum, teṉ intiyāvil amarāvati eṉṉum iṭattil viyakkattakka poruḷkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum kiṭittaṉa. mēṟkoṇṭu ārāycci naṭatta araciyalāriṭam, paṇam illi. ātaliṉ - intiya ārāycci vēli mantamākavē naṭipeṟṟu vantatu.

cintuveḷi nākarikam

intiyāviṟ kuṭipukunta āriyar mutaṉ mutal taṅkiyirunta iṭam cintuveḷiyē yākum. avviṭattiṟṟāṉ avarkaḷ rikvētam pāṭiṉar. avarkaḷ, aṅkut taṅkaṭkumuṉ irunta paṇṭi makkaḷōṭu pōr ceyya vēṇṭiyavar āyiṉar. ‘appakivar nalla nakaraṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭu māṭa māḷikikaḷil ciṟanta celvapperukkattōṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ vēḷvi ceyyātavarkaḷ; uruvavaḻipāṭu koṇṭavar; taṭṭi mukkuṭiyavar; kuḷḷarkaḷ; māyā jālaṅkaḷil vallavarkaḷ; vāṇipat tiṟami uṭiyavarkaḷ’ eṉṟellām rikvētam kūṟukiṉṟatu. ikkuṟippukaḷāl āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa intiya makkaḷ cintu veḷiyil ciṟanta paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭu vāḻntaṉar eṉṉum ceyti pulaṉākiṉṟatu. iḵtu uṇmiyē eṉpati uṇarttavē pōlum, cintuveḷiyil uḷḷa harappāvum moheñco-tarōvum aṟiñar kaṇkaṭkuk kāṭci aḷittaṉa.[15]

1920 ām āṇṭil pañcāp maṇṭilattil, māṉṭ kōmarik kōṭṭattil, rāvi - caṭlej yāṟukaḷukku iṭiyil, lāhūr - mulṭṭāṉ puki vaṇṭippātiyil harappā eṉṉum ārāyccikkuriya iṭam akappaṭṭatu. 1922il cintu maṇṭilattil uḷḷa larkkāṉāk kōṭṭattil 2100 ce.mī. uyaram uṭiya maṇmēṭu oṉṟu kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. ataṉuḷ putintuḷḷa nakaramē moheñco-tarō eṉpatu. ammēṭṭiṉmēl neṭuntūṇ oṉṟu niṉṟiruntatu. iv viraṇṭu iṭaṅkaṭkum iṭippaṭṭatolivu 640 ki.mī. ākum. iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟitaḷavu tōṇṭip pārtta poḻutu, ‘ivi ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ’ eṉpati aṟiñar aṟintu koṇṭaṉar; iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum palucistāṉattilum kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi oppiṭṭup pārtta ārāyccik kuḻuviṉ talivarāṉa sar jāṉ mārṣal eṉṉum pēraṟiñar peruviyappuk koṇṭār; apporuḷkaḷi ārāycci ceytu tām aṟinta ceytikaḷi mēṉāṭṭup pattiriki oṉṟil veḷiyiṭṭār. intiya aracāṅkam makiḻntu paṇa utavi ceytatu. ataṉāl harappāvum moheñco tarōvum cuṟucuṟuppōṭu ārāycci ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaṭkum iṭippaṭṭa palvēṟu. iṭaṅkaḷilum cintuyāṟu kaṭalōṭu kalakkum iṭam variyuḷḷa, iṭaṅkaḷilum kīrtar malittoṭari aṭuttuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟitaḷavu ārāycci vēlikaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ virivāṉa ceytikaḷi aṭutta pakutiyiṟ kāṇka.

﻿2. cintu veḷiyiṟ putiyuṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷ

cintu yāṟu

ip pēriyāṟu tipēt pīṭapūmiyil uḷḷa kilāca malikaḷil, kaṭalmaṭṭattiṟku mēl āṟu ilaṭcam ce. mī. uyarattil uṟpattiyākiṉṟatu. itaṉ arukil caṭlej eṉṉum itaṉ kiḷiyāṟum piramaputtirā eṉṉumpēriyāṟum uṟpatti ākiṉṟaṉa. itiṉiṉṟum 104 ki. mī. toliviṟṟāṉ kaṅkip pēriyāṟu uṟpatti ākiṉṟatu. ‘cintu’yāṟu tipēt, kāṣmīr nāṭukaḷil vaṭamēṟkāka 1280 ki.mī. ceṉṟu, kiljiṭ haṉsā eṉṉum iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaṭku iṭiyil teṟku nōkkit tirumpip pañcāp maṇṭilatti aṭikiṉṟatu. 80 ki.mī. teṟkē oṭiya piṉṉar, āpkāṉistāṉattu yāṟākiya ‘kāpūl’ yāṟu itaṉuṭaṉ aṭṭāk eṉṉum iṭattiṟku arukil kalakkiṉṟatu. aṭṭākkukku 752 ki. mī. teṟkē, pañcāp maṇṭilatti maruta nilamākat tikaḻac ceytu varukiṉṟa jīlam, cīṉāp, rāvi, piyās, caṭlej eṉṉum aiyāṟukaḷum cintuvil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. cintu āṟu kaṭaliṟ kalakkum iṭattil uḷḷa pukār nilam (Delta) 3000 caturak kal parappuṭiyatu. cintu yāṟu aṭittukkoṇṭu varum ērāḷamāṉa maṇalum maṇṇum vantu cērvatālum, cintuyāṟu aṭikkaṭi taṉ pōkki māṟṟikkoḷvatālum āṟṟu mukattil uḷḷa kiḷi yāṟukaḷ aṭikkaṭi tam pōkki māṟṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉa. cintu yāṟṟiṉ nīḷam ēṟakkuṟiya 3200 ki. mī. ākum.itaṉ pāyccal peṟṟuḷḷa nilam 960,000 ca.kimī ākum. āṇṭil mē mātam mutal ākasṭ mātamvari imaya maliyil uḷḷa paṉikkaṭṭikaḷ urukuvatāl, cintu yāṟu aḷavu kaṭanta veḷḷap perukkeṭuttu mikka vēkamāka ōṭi varum. ivveḷḷattāl uṇṭāṉa aḻivukaḷ pala; aḻinta nakaraṅkaḷ pala; inta yāṟṟil mīṉkaḷ mikkuḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa mukkuṭiy mutalikaḷ mikutiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta vaṟṟāta vaḷamuṭiya yāṟṟāl cintu veḷi mikka viḷiccaliyuṭiya ciṟanta maruta nilamākak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.[1]

cintu yāṟu itukāṟum patiṉeṭṭu muṟi taṉ pōkki māṟṟikkoṇṭatām. iḵtu 1928 vari moheñco tarōvukku aintāṟu kaṟkaḷukku ōṭikkoṇṭiruntatu; avvāṇṭil, tiṭīreṉat taṉ pōkku māṟi, moheñcetarēvukku 1.5 ki. mī. : toliviṟkuḷ ōṭalāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl, ippoḻutu tōṇṭik kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa moheñco-tarōvukkum aṅkuḷḷa puti poruḷ kāṭcic cālikkum ūṟu nērātirukka, nūṟāyiram rūpāyc celavil 300 ce. mī. uyarattiṟkuc cuvar oṉṟi 1.5 ki.mī . cuṟṟaḷavil eḻuppuvateṉṟu intiya aracāṅkattār muṭivu ceytaṉar.

pañcāp maṇṭilam

cintu yāṟu pāyum veḷi iṉṟu pañcāp, cintu eṉṉum iraṇṭumaṇṭilaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu irukkiṉṟatu. pañcāp maṇṭilam, cintu māṟum ataṉ upanatikaḷākiya mēṟcoṉṉa aiyāṟukaḷum pāykiṉṟa vaḷamuṭiya nāṭṭakum. payir viḷiccalukkuriya cattuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ inta yāṟukaḷāl aṭittukkoṇṭu varappeṟṟu immaṇṭilam ciṟappuṟṟu irukkiṉṟatu. iṅkuppayirttoḻil nirampa naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu; kōtumi, nel mutaliya kūla vakikaḷum parutti, eṇṇey vitikaḷ, karumpu mutaliyaṉavum ērāḷamākap payirākiṉṟaṉa; kālnaṭip paṇṇikaḷ pala naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, āṉāl, kaṉipporuḷkaḷ arukiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cintu maṇṭilam

cintu maṇṭilam eṉpatu ‘tār’ pālivaṉattukku mēṟkilum, palucistāṉattiṟkuk kiḻakkilum , pañcāp maṇṭalattiṟkut teṟkilum, ‘kaṭc’ vaḷikuṭāviṟku vaṭakkilum uḷḷa nilapparappākum. itaṉ parappu ēṟakkuṟiya 53 āyiram catūrakkal ākum.[2] iṅku nel, kōtumi, pārli mutaliya kūla vakikaḷum piṟavum viḷikiṉṟaṉa. āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaṭku muṉ cintu maṇṭilam kāṭukaḷ ceṟintu iruntatu; mikunta maḻi peṟṟiruntatu: akkālattil cintu yāṟum ki. pi. 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari oṭikkoṇṭirunta ‘mahāmihrāṉ’ eṉṉum yāṟum pāyappeṟṟup peruvaḷam peṟṟatāka iruntatu. eṉiṉum, immaṇṭilam aṭikkaṭi veḷḷak kēṭṭiṟku nilikkaḷaṉāka iruntami varuntaṟkuriyatu.

kīrtar malit toṭar

immalittoṭar cintuviṉ mēṟkil irukkiṉṟatu.itaṉ mēṟkil palucistāṉam uḷḷatu. immali nāṭṭil paṇṭik kālattil palavaki vilaṅkukaḷ vāḻntiruntaṉa. aṭivārap pakutikaḷil kaṟkāla maṉitarum cempuk kāla maṉitarum vāḻn tiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. innūlil ārum ceytikaṭku immalittoṭarip paṟṟiya aṟivu iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. cintu maṇṭilattippaṟṟiya ippotuvivaraṅkaḷi immaṭṭōṭu niṟutti, cintu veḷiyiṟ kaṇṭaṟiyappeṟṟa paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷippaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷik kāṇpōm.

harappā

harappā eṉṉum paṇṭi nakaram pañcāp maṇṭilattil iraṇṭu yāṟukaṭku iṭiyē amintiruttalāl, mikap paḻiya kālattil uyarnta nākarikam vāynta paṭṭaṇamāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. innakaram maṇmēṭiṭṭup pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. atikārikaḷ itaṉ arukil pukivaṇṭip pāti pōṭṭa poḻutu, appātikku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷi, immaṇmēṭṭit tōṇṭi uḷḷē irunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi liruntu eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭaṉar. itaṉāl,harappā nakaram citinta niliyi eytiyatu; itaṉ muḻu nilimi irunta vitatti nām iṉṟu aṟiyakkūṭavilli. epar aleksāṇṭar kaṉṉiṅhām eṉṉum ārāycci nipuṇarkkuc cittira eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭa cila muttirikaḷiyē innakaram īntatu. atu mutal innakaram ārāycciyāḷar kavaṉattit taṉpāl iḻuttatu. iṅku 1920il naṭipeṟṟa ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāy iṅku vāḻntavarākak karutappaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ kallaṟi oṉṟum maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum vēṟu cila muttirikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. atē camayattil 1922 il cintu maṇṭilattu larkkāṉāk kōṭṭattil uḷḷa moheñco-tarō eṉṉum nakarattiṉ oru pakuti tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. aṅkuc cila poruḷkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ oṉṟāka iruttali aṟinta ārāycci nipuṇarkaḷ, iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum mummuramāka ārāycci ceyyalāyiṉar. eṉavē, harappā nakarattil pukivaṇṭippāti amittōrāl toṭappaṭātirunta pakutikaḷ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.

paṇṭi harappā nakarattiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu 4 ki.mīṭṭarākalām. aṅku āṟu maṇ mēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ periyatu 29,100 ce.mī.nīḷam, 23,400 ce.mī. akalam uṭiyatu. mēṭukaḷiṉ uyaram 750 ce. mī. mutal 1,800 ce. mī. aṭivari iṭattiṟku ēṟṟavāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. harappā eṭṭu aṭukkukaḷiyuṭiya nakaram: aḵtāvatu eṭṭumuṟi putuppikkappaṭṭa nakaram. itaṉ kālam ki. mu. 3500-ki.mu. 2,750 eṉṉalām. itu moheñco-tarōvi viṭac ciṟitu muṟpaṭṭatu.[3]

mātō carup vāṭs eṉṉum pēraṟiñar innakara ārāycciyil pērukkam kāṭṭiṉār; pala poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya viyattaku ceytikaḷi intiyap putiporuḷ ārāycci niliya veḷiyīṭukaḷil veḷiyiṭṭār; iṟutiyil harppāvippaṟṟiya ārāycciyi iraṇṭu pakutikaḷāka aṇmiyil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. avar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷ piṉ varum pakutikaḷil āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭum ātaliṉ, iṅkuk kūṟātu viṭappaṭṭaṉa.

moheñco-tarō (1922-23il naṭipeṟṟa ārāycci)

itu maṇ mūṭupaṭṭu irunta āriyar varuvataṟku muṉpu mika uyarnta nākarikattuṭaṉ irunta nakaramākum. iḵtu iṉṟu cintu āṟṟiṉ mēṟkē irukkiṉṟatu. itaṉic cuṟṟilum vēṟu cila maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 2100 ce. mī. uyaramuḷḷa oṉṟaṉmītu peḷatta stūpi oṉṟu kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ataṉik kaṇṭa pāṉarji eṉṉum ārāycciyāḷar. ataṉ cuṟṟuppuṟattit tōṇṭum vēliyil īṭupaṭṭār. ammaṇ mēṭṭiṉuḷ nāṟkōṇamuḷḷa muṟṟam oṉṟik kaṇṭār; ammuṟṟattic cūḻ irunta muppatu ciṟṟaṟikaḷik kaṇṭār. avaṟṟuḷ or aṟiyil akkaṭṭiṭattiṉ kālattik kuṟippaṉapōlac cila nāṇayaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi kuṣāṉ aracarkālattavi vācutēvamaṉṉaṉāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭavi. pāṉarji mikka makiḻcci aṭintavarāy mēlum maṇmēṭṭi akaḻntār. ammēṭu tarimaṭṭa aḷavukku veṭṭappaṭṭa pōtu, aṅkoru cittira eḻuttukkaḷikkoṇṭa muttiri kiṭittatu. avarmēlum nilatti akaḻnta pōtu, iraṇṭu muttirikaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttār.

immūṉṟu muttirikaḷum ārāyccik kūṭatti aṭintaṉa. atē kālattil harappāvil ārāycci nikaḻttiya irāy pakatūr tayārām sahṉi eṉṉum pēraṟiñar, harappāvil kaṇṭeṭutta cittira eḻuttukkaḷikkoṇṭa muttirikaḷi ārāyccik kūṭattiṟku aṉuppiṉār. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta muttirikaḷiyum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiyum naṉku cōtittu, avaṟṟiṉ orumippāṭṭi uṇarnta ārāyccik kuḻuttalivar sar jāṉ mārṣal viyappuṟṟār; avaṟṟōṭu palucistāṉattil kiṭitta poruḷkaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭiruttalik kaṇṭu peruviyappuṟṟār. aṉṟu (1924) mutal moheñco-tarō maṇ mēṭukaḷ tam meyyuruvi mella mella ulakattiṟku uṇarttalāyiṉa.

stūpattiyuṭiya maṇmēṭu

peḷatta stuāpattik koṇṭa maṇ mēṭṭiṟkuk kāla kal toliviṟṟāṉ moheñco-tarō nakarattikkoṇṭa maṇ mēṭu irukkiṟatu. stupattic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa maṇ mēṭukaḷ ēṟakkuṟiya 750 ce mī uyaramuṭiyaṉa. moheñco-tarō nakarattiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷ inta maṇmēṭukaṭkuḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttākum. stupattik koṇṭa maṇmēṭu uḷḷa iṭamē, moheñco-tarō ceḻippuṭaṉ irunta kālattilum ciṟanta iṭamāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum: ataṉ aḻiviṟku 2500 āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarum avviṭam uyariya niliyil iruntamiyāṟṟāṉ, peḷattarkaḷ aṅku stuāpiyi eḻuppiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. anta maṇ mēṭṭil stupiyum peḷattar paḷḷiyum naṉku eḻuppappaṭṭu iruttaliṉ, avaṟṟi ivvamayam eṭukkakkūṭavilli. eṉiṉum, avi nāḷaṭivil eṭukkappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil eṉṉa ariya poruḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavō, aṟintavar yāvar?

1925-1934 vari naṭipeṟṟa ārāycci

aṟiñar pāṉarji stāpaṅkoṇṭa maṇmēṭṭil ciṟitiṭattit tōṇṭiyapiṉṉar, iṉṟiya moheñco-tarō nakaram putiyuṇṭirunta maṇ mēṭṭit tōṇṭum paṇiyil irāvpahatūr kē. eṉ. tīkṣit eṉpavar. muṉintār. avarukku utaviyāḷar palarum iruntaṉar. aṉivarum ceyta ārāycci vivaraṅkaḷ car jāṉ mārṣal mēṟpārviyil mūṉṟu pakutikaḷiyuṭiya peru nūlāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.[4] 1927il ivvārāyccik keṉṟē ṭākṭar ī. jē. hec. makkē eṉpavar amarttappaṭṭār. avar ippakutiyil ārāycciyi naṭattiṉār; tam ārāycciyiṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟippaṟṟi virivāṉa nūlkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷār.[5] avarukkuppiṉ, moheñco-tarōvil putitāka vikkappaṭṭuḷḷa poruṭkāṭcic cāliyiṉ talivarākiya kē. eṉ. pūri eṉpavarum, kyū. em. moṉīr (Q.M.Moneer) eṉpavarum vēṟu cilarum ārāyccip paṇiyil iṟaṅkiṉar. ipperumakkaḷiṉ iṭiviṭā uḻippāl, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ maṇṇuḷ maṟintu kiṭanta mānakaramākiya moheñco-tarōviṉ pattil oru pākam veḷip paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appakutiyiliruntu ulakatti viyappāl tiṭukkiṭac ceyyum pala ariya vivaraṅkaḷ veḷippaṭṭaṉ.

kuṟippiṭattakka ceytikaḷ

maṇṇuḷ maṟinta mānakarattippaṟṟiya ariya vivaraṅkaḷ aṭuttuvarum pakutikaḷil virivākak kūṟappaṭumātaliṉ, īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭattakka cilavaṟṟiyē kūṟutum:

innakaram harappāvippōlap pala aṭukkukaḷi uṭiyatu. aṟiñar ituvari ēḻu aṭukkukaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar; ‘avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyilum pala aṭukkukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, nīr ūṟum iṭmāka iruppatāl avaṟṟit tōṇṭik kāṇal iyalātupōlum! eṉṟu kūṟi varuntukiṉṟaṉar.

immānakaram harappāvip pōla aḻivuṟavilli. nakara amippu, terukkaḷiṉ amippu illaṅkaḷiṉ amippu ivi kaṇṭu iṉpuṟat takkavi. varici variciyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa illaṅkaḷ, māṭamāḷikikaḷ, maṇṭapaṅkaḷ, nīrāṭum kuḷam, kaḻitīrppāti, aḵtu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷa uyariya muṟi. iṉṉa piṟavum kaṇṇik kavarvaṉavākum. oviyaṅkaḷum cittirak kuṟiyōṭukūṭiya eḻuttukkaḷum koṇṭa kaṇakkaṟṟa muttirikaḷ aṟiñari meymaṟakkac ceytaṉa. pala niṟaṅkoṇṭa palavaṭivaminta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, pommikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ, paṇpaṭṭa maṇṇālum cuṇṇāmpuk kallālum aminta palavakipporuḷkaḷ, caṅku-cippi-paṇpaṭṭa maṇ. uyarnta kaṟkaḷ ivaṟṟālāya aṇi vakikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ulaka ārāycciyāḷar tam kavaṉattit tampāl īrttaṉa. ip poruḷkaḷiṟ pala, nām muṟpakutiyiṟ kūṟiya aciriyā, pāpilōṉiyā, mecopoṭṭōmiyā, ēlam, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭitta poruḷkaḷip peritum ottiruttalik kaṇṭa ārāycci aṟiñar tiṭukkiṭṭaṉar. ivvariya poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷatu kālam ēṟak kuṟiya ki.mu.3250-ki.mu.2750 āka irukkalām eṉa matippiṭṭaṉar. immānakaram paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi ittuṭaṉ niṟutti, iccintu veḷiyilēyē putiyuṇṭu kiṭakkum piṟa nakaraṅkaḷip paṟṟik kavaṉippōm.

cāṉhu-tarō

aṟiñar eṉjī.majūmtār, cintu āṟṟiṉ kiḻakkuppuṟattil oru putiya iṭattik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. apputiya iṭattil mūṉṟu maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. anta iṭam 30000 ce. mī. nīḷamum 2100 ce.mī. akalamum uṭiyatu, maṇmēṭukaḷ mūṉṟum 300, 510, 570 ce.mī. uyaram iruntaṉa. anta iṭam oru kālattil ‘periyatoru nakaramāka iruntiruttal kūṭum’ eṉpatu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṟatu. aṟiñarkaḷ ataṟkuc cāṉhu-tarō eṉṟu peyar iṭṭuḷḷaṉar. aṟiñar majūmtār, cāṉhu-tarōvil ārāycci nikaḻttiya aḷavil, vevvēṟu vakiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷār.

aṅkuḷḷa vīṭukaḷ pala ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavi; cuṭṭa ceṅkal koṇṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭaviyum cila uḷa. aṅku akaḻappaṭṭa variyil 300 poruḷkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ irattiṉa maṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi. immaṇikaḷmītu ‘8’ pōṉṟa vaṭivam kāṇappaṭṭatu. aṅkuc cippiyālāṉa vaḷiyal tuṇṭukaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. oru vaḷiyalil iraṇṭu tuḷikaḷ iruntaṉa. aḻakiya pantu oṉṟu vaḷinta kōṭukaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭatu. muttirikaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil iru maṉitar villum ampum ēnti niṟpatupōlac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avarkaḷ arukil oru maliyāṭu niṟpatu pōlak kāṇappaṭṭatu. viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. ciṟu tēr uruḷikaḷum uṭinta tērkaḷ iraṇṭum maṇṇil ceytu cūḷi iṭappaṭṭavi. erumittali oṉṟu cevvaṇṇam pūcik kaḻuttil tuḷiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. itu pōlat tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭa kuraṅku kāṇṭā mirukam mutaliyaṉavum kiṭittaṉa. paṟavi oṉṟu mikka aḻakāyc ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ōr ūtukuḻal naṉṉiliyil kāṇap paṭṭatu. ivi aṉittum cenniṟam pūcappaṭṭavi ākum. ivi tavirak kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyta ‘tarippeṇ’ tēvatiyiṉ patumi oṉṟu kiṭittatu. iḵtu 15 ce. mī. uyaramuḷḷatu. aṅku 198 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa avi pala niṟaṅkaḷil palvaki oviyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ orupuṟak kippiṭiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ orupuṟak kippiṭiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭa pāṇṭam oṉṟē ciṟantatākum. avaṟṟuṭaṉ cempuk katti, vaḷiyal tuṇṭu, uḷi, īṭṭimuṉi mutaliyaṉavum nirampak kiṭittaṉa.

cāṉhu-tarōvil kiṭitt poruḷkaḷ, moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittavaṟṟukku oppākavē irukkiṉṟaṉ. aṅkuk kiṭitta tāḻikaḷil tīṭṭappeṟṟa oviyaṅkaḷ moheñco-tarō oviyaṅkaḷi ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟil maramum maṟṟoṉṟil veḷḷāṭum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa iraṇṭu muttirikaḷ aṅkuk kiṭittaṉa. eṉavē, cāṉha-tarōvi mēlum naṉku akaḻntu ārāycci nikaḻttiṉāl, paṇṭik kālattiṟku uriya poruḷkaḷ pala kiṭittal kūṭum. āyiṉ, apputiya iṭatti akaḻntu pārkkum urimiyi intiya aracāṅkattāriṭamiruntu, or amerikka ārāyccik kaḻakattiṉar peṟṟuviṭṭaṉar. iṉi akkaḻakattiṉar. tam poruṭcelavil avviṭattu ārāyccippaṇi āṟṟuvar pōlum!

lohuñ-co-tarō

cintu āṟṟiṉ mēṟkuk kariveḷiyil, piyārōkōtattiṟku arukil moheñco-tarōvukku 96 ki.mī. teṟkil oru periya maṇmēṭu iruppatākat terintatu. avviṭam irunta maṇmēṭu 27,000 ce.mī.nīḷamum 18000 ce.mī. akalamum 690 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭiyatu. ammaṇmēṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyit tarimaṭṭam variyil tōṇṭi ataṟkuk kīḻum 330 ce.mī. vari aṟiñar majūmtār cōtaṉi purintār. aṅkuc ceṅkaṟkaḷālākiya - kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku aṭiyil avaṟṟukkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattucciṉṉaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantavi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷākum. ivi cenniṟam pūcappaṭṭavi. accenniṟattiṉ mītu kaṟuppu vaṇṇattil cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.ittakiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala moheñco-tarōvilum kiṭiait tuḷḷami kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu, ippāṇṭaṅkaṭku mēṟpuṟattil kāṇappaṭṭa vēṟuvakip pāṇṭaṅkaḷ piṟkāla nākarikattic cērntavi ākum eṉṟu majumtār karutukiṉṟār. ākavē, ip pakutiyilum iru vēṟupaṭṭa nākarikam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu karutattakkatu. piṟa poruḷkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi kaḷimaṇṇālāya erutup paṭivaṅkaḷ ākum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kaḻuttil tuḷiyiṭppaṭṭuḷḷatu. mukkōṇa vaṭivil aminta kaḷimaṇ appaṅkaḷ, uyarnta kaḷimaṇṇālāya vaḷiyalkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta palavaki maṇikaḷuḷ mākkal maṇikaḷ palavākum avi taṭṭi vaṭivattil amintuḷḷaṉa. ammaṇikaḷ moheñco-tarōvilum mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. mākkallālāṉa muttiri oṉṟum kiṭittatu. ataṉmītuḷḷa eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ moheñco-tarōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa muttirikaḷil uḷḷa eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiyē ottuḷḷami viyappūṭṭuvatākum.

ivvaṇṇam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa putiya maṇmēṭu irunta iṭam mohāñco-tarō eṉṉum peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. anta iṭaṅkaḷil ārāyccit toṇṭāṟṟiya aṟiñar majumtār piṟitōr uṇmiyiyum veḷippaṭutti yuḷḷār. aḵtāvatu, ‘akkāla makkaḷ, kuṉṟukaḷiṉ aṭip puṟaṅkaḷ vacippataṟku ēṟṟa taṭpa veppa niliyuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyum taṟkāppu araṇ uṭiyavikaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar’ eṉpatē ākum.[6]

itu cintu maṇṭilattil ‘ṣahaṭaṭpūr’ eṉṉum ūrukku vaṭamēṟkil 6.5 ki.mī. tolivil uḷḷa periya maṇmēṭākum. itaṉ arukil 0.5 ki. mī. tolivil vēṟoru maṇmēṭu irukkiṉṟatu. moheñco-tarō maṇmēṭṭiṉ mītu irukkum stūpam pōṉṟa peḷatta stūpi oṉṟu iṅkum irukkiṉṟatu. inta stūpam uḷḷa maṇmēṭṭic cuṟṟilum ciṟiya maṇmēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉittum koṇṭa periya maṇ mēṭētakañcotarō eṉpatu. itaṉ parappu aintu ēkkar ākum. maṇmēṭṭiṉ uyaram 600 ce.mī. mutal 1200 ce.mī. ākum. iṅkuc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ 10 ce.mī. kaṉamuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuc cittiram tīṭṭappeṟāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē mikutiyākak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. immaṭ pāṇṭac citivukaḷum ceṅkaṟkaḷumē ippakutiyil peruvāri yākak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. cil ceṅkaṟkaḷ 28 ce.mī. caturamuṭiyaṉa; 5.5 ce. mī. kaṉamuṭiyaṉa. uyartaramaṇikaḷ iraṇṭu kiṭittaṉa. inta iṭatti mēlum akaḻntu ārāycci ceyyavilli. cittiram tīṭṭap peṟāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ iruttalik kāṇiṉ, iṅku mikap paḻiya nakaram iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.[7]

cak pūrpāṉi siyāl

harappāvukkut teṉkiḻakkē ēṟakkuṟiya 20.5 ki. mī. tolivil piyās āṟṟiṉ ularnta paḻiya paṭukki oṉṟil kāṇappaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷi aṟiñar vāṭs eṉpavar akaḻntu pārttār. anta iṭam ‘car pūrpāṉisiyāl’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṟatu. aṅku ērāḷamāṉa uṭinta maṭpāṇṭa oṭukaḷum, ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭikaḷum, aṭippuṟam kuvintuḷḷa nīr aruntum ēṉaṅkaḷum, niṟan tīṭṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭac citivukaḷum, uruṭṭu vaṭivāṉa ēṉaṅkaḷum, mōtiraṅkaḷum, pīṭaṅkaḷ mītu vikkum kalaṉkaḷum, muṭṭi vaṭivak kōppikaḷum, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷum, vaḷiyalkaḷum, kuraṅku aṇil, kompuṭiya erutu, kallāl kaṇkaḷ vikkappeṟṟa mayil ākiya paṭivaṅkaḷum, taliyaṟṟa peṇ paṭivaṅkaḷum, kātaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya ciṟṟurukkaḷum, iruntaṉa. aṅku mēlum pala niṟaṅkaḷuṭiya cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇ maṇikaḷ, vaḷiyalkaḷ, mākkallāl ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ, irattiṉak kal maṇikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum kiṭittaṉa. potuvil avviṭattil nikaḻttiya ārāycciyiṉ mūlam aṅkuk kiṭittirukkum poruḷkaḷ aṉittum moheñco. tarō harappā ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nākarikattiṟku iyintaviyākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu aṟiñar vāṭsiṉ karuttākum.[8]

ali murāt

cintu veḷiyil ārāyccikkuriya maṟṟōr iṭam ali murāt eṉpatu. aṅkuc carittira kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭu periya maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ nīḷam 33000 ce.mī, akalam 33000 ce.mī. uyaram 810 ce.mī. ākum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉ uyaram 750 ce. mī. ākum. ataṉ teṉ pakutiyit tōṇṭiya poḻutu kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kiṇaṟu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉ uṭpuṟak kuṟukkaḷavu 20 ce. mī. ākum. akkiṇaṟṟi aṭuttu vaḷivāṉa kaṟcuvar oṉṟu 12.5 ce.mī. uyarattil kāṇap paṭṭatu. ataṉ nīḷam 5100 ce.mī. eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu kōṭṭikkaminta matiṟ cuvarāka iruntirukkalām. accuvarkku uṭpaṭṭa iṭattil pala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iruntamik kuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi yāvum kaṟkālatti niṉippūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avviṭattil kiṭitta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa pala iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷip pōlavē vaṇṇamiṭappaṭṭum cittiram tīṭṭappaṭṭum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avviṭattil kaḷimaṇ appaṅkaḷ āyirak kaṇakkil kiṭittuḷḷami peritum viyappūṭṭu vatākum.iviyaṉṟi, muḻuvēlippāṭu amintuḷḷa pīppāy vaṭivam koṇṭa uyartarak kal oṉṟu 5 ce.mī.nīḷattil kāṇap paṭṭatu. itē vaṭivaṅkoṇṭacemmaṇi oṉṟum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭatu. avviṭam muḻuvatum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭiṉ, vēṟu pala poruḷkaḷum piṟavum aṟital kūṭumeṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[9]

pāṇṭi vāhi

ippeyaruṭiya ciṟiya kirāmam larkkāṉākkōṭṭattil kīrtar malittoṭariṉ aṭivārattil uḷḷatu. itaṉ arukil itaṉ peyariyē koṇṭi maṇmēṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atu 13500 ce. mī. nīḷamum 7500 ce. mī. akalamum 630 ce. mī. uyaramum uṭiyatu. eṉiṉum, ataṉ paḻiya aḷavu mikap periyatāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ammaṇ mēṭṭil kuṟippiṭṭa mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷil ārāycci naṭipeṟṟatu. mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷum piṟavum avviṭattum kiṭittaṉa. ammaṇmēṭum muṉ collappaṭṭamēṭukaḷippōlavē muḻu ārāyccikku uṭpaṭumāyiṉ, pala uṇmikaḷ veḷiyākum.[10]

amri

amri eṉpatu karāccik kōṭṭattil vaṭakku elliyil cintu āṟṟiṉ kariyil uḷḷa ciṟṟuār ākum. cintu, āṟṟil pirayāṇam ceyta parṉas eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñar cumār 100 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē, ivviṭattippaṟṟik kīḻ varumāṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār: “amri eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ iṉṟu viḷaṅkum ciṟṟur, oru kālattil mikap periya nakaramāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itu cintuviṉ veḷḷattiṟku aṭikkaṭi iriyāki maṟintiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉiṉum, iṉṟuḷḷa kirāmattiṉarukil 1200 ce.mī. uyarattil maṇ mēṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉit tōṇṭip pārppiṉ pala uṇmikaḷ veḷippaṭal kūṭum”.

ivvaṟiñar oru nūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉ iṅṅaṉam eḻutic ceṉṟami iṉṟu uṇmi ākiviṭṭatu. ivar āppi maṇmēṭu, kirāmattiṉ vaṭamēṟkil amintuḷḷatu. ataṉ arukil vēṟu pala maṇ mēṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭac paṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila maṇmēṭukaḷil cuṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaccitivukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.avviṭatti ārāycci ceyyac ceṉṟa majumtār oru maṇmēṭṭil 1500 ce. mī. nīḷamum 360 ce. mī. akalamum 150 ce. mī āḻamum uḷḷa kuḻi citivukaḷ kāṇap ceṅkaṟkaḷāl ākiya oṉṟit tōṇṭip pārttār. mūṉṟu kaṟcuvarkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ teṉpaṭṭaṉa. ārāycciyā ḷar accuvar kaḷiṉ aṭimaṭṭam vari tōṇṭavē, avviṭam tarimaṭṭattiṉum kīḻnōkkic ceṉṟatu: akkuḻiyiliruntu 253 poruḷkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cittiram tīṭṭappaṭṭa viḷiyāṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷ, maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, patumikaḷ, vaḷiyal tuṇṭukaḷ, maṇikaḷ, maṇ appaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ciṟappittukkūṟat takuvaṉa. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ 214 kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.ivi palavaki niṟaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. anniṟaṅkaḷukkumēl mīṉ cetil, vaṭṭa malar, māvili, aracili, alakil malaruṭaṉ viḷaṅkum mayil, caturaṅkappalaki, muṭṭi, paṭṭikaḷ, paṭṭikaḷukkuḷ mukkōṇa vaṭivam mutaliya ōviyaṅkaḷ paṟpala vitamākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. potuvāka, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cenniṟamē pūcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittavaṟṟi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.

piṟa poruḷkaḷil nīṇṭu uruṇṭa pacci maṇikaḷum, kumiḻpōṉṟa maṇikaḷum kuṟippiṭat takkavi. immaṇikaḷ mītu vaḷiyaṅkaḷ variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vaḷiyal tuṇṭukaḷ 25 kiṭittaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟil cenniṟap puḷḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.patumikaḷuḷ citint tērkaḷ 12 kiṭittaṉa; erutuppatumikaḷ 8 kiṭittaṉa. or erutut tali kaḻuttil tuḷiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭatu. ittalikkuriya uṭal kāṇappaṭa villi. ivi tavira, 10 maṇ appaṅkaḷ kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ moheñco-tarō nākarikattuṭaṉ peritum ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[11]

kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉ

‘civālik malikku aṭippuṟam uḷḷa ‘ampālā’ kōṭṭattil ‘rupār’ nakarukku arukil, cintu āṟṟiṉ kiḷiyākiya sṭlej āṟṟiṉ paṭukkiyil irukkum kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉ eṉṉum ciṟṟuāric cārntuḷḷa maṇmēṭṭi aṟiñar vāṭs akaḻntu ārāycci nikaḻttiṉār. ammēṭu 12 ēkkar parappuḷḷatu; 360 ce. mī. mutal 900 ce.mī. vari uyaramuṭiyatu. aṅku avar kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟi, harappāvilum moheñco-tarōvilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭitta poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttapōtu, avi peritum oṉṟupaṭṭa orē nākarikattiṟku uriyaviyāka iruntaṉa. kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉil kiṭitta kaḷi maṇṇālāya ceṅkaṟkaḷ, kaḷimaṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ, appaṅkaḷ, mōtiraṅkaḷ, curikkuṭukki pōṉṟa nīrkkuvaḷikaḷ, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ vikkum pīṭaṅkaḷ, maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, ivaṟṟiṉ uṭinta pakutikaḷ, vaḷiyal tuṇṭukaḷ, ciṟu niṟik kaṟkaḷ, muṭṭi vaṭivattil irunta nīr aruntum kuvaḷikaḷ mutaliyaṉa cintu veḷimakkaḷ nākarikattiyē oruvāṟu oppaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.[12]

nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷ

cintu āṟṟiṉ kiḻakkuk karimītuḷḷa ‘kirppūr’ camattāṉattilum, kattiyavāriluḷḷa ‘limpṭi’ camattāṉattic cērnta, ‘araṅpūr’ eṉṉum iṭattilum, larkkāṉāvukku arukil uḷḷa ‘jukār’ eṉṉum iṭattilum, kājiṣā eṉṉum iṭattilum ivvaki ārāyccip paṇi naṭipeṟṟatu. innāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum nikaḻttiya ārāyccit toṇṭiṉ palaṉākak kiṭitta paṇṭip poruḷkaḷi nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟiṟkum moheñco-tarō, harappā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta poruḷkaḷukkum, iṭiyē ciṟu vēṟupāṭum illi eṉalām. ivi muḻuvatum cintu veḷi nākarikattuṭaṉ iyintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cintu veḷiyil maṭṭum oru nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷum paḻiya carittira kālattiṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa.ivaṟṟi ārāycci ceytu pārppiṉ, iṉṟuḷḷa intiya varalāṟu aṟputa māṟutali aṭiyum eṉpatil aiyamilli.
