﻿3. piṟa maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉ puti poruḷkaḷ

ārāycci iṭaṅkaḷiṟ pāyum āṟukaḷ

intiyāvil ārāyccikkuriya piṟa iṭaṅkaḷi aṟiyumuṉ, avviṭaṅkaḷ amintuḷḷa āṟṟu veḷikaḷip paṟṟi aṟital iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. ātaliṉ, cintu nīṅkalākavuḷḷa kaṅki, narumati,kōtāvari,kiruṣṇi, kāviri, tāmiraparaṇi, periyāṟu eṉṉum yāṟukaḷip paṟṟiyum avi pāyum iṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum īṇṭu mutalil kuṟippiṭuvōm.

kaṅki

imayamaliyiṉ teṉ paḷḷattākkukaḷiliruntu puṟappaṭum ‘pākīrati alakaṇṭā’ eṉṉum iraṇṭu ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ kalappē kaṅki yāṟu ākum. iḵtu allakapāti aṭiyumvari mikka nīrōṭṭam uṭiyataṉṟu. allakapātil mikap periya yāṟākiya yamuṉi eṉpatu kaṅkiyiṟ kalakkiṉṟatu. aṅkiruntu kaṅkiyāṟu aikkiya maṇṭilattilum vaṅkāḷa maṇṭilattilum pāyntu vaṅkakkaṭaliṟ kalakkiṉṟatu. itaṉ motta nīḷam 2490 ki.mī. ākum. itil kalappuṟum yāṟukaḷ-cartā, irām kaṅkā, kumṭi, kōkrā, cōṉi, kaṇṭakkuci eṉpaṉa. itaṉ caṅkamukat tuṟiyil uḷḷa pukar nila veḷi 128 mutal 352 ki. mī. akalamuṭiyatu. itaṉ pāyccalip peṟum iṭam 39,000 caturak kaṟkaḷ ākum. itu mikap paḻiya kāla mutalē ciṟappuṭiyāṟākak karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itu pāykiṉṟa nilam kaṅki veḷi eṉappaṭum. itaṉ kariyōrattil irunta paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ (vaṭa) maturi, keḷacāmpi, pirayāki, vāraṇāci (kāci) mutaliyaṉa.

cintu veḷiyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya āriyar nāḷaṭivil kaṅkiyamuṉi yāṟukaṭku iṭiyil uḷḷa nilappakutiyilum kuṭiyēṟiṉar. aṅkaṉam avarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟum poḻutu aṅku vāḻntirunta paṇṭi makkaḷōṭu pōriṭṭaṉar. “kiruṣṇaṉ eṉpavaṉit talivaṉākak koṇṭa ‘kiruṣṇar’ (kaṟuppar) yamuṉik kariyil vāḻntiruntaṉar. avaruḷ aimpatu āyiram pēri āriyar talivaṉāṉa intiraṉ koṉṟāṉ”[1] eṉṟu rikvētam kūṟuvatiliruntu, kaṅki veḷiyiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷil paṇṭi makkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar[2] eṉṉum uṇmiyi uṇaralām.

narumati-tapati yāṟukaḷ

ivi vaṭa intiyāviṟkut teṉ elliyākavuḷḷa vintiya-cātpūrā malit toṭarkaḷiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mēṟku nōkkic ceṉṟu arapik kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. naṭu intiyāviṉ teṟkil uḷḷa malik kōṭṭaṅkaḷum, naṭu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭakkil uḷḷa malikkōṭṭaṅkaḷum muṟiyē vintiyacātpūrā mali nāṭukaḷiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa. ivviru malit toṭarkaṭkum iṭiyē ciṟanta yāṟākiya tarumati oṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟatu.inta irumalit toṭarkaḷic cārnta nāṭukaḷil, pala vakippaṭṭa malivāṇar vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. immali nāṭukaḷil, aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ mikkuḷḷaṉa ivaṟṟik kaṭantupōtal iyalāta ceyal, pōka virumpupavar, kariyōraṅkaḷ vaḻiyākavum kaṭal vaḻiyākavum pōkalām. immalip pakutikaḷāṟṟāṉ vaṭa intiya varalāṟu vēṟākavum teṉ intiya varalāṟu vēṟākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa.

teṉṉāṭu

vintiya-cātpūrā malittoṭarkaṭkut teṉpāṟpaṭṭa nilappakuti teṉṉāṭu allatu ‘ṭekkāṉ’ eṉappaṭum. im mēṭṭup pakuti kiḻakku nōkkiyum teṉkiḻakku nōkkiyum carintuḷḷatu. ataṉāl makā nari, kōtāvari, kiruṣṇi, kāviri eṉṉum periya yāṟukaḷ kiḻakku nōkki ōṭi vaṅkak kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. inta yāṟukaḷ pāyum nilappakutikaḷ vaḷamuṭiyaṉavākum makkaḷ tokuti mikkuṭiyaṉavākum. inta yāṟukaḷ mēṟku malit toṭarcciyil uṟpattiyāvaṉa.

kōtāvari

intap periyāṟu 1440 ce mī nīḷamuṭiyatu. itaṉ camaveḷiyil arici, pukiyili, eṇṇey vitikaḷ, karumpu mutaliyaṉa peruvāriyāka viḷikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi aṭuttuḷḷa kāṭukaḷil tēkka maraṅkaḷ mikutiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta yāṟṟiṉ neṭunturam hitarāpāt nāṭṭil aṭaṅki iruttalāl, inta yāṟṟāl uṇṭākum perumpayaṉi annāṭē peṟṟuvarukiṉṟatu.

kiruṣṇi

itu 1280 ce mī nīḷa muṭiyatu. 180 ce. mī. tolivu pampāy maṇṭilattil oṭippiṉpu hitarāpāttiṟkuṭ pukuntu, piṉṉarc ceṉṉi maṇṭilatti aṭikiṉṟatu. itaṉ pāyccalip peṟṟa maruta nilaṅkaḷil nel mutaliya palavakip poruḷkaḷ vaḷamuṟap payirākiṉṟaṉ.

kāviri

inta yāṟē tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ ciṟappup peṟṟatākum. itu mēṟku malit toṭaril tōṟṟamāki, micūrnāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē tamiḻ nāṭṭi aṭintu vaṅkak kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟatu. itaṉ nīḷam 760 ce. mīṭṭarākum. itaṉ payaṉi aṭintuvarum nilam 76,800 ca. ki. mī parappuṭiyatu. itu tañcāvūrk kōṭṭattil kaṭalōṭu kalakkiṉṟatu. itu poṉṉik koḻikkum perumi yuṭiyatātaliṉ, ‘poṉṉi eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.

tāmiraparaṇi

inta yāṟum poṉ koḻikkum perumi peṟṟatu. itu tirunelvēlikkōṭṭattiṟ pāykiṉṟatu. itaṉi aṭuttuḷḷa maruta nilaṅkaḷ mikka viḷiccali nalkukiṉṟaṉa. itu pāyum “tirunelvēlik kōṭṭattil paṇṭip pukaḻpeṟṟa koṟki mutaliya pāṇṭiyar nakaraṅkaḷ maṟintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

pēriyāṟu

itumēṟku malittoṭarcciyil tōṉṟi mēṟku nōkki ōṭi arapik kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟatu; tiruvāṅkūr nāṭṭil tōṉṟik kocci nāṭṭiṟ pāykiṉṟatu.

āṟṟu veḷikaḷil paṇṭi makkaḷ

intiyāvil uḷḷa inta yāṟukaḷ pāyap peṟṟuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum mali nāṭukaḷilum paḻiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum putiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum cempukkāla maṉitarum veṇkalakkāla maṉitarum irumpukkāla maṉitarum kirāmaṅkaḷiyum nakaraṅkaḷiyum amittuk koṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉpatu kūṟāmalē viḷaṅkum. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭitta vaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉi, accāṉṟukaḷi oraḷavu muṟiyē kāṇpōm.

aikkiya maṇṭalattup puti poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvum harappāvum tōṇṭik kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku muṉṉarē-cintuveḷi nākarikam paravi irunta uṇmi veḷippaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē-kaṅki āṟṟup pāyccal nāṭukaḷil cempiṉālāya vāṭkaḷum, varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kattikaḷum, maṉita uruvampōlac ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. maṉita uruvam pōṉṟa poruḷ moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta muttirikaḷ cilavaṟṟuḷ kāṇappaṭum maṉita uruvam pōṉṟē iruntaṉa.

jeṉaral kaṉṉiṅkām eṉpārum piṟarum kaṅki āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷākiya kājippūr, kāci ākiya kōṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷil nikaḻttiya ārāycci mūlam, cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa karuvikaḷ maṭṭum iṉṟip pala oviyaṅkaḷum civappuk kaṟkaḷālāya maṇikaḷum, cintu āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷil kiṭittavaṟṟi otta pala poruḷkaḷum veḷippaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vāraṇāci kācik kōṭṭattil cila cempuk karuvikaḷum cakkimukkik kaṟkaḷum kiṭittaṉa. ivipōlavē, kaṅki āṟṟiṉ etir kariyiluḷḷa kājippūrk kōṭṭattilum pala poruḷkaḷ kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa.

keḷacāmpi tōṉṟiya kālam

aikkiya maṇṭilattiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil paṇṭikkālap poruḷkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ōriṭattil oṉpatu vāyccikaḷ uṭpaṭap patiṉāṟu ceppuk karuvikaḷ kiṭittaṉa; irupuṟamum avvāṟu kaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya cempālāya ampu muṉikaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa.vaṭamaturiyil oriṭattil irunta maṇmēṭṭit tōṇṭiyapōtu cempālāya pala vāyccikaḷum ampu, vēl, pōṉṟa eṟipaṭikaḷum kiṭittiṉa. ‘ṣājahāṉpūr’ kōṭṭattil cempālāya īṭṭimuṉi oṉṟu kiṭittatu. intac cempu īṭṭi ippoḻutu ilakṣmaṇapuri-poruṭ kāṭci niliyattil kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu. itupōla, ‘iṭṭavā’ kōṭṭattil kiṭitta piṟitōr īṭṭi ippoḻutu piriṭṭiṣ poruṭkāṭci niliyattil uḷḷatu. piṟitōr iṭattil maṉita uruvam pōṉṟa karuvi oṉṟum nīṇṭa kaṉatta vāḷ oṉṟum kiṭittaṉa. kaṅki āṟṟaṅkariyil uḷḷa ōriṭattil pala ampu muṉikaḷum kalluḷikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. kāṉpūr kōṭṭattil cila iṭaṅkaḷil cempālāya maṉita uruvaṅkaḷum ampu muṉikaḷum īṭṭikaḷum kalluḷikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. piṟitōriṭattil ciṟuttuk kuṟukiya uḷi oṉṟu kiṭittatu. iḵtu 12.5 ce. mī. uyaramuṭiya kalluḷi ākum. intac cempuk kālattiṟṟāṉ keḷacāmpi eṉṉum varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭiya nakaram kaṭṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu irāvpakatūr tīkṣajit karutukiṉṟār. sar jāṉ mārṣal eṉṉum aṟiñar ‘pīṭṭar’ eṉṉum ūril oru pakutiyi maṭṭum akaḻntu ārāycci naṭattiyataṉ mūlam, kallālum cempālum ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar.

pīkār maṇṭilattup puti poruḷkaḷ

pīkār maṇṭilattil kaṅkiyum cōṉiyāṟum kalakkum iṭattil mōriyar talinakaramāṉa pāṭalipuram amintiruntatu. atu carittirap pukaḻpeṟṟa paṇṭi nakaramākum.aḵtu iṉṟuḷḷa pāṭalipurattiṟkum hājippūrkkum iṭiyil irunta nakaram. ataṉ perum pakuti kaṅkiyāṟṟāl cētamuṟṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟu aṅkuḷḷār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum, ārāycciyāḷar nilatti akaḻntu pārttapōtu, acōkaṉatu araṇmaṉiyiṉ citivukaḷiyum piṟa ariya periya poruḷkaḷiyum kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar. ippoḻutuḷḷa putiya pāṭalipura nakara capiyiṉar aṇmiyil puti kaḻinīr vaṭikālkaḷi nilattiṟku aṭiyil amippataṟkāka nilatti akaḻnta pōtu mōriya vamicattavar.innāṭṭiāṭcipurinta kālattiṟku uriyapaṇṭik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar.[3]

naṭu maṇṭilattu veḷḷit tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ

naṭu maṇṭilattil or iṭattil orē camayattil 424 cempuk karuvikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. cōṭṭā nākapurippakutiyil cempuk kālattiṟku uriya iṭaṅkaḷ pala iṉṉum akaḻntu pārkka vēṇṭupaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. immaṇṭilattil ōr ūril, tiruttamākac ceytu muṭikkappaṭāta uḷikaḷ mūṉṟu uṭpaṭap pala ciṟu cempuk karuvikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar ivaṟṟi mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. cōṭṭā nākapuriyiṉ teṉpākattil cempuc curaṅkaḷ iruppatāl, anta veḷiyil mēlum pala iṭaṅkaḷil paṇṭik kālap poruḷkaḷō aṉṟic cempuc curaṅkaṅkaḷō iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. itē maṇṭilattil piṟitōriṭattil 162 veḷḷit tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa cempukkālattiṟku uriya poruḷkaḷuḷ ivitām mikac ciṟantavi eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. jappalpūril veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa kaltaccar uḷi oṉṟu kiṭittatu. ivvuḷi nīṇṭu vaḷintatāyk kūriya muṉi uṭiyatāy irukkiṟatu.[4]

orissāvil iraṭṭik kōṭarikaḷ

orissā (uriyā) maṇṭilattic cērnta ‘mayūrppañc’, camattāṉattil irupuṟamum kūrmi uṭiya cempālākiya iraṭṭik kōṭarikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. inta mākāṇattil kanta kiri (Khanda Giri) eṉṉum malip piratēcattil aḻakiya vēlippāṭaminta malik kukikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta iṭam ārāyccikku mikavum uriyatākum.[5] vaṅkāḷattilum cempālāṉa karuvikaḷ cila kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. ippakutikaḷil virivāṉa muṟiyil ārāycci naṭipeṟiṉ varalāṟu eḻutat toṭaṅkappaṭṭa kālattiṟkum ataṟku muṉṉiya kālattiṟkum uḷḷa uṟavupaṟṟiya tuṇukkaṅkaḷ palavum, cempuk kālattil nam nāṭṭiṉ evvep pakutikaḷil makkaḷ evvenniliyil iruntaṉar eṉpatum, ataṟkuppiṟ paṭṭa kāla vivaraṅkaḷum kiṭikkappeṟalām eṉpatu aṟiñarkaḷ karuttākum. -

ṭekkāṉ

intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapuṟattuc ceyti ivvaṇṇam iruppat teṉpuṟattik kavaṉippōm; narumati, tapati ākiya āṟukaḷ pāyum iṭaṅkaḷ payaṉ aṟṟaviyā? ceyppūr, pikkāṉīr, jeysālmar, kāmpē vaḷikuṭā, purōc ākiya iṭaṅkaḷic cūḻntuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ aṟiñar kavaṉatti īrkkavilliyō? ‘illi’ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭal muṭiyātu. inta iṭaṅkaḷilum, cintu veḷi nākarikam paravi iruntatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ pala kiṭittal kūṭum aṟiñar ituvari ippakuti kaḷil tīviramāka ārāycci nikaḻttavilli. eṉiṉum, īṇṭum cintu veḷi nākarikam paravittāṉ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cintu āṟṟup pāṅkil vāḻnta makkaḷ kaṉi poruḷkaḷukkākavum vāṇipam paṟṟiyum intap pakutikaḷil vāḻntirunta makkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭuṟavu peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. kaṅki āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷiviṭa intappakutikaḷil, ikkarutti meyppippataṟku vēṇṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ pala veḷippaṭum eṉa ārāycciyāḷar nampukiṉṟaṉar.appaṇṭik kālac civappumaṇ kaṟkaḷum, ceṅkaṟkaḷum, civappu maṇṇālāya maṇikaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, piṟa perruḷkaḷum karampē, purōc ākiya tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ mūlamākavē, veḷināṭṭārukku aṉuppappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu cintu veḷi nākarik kālattil inta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ veḷināṭṭu vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭup peruñ ciṟappup peṟṟaviyākavē amintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāyciciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

vintamalip pakutikaḷil putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ kiyāṇṭa nalluruvil amintuḷḷa iyantiraṅkaḷ, kal uḷikaḷ, tāḻikaḷ, uralkaḷ, palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa piṟa poruḷkaḷ, uyartara maṇikaḷālāṉa poruḷkaḷ, cittira varṇa vēlippāṭaminta kalaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kukikaḷiliruntum maṇ mēṭukaḷiliruntum eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. kattiyavāril uḷḷa ‘limpṭi’ nāṭṭil ‘araṅppūr’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kiṭitta ērāḷamāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittavi pōlavē iruttal kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. ippakutiyil mēlum virivāṉa ārāycci naṭipeṟiṉ, cintu veḷi nākarikam narumati-tapati yāṟukaḷ pāykiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum paraviyirunta paṇpu pulaṉākum.

parōṭāvil putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ kiyāṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ peruvāriyākak kiṭittaṉa.avi cittiratti yuṭiya palaniṟap pāṇṭaṅkaḷ cittiram illāta pāṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉa iruvaki yākum. avaṟṟuḷḷum karaṭumuraṭāṉavi, maḻa maḻappu uṭiyavi, kaṇṇukku iṉiyavi, cittiram tīṭṭap peṟṟavi eṉa nāṉku vaki uṇṭu. ivi nērē kaṇṭu iṉpuṟat takkaviyē aṉṟi eḻuttāl aṟintu iṉpuṟuttakkavi alla.

hitarāpātil pala iṭaṅkaḷil ittkiya pal vakippaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ‘pallārik kōṭṭattil uḷḷa irāmaṉ turkka malikaḷic cārnta tārvār-pāṟikaḷiṉ mēṟkuppuṟa elliyil, putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ vittirunta palavaki niṟaṅkaḷ koṇṭa kaḷimaṇ poruḷkaḷum piṟavum peruvāriyākak kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa’, eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

micūr nāṭṭilum muṉ coṉṉa pala niṟappāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; paḻiya kaṟkāla makkaḷum putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷum kaṭṭiviṭṭuc ceṉṟa oru vakik kaṟkōvilkaḷum cavak kuḻikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

ceṉṉi maṇṭilam

pallāri, aṉantappūr, kaṭappi, karnūl eṉṉum nāṉku kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mēṟcoṉṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭaṉa vākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peruṅ kaṟkaḷi aṭukkik kaṭṭappaṭṭa palavakic camātikaḷ pallārik kōṭṭattil mikkuḷḷaṉa. aṅku itukāṟum 2000kku mēṟpaṭṭa camātikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉa jē.ci.pireḷaṉ eṉṉum ārāycciyāḷar aṟintuḷḷaṉar. aṉantappūrkkōṭṭattil ‘hintuppūr’ eṉṉum puki vaṇṭi niliyattukkuk kiḻakkē 7 ki. mī. tolivil ‘temakeṭiyapallē (paḷḷi)’ eṉṉum ciṟṟuār uḷatu.ataṉ kiḻakkilum teṟkilum pala ciṟiya kuṉṟukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi ēṟattāḻa orē toṭarcciyāka uḷḷaṉ. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kukikaḷākac ceyyappaṭa muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpataṟkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku nāṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa kalluḷikaḷ, vēṟu pala kaṟkaruvikaḷ, cuttikaḷ mutaliyaṉa akappaṭṭaṉa. kuṇṭakkal pukivaṇṭi niliyattiṟku arukil maraccippu oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. atu putiya kaṟkālap peṇkaḷatu kūntal alaṅkārattiṟku vēṇṭap paṭṭatākum. aḵtu eṭukkappaṭṭa iṭattil matippukkuriya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ palavum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. karnūl kōṭṭattil uḷḷa pillacurkkam kukiyil paṟkaḷāl āṉa tāyittukaḷ kiṭittaṉa.[6] ittakiya mali muḻikaḷi ārāyvatāl pala paṇṭip poruḷkaḷ kiṭikkum. ‘malippiratēcaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil añcātu nuḻintu, kukikaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, ārāycci nikaḻttutal aṟiñar kaṭaṉākum eṉṟu puṭ (Foote) eṉṉum ārāycciyaṟiñar aṟintuḷḷaṉar. kiruṣṇā cillāvil ‘kuṭivāṭā’vil irukkum periya maṇmēṭukaṭkuk kiḻakkup pakkattil, putiya kaṟkālattukku uriya cittiram tīṭṭap peṟṟa ‘kutir’ oṉṟu akappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ētēṉum oru kūlap poruḷic cēmittu vikkappeṟum periya ‘kutir’ ākum. itil paṭṭiyāka niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appaṭṭikku iṇṭayil palavaṇṇa vēlippāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṇpaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ kāppukaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil akappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉmītu vēlippāṭukaḷ niṟintuḷḷaṉa. cippi vaḷiyalkaḷ palavaki vēlippāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiyaṉavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

ceṉṉikku aṭutta pallāvarattil kaḷimaṇṇum maṇalum kalantu ceyyappeṟṟa (terra cotta) maṉita uruvam oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ kālkaḷ kuṭṭiyāka iruntaṉa. aṅkup piṇam putikkum tāḻikaḷ palavuṅ kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. cēlattil pallāri mutaliya kōṭṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷip pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷē mikutiyākak kiṭittaṉ, cenniṟa maṇṇālāya peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa. ippeṇ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kūntal alaṅkāram ārāycciyāḷar karuttik kavarntatu. kūntal pala curuḷ curuḷākat talimuḻuvatum curuṭṭappaṭṭu, accuruḷkaḷ mītu uyarnta cīppukaḷ cerukappaṭṭamātiri kāṇappaṭṭatu.[7] ivvaki alaṅkāram koḷḷum peṇkaṭkuk kaḻuttaṇi (aṭṭiki pōṉṟatu) avaciyamākum. peṇkaḷ uṟaṅkumpoḻutum akkūntaṟ curuḷkaḷ aviḻāmal iruttaṟku kaḻuttaṇiyē vēṇṭaṟpālatu. iṅṅaṉam utavum kaḻuttaṇi oṉṟu micūrppakutiyil kāviriyāṟṟiṉ kari arukil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu.cēlam kōṭṭattil kuṭicikaḷip pōṉṟa piṇappeṭṭikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkapṭṭaṉa.ivipōṉṟavi pala kūrccarattilum akappaṭṭaṉa.[8]

makiḻccikkuriya maṇmēṭu

kōyamutturk kōṭṭam pallaṭam tālukāvil uḷḷa ceṭṭipāḷiyam eṉṉum ūrukku aṇmiyil vayal veḷiyil oru maṇmēṭu irukkiṉṟatu. ataṉ nīḷam 2550 ce.mī; akalam 2130 ce. mī. ataṉ uyaram mikak kuṟivākum.ataṉ oru puṟam ciṟiya kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷiṉ kuviyal irukkiṉṟatu. ammēṭuḷḷa iṭittil maṭpāṇṭac citivukaḷum pala vaki ulōkap poruḷkaḷum elumpukaḷum kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. mēṭṭiṉ naṭu iṭattil kaṟkuki pōṉṟu oṉṟu kāṭci aḷittukkoṇṭu iruntatu. ārāycciyāḷar ataṉit tōṇṭip pārtta pōtu itu kallālāṉa piṇakkōvil[9] eṉpatu terintatu. aḵtu oru nīḷa catura aṟiyākum; 240 ce.mī. nīḷamum 142.5 ce.mī akalamum 15 ce. mī. uyaramum uṭiyatu; peruṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ataṉ kūriyāka aminta kal 322.5 ce. mī nīḷamum 20 ce. mī. akalamum 30 ce. mī. uyaramum koṇṭiruntatu. akkōviliṉ uṭpuṟattil palavakip paṇṭipporuḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. maḻamaḻappāṉa kaṟuppuniṟa maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷ nalla niliyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭami kuṟippiṭattakkatu: nīr aruntum cempuk kōppi oṉṟu kiṭantatu; vēṟoru kōppiyiṉ mūṭimītu āṭu (māṉ?) niṟpatu pōlac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; ik kōppi pāracīkattil “lūrittāṉ’ eṉṉum iṭattil kiṭitta veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷ niṉippūṭṭuvatu’ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.ivi kiṭitta maṇmēṭṭukku arukil vēṟu pala maṇmēṭukaḷ āṅkāṅku irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi aṉittum ārāyccikku uṭpaṭumāyiṉ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉ intiya nākarikatti aṟiya vacati uṇṭākum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[10]

putukkōṭṭiyil tāḻikaḷ

putukkōṭṭiyit tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ puti poruḷ ārāyccikkuriya tiruppatiyākac collalām. aṅku muṉ coṉṉa palavaki maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. aṅkup putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷuṭiya piṇam putikkum palavaki muṟikaḷik kāṇalām. maṇ tāḻiyil uṭal uṭkāra vikkap paṭṭu, ataṉmēl maṇal pātiyaḷavu parappapṭṭu, avvaḷavukku mēl ariciyum piṟa kūlapporuḷkaḷum koṇṭa taṭṭoṉṟu vikkappaṭṭut tāḻi putikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. attāḻiyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil iṟantavaṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkaruvikaḷum piṟavum vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tāḻi, maṇal nirampap parappappaṭṭu mūṭiyiṭṭup putikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tāḻiyip putitta kuḻi, maṇal pōṭappaṭṭup pāṟiyāl mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ap pāṟiyiṉ mītu mīṇṭum maṇalkoṭṭip pāti muṭṭivaṭivam pōṉṟa pāṟi oṉṟāl mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.ippāṟiyic cuṟṟi oru muḻam uyaramuṭiya kaṟkaḷ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. putukkōṭṭi camattāṉattil nīrppaciyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiṉ arukirla āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tāḻikaḷ putikkappeṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷ pala kaṟkaḷ vari uḷḷaṉa. ivviṭaṅkaḷ, cuṟṟilum uḷḷa tariyiviṭc ciṟitu uyarntuḷḷaṉa. ippakutikaḷi mēlākat taṭṭiṉāl avaṟṟiṉ oci naṉku kēṭkum. ittāḻikaḷ uruvattil palavāṟu vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; cila 120 ce. mī. uyaramum 105 ce. mī. kuṟukkaḷavum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. [11]cila tāḻikaḷ kōṭukaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. irumpuk kālattuc cavap peṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭāka pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟil piṇamum maṟṟoṉṟil avviṟantavar payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷum vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intat tāḻikaḷ uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷic cōtittup pārttāl, paṇṭikkāla makkaḷippaṟṟic cuvi payakkum ceytikaḷ pala naṉkuṇaralām.

tiruccirāppaḷḷi, tirunelvēlik kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum mēṟcoṉṉa palavakiyāṉaviyum pala niṟamuḷḷaviyumāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. tirunelvēlik kōṭṭattic cērnta āticca nallūril tāmiraparaṇiyāṟṟuk kariyōram, ēkkar oṉṟil ēṟakkuṟiya āyiram tāḻikaḷ vītam 114 ēkkarkaḷil tāḻikaḷ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟuḷ irumpu, veṇkalam, poṉ ivaṟṟālāṉa poruḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. kuṭicit tāḻikaḷ (hut urns) cila cintu maṇṭilattil uḷḷa pirāmaṇa pāttilum kiṭittuḷḷami kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ṭekkāṉ pakuti vaṭa intiyāviviṭap paḻamiyāṉatu; vaṭa intiyā kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyiruntapōtu ṭekkāṉ pīṭapūmi taṉittiruntatu. ātaliṉ, atuvē putiporuḷ ārāyccikkup peritum poruttamuḷḷa iṭam ākum eṉṟu nila nūl nipuṇar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.

tiruvitāṅkūr

itu paṇṭic cēranāṭṭiṉ perum pakutiyākum. ippakutiyil malikaḷ pala. ammalikaḷ mītum, avaṟṟi aṭuttuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum paḻiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum putiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum irumpukkāla maṉitarum iruntamikkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittavaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉa. ammakkaḷ, iṟantavar uṭalaṅkaḷi erittu uṇṭāṉa cāmpaliyum elumpukaḷiyum perum tāḻikaḷil aṭittu avviṟantavar payaṉpaṭuttiya palavakik karuvikaḷum pāṇṭaṅkaḷum piṟavum attāḻikaḷuḷ aṭakkip putittaṉar. avviṭaṅkaḷukkumēl oḻuṅkāṉa pāṟik kaṟkaḷi naṭṭuc ciṟu maṇṭapaṅkaḷ amittuḷḷaṉar. ittakiya maṇṭpaṅkaḷ pala maliyariyar, mutuvar eṉṉum nākarikamaṟṟa makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōriṭattil naṭattiya ārāycciyil akappaṭṭa tāḻiyiṉ vāy 37.5 ce.mī. aṅkulam kuṟukkaḷavu uṭiyatu; 192 ce. mī. uyaramuṭiyatu. ataṉuḷ eṭṭuc ciṟu tāḻikaḷum, uṇavuṇṇum kalaṉkaḷum, nīraruntum kalaṉkaḷum, cāṭikaḷum, caṭṭikaḷum iruntaṉa. aviyāvum karuppuk kaḷimaṇṇālum civappuk kaḷimaṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭavi. attāḻiyiṉ mēṟpuṟattil 75 ce.mī nīḷamuḷḷa vāḷ oṉṟu kiṭittatu.ataṉ kippiṭi aḻintatu pōlum! attāḻikkuḷ iraṇṭu irumpu īṭṭit talikaḷ kiṭantaṉa. piṟitōr iṭattil veṇkala viḷakkoṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkppaṭṭatu. ittakiya paḻaṅkālap putipporuḷkaḷippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ ārāycciyāḷarāl avvappoḻutu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.

ṭekkāṉ pakuti taṉittiruntatā?

paḻiya putiya kaṟkālaṅkaḷilum ivaṟṟi aṭuttut tōṉṟiya irumpuk kālattilum kiṭitta poruḷkaḷip piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭitta poruḷkaḷōṭu vittu ārāykiyil, ṭekkāṉ pakuti veḷi nāṭukaḷōṭu toṭarpuṟṟu iruntatu eṉṉum pēruṇmiyi uṇaralākum: (1) pallāvarattil akap paṭṭa maṉita uruvam irākkil uḷḷa ‘pāktāt’ nakaril kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa uruvaṅkaḷi ottuḷḷatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. (2)kāl koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷ pala heṉṟik ṣlīmaṉ eṉpārāl tōṇṭi eṭukkappeṟṟa ‘ṭrāy’ nakarattil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. (3) micūrp pakutiyil kaṇṭu eṭukkappeṟṟa ‘svastikā’ vaṭivil aminta poruḷ, ‘ṭarāy’ nakaril akappaṭṭa svastikaḷ vaṭivattip peritum ottuḷḷatu.[12] (4) ‘ceṭṭipāḷiyattiṟ kiṭitta māṉ allatu āṭu niṟpatāka uṭiya kōppi mūṭi oṉṟu pāracīkattillūristāṉil kiṭitta veṇkalap poruḷkaḷi ottuḷḷatu. iṉṉa piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl appaṇṭik kāla makkaḷ āciya, āppirikka, airōppiya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ aṟiviyal tuṟiyilum vāṇipat tuṟiyilum nerukkamāṉa toṭarpu koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum pēruṇmi pulaṉākum.[13]

paṇṭit tamiḻ nakaraṅkaḷ

‘vaṭintiyāvi viṭatteṉṉāṭu paḻimi vāyntatātaliṉ, meyyāṉa ārāyccikku uriya iṭam teṉṉāṭē ākum’ eṉṟu, nilanūṟ pulavar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉpati muṉṉarē kuṟippiṭṭōm allamō? ātalāl, muṉ coṉṉa teṉpakutikaḷil ārāycci ceytal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. mēlum, tamiḻakatti āṇṭa muṭiyuṭi mūvēntar tam talinakaraṅkaḷil pērārāycci naṭattal mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. cōḻanāṭṭil uṟiyūr, tiruvārūr, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉpaṉa mikka paḻimi vāyntaṉa. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam neṭuṅkālamāka mēṉāṭukaḷuṭaṉum kīḻnāṭukaḷuṭaṉum vāṇipam ceytu vanta nakaramākum. atu ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaṭalukku iriyāṉateṉṟu maṇimēkali kūṟukiṉṟatu. tirumaṟikkāṭu eṉappaṭum vētāraṇyam paḻiya kālattil oru tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉamāka iruntatu. anta iṭattiṟkum yāḻppāṇattiṟkum neṭunturamilli iṉṟum ciṟuvaṇikar tam poruḷkaḷip paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟik koṇṭu yāḻpāṇat tīvukaṭkuc ceṉṟu vāṇipam ceykiṉṟaṉar. at tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamum ārāyccikkuriyatākum. atip pōlavē pāṇṭiyar paḻampatiyākiya alivāy (kapāṭapuram) kaṭal kōḷāl aḻivuṟṟateṉṟu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uriyum, cilappatikāra uriyum ceppukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭṭiṉam ciṟanta nākarikattōṭu irunta pāṇṭiyar paḻampati eṉṟu vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. irāmāyaṇattiṉ kālam ēṟakkuṟiya ki.mu.1000kku muṟpaṭṭateṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, irāmaṉatu cama kālattavarākiya vāṉmīki muṉivar coṉṉa ki.mu.1000kku muṟpaṭṭa ‘alivāy’ irunta iṭam eṅkē? eṉpati aṟiñar tēṭippārttu, avviṭatti ārāytal avaciyamaṉṟō? koṟki, muttukkup peyarpōṉa paḻampaṭṭiṉamākum. iṅkiruntutāṉ vētakāla āriyarkku muttukkaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum’ eṉpar tiru.pi.ṭi.cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār. eṉavē, koṟkiyum mikka pḻimi vāynta pakutiyākum.

cēra nāṭṭil arapikkaṭalil tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāka viḷakkamuṟṟu iruntavi muciṟi, toṇṭi mutaliyaṉa. aṅkiruntu urōmap perunāṭṭiṟkum piṟa iṭaṅkaṭkum miḷaku, tantam, tēkku, akil, vācaṉipporuḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cumār ki.mu.1000il vāḻntirunta cālamaṉ maṉṉarkku mayil mutaliyaṉa aḷitta iṭam cēra nāṭākum. eṉavē, ittuṟimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷum paḻimi vāyntaṉavē ākum. kocci nāṭṭil aḻinta niliyil uḷḷa vañci mānakaram paḻiya cērar talinakaramākum. atu ki.mu.-vilēyē peruñ ciṟappu vāynta nakaramāka iruntatu. ataṉiyum akaḻntu ārāycci purital naṟpalaṉi aḷikkum. ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu jillāvil uḷḷa mahāpalipuram oraḷavu paḻimi vāynta tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉam. aṅku arēpiyāviliruntu kutirikaḷ iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.

iviyellām ki.mu.4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē peruñ ciṟappuppeṟṟa paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ ākum. iv viṭaṅkaḷil virivāṉa ārāycci nikaḻttal cintu veḷi ārāyccikkup peruntuṇi purivatākum! tamiḻakattup paḻimiyiyum kaṇikkap pērutavi purivatākum.

caṅku cāṉṟu pakarum

cintu āṟṟup pāyccal peṟṟa pakutikaḷil nikaḻttiya ārāycciyiliruntu, aṅku vāḻntirunta makkaḷ caṅkip peritum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. ‘intac caṅkukaḷ cintuveḷi makkaḷukku eṅṅaṉam kiṭittaṉa? ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam avarkaḷukku eṅkiruntu vantatu? caṅkip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟi eṅkup peruki iruntatu? eṉpavaṟṟik kavaṉittōmāyiṉ, cintu veḷi makkaḷukkum tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷukkum irunta toṭarpu teṟṟeṉat teriyum.

intiya nāṭṭiṉ teṉkiḻakkukkōṭiyil(tamiḻ nāṭṭil) vāḻntirunta tamiḻ makkaḷiṭamiruntē caṅkukaḷ cintup piratēcaṅkaḷil vāḻntirunta makkaḷukkup pōyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[14] ‘caṅku aṟukkum kulattavar eṉṟē oru pakuti makkaḷ, tamiḻ nāṭṭil mikap piṟpaṭṭa kālam variyilum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. caṅkukaḷum muttukkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ciṟanta vāṇipap poruḷkaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉpatic caṅka nūlkaḷālum piṟa cāṉṟukaḷālum aṟiyalām.

“vēḷāp pārppāṉ vāḷaran tumitta

vaḷikaḷin toḻinta koḻuntu”





eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟu aṭikaḷ cintikkaṟpālaṉa.

mēṉāṭṭār muyaṟci

ittakiya ārāyccit toṇṭil namnāṭṭuc celvarkaḷ viruppaṅ koḷvatilli. mēṉāṭukaḷil uḷḷa celvarkaḷō iṅṅaṉam iruppatilli, putumikaḷik kāṇuvatil oru cārār īṭupaṭṭāl, toṉmic ceytikaḷi aṟivatil piṟitoru cārār īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvulakiṉ pala mūlikaḷukkum ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkuḷḷa viyattaku vivaraṅkaḷi ellām kaṇṭaṟintu, appōtikkappōtuveḷiyiṭutaṟku eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭuccelvar cilar kūṭi ōr iyakkam tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉar. avviyakkattiṉar, tāṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkum putumikaḷiyellām avvāṟu āṅkāṅkē paṭam piṭittuc ceṉṟu, avaṟṟiṟku uriya vivaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ tiṅkaḷtōṟum or itaḻāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.maṟṟoru piriviṉar ulakiluḷḷa uyarnta malikaḷ mītēṟi āṅkuk kiṭikkum aṟputa vivaraṅkaḷic cērttu veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ulakil mika uyarntatum, itukāṟum evarālum ataṉ ucciyil ceṉṟu kāṇutaṟku iyalātatumāka viḷaṅkip perumi peṟṟirukkum imayamalimītu ēṟutaṟku mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñar palar muyaṉṟuḷḷaṉar; iṉṟum muyaṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟum oru kūṭṭattiṉar kaṭaliṉ aṭippakutikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu, aṅku vāḻum uyirkaḷiṉ aṟputac ceyalkaḷiyum nikaḻccikaḷiyum aṟintu, avaṟṟiṉi veḷippaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē nilatti akaḻntu maṇṇukkuḷ maṟintu kiṭakkum pala paṇṭip poruḷkaḷi veḷippaṭutti ārāycci ceyvatilum ammēṉāṭṭu makkaḷ ārvaṅkāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.

nammavar kaṭami

mēṉāṭṭārip pōla nam nāṭṭuc celvarkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum ittakiya tuṟikaḷil ārvaṅ kāṭṭi uṟcākamum ūkkamuṅ koṇṭu uḻittāl, piṟa nāṭukaḷiviṭa nam nāṭu pala vakiyāṉa aṟputa vivaraṅkaḷi ulakukku viḷakki viyakkavikkum eṉpatil aiyamilli.

* * *





﻿4. nakara amippum āṭci muṟiyum

maṟintāriṉ maṇmēṭukaḷ

moheñco-tarō nakaram amintuḷḷa iṭattil pala maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ periyatu 117,000 ce.mī. nīḷamum 60,300 ce mī akalamum uṭiyatu. itu teṟku vaṭakkāka amintuḷḷatu. itaṟku 18000 ce mī mēṟkē itaṟkaṭutta periya maṇmēṭu oṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu. ituvum teṟku vaṭakkāka amintuḷḷatu. itaṉ nīḷam 39,500 ce mī akalam 29700 ce.mī periya mēṭṭiṟku vaṭakkilum kiḻakkilum ciṟiya maṇ mēṭukaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa.mutalil kuṟippiṭṭa iraṇṭuperiya maṇmēṭukaḷum mutalil oṉṟākavē iruntaṉavā. aṉṟi iraṇṭākavē iruntaṉavā eṉpati iṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟal iyalātu. ivviraṇṭukkum iṭiyil uḷḷa iṭattit tōṇṭip pārtta piṉṉarē muṭivu kūṟutal iyalum. ivviraṇṭu periya maṇmēṭukaḷum nakaramākavum, cuṟṟiyuḷḷa piṟa ciṟiya maṇ mēṭukaḷ nakarattic cērnta pakutikaḷākavum irukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. immaṇmēṭukaḷ ciṟitumaṅkiya cenniṟattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

āṟṟōram aminta nakaram

putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷum cempukkāla makkaḷum aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, āṟṟōraṅkaḷil nakaraṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉṟu mutaṟ pakutiyiṟ kūṟiṉōm. nām kūṟiyāṅkē, ivviru kālaṅkaḷiyum cērnta cintu nāṭṭu mak kaḷ tam vāḻviṟku uriya iṭaṅkaḷi āṟṟōraṅkaḷil amittuk koṇṭiruntami kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. moheñco tarō cintu āṟṟiṉ kariyil amintiruntatu. harappā, rāvi āṟṟiṉ kariyil amintiruntatu. marakkalap pōkkuvarattu vacati karutiyē innakaraṅkaḷ āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. moheñco-tarōvil paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷ atē āṟṟil yātoru taṭiyum iṉṟic ceṉṟu arapikkaṭali aṭiya vacatiyuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉamē veḷi nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ kappalkaḷ mūlam vantu cintu maṇṭilat tuṟimukatti aṭinta piṉ, apporuḷkaḷip paṭakukaḷ ēṟṟikkoṇṭu moheñco-tarōvi aṭitalum eḷitāṉatu. ippōkkuvaravu vacati karutiyē appaṇṭi vāṇipam pukaḻ peṟṟa makkaḷ āṟṟōraṅkaḷil aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷi amittuk koṇṭu vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum.

nakaram aminta iṭam

moheñco-tarō nakaram amintuḷḷa iṭam mikap paranta camaveḷiyākum. innakaram amintuḷḷa iṭam oru caturamil parappuṭiyatu. itil pattil oru pākamē ippoḻutu tōṇṭappeṟṟu ārāycci naṭipeṟṟuḷḷatu. tōṇṭappaṭāta pakuti 15, 29, 92, 160 ca. ce. mī. parappuṭiyatu. appakutiyum tōṇṭappeṟṟu oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil ārāycci naṭipeṟumāyiṉ, innakarattippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ uḷḷavāṟu uṇartal kūṭum. āyiṉum, iṉṟuḷḷa niliyil tōṇṭi eṭukkappeṟṟa pattil oru pākatti ārāynta aḷavil innakarattip paṟṟiya tam karuttukkaḷi ārāycci yāḷar virivākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.

terukkaḷiṉ amippu

innakarattiṉ terukkaḷ kiḻakku mēṟkākavum, teṟku vaṭakkākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. teṉkiḻakkup paruvakkāṟṟu, vaṭakiḻakkup paruvakkāṟṟu eṉpavaṟṟik kavaṉittē - nalla kāṟṟōṭatti etirpārttē itterukkaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa vātalvēṇṭum.kāṟṟunakariṉ akaṉṟaterukkaḷil vīcumpoḻutu, kuṟukkēyuḷḷa ciṟiya terukkaḷilum pukuntu ellā makkaṭkum naṟkāṟṟu nukara vacati uṇṭākal iyalpē. innōkkam paṟṟiyē ciṟiya terukkaḷ periya terukkaḷil vantu kalakkumāṟu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kāṟṟōṭṭam karuti iṅṅaṉam terukkaḷi amittuḷḷa muṟi, uyariya nākarika makkaḷ eṉap pōṟṟap paṭṭa pāpilōṉiyar nakaraṅkaḷilum kāṇakkūṭavilli eṉpatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu.

periya terukkaḷic ciṟiyaterukkaḷ orē nērāka veṭṭic ceṉṟuḷḷaṉa. oru perunteru mukkāl kal nīḷam uṭiyatu.itu nakara maṇmēṭṭiyē iraṇṭākap pirittuḷḷatu. itaṉ akalam 990 ce.mī. itu vaṇṭip pōkkuvaraviṟkē ivvaḷavu akalamāka amikkappaṭṭiruttalvēṇṭum. itaṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷilum makkaḷ naṭantu cellum naṭippātiyum amintuḷḷatu. itteruvil cintu maṇṭila vaṇṭikaḷ mūṉṟu orē variciyiṟ pōka vacati uṇṭeṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.itaṉ iṭiyiṭiyē ciṟiya terukkaḷ pala cantikkiṉṟaṉa. inneṭun teruviṉ mēṟkup puṟattil kuṟippiṭattakka periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. itiviṭap periya teru oṉṟu ciṟitaḷavē tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉāl atippaṟṟi oṉṟum ippoḻutu kūṟutaṟkilli.540 ce.mī akalamuṭiya terukkaḷ cila irukkiṉṟaṉa. 390 ce.mī. akalamuḷḷa terukkaḷ cila, 270 ce. mī. akalam mutal 360 ce mī akalam vari uḷḷa terukkaḷ pala. 120 ce.mī. akalamuṭiya cantukaḷ pala ikkuṟunterukkaḷ, neṭun terukkaḷiyum naṭuttaramāṉa terukkaḷiyum pala iṭaṅkaḷil iṇittuḷḷaṉa. potuvākak kūṟiṉ, ellāt terukkaḷukkum. iṇippu irukkiṉṟatu.

cila terukkaḷ uṭinta ceṅkal tuṇṭukaḷiyum maṭpāṇṭac citivukaḷiyum koṭṭik keṭṭippaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala terukkaḷ puḻutipaṭintuḷḷaṉa. ciṟiya terukkaḷ periya terukkaḷiviṭanaṉṉiliyil amintuḷḷaṉa. perutta vāṇipamē innilimikkuk kāraṇamākum. cila cantukaḷiṉ muṉi vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ mītu, cumitūkkic ceṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷ uṟintu ceṉṟa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cila muṉi vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ vaḷivākavē amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl poti ēṟṟic cellum vilaṅkukaḷum vaṇṭikaḷum cantiviṭṭut tirumpumpōtu vīṭṭuc cuvarip paḻutākka iṭamirātaṉṟō? mika nuṭpa aṟivuṭaṉ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷa iccuvar amippuc cumēriyar nakaramāṉa pukaḻ vāynta ‘ur’ eṉpatil amintuḷḷa cila muṉi vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpirunta ippaṇṭi makkaḷ, ivvaḷavu matinuṭpam vāykkap peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpati eṇṇa eṇṇa ārāycciyāḷar peruviyappuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

kālvāy amippu

moheñco-tarōvil kālvāy illāta neṭun teruvō kuṟunteruvō illi. kālvāykaḷ aṉittum, orē aḷavil veṭṭic cuṭṭut tēyttu vaḻavaḻap pākkiya ceṅkaṟkaḷāl amintavi. potuvāka ellāk kālvāykaḷum 50 ce. mī. āḻamum 22 ce. mī akalamum uṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ikkālvāykaḷip pōlavē illaṅkaḷil amikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ciṟiya kaḻinīrk kālvāykaḷum ittakiya ciṟanta muṟiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭē kaṭṭappeṟṟavi ākum. ivvīṭṭu vaṭikālkaḷ terukkālvāyuṭaṉ cērum iṭaṅkaḷil, catura vaṭivil ceṅkaṟkaḷ koṇṭu kaṭṭappeṟṟ ciṟu kuḻikaḷ amin tuḷḷaṉa. akkuḻikaḷ 22 ce. mī. caturamum 45 ce. mī. āḻamum uṭiyavi. akkuḻikaḷil 90 ce. mī. uyaramuṭiya tāḻikaḷ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attāḻikaḷiṉ aṭiyil ciṟiya tuḷikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. vīṭṭu vaṭikālkaḷ vaḻiyē kaḻinīruṭaṉ kuppi kaḷaṅkaḷ vantu tāḻikaḷil viḻutal iyalpu. tāḻikaḷiṉ aṭiyil uḷḷa ciṟiya tuḷikaḷ vaḻiyē kaḻivunīr toṭṭiyil nirampit teruk kālvāyil kalakkum. annīruṭaṉ vanta kuppi kūḷaṅkaḷ tāḻiyiṉ aṭiyilēyē taṅki viṭum nakarāṇmik kaḻakap paṇiyāṭkaḷ akkuppi kūḷaṅkaḷi avvappōtu tāḻikaḷiliruntu appuṟappaṭuttit tūymi ceyvar. ‘ā! icciṟanta muṟi vēṟu entap paṇṭi nakarattilum iruntatāka yām kaṇṭatilli; kēṭṭatu milli’ eṉṟu cār jāṉ mārṣal pōṉṟār kūṟip peru viyappu eytiyuḷḷaṉar. ivviyattaku muṟi, nākarikam mikunta ikkālattilum ceṉṉi, kalkattā pōṉṟa periya nakaraṅkaḷil uṇṭē tavirap piṟa paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷil illi eṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. ivvariya kālvāy amippu muṟi 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷāl kikkoḷḷappaṭṭiruntatu eṉiṉ, avar tam aṟivu nuṭpamum cukātāra vāḻvil avarkkirunta ārvamum veḷḷiṭi maliyām. ceṉṉi pōṉṟa periya nakaraṅkaḷil nilattiṉ aṭiyil cākkaṭikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, moheñco-tarōvil tarimītē cākkaṭikaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. innakarattuc cākkaṭi amippu muṟiyē nāḷaṭivil pātāḷac cākkaṭikaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷatu eṉpatum kavaṉittaṟ kuriyatu.

cuvarukkuḷ kaḻinīrk kuḻi

mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varum kaḻinīrik kīḻē uḷḷa tāḻiyiṟ koṇṭu cērkkac cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇṇālāya periya kuḻikaḷ cuvar arukil patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl cila periya vīṭukaḷil ikkuḻikaḷ veḷiyē tōṉṟātavāṟu cuvarukku uḷḷēyē amintiruttal viyappiṉum viyappākum. tāḻiyiṉ arukil uḷḷa ikkuḻikaḷiṉ vāy 8 ce. mī. akalamum 10 ce. mī. nīḷamum uṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.

mūṭappeṟṟa kālvāykaḷ

terukkaḷil oṭum periya kālvāykaḷ mītu nīṇṭa catura vaṭivil 30 ce. mī. nīḷamuṭiya ceṅkaṟkaḷ patittu neṭuka mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil 56.5 ce. mī. nīḷamuḷḷa kaṟkaḷum mūṭuvataṟkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, iṭi iṭiyē kaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālum karuṅkaṟkaḷālum, cuṇṇak kaṟkaḷālum ceyyap peṟṟa mūṭikaḷ poruttappeṟṟu irukkiṉṟaṉa. kālvāykaḷil cakati paṭintu kaḻivunīr eḷitil oṭa iyalātavāṟu taṭiyuṇṭākā tiruttalip pārttuk koḷḷavē ikkaṟkaḷ aḻuttamāka vittu mūṭappaṭāmal poruttappeṟṟu uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu eḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu.

iṭi iṭiyē perun toṭṭikaḷ

nīṇṭa kālvāykaḷukku iṭiyiṭiyē mūli muṭukkukaḷil kaḻivunīr tēṅkuvataṟkup peruntoṭṭikaḷ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu nīri vēṟu vaḻiyē koṇṭu cellum kālvāykaḷ toṭṭikaḷuṭaṉ iṇippuṇṭu irukkiṉṟaṉa. akkuṭṭikaḷiliruntu nīṇṭa tāṭikaḷi viṭṭu iru puṟattuk kālvāykaḷiyum iyaṉṟa aḷavu tūymi ceyyavē avi amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. akkuṭṭikaṭkum akaṉṟa mēlmūṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. appaṇṭik kāla ulakattic cērnta enta nākarika nāṭṭilum icciṟanta kālvāy amippu muṟi illi, illi eṉṟu ārāycci aṟiñar ovvoruvarum viyantu kūṟutal kavaṉittaṟ kuriyatākum.

matakuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ

periya kālvāykaḷ muṭivuṟum iṭaṅkaḷil 120 ce. mī. allatu 150 ce. mī. uyaramum 75 ce. mī. akalamum uḷḷa matakukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ammatakukaṭku mēl vaḷinta uttiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya kūri vēyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakiya matakukaḷiyuṭiya peruṅ kālvāykaḷ potuvāṉa nāṭkaḷil kaḻivu nīrukkākap payaṉpaṭuvatē pōṉṟu, veḷḷam varum kālaṅkaḷil avveḷḷa nīri vaṭiyac ceyvataṟkum payaṉ palṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itukāṟum kūṟappeṟṟa ellāp poruḷkaḷum aḻiyāmal uṟutiyāka iruttalikkoṇṭu ivi evvaḷavu uṟutiyākak kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpati eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷalām.

paṉmuṟi uyarttap peṟṟa kālvāykaḷ

moheñco-tarō nakarattiṉ vaḷappattiṟkum perumikkum kāraṇamāk irunta cintu yāṟē ataṉ. aḻiviṟkum kāraṇamāka iruntatu eṉṉum uṇmi, annakaram paṉmuṟi tiruttiyum uyarttiyum amikkappaṭṭiruppatiliruntu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mutalil annakaric cama tariyil amitta makkaḷ, piṉṉarc cintuyāṟṟiṉ veḷḷak koṭumikku añci, annakarattiṉ aṭi maṭṭattic ciṟitu ciṟitāka uyarttik koṇṭē pōyiṉar; piṟaku uyarnta mēṭṭil nakarattip putuppittuk kaṭṭiṉar. iṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ pala aṭukkukaḷi kaṭṭiṉar eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷarkku naṉku pulaṉākiṟatu. aṟiñar ituvari ēḻu aṭukkukaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ‘ēḻāvataṉ aṭiyilum pala aṭukkukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl aṅku nīr ūṟṟam ēṟpaṭutalāl, tōṇṭip pārttal iyalātatāka irukkiṟatu’ eṉṟu. kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar.[1] ‘mutalil amikkap peṟṟa nakarttiṉ kālam ēṟakkuṟiya ki.mu.2800 āka irukkalām; ēḻām muṟi amikkappeṟṟa nakarattiṉ kālam cumār ki.mu. 2500 āka irukkalām’ eṉṟu makkē pōṉṟa ārāycci aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[2]

ivvāṟu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ uyara uyarap paḻiya kālvāykaḷ payaṉila āyiṉa, ataṉāl putiya kālvāykaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.pala iṭaṅkaḷil paḻiya kālvāykaḷiṉ pakkac cuvarkaḷ uyarttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil paḻiya kālvāykaḷ mītē ceṅkaṟkaḷi aṭukkip putiya kālvāykaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pakutikaḷil paḻiya kālvāykaḷ iṭittut taḷḷappaṭṭup putiya kālvāykaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam kālvāykaḷ, paṉmuṟi tiruttappeṟṟum putiyaṉavāka amikkappeṟṟum uḷḷaṉa.[ku 1]

malam kaḻikka otukkiṭam

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa perumpālāṉa illaṅkaḷil malam kaḻippataṟku uriya otukkiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi iṉṟum nam nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ taḷa variciyum puṟaccuvarkaḷum cevviyākavum uṟutiyākavum amikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avi nāḷtōṟum nakarāṇmik kaḻakattār amarttiyirunta tōṭṭikaḷāl tūymi ceyyappeṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpataṟkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa. cila māḷikikaḷiṉ mēṉ māṭaṅkaḷilēyē ittakiya otukkiṭaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkiruntu malamum nīrum, cuvarukku uḷḷē allatu veḷiyē amittuk kīḻē uḷḷa tāḻiyōṭu iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kuḻi vaḻiyē kīḻē ceṉṟuviṭa vacati yiruntatu eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivvotukkiṭaṅkaḷ nīrōṭum aṟiyi aṭuttu amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi peritum iṉṟu cintu maṇṭilattil uḷḷa malam kaḻikkum iṭaṅkaḷiyē ottuḷḷami kuṟippiṭat takkatu.[3]

nakara āṭci muṟi

ikkāla intiyāvil nākarikam mikkuḷḷaṉavākak karutap paṭum nakaraṅkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇa iyalāta cukātāra muṟikaḷ 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa moheñco-tarōvil mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu peru viyappukku uriyatākum aṉṟō? ovvoru vīṭṭilum cantilum kuṟunteruvilum neṭunteruvilum cukātāra vitikaḷ kaṇṭippākak kik koḷḷappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṉal oru pōtum mikiyākātu. neṭun terukkaḷiviṭak kuṟunterukkaḷum cantukaḷum mikka tuymiyāka iruntaṉa eṉiṉ immuṟikku nērmāṟāṉa muṟiyil ikkāla nakaraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉ - akkāla makkaḷiṉ oḻuṅkumuṟiyum, kaṭṭut tiṭṭaṅkaṭku avarkaḷ aḷittu vanta peru matippum, cukātāra muṟiyil vāḻavēṇṭu meṉṟu avarkaḷ virumpi naṭantuvantamiyum, nakarāṇmik kaḻakattāriṉ āṭcit tiṟaṉum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa allavā? terukkaḷil puḻuti kiḷampātirukka nāḷum taṇṇir teḷikkap paṭṭu vantatu. pattup iitiṉintu vīṭukaṭku oṉṟāka vikkap paṭṭirunta toṭṭikaḷilēyē kuppi kūḷaṅkaḷ koṭṭap paṭṭu vantaṉa. nakarattiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil ikkālattiya potu iṭaṅkaḷ - ikkālattup pūmpoḻil (Park) pōṉṟavi - iruntaṉa eṉṟu niṉippataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.

orunakaram naṉmuṟiyil amintuḷḷatu. eṉiṉ, atu paranta iṭattil amintirutal vēṇṭum nīṇṭu akaṉṟaterukkaḷip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum: ellāp pātikaḷum vilakkiṉṟit tūymiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum kaḻinīric cevvaṉē koṇṭu cellum oḻuṅkāṉa kālvāykaḷ amintiruttal vēṇṭum. vīṭṭu acuttap poruḷkaḷi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ appuṟappaṭuttum ellā vaki vacatikaḷum naṉku amintiruttal vēṇṭum; orō vaḻi makkaḷ kalantu iṉpuṟat takka potu iṭaṅkaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum. veḷḷa nīri uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ veḷikkoṇṭu cellattakka periya cākkaṭikaḷ naṉmuṟiyil amintiruttal vēṇṭum kaḻinīrp.pātikaḷ mū ppaṭṭiruttal mikka avaciya mākum.ittakiya ellā vacatikaḷiyum peṟṟuḷḷa nakarmē cukātāra muṟiyil amikkappaṭṭa nakaram eṉappaṭum. ivvacatikaḷ aṉittum moheñco-tarōvil iruntaṉa. eṉavē, innakara makkaḷ ciṟanta nākarikattip pēṇiya perumakkaḷ eṉṉalām. innakara āṭciyiṉar, akkāla ulakil irunta piṟa nakara āṭciyiṉariviṭa mika uyarnta aṟivuṭiyavar eṉṉal mikiyākātu.

cukātāram oru pāl iruppa, nakariṉ pala pākaṅkaḷil kāvaṟ. kūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatiliruntu, nakara āṭciyiṉar nakarattip pātukātta muṟiyum naṉku viḷaṅkum. pala periya terukkaḷiṉ kōṭikaḷil ittakiya kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ amintiruntaṉa. moheñco-tarō vāṇipam mikunta nakaram aṉṟō? aṅku vaṇikar taṅkuvataṟkākap periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ arukil kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. vāṇipap poruḷkaḷic cēmittu vikka viṭutikaḷ pala iruntaṉa; veḷināṭṭu vaṇikarum uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikarum celvapperukku uṭiyavarkaḷ. ātaliṉ, poruḷukkum celvattukkum ūṟu nērātiruttaṟ poruṭṭē nakara āṭciyiṉar āṅkāṅkuk kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷi amittuk kāvalāḷikaḷi vittu, nakarattik kāttu vantaṉar.

nakarāṇmik kaḻakattil perumpālār vaṇikarākavē iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉṉi? innakaram vāṇipap. perukka muṭiya nakaramātaliṉ eṉka. mōriyar āṭcik kālattil irunta makkaḷ piratinitikaḷ aṭaṅkiya maṉṟam[4] allatu kupta maṉṉar āṭcik kālattē irunta nakarāṇmik kaḻakam[5] mutaliyavi moheñco-tarō pōṉṟa paḻiya intiya nakaraṅkaḷiliruntē tōṉṟiṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟintu viyakkiṉṟaṉar.[6]

avaciyamē aṟivi vaḷarppatu

innakaratti amitta paṇṭi makkaḷiṉ cuṟṟup puṟaṅkaḷil kīrtar maliyaṭivārap pakutikaḷil irunta makkaḷ kaṟkukikaḷilum kallāl āṉa vīṭukaḷilum vacittaṉarē aṉṟi nakaraṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭu vāḻntilar. eṉavē, moheñco-tarō, harappā pōṉṟa paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ annakara makkaḷiṉ aṟivu nuṭpattik koṇṭē amintaṉavātal vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ vēṟevariyum pārttu nakara amippu muṟiyik kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu iṉṟuḷḷa ārāycci niliyikkoṇṭu kūṟutal iyalātatu. ēṉeṉil, atē kālattil ciṟappuṟṟirunta ekiptiyar, cumēriyar nakaraṅkaḷ ivvaḷavu ciṟappuṭiyaṉavāka illāmiyē iṅṅaṉam vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākum.

piṟa nāṭukaḷil illāta nakara amippu

“nalla tiṭṭaṅkaḷ iṭṭuc ciṟanta cukātāra muṟiyil nakaraṅkaḷi amittavarkaḷ cintu maṇṭila makkaḷē āvārkaḷ. ittakiya tiṭṭam ki.mu.2000 vari ‘ur’ eṉṉum nakaril tōṉṟiyatākak kūṟal iyalātu. atē kālattiṟṟāṉ pāpilōṉiyāvilum ittiṭṭam tōṉṟiyatu. ekiptil uḷḷa ‘kahuṉ’ eṉṉum nakaril paṉṉiraṇṭām araca parampariyiṉar āṇṭakālattētāṉ itu pōṉṟa tiṭṭam tōṉṟiyatu. eṉavē, mikka pukaḻpaṭitta ekiptiyarum pāpilōṉiyarum cumēriyarum appaḻaṅkālattil kaṇṭirāta nakara amippu muṟiyē ic cintu makkaḷ kiyāṇṭiruntaṉar eṉiṉ, avarkaḷ pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka innāṭṭilēyē iruntu immuṟikaḷiṟ kitērntavarātal vēṇṭum. cumār ki.mu.1500 il intiyāvukku vanta āriyarkku muṉṉarē pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka mika uyarnta nākarikattil tiḷittiṉarātalvēṇṭum. avarkaḷatu nākarikam cumēriyar, ēlattavar tam nākarikaṅkaḷiviṭa mikavum uyarntatākum.[7]

﻿5. kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

kaṭṭiṭa amippu muṟi

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ akaṉṟa kāṟṟōṭṭam mikka terukkaḷi amittu, nakara amippil taṅkaṭkirunta paṇpaṭṭa aṟivi ulakattiṟku uṇarttiyatu pōṉṟē, kaṭṭi amippu muṟiyilum taṅkaḷukku irunta paṇpaṭṭa aṟivit taṅkaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilāka viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar. apperumakkaḷ, taṅkaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkuc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷiyum ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭutti uḷḷaṉar. maḻi, veyil, veḷḷam, puyaṟkāṟṟu ivaṟṟukku eḷitil āṭpaṭupavi kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ puṟac cuvarkaḷē ākum. ātaliṉ avvaṟiñarkaḷ, appuṟac cuvarkaḷic cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālēyē amittuḷḷaṉar. mēṟ coṉṉavaṟṟāl tuṉpuṟuttappaṭāta uṭpuṟac cuvarkaḷi ularnta cūḷiyiṭappaṭāta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl amittuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ ikkālattiya cimeṉṭi aṟiyār: kāṉkrīṭ eṉpatiyum aṟiyār. ātaliṉ, akkāla muṟikku ēṟṟapaṭi kaḷimaṇ cāntiyē cuvarkaḷiṉ mēṟpūccākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cila illac cuvarkaḷit taviṭu kalanta kaḷimaṇ cāntāl meḻukiyuḷḷaṉar. cuvar, tari, kūri immūṉṟum kaḷimaṇ cāntāl āṉavi. avarkaḷ mara uttiraṅkaḷip pōṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ mītu nāṇaṟpāy parappi, appāykaḷ mītu kaḷimaṇ cāntik kaṉamāyp pūcik kūri amittu vāḻntaṉar; uttiraṅkaḷi nuḻippataṟkeṉṟē cuvarkaḷiṉ talippuṟaṅkaḷil catura vaṭivil ciṟiya tuḷikaḷi viṭṭiruntaṉar. nām avaṟṟi iṉṉum teḷivuṟak kāṇalām. kaṭṭiṭap pulavarkaḷ cuvarkaḷiyum tariyiyum mēṟkūriyiyum oḻuṅkupaṭutta maṭṭap palakiyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. appalakikaḷ pala moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi pala aḷavukaḷ uṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ, cāḷaraṅkaḷ vāyiṟpaṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉa oḻuṅkāka amintiruntatiliruntu, akkālattuk kottarkaḷ kaṭṭiṭa amippil niṟinta aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷākat tikaḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.

aṇi aṇiyāṉa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

ovvoru teruvilum vīṭukaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu iṭiveḷiyiṉṟic cērntāṟpōlavē amintuḷḷa . āyiṉ piṟkālatatil cintunatiyil veḷḷam uṇṭāki, ataṉāl oru muṟi nakara amippukkup paṅkam ēṟpaṭṭatum, aṇi aṇiyāka amikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ cila māṟutal kaḷip peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ tarimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉam mummuṟi tari maṭṭam uyarttappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ivvāṟu tarimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭapiṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭa eḻuppappaṭṭa cuvarkaḷō vīṭukaḷō aṇi aṇiyāka iṉṟic ciṟitu uyarttum tāḻntum muṉṉum piṉṉumāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. nakara aṭimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭa piṉ pōtiya iṭam iṉmiyiṉ, ivvāṟu tāṟumākak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. toṭakkattil amintuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭa amippum, piṉṉar amintuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭa amippum kāṇpōrkku naṉku kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. akaṉṟa neṭunterukkaḷil uḷḷa illaṅkaḷi viṭakkuṟun terukkaḷil uḷḷa illaṅkaḷē naṉṉiliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa.

parutta cuvarkaḷ

perumpālum ellāk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷum paruttaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi 105 ce. mī. mutal 180 ce.mī. vari taṭittaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cintu natiyiṉ veḷḷatti etirnōkkiyē iccuvarkaḷ ivvaḷavu kaṉamāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa mēṉ māṭaṅkaḷi amikkumpoḻutu avaṟṟiṉ pārattit tāṅka vallaviyāka ivi amintiruttal vēṇṭum aṉṟō? an nōkkam paṟṟiyum iccuvarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟalām. oru periya kaṭṭiṭattiṉ puṟac cuvar 172.5 ce. mī. kaṉamuṭiyatākavum, piṟa cuvarkaḷ 105 ce. mī, 145 ce. mī, 132.5 ce. mī. kaṉam uṭiyaṉa vākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. ivi avvappōtu putuppikkap paṭṭaṉa. ātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.

neṭuñcuvarkaḷ

kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ ōraḷavil illi. periya terukkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ 540 ce. mī. uyaram uṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟun terukkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ 540ce.mī. mutal 750 ce. mī. vari uyaram uṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. innakaram tōṇṭappaṭṭa poḻutu, ārāycciyāḷar mikunta kavaṉattuṭaṉ mēṟpārvi celutti vantamiyiṉ, ikkaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ ciṟitum paḻutukku uḷḷākāmal kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi naṉku kavaṉippiṉ, tarimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭapōtellām iviyum uyarttappaṭṭu vantaṉa eṉpati eḷitil uṇartal kūṭum.

palavakik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil pala tiṟappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila iraṇṭu aṭukkum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭaviyum uṭiya māṭivīṭukaḷ cila akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷi uṭiya orē aṭukku uṭiyavi. ivvillaṅkaḷ ellām mēlē taḷam iṭappaṭṭavi; cuṟṟilum kippiṭik cuvarkaḷi uṭiyavi; mēltaḷattil peyyum maḻinīr uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē kīḻē viḻak kuḻikaḷiyuṭiyavi.ik kuḻikaḷ maṇṇālum marattālum ceyyappaṭṭavi. cila kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ aṅkāṭikaḷāka iruntavi; cila kōvilkaḷ eṉṟu karutat takkavi: pala eḷiyavar illaṅkaḷ.

eḷiyavar illaṅkaḷ

ciṟiya illaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa nāṉkintu aṟikaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi 900 ce.mī nīḷamum 810 ce. mī. akalamum uṭiyaṉa. ivi aṉittum ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭē amittavi ākum. ivaṟṟiṉ tari cāṇattāl meḻukappaṭṭu vantatu. iṅṅaṉam ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭu uṟutiyuṭiya illaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻa iyalāta makkaḷ, nakarattiṉ veḷip puṟattil uṟutiyaṟṟa poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu kuṭilkaḷ amittu vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum.

periya muṟṟamuṭiya illaṅkaḷ

cila periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil periya-akaṉṟa muṟṟam irukkiṉṟatu. ataṉic cūḻap pala aṟikaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. avvaṟikaḷ ikkālattup periya aṟikaḷ pōṉṟavi alla; eṉiṉum kāṟṟōṭṭam koṇṭaṉavākavē amintuḷḷaṉa. ovvōr aṟiyilum kāṟṟum veḷiccamum pukat takkavāṟu cāḷaram amintuḷḷatu. kāṟṟōṭṭam naṉku amintirukkat takkavāṟu vīṭṭiṉ vāyiṟpaṭiyum kūriyum uyarttik kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya illaṅkaḷ ceṉṉiyil vaṇṇārappēṭṭiyilum irāyapurattilum kāṇalām. cintu mākāṇattilum pala iṭaṅkaḷil ittakiya akaṉṟa muṟṟamuṭiya illaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.

pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ vāḻnta vīṭukaḷ

periya vīṭukaḷiṟ cila iṭiyē neṭuñcuvarkaḷ vittup pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attakiya illaṅkaḷ uṭaṉ piṟantōr pirintu vāḻvataṟkeṉṟē amikkappaṭṭavi ākum. cila iṭaṅkaḷil cilavīṭukaḷ avaṟṟiṉ tāyc cuvarkaḷukkum. aṭutta vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷukkum iṭiyil ciṟu iṭiveḷi viṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl akkāla makkaṭkuḷ cuvarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭum tolli iruntatu eṉpatum, ataṉi nīkkavē immuṟi kiyāḷappaṭṭatu eṉpatum eḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya periya vīṭukaḷil kūlaṅkaḷic cēmittuvikkum kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ tariyiṟ patikkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. cila illaṅ kaḷil māṭaṅkaḷum cuvar aṟikaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa. cila vīṭukaḷil 1ṟ kalaṉkaḷ vittaṟkuriya marap peṭ kaṅkaḷ (Cup boards) cuvarkaḷilēyē iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

celvar tam māṭa māḷikikaḷ

ivi, iraṇṭum iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa aṭukkukaḷu - muṭiya māḷikikaḷ ākum. ivi periya kūṭaṅkaḷ, akaṉṟu nīṇṭa tāḻvāraṅkaḷ, akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷ, iṭi kaḻikaḷ, ciṟiya periya vāyilkaḷ, pala aṟikaḷ ivaṟṟi uṭiyaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉ mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷ ceṅkal taḷa varici uṭiyaṉa. cila māḷikikaḷil uḷḷa mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷil paṭukki aṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa; vēṟu cila māṭaṅkaḷil paṭukki aṟikaḷ, nīrāṭum aṟikaḷ, malaṅkaḻikka otukkiṭam mutaliyaṉa amintuḷḷaṉa. mēṉmāṭaṅkaṭkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ pala iṉṟum naṉṉiliyiṟ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. avi iṉṟu nam vīṭukaḷil uḷḷa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. paṭikkaṭṭu illāta māṭaṅkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.avaṟṟukku mara ēṇikaḷ payaṉ paṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ perumpālāṉa vīṭukaḷil teruppuṟamē amintuḷḷati nōkka kīḻk kaṭṭiṭattil vēṟu palar kuṭiyiruntaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. cila vīṭukaḷil paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uṭpuṟat tākavē amintuḷḷaṉa. oru māḷikiyil iraṇṭu paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu aṇmiyilēyē amin tuḷḷaṉa. paṭikkāka viṭappaṭṭa palaki ovvoṉṟum 21 ce. mī akalamum 5.5 ce. mī. kaṉamum uṭiyatu. ﻿ paṭikkaṭṭiṉ akalam 100 ce.mīṭṭarākum. iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭu paṭikkaṭṭu vittuḷḷa illam perun talivaṉuṭiya illamākavē iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. vēṟoru periya māḷikiyiṉ muṉpuṟam 2550 ce. mī. akaṉṟuḷḷatu piṉpuṟam 290 ce mī akaṉṟuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam illattiṉ irupuṟamum paranta iṭamviṭṭuk kaṭṭum muṟi appaṇṭik kālattilēyē vaḻakkil iruntateṉpatu peru viyappukkuriya ceytiyē aṉṟō?

aracaṉatu araṇmaṉiyō?

itukāṟum tōṇṭik kaṇṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ aṉittiṉum mikap periyatāyuḷḷa māḷiki oṉṟē aṟiñar kavaṉatti mikutiyum kavarntatākum. ammāḷiki 7260 ce.mī nīḷamum 3360 ce.mī. akalamum uṭiyatu. ati aṭuttuc caṟṟuk kuṟinta aḷavu 350 ce.mī.nīḷamum 3480 ce.mī akalamum uṭiya periya māḷiki oṉṟu uḷḷatu. ataṟku aṭuttāṟpōl maṟṟoru māḷiki irukkiṉṟatu. ataṉ vaṭapuṟac cuvar 2610 ce. mī nīḷam: teṉpuṟac cuvar 2800 ce. mī nīḷam mēṟkuppuṟaccuvar 140 ce.mī. nīḷam kiḻakkuppuṟac cuvar 1465 ce. mī nīḷam akkaṭṭiṭattuḷ pala aṟikaḷum oru kiṇaṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa; ovvoru puṟattum 125 ce. mī akalamuḷḷa caturat tuṇkaḷ cila irukkiṉṟaṉa. ittūṇkaḷ akkaṭṭiṭa vāyiliṉ vaḷivukaḷit tāṅkupavi eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. akkaṭṭiṭattiṟku mēṉ māṭi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. aḵtu iṉṟu kāṇumāṟilli.

akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi aṭuttuc ciṟu viṭutikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. avi 1680 ce.mī nīḷamuṭiyaṉa. avi ovvoṉṟilum naṭuvil neṭuntuṇkaḷik koṇṭa maṇṭapamum pala kūṭaṅkaḷum uḷvaḻikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya pala aṟikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. anta aṟikaḷ cāmāṉkaḷi vippataṟkum, camiyal ceyvataṟkum, paṭukkikkum, uṇavu uṭkoḷḷavum, piṟa tēvikaṭkākavum taṉittaṉiyē amikkappaṭṭavi ākum. iṅṅaṉam amin tuḷḷa periya māḷiki āṭci urimiyuṭiya peruntalivaṉatu araṇmaṉiyākavum, ataṉi aṭutta māḷikikaḷ avaṉukku aṭutta uttiyōkattarkaḷiṉ māḷikikaḷākavum, immāḷiki kaḷiṉ veḷippuṟam uḷḷa viṭutikaḷ kāvalāḷar ēvalāḷar illaṅkaḷākavum iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttākum.

tiyalārkkut taṉi aṟikaḷ

illaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil kālnaṭikaḷik kaṭṭuvataṟkut taṉi iṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavākat teriyavilli. ātalāl ammakkaḷ vīṭṭu muṟṟuḷilēyē oru mūliyil avaṟṟik kaṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ‘camiyal aṟikaḷ illāta illaṅkaḷil camiyal vēḷiyum muṟṟattilē naṭipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum’, eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ karācci vikṭōriyāk kaṇkāṭcik cāliyiṉ kāppāḷarākiya aṟiñar ci.ār.rāy eṉpār, ‘ovvoru vīṭṭiṟkum vāyiṟpaṭi uṇṭu. ataṉi aṭuttut tiṟanta ciṟṟaṟi oṉṟu vāyiṟ kāvalaṉukkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ piṉpuṟam amarntu pēcutaṟkuriya kūṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟiṟkup piṉpuṟam paṭukki aṟikaḷ, camiyal aṟikaḷ, tiyalār irukkat taṉi aṟikaḷ mutaliyaṉa irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[1]

nīrāṭum aṟikaḷ

ovvōr illattiluma nīrāṭat taṉi aṟi irukkiṟatu. māḷikikaḷil oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa nīrāṭum aṟikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi teruppakkam amintuḷḷaṉa; melliya ceṅkaṟkaḷāl taḷavarici iṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa; ivaṟṟiliruntu kaḻinīr ōṭa vaṭikālkaḷ naṉmuṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. iv vaṭikālkaḷ teruk kālvāyuṭaṉ iṇikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvaṭikālukku uriya puḻiaiyuḷḷa iṭam nōkki nīrāṭum aṟiyiṉ tarimaṭṭam carintu celvatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu. ittakiya nīrāṭum aṟi oṉṟu eṣṉaṉṉāvil irunta akkēṭiyar araṇmaṉiyuḷ iruntatu. akkēṭiyar nīrāṭum aṟi amippatic cintup piratēca makkaḷiṭamiruntē kaṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli.[2]

camiyal aṟikaḷ

ovvoru vīṭṭārum perumpālum muṟṟattaṇṭi camiyal ceytu vanta pōtiṉum, camiyalukkeṉṟu taṉi aṟiyiyum vittiruntaṉar. anta aṟiyil viṟku vikkavum piṟa camiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi vikkavum taṉi mēṭikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila periya vīṭṭuc camiyal aṟikaḷ akaṉṟu irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ periya tāḻikaḷ patikkappaṭ ṭiruntamikkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. attāḻikaḷ nīric cēmittu vikkap payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. mēlum, camiyal aṟiyil camiyalukkuriya cāmāṉkaḷi vikkap pala maṇ cāṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ippaḻakkam iṉṟum moheñco-tarōvic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kirāmattāriṭam iruppatik kāṇalām. camiyal aṭuppukaḷ cilavē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avi mecopoṭṭēmiyāvil kiṭitta aṭuppukaḷip pōlavē iruttal viyappukku uriyatē.[3]

aṅkāṭiyō? ampalamō?

ōriṭattil mikap periya maṇṭapam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. atu 76500 ca. ce. mī. parappuṭiyatu. ataṉ kūriyi irupatu ceṅkal tūṇkaḷ tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivi nāṉku nāṉkāyccaturam caturamāka niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. apperiya maṇṭapam potu aṅkāṭiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar apippirāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cilar, ‘potu makkaḷiṉ iṟivaṇakkattiṟkuriyatākavum, potu viṉikku uriyatākavum iruntirukkalām’, eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa yāṇṭukaṭku muṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa oṉṟippaṟṟi iṅṅaṉam palatiṟappaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ eḻutal iyalpaṉṟō?

cila iṭaṅkaḷil aṟikkuḷ aṟiyākap pala illaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. avi aṅkāṭikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, uḷḷaṟikaḷ kaṭic cāmāṉkaḷ vittaṟ kuriyavi eṉṟum, aṟiñar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cila illac cuvarkaḷic cuṟṟi 120 ce. mī. akalam taḷavarici iṭappeṟṟa mēṭikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi cillaṟik kaṭikaḷ vikkap payaṉpaṭṭavi eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. cilar, ‘avi veḷḷap pātukāppukkeṉṟē uyarttap paṭṭavi’ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

kaḷḷukkaṭiyō? taṇṇīrppantalō? uṇṭiccāliyō?

cila iṭaṅkaḷil periya aṟikaḷik koṇṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ cila nilattil aḻuntip patintuḷḷaṉa.ivi perumpālum teruk kōṭikaḷiṟṟām amintuḷḷaṉa.ivaṟṟuḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl ceytu cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa periya tāḻikaḷ pala tariyiṟ putintu kiṭantaṉa. ivi kaḷḷuk kaṭikaḷāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. tīkṣat pōṉṟōr, ‘ivi nīr mōr, taṇṇir mutaliya pāṉaṅkaḷi vaḻippōkkarkku viliyiṉṟi vaḻaṅkum aṟaccāvaṭikaḷāka iruntiruttalvēṇṭum’, eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. ‘ivi nakara māntar oṉṟukūṭi aḷavaḷāvutaṟkaminta uṇṭic cālikaḷāka irukkalām’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē kūṟukiṉṟār.

vēṭkōvar viṭutikaḷ

nakarattiṉ oru puṟam irunta illaṅkaṭku arukil pala kāḷavāykaḷum ari kuṟiyākac cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avviṭattil maṭpāṇṭat toḻilāḷar vēṭkōvar perumpāṉmiyiṉarāka vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum. toṭakkattil ammakkaḷ nakarattiṉ puṟampē iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum (kāḷavāykaḷ nakaril iruttal cukātārak kuṟivātaliṉ), nāḷaṭivil caṉattoki kuṟiyak kuṟiya nakarattiṉ oru pakutiyiṟ kuṭiyēṟalāyiṉar eṉṟum aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[4]

vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷ

moheñ cotarō nakarattu illaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷ perumpālum perun terukkaḷil vikkap paṭāmal kuṟukkup pātikaḷilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa. immuṟiyāl terukkaḷ aḻakākak kāṇappaṭal iyalpē aṉṟō? cila periya māḷikikaḷiṟṟām akaṉṟum uyarntum uḷḷa vāyilkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉiya illaṅkaḷil ellām uyarattilum akalattilum kuṟukalākavuḷḷa vāyilkaḷē amintuḷḷaṉa. periya vīṭṭu vāyil 235 ce.mī. akalamuṭiyatu; āṉāl 150 ce. mīṭṭarukku mēṟpaṭṭa uyaramuṭiyatāka illi. ipperiya vāyilkaḷi uṭiya vīṭṭār pala erutukaḷiyum kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷiyum piṟa kālnaṭikaḷiyum vittiruntavar ātal vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷ avaṟṟi vīṭṭu muṟṟattilēyē kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. iṅku, avi pulliyum vikkōliyum tiṉṉa amikkap paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ irunta aṭiyāḷam terikiṉṟatu. perumpālāṉa vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷ 120 ce. mī. uyaramē uḷḷaṉa. ivi iṅkaṉam amintiruttaṟku iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷ kūṟalām: oṉṟu veḷḷam vīṭṭiṟkuḷ eḷitil varāmal taṭuppataṟkāka irukkalām; maṟṟoṉṟu, moheñco-tarō makkaḷ cumēriyarip pōlak kuḷḷamāṉavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum. mēlum, avarkaḷ mēṉmāṭikku iṭṭa uttiraṅkaḷ tarimaṭṭattiliruntu 180 ce. mī.kku uḷḷēyē iruttalum kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. eṉavē piṉṉatē peritum poruttamuṭiya kāraṇamākum.[5] vīṭṭuk katavukaḷ ellām marak katavukaḷēyākum. cintup piratēcattil appaḻaṅkālattil kāṭukaḷ mikutiyātaliṉālum vīṭṭuc cuvariṉ kaṉattiṟku ēṟṟa katavu amittal kaṭaṉātalālum marakkatavukaḷ mikka parumaṉ uṭiyaṉavāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār.

tuṇkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kāṇappaṭṭa tuṇkaḷ ellām ceṅkaṟkaḷāl āṉaviyē. avi perumpālum caturat turaṇkaḷēyākum. cila aṭiyil 2700 ca. ce. mī. rākavum, mēl pōkappōka 2250 ca. ce.mī.rākavum uḷḷaṉa. pala muṟṟum 2700 ca. ce mī parappuṭiyaṉavākavē uḷḷaṉa. cumēriyāvil atē kālattil vaṭṭattuṇkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. cumēriyarōṭu neruṅkiya vāṇipam ceytuvanta cintup piratēca makkaḷ avvaṭṭat tūṇkaḷit taṅkaḷ illaṅkaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttāmi viyappukkuriyatākum. tūṇkaḷit tāṅkum kalvaḷiyaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittaṉ. āṉāl avi ‘yōṉikaḷ’ eṉṟu tayārāma cahaṉi kūṟukiṟār.[6]

iṉi, harappā nakarattu illaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya cila vivaraṅkaḷik kāṇpōm:

harappāvil kaṭṭiṭa amippu

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ cukavāḻviṟkēṟṟa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu vāḻntāṟ pōlavē harappā makkaḷum mikac ciṟanta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar. periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi mikka uṟutiyāka iruttal vēṇṭi, naṉku cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ciṟiya illaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi iṅṅaṉam uṟutiyāka illi. avi uṭinta maṇ ōṭukaḷ, ceṅkaṟkaṭṭikaḷ, maṭpāṇṭa oṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyē koṇṭiruntaṉa. periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷum tarikaḷum cākkaṭikaḷum oruvitak kal koṇṭu naṉṟāka vaḻavaḻappākat tēyttu ikkālac ‘cimeṇṭ’ pūcappeṟṟaṉapōl amintuḷḷaṉa. taḷavariciyil uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ aṉittum oḻuṅkākavum vaḻavaḻappākavum aṟuttuc cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭaviyākum. māṭip paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷavipōlat tiṟampaṭa amintuḷḷaṉa. kuppi koṭṭum toṭṭikaḷ, kaḻinīrt tēkkaṅkaḷ, kālvāykaḷ, vaṭikālkaḷ mutaliyaṉa moheñco-tarōvil iruppavaṟṟiviṭa nalla niliyil uḷḷaṉa. iviyaṉṟik kiṇaṟukaḷ āṅkāṅkē kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi vīṭṭukkoṉṟāka amintuḷḷami kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

iruvaki illaṅkaḷ

harappāvil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi iruvakiyiṉavākap pirikkalām;avi kuṭi iruttaṟkuriya illaṅkaḷ, potuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa ākum. kuṭikku uriya illaṅkaḷ perumpālum ciṟantaṉavāyum māṭikaḷ uṭiyaṉavāyum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil viruntiṉarkkut taṉi aṟikaḷum tiyalārkkut taṉi aṟikaḷum akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷum iruntami kuṟippiṭat takkatu. potuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa vaṭṭavaṭimāṉa mēṭikaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa.

peruṅ kaḷañciyam

aṟiñar oru maṇmēṭṭit tōṇṭiyapōtu ataṉ aṭiyil kaḷañciyam oṉṟu irukkak kaṇṭaṉar. itu 50-10 ce.mī. nīḷamum 4050ce.mī akalamum uṭiyatu. itaṉ cuvarkaḷ 1560 ce.mī. uyaramum 270 ce.mī. kaṉamum uṭiyaṉa. ivi iraṇṭu varici kaḷākak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivviraṇṭu varicikaṭku iṭiyē uḷḷaturam 690 ce mī ākum. inta iṭiveḷikkumēl kūri iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. inta iru varici neṭuñ cuvarkaḷuḷ ovvoru variciyilum āṟu maṇṭapaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru maṇṭapattiyum iṇikkum vakiyil aintu iṭikaḻikaḷ viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru maṇṭapamum mūṉṟu neṭuñcuvarkaḷ eḻuppappeṟṟu nāṉku aṟikaḷ pōlappirintu uḷḷatu. maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ tarip pakutiyil marappalakikaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvaḷavu aṟputamāṉa vēlippāṭṭuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum immaṇṭapaṅkaḷ tāṉiyaṅkaḷ koṭṭivikkap payaṉpaṭṭa kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ ākum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. iṅkilāntilum cermaṉiyilum paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟiyum, varikaḷip paṇṭaṅkaḷākavē vāṅkum vaḻakkamum irunta paṇṭik kālattil, araciyalār, apporuḷkaḷic cēmittu vippataṟkākak kaṭṭiyirunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷip pōlavē harappāvil kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaḷañciya maṇṭapaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, ivi araciyalār araciṟikkākat tiraṭṭappeṟṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷ vikkum kaḷañciyaṅkaḷākavē iruttal kūṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar eṇṇamākum.

toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ

harappāvil toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ amintuḷḷa muṟi kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. oru variciyil ēḻu vīṭukaḷ irukkumāṟu iraṇṭu varicikaḷ oṉṟākak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil nīṇṭa kuṟukiya pāti oṉṟu celkiṉṟatu. appāti iru pakkaṅkaḷilum potut terukkaḷil kalakkiṉṟatu. ivvillaṅkaḷ ekiptil uḷḷa kel-el amarṉā[7] eṉṉum iṭattil amintuḷḷa toḻilāḷar. illaṅkaḷippōlavē iruttal kuṟippiṭat takkatu. āṉāl, piṉṉavi orē variciyil amintuḷḷaṉa; iṭiyil pāti viṭappaṭṭila. mēlum, harappāvil uḷḷa toḻilāḷar illam ovvoṉṟilum periya muṟṟamum mūṉṟu aṟikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ekiptiya illaṅkaḷ amintila. mēlum, harappāviṉ illaṅkaḷ ki.mu.2500kku muṟpaṭṭaṉa; āṉāl, ekiptiya illaṅkaḷ ki.mu.2000kkup piṟpaṭṭaṉa. ip piṟkāla illaṅkaḷiviṭa harappāvil uḷḷa muṟkāla illaṅkaḷ cukātāra vacatiyōṭu piṟa vacatikaḷum poruntiyirukkumpaṭi amikkappaṭṭiruttal peruviyappukku uriyatē ākum. veḷiyiliruntu pārppavarkku uṭpuṟam tōṉṟātavāṟu ivvillaṅkaḷiṉ talivāyil vaḷinta cuvarkaḷuṭaṉ amintuḷḷatu. ivviṭutikaḷil palavakit toḻilpuri makkaḷ vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇutaṟkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.

vīṭṭiṟku uriya vīṭukaḷē

‘aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṉum pēṟukaḷ nāṉkaṉuḷ, vīṭu’ eṉpatu ‘iṉpam’ allatu ‘mōkṣam’ eṉap poruḷpaṭum. maṉitaṉ ivvulakil taṉ uyirukkuyirāṉa peṟṟōruṭaṉum maṉivi makkaḷuṭaṉum nīṇṭa nāḷ kalantu uṟavāṭi iṉpam nukarum iṭamāka uḷḷatu vīṭē aṉṟō? avvillam nalla cukātāra muṟippaṭi amintirukkumāyiṉ, nōy noṭiyiṉṟi uṭal vaḷam kuṉṟāmal cuṟucuṟuppāka viṉi yāṟṟip poruḷiṭṭi illaṟameṉṉum nallaṟam tuyttu iṉpa vāḻvu vāḻalām aṉṟō? iṅṅaṉam iṉpam (vīṭu) tarattakka illam amittu vāḻntamiyāṟṟāṉō, nam muṉṉōrāya paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ mōkṣatti ‘vīṭu’ eṉṉum peyarāl aḻittaṉar? ipporuṭ poruttam uṭiyaṉavākavē harappā, moheñco-tarō eṉṉum iraṇṭu nakaraṅkaḷilum irunta paṇṭik kāla vīṭukaḷ viḷakkamuṟṟiruntaṉa. ekiptiyar, cumēriyar mutaliyōr piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvatilum cittira vēlippāṭu mikunta illaṅkaḷi amippatilum taṅkaḷ karuttic celuttiṉarē aṉṟic ciṟanta cukātāra muṟikku ēṟṟavāṟu illaṅkaḷi amittu vāḻntilar. ‘vīṭu’ (illam) eṉpati vīṭu (mōkṣam) ākkiya perumi āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa cintuppiratēca makkaṭkē uriyatākum.
