﻿6. kiṇaṟukaḷ - ceykuḷam - ceṅkaṟkaḷ

5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil perumpālum vīṭṭukkoru kiṇaṟu iruntateṉak kūṟalām. ārāycciyāḷar nakarattit tōṇṭi ārāycci naṭattumpoḻutu pala kēṇikaḷ iruttalik kaṇṭaṉar. āṉāl avi aṉittum tuārntu kiṭantaṉa. harappāvil aṟiñar vāṭh eṉpār ārāycci nikaḻttiya pōtu oru peruṅ kiṇaṟṟil nīr iruttalik kaṇṭār. akkiṇaṟṟil 245 ce.mī. uyaram taṇṇīr iruntatām. avvaṟiñar ataṉit tūymi ceytu aṅku vēli ceytu vanta toḻilāḷarkkup payaṉpaṭumāṟu ēṟpāṭu ceytaṉarām. harappāvil piṟitōr iṭattil 180 ce. mī. cuṟṟaḷavuṭiya kiṇaṟu oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. pala kiṇaṟukaḷ nakara aḻiviṉāl aḻintu viṭṭaṉa. pala irunta iṭam teriyāmal maṇṇukkuḷ maṟippuṇṭaṉa.

iṉṟum curappuṭiya kiṇaṟukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ 107.5 ce.mī. cuṟṟaḷavu mutal 270 ce.mī. cuṟṟaḷavu vari pala tiṟappaṭṭaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala maṇṇuḷ putintuviṭṭaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar avaṟṟik kaṇṭaṟintu avaṟṟil aṭintuḷḷa maṇ kal mutaliyavaṟṟi appuṟappaṭutti, cakatiyi nīkkit tūymi ceyyum vēliyil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. cila kiṇaṟukaḷ naṉṉīrc curappuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. annīrē aṅku ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñarkkum toḻilāḷarkkum uṇṇavum kuḷikkavum uriyatāyiṟṟu.kēṇi nīrip payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟku muṉ avarkaṭkut tēvippaṭṭa nīr 3kimī toliviliruntu vaṇṭiyil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu attolli muṟṟum nīṅkiviṭṭatu. cumār 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ iṉṟum curappuṭiyaṉavāka iruttal viyappukkuriyatē aṉṟō?

māḷikikaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ

cila māḷikikaḷil uḷḷa paḻaṅkiṇaṟukaḷ teruppuṟam illāmal māḷikikku aṇmiyil amintiruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. anniliyil avi māḷiki makkaṭkē payaṉ paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl ammāḷikiyil piṟkālattil vīṭṭiṉ teruppuṟamākak kiṇaṟukaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. avi piṟkālattil potumakkaḷ nalaṅkarutiyē muṉpuṟam amikkap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam teruppuṟam amintuḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ cila aṟikaḷil uḷḷami kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. avvaṟiyil kiṇaṟṟurukil periya taṇṇīrp pāṉikaḷ putikkappaṭṭiruntamikkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa. avaṟiyiṉ tari naṉku paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kiṇaṟṟarukil cintum nīr aṅkut tēṅkiyirāmal uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē teruk kālvāykkum iṭiyē ciṟu vaṭikāl oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. nīr eṭukkum peṇkaḷ uṭkāruvataṟkuk kiṇaṟṟaṇṭi mēṭi oṉṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu kiṇaṟṟic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cuvar kuḻantikaḷ eṭṭip pārkka muṭiyāta aḷavu uyarntuḷḷatu.

terumutal kiṇaṟuvari ciṟiya vaḻi viṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veḷiyār kiṇaṟṟaṇṭi varuvataṟkeṉṟē avvaḻi amikkappaṭṭatu pōlum piṟar taṇṇīr eṭukkumpoḻutu māḷikikkuriya maṭantiyarum taṇṇir eṭukkavēṇṭumāyiṉ eṉceyvatu? ataṟkeṉṟu takka ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. veḷiyārkkum vīṭṭārkkum kiṇaṟṟaṇṭi yātoru campantamum irātavāṟu kiṇaṟṟiṉ naṭuvil melliya maṟivu amikkappaṭṭiruntatu eṉpataṟkuriya aṭiyāḷam terikiṟatu. kēṇikaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟamum uṭpuṟamum mikka kavaṉattuṭaṉum oḻuṅkuṭaṉum kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ ōrac cuvarkaḷ mūṉṟu mutal aintu aṭukkuc ceṅkaṟkaḷ vittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippātukāppukkuriya ēṟpāṭṭiṉāl kiṇaṟṟu nīr cuvarkaḷil taṅki ūṟu viḷikkātu eṉpatu teḷivām.

kayiṟum uruḷikaḷum

kiṇaṟukaḷiṟ kuṭaṅkaḷiviṭṭu nīri mukattal ikkālattum cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻakkamāka irukkiṉṟatu. itu pōṉṟē appaṇṭik kālattum moheñco-tarōvil ivvaḻakkam iruntatu. akkāla makkaḷ taṭitta kayiṟukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu, kiṇṟukaḷiṉ mēṟcuvaril ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa urāyppukaḷ mūlam aṟital kūṭum.potuk kiṇaṟukaḷil makaḷir palar orē kālattil nīri eṭukkum paḻakkam iṉṟum iruppatu pōlavē appaḻaṅkālattum iruntateṉpatik kiṇaṟṟiṉ mēṟcuvar mītuḷḷa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷik koṇṭu kūṟalām. pal kēṇikaḷil uruḷikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇṇālāya cila patumikaḷ vāyilāka aṟiyakkiṭakkiṟatu.

palamuṟi uyarttappaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ

cintu āṟṟiṉ veḷḷap perukkiṟku añci nakarattiṉ tarimaṭṭam uyarttappaṭṭapōtellām ikkiṇaṟukaḷum māṟupāṭu aṭintukoṇṭu vantaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ iṅkaṉam mummuṟi putuppikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati, avaṟṟiṉ mēṟcuvarkaḷ mūlam naṉkaṟiyalām. vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ nakara aṭimaṭṭam uyarttappaṭṭa kālattum putuppikkappaṭavilli. cila iṭaṅkaḷil putuppikkap paṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ paḻiyavaṟṟiviṭa eḷiyaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭalāl, annakara makkaḷ putuppikkum toḻilil veṟuppuṟṟaṉar eṉṟō, allatu celva niliyil pātikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟō kūṟalām eṉpar ārāycciyāḷar. akkālattut taṇṇir maṭṭattiṟkum ikkālattut taṇṇir maṭṭattiṟkum 600 ce.mī. vēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉāl, akkiṇaṟukaḷi aṭimaṭṭam vari tōṇṭik kāṇpatu māṭṭāmiyāka irukkiṉṟatu. ‘cila kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ - aṭimaṭṭattiyēṉum cōtittup pārttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil kuḻantikaḷum periyavarkaḷum ki tavaṟip pōṭṭa poruḷkaḷ cilavēṉum kiṭittal kūṭum. nām avaṟṟik koṇṭu pala uṇmikaḷ uṇartal kūṭum’ eṉṟu ārāycci yāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ārāycci uṭiyārkku akappaṭṭa ciṟu poruḷum aṟputamākak kāṭciyaḷikkum aṉṟō!

aḻakiya ceykuḷam

moheñco-tarōvil itukāṟum naṭanta ārāycciyil veḷippaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantavi araṇmaṉi eṉṟu karutattakka kaṭṭiṭam oṉṟum aḻakiya ceykuḷam oṉṟumē ākum. piṉṉatu ikkālap poṟivalāḷarum tikippuṟumāṟu amintuḷḷatu. itaṉi. car jāṉ mārṣal 1925-1926il kaṇṭaṟintār. itaṉil taṇṇir niṟkum iṭam maṭṭum cumār 1200 ce.mī nīḷamum 690 ce.mī. akalamum 240ce.mīāḻamum uṭiyatu.ikkuḷattiṟku oḻuṅkāṉa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. ikkuḷattic cuṟṟi nāṟpuṟamum naṭivaḻi irukkumāṟu 135.ce.mī. akalamuḷḷa cuvar eḻuppappaṭṭuḷḷatu. accuvarukkum appāl kuḷattic cuṟṟi 210 ce.mī akalamuḷḷa peruñcuvar oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. ataṉ mītum naṭivaḻi amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

kuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭamum uṭcuvarkaḷum

icceykuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭam, naṉṟāy iḻittu vaḻavaḻappākkappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷum ikkālattuc ‘cimeṇṭ’ pōṉṟa oruvaki nilak kīlum[1] koṇṭu taḷavarici iṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṭimaṭṭac cuvarkaḷum ivviraṇṭāl ākkappaṭṭaṉavē ākum. iti amitta aṟiñar pārāṭṭutaṟku uriyavarē āvar eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttākum. apperumakkaḷ mutalil cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa vaḻavaḻappāṉa ceṅkaṟkaḷi oruvaki veḷḷik kaḷimaṇ[2] koṇṭu oṭṭavittuḷḷaṉar; ivvaṇṇam nāṟpuṟamum 120 ce. mī. kaṉattil cuvarkaḷ eḻuppiṉar; piṉṉar accuvarkaḷ mītu 2.5 ce.mī. kaṉattil nilakkīl pūciyuḷḷaṉar; iccuvarukkup piṉpuṟam naṉṟāyc cuṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭu maṟṟoru varicic cuvar kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar; ataṉi oṭṭic cuṭāta ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷālāṉa cuvar oṉṟi iṇittuḷḷaṉar. taṇṇir ūṟippaḻutākāmal iruppataṟkākavē iccuvarkaḷ ivvaḷavu vēlippāṭukaḷōṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

nilak kīl

icceykuḷattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa nilakkīl cintup piratēcattilō aṉṟi intiyāviṉ piṟapakutikaḷilō illi. eṉavē, itu veḷi nāṭṭiliruntē koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum. avveḷināṭu yātu? cumēriyāviṟṟāṉ innilakkīl uṇṭu. iḵtu aṅkētāṉ peritum ippaṇṭik kālattil payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. cumēriyar kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi uṟutiyum aḻakum ceytatu innilakkīlē ākum. eṉavē, vāṇipattil cumēriyarōṭu nerukkam koṇṭirunta cintup piratēca makkaḷ innilakkīli avarkaḷiṭamiruntē peṟṟaṉarātal vēṇṭum. iḵtu iṅṅaṉam neṭuntuārattiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatāka iruntatāṟṟāṉ, kiṇaṟukaṭkō piṟavaṟṟukkō payaṉpaṭuttappaṭāmal ivvariya ceykuḷattiṟku maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa teṉpati uṇartal vēṇṭum. ivvariya poruḷi anneṭuntura nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum koṇarntu amikkappaṭṭa icceykuḷam, oru kuṟippiṭṭa naṟkāriyattiṟkākavē payaṉpaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum eṉpatum aṟiyattakum.

kuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē

icceykuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē eṭṭu aṟikaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi mikka uṟutiyāṉa cuvarkaḷik koṇṭavi: veḷiyiliruntu pārppavar uṭpuṟattic ciṟitum kāṇātavāṟu iyaṉṟa uṟutiyāṉa katavukaḷi uṭiyavi; mēlē ēṟap paṭikkaṭṭukaḷi uṭiyavi. ovvōr aṟiyiṉ mēlum aṟi iruntateṉpataṟku uriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. mēl aṟiyil iruntavar paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ vaḻiyē uṭpuṟamākavē kīḻ aṟikku vantu, arukil uḷḷa kiṇaṟṟiliruntu cēvakaṉ cēmittu vikkum nīril nīrāṭiviṭṭu mēlē pōyviṭuvar. avvaḻukku nīr ovvōr aṟiyilum uḷḷa tuḷi vaḻiyē veḷiyil uḷḷa kaḻinīrk kālvāyiccērum. iv aṟikaḷil vāḻntavar camayaccārpuṭiya perumakkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ivaṟikaḷum icceykuḷamum stūpi niṟkum iṭattiṟku aṇmiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl,stuāpiyiṉ aṭiyil maṟintukiṭappatu, ivviraṇṭiṟkum toṭarpuṭiya kōvilākavō aṉṟic camayac cārpuṭiya piṟitoru kaṭṭiṭamākavō iruttal vēṇṭum[3]

kuḷattiṟku nīr vacati

icceykuḷattiṟku arukē, itil nīri nirapputaṟkeṉṟē amintaṉa pōla mūṉṟu periya kiṇaṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ nīrē kuḷattiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. kuḷattu nīr tūymi aṟṟavuṭaṉ appuṟappaṭuttappaṭal vēṇṭum aṉṟō? ataṟkākavē icceykuḷattiṉ mūli oṉṟil catura vaṭivil puḻi oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. aḵtu oru maṉitaṉ tārāḷamāka nuḻintu cellakkūṭiya aḷavu akaṉṟatāka irukkiṉṟatu. atu, vēṇṭum pōtu taṇṇiri vaṭiyāmal taṭuttu vippataṟkākavum, vēṇṭātapōtu taṇṇīri veḷiviṭutaṟkākavum amintuḷḷa matakippōlac ciṟanta vēlippāṭuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, icceykuḷattiṉ vēlippāṭu oṉṟē ārāycciyāḷarip peritum viyappuṟac ceytateṉṉal mikiyākātu. itaṉ arukil uḷḷa piṟa maṇ mēṭukaḷum tōṇṭappaṭṭa piṉṉarē itaṉ arumi perumikaḷ mēlum viḷakkamākum.

ceṅkaṟkaḷ

vīṭukaḷ, kiṇaṟukaḷ, kuḷaṅkaḷ, kaḻinīrppātikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟukkum moheñco-tarōvil payaṉpaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷippaṟṟiya cila vivaraṅkaḷi aṟital iṅku avaciyamākum. cintup piratēca makkaḷ cumēriyāviliruntu nilakkīlik koṇṭuvantavāṟē ceṅkalceyyum muṟiyiṉiyum piṟariṭam kaṟṟaṉarā? aṉṟi iyalpākat tāmē uṇarntaṉarā? eṉṉum viṉākkaṭku viṭi kāṇal kaṭamiyākum.

paṇṭi nāṭukaḷ

ekiptu, cumēriyā mutaliya paṇṭip pukaḻ paṭitta nāṭukaḷil cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkamē appaṇṭi nāṭkaḷil eḻuntilatu. urōma aracu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarē ceṅkaṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟiyi am mēṟkuppuṟa nāṭukaḷ aṟiyalāyiṉa. ekiptil kallip pōṉṟa oruvakik kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittatu. ataṉāl, ammakkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷic ceyyavēṇṭiya avaciyam eḻavilli. cumēriyarkkum aṅṅaṉamē. eṉavē, cintup piratēca makkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷic ceyyum muṟiyip piṟa nāṭṭāriṭam kaṟṟilar: tāmākavē ammuṟiyik kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar eṉak kūṟal tavaṟākātu. ‘avaciyamē ārāycciyiṉ tāy’ eṉpatu uṇmi yaṉṟō? avarkaḷ, cintu āṟṟaṅkariyil iyaṟkiyil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇṇippatamākki aṟuttukkāyavittup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; atu kaṭuveyilukkum maḻikkum āṟṟātatik kaṇṭapiṉ, ataṉic cūḷiyil iṭṭuc cuṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttiṉarātal vēṇṭum iṅṅaṉam apperumakkaḷ kiyāṇṭa muṟiyē namnāṭṭil iṉṟaḷavum kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.

ceṅkaṟkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭa muṟikaḷ

appaṇṭi nāṭkaḷil veṭṭa veḷikaḷil kaḷimaṇ ceṅkaṟkaḷi aṟuttuk kāyavittu, piṉ viṟakukaḷikkoṇṭu tī mūṭṭic ceṅkaṟkaḷic cuṭṭu vantaṉar. immuṟi kāḷavāykaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarum ikkālattum vaḻakkil iruttalāl, cintup piratēcattilum appaḻaṅkālattil orupāl immuṟiyum piṟitorupāl kāḷavāyil cuṭum muṟiyum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. moheñco-tarō makkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷic cūḷiyiṭuvataṟkākavēp payaṉpaṭuttiya kāḷvāykaḷ vaṭṭa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāḷavāykaḷ aṭiyil neruppu muṭṭappaṭum. ivaṟṟiliruntu pukiyi veḷippaṭutta pukip pōkkikaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.

ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil cila vīṭukaḷiṉ uṭ puṟaccuvarkaḷ ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavi. pala iṭaṅkaḷil nilattiṉ aṭimaṭṭa aḷavi uyarttuvataṟkāka ivvularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ivvularnta kaṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam appaṇṭik kālam mutal iṉṟaḷavum innāṭṭil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. iṉṟiya cintu mākāṇattil immuṟi nirampak kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

ceṅkaṟkaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil irunta ceṅkal aṟukkum toḻilāḷar matinuṭpam vāyntavarātal vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ ovvoru ceṅkalliyum ataṉ payaṉukku ēṟṟavāṟu ceytuḷḷaṉar. ovvoṉṟum kōṇalum mēṭupaḷḷaṅkaḷum iṉṟi naṉṟāy aṟukkappaṭṭuc camaṉākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ patamaṟintu cūḷiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru ceṅkallum ēṟakkuṟiya nīḷattiṟ pāti akalamum akalattiṟ pāti uyaramum (1 : ½ : ¼) uṭiyatāka amintiruttal peritum viyakkattakkatu. ivvaḷavil amintuḷḷa ceṅkaṟkaḷē palavākum. kaḻinīrp pātikaṭkuc ceyyappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷum piṟa kuṟippiṭṭa kāriyaṅkaṭkeṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷum aḷaviṟ periyaṉavum ciṟiyaṉavumāka irukkiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ cila 55 ce. mī nīḷamum 28 ce. mī. akalamum 7.5 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭiyavi; cila 5 ce. mī. nīḷamuṭiyavi; cila 23 ce. mī. nīḷamuṭiyavi. icceṅkaṟkaḷ tēvikku ēṟṟa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭavi ākum. cila cama caturamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa; cila nīḷa caturamāka uḷḷaṉa; cila vaḷivākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa; cila kaṇṇāṭipōla vaḻa vaḻappākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila mūlikaḷukku ēṟpa mukkōṇamāka veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. “āriyar intiyāvil kālaṭi eṭuttu vippataṟku muṉ intiyāvil nākarikam eṉṟu oṉṟu iruntatē illi” eṉṟu niṉittavarum pēciyavarum eḻutiyavarumē viyakkumāṟu iccintup piratēca nākarikam ceṅkaṟkaḷilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉiṉ, ippiratēcattup paṇṭip perumiyi eṉṉeṉpatu!



* * *





↑ Bitumen.

↑ Gypsum Mortar.

↑ Dr.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, pp.57, 58.





* * *





﻿7. vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷum viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷum

vīṭṭukkuriya poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻviṟkut tēviyāṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu aṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiliruntu naṉkaṟiyalām. apporuḷkaḷ kaḷimaṇṇālum marattālum cempālum veṇkalattālum caṅku, veṇkal mutaliyavaṟṟālum ceyyap paṭṭavi. vīṭṭukkuriya perumpālāṉa poruḷkaḷ kaḷi maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavē ākum. ciṟappuṭi nāṭkaḷil caṅkālum veṇkallālum ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. maṭṭāṇṭap poruḷkaḷē mikutiyāka iruttaliṉ, avaṟṟip paṟṟi mutaṟkaṇ pēcuvōm.

maṭpāṇṭa māṇpu

enta iṭattil nilatti akaḻntu ārāycci ceyyiṉum, aṅkuk kiṭikkum palatiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷuḷ ārāycciyāḷar maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiyē ciṟantaviyāka matikkiṉṟaṉar. oru nakaram aḻivuṟumpōtu-tuṟakkappaṭumpōtu annakarattār viṭṭuc celvaṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē ākum. piṟar paṭiyeṭuppiṉālum cētamākātaṉavum kavarappaṭātaṉavum matikkappaṭātaṉavum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē ākum. ivviru kāraṇaṅ kaḷālum ammaṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ citivukaḷum annakara makkaḷiṉ uṇminākarikatti uḷḷavāṟu uṇarttuvaṉavākum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu, aviyē ammakkaḷiṉ kālam, aṟivu, āṟṟal iṉṉa piṟavum uṇmiyāka uṇarttum āṟṟal uṭiyaṉa. ikkāraṇaṅkaḷāṟṟāṉ cintuppiratēca ārāycciyāḷar, cintup piratēcattil uḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷiyum citinta maṇ oṭukaḷiyum viṭāmaṟ pātukāttu varukiṉṟaṉar; moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa illaṅkaḷilum kaḻinīrp pātikaḷilum citintum citiyāmalum kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷic cēmittu vittuḷḷaṉar.

palavaki maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ mutal vīṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭ maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷ vari yāvum pala tiṟappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷi uṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. cila naṉṉiliyil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; cila arikuṟiyāṉa niliyil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; pala citinta niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟukkum harappāvil kiṭittuḷḷa pala tiṟappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaṭkum ciṟitaḷavē vēṟupāṭu uḷḷatu. ivvēṟupāṭu koṇṭu, harappā nakaram moheñco-tarōvi viṭac ciṟitu muṟpaṭṭatāka irukkakkūṭumō eṉṟu aiyuṟuvārum uḷar. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum nāṭōṟum kiyāḷappaṭṭa maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷip pōṉṟavi ekiptilum pāpilōṉiyāvilum nāṭōṟum vīṭukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, akkālattu nākarika nāṭukaḷil ellām immaṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷ peritum oṉṟu pōlavē iruntaṉa eṉak kūṟal oruvāṟu poruntuvatākum.

kaḷimaṇ kalavi

cintu āṟṟukkaḷi maṇṇē moheñco-tarō makkaṭkup pērutavi purintatākum. ammaṇṇil ōraḷavu aprakamum cuṇṇāmpum aṇukkaḷ vaṭivil kalantiruntaṉa. maṭpāṇpu viṉiyāḷar ammaṇṇi ōraḷvu teḷḷiya maṇaluṭaṉ kalantu naṉṟāka arittu, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiyum viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷiyum ērāḷamākac ceytaṉar. ikkalaviyiṉāl poruḷkaḷ uṟutiyāka irukkum eṉpati avviṉiyāḷar naṉku aṟintiruntami viyappukku uriyatē ākum. ippalavakip poruḷkaḷi ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ, ‘immaṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷē ulakil paḻimiyum uṟutiyum aḻakum poruntiyavi’ eṉṟu kūṟi viyakkiṉṟaṉar.

vēṭkōvar uruḷikaḷ

maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉittum vēṭkōvar uruḷiyik koṇṭē ceyyappaṭṭavi ākum. vēṭkōvar, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi uruḷikaḷ mūlam uruvākkiya piṉṉar, avaṟṟiṉmītu ceṅkāvi niṟam pūcik kāyavittu, piṉṉar avaṟṟi vaḻavaḻappākki, iṟutiyiṟ cūḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ivvēlikaṭkup piṉṉarē ammaṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṇkavar vaṉappup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam uruḷikaḷ tuṇikkoṇṭu ceyyappaṭāmal vēṭkōvar tam kikaḷik koṇṭē ceyyappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi makaḷirāl ceyyappaṭṭu, vīṭṭiṟkuḷḷēyē cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉavāka iruttalkūṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē pōṉṟa ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

kāḷavāy

vēṭkōvar payaṉpaṭuttiya uruḷikaḷil oṉṟēṉum iṉṟu kiṭittilatu. āyiṉum, avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉa kāḷavāykaḷ cila citinta niliyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi vaṭṭavaṭivil 180 allatu 210 ce. mī. cuṟṟaḷavu uṭiyaṉavāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippākattil. niṟiyat tuḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cūḷiyiṭappaṭa vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷ kāḷavāyiṉ mītuḷḷa camamāṉa iṭattil vikkappaṭṭaṉa; aṭiyil tīmūṭṭappaṭṭatu. tī tuḷikaḷ mūlam mēṟceṉṟu, mēlē vikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi vēkac ceytatu, mēṟpuṟattil kuḻi oṉṟu iruntatu, ataṉ vaḻiyākavē puki veḷicceṉṟatu. cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷik kāṇkiyil, kāḷavāykaḷ mutaṟṟaramāṉa muṟiyil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatum, vēṭkōvar paṇpaṭṭa viṉiyāḷar eṉpatum eḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. merukiṭum karuvikaḷ

vēṭkōvar maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi merukiṭa eṉṟē oruvakik karuviyip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. akkaruvi elumpiṉāl āṉatu. atu 38 ce. mī nīḷamum 4 ce. mī. akalamum 10 ce.mī. kaṉamum uṭiyatu. akkaruvi oṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarukkuk kiṭittatu. atippōlavē ciṟiya aḷavuḷḷa karuvikaḷ cilavum kiṭittaṉa. ivi allāmal ciṟiya elumput tuṇṭukaḷum kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi merukiṭuvataṟkākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. harappāvil 42 ce. mī. nīḷamuḷḷa merukiṭum karuvi oṉṟu kiṭittatu. ikkaruvikaḷ tariyiyum cuvarkaḷiyum vaḻavaḻappākkavum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. vēṭkōvar illaṅkaḷil immerukiṭum paṇiyip peṇpālārē ceytiruttal kūṭiyatē eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar: cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷmītu aḻakiya niṟaṅkaḷip pūciyavarum oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭiyavarum makaḷirākavē iruttalum kūṭiyatē. eṉṉi? iṉṟum moheñcertarōvic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa irāmaṅkaḷil ivvēlikaḷic ceytu varupavar peṇmaṇikaḷē ātaliṉ eṉka.[1]

palaniṟap paṇṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭkalaṉkaḷuḷ oru cila tavirap perumpālāṉa ētēṉum oruvaki niṟam tīṭṭap paṭṭaṉavākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭpala, cenniṟampūcappaṭṭuk kaṟuppup paṭṭikaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. civappu karumi eṉṉum iraṇṭu niṟaṅkaḷē peritum payaṉpaṭṭu iruppiṉum pacumi, veṇmi, cāmpal niṟaṅkaḷum mikutiyākavē payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mañcaḷ niṟam arumiyākavē payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu veḷutta mañcaḷ paṭṭimītu kaṟuppuppaṭṭi aṭikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu paḻuppu niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭu ataṉmītu kaṟuppuppaṭṭi aṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

ippaṉṉiṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ moheñco-tarōvukkut teṟkē 128 ce.mī. tolivil uḷḷa amrīyilum kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ civappum kaṟuppum mikutiyākap pūcappaṭṭaviyē palavākum. moheñco-tarōviṟku vaṭamēṟkil 176 ce. mī. tolivil uḷḷa ‘nāl’ eṉṉum paḻampatiyiṟ kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala pacci niṟam pūcappaṭṭavi ākum. iṅṅaṉam maṭpāṇṭaṅkaṭku niṟam pūcum vaḻakkam appaṇṭikkālam mutal iṉṟu vari innāṭṭil iruntu varutal kavaṉikkat takkatu. ippaḻakkam iṉṟum moheñco-tarōvukku arukil uḷḷa cila kirāmaṅkaḷilum iruntu varukiṟatu.

niṟaṅkaḷip pūcuvāṉēṉ?

maṭkalaṅkaḷil kaṭpulaṉukkut teriyāta pala ciṟiya tuḷikaḷ iruttal kūṭum. niṟaṅkaḷ uḷḷum puṟamum pūcappaṭuvatāl attuḷikaḷ aṭipaṭum; aḻakum koṭukkum. ivviru kāraṇaṅkaḷi naṉku uṇarnta appaṇṭi nakarattu vēṭkōvar tām ceyta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaṭku niṟam pūciṉar eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṉṟār. iṅṅaṉamē taṇṇīr cēmittuvikkap payaṉpaṭṭa periya tāḻikaḷ nilakkīl pūcappaṭṭiruntaṉavām.

oviyam koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil oviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. civappu niṟam pūcappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu kaṟuppu niṟaṅkoṇṭu tīṭṭappaṭṭa oviyaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa, ūrvaṉa, paṟappaṉa, naṭappaṉa, nīntuvaṉa mutaliyaṉa oviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷē pala vilaṅkukaḷiṉ oviyam pul tariyuṭaṉum kāṭukaḷuṭaṉum cērttu iyaṟkik kāṭciyāka eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavikaḷ marakkiḷikaḷil iruttal pōlavum, maraṅkaṭku aṭiyil iruppaṉa pōlavum tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭu kāṭṭukkōḻikaḷ putar arukil iruttalippōla eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. oru tāḻiyiṉ mītu pala vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷa. ittakiya cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ēlam, cumēr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu maṉita uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭilatu. āṉāl harappāvil kiṭitta uṭinta maṭkala oṭu oṉṟiṉ mītu maṉitaṉ uruvamum kuḻanti uruvamum tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi allāmal, moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu mīṉtōl, oru vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ malar, vāyil malar vittukkoṇṭu niṟkum mayil malarkkottukkaḷ, māvili, aracili mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟiṉmītu kaḻukukaḷ varici variciyākap paṟappaṉa pōlavum, mayil oṉṟu paṟappatu pōlavum variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟiṉmītu ōṭam oṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷatu. cintu āṟṟil pala ōṭaṅkaḷit tiṉamum kaṇṭa ammakkaḷ, ōṭattiṉ paṭattit tīṭṭap peritum virumpiṉarilar. eṉṉi? ōṭam tiṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭam itukāṟum oṉṟē kiṭitta tātaliṉ eṉka.

eṅkum ellā eḻiluṟu oviyam

oru maṭpāṇṭattiṉmītu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vaṭṭam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷatu. ivvaṭṭaṅkaḷ piḻipaṭāmal ami vataṟkāka, mutalil caturak kōṭukaḷ iḻukkappaṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ utaviyāl vaṭṭaṅkaḷ oḻuṅkāka amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv amippu muṟi piṟa nāṭṭu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu kāṇumāṟu illi eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. moheñco-tarōvilum immuṟi cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītē amintuḷḷatu. vēṟu cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu kiyāl vaṭṭaṅkaḷ iḻukkappaṭṭuḷa. eṉavē, avvaṭṭaṅkaḷ naṉṟāka amintila.

vēṟu pala oviyaṅkaḷ

cila tāḻikaḷ mītu maraṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṭimarattik kuṟikkat taṭitta kōṭukaḷum kiḷikaḷik kuṟikka melliya vaḷinta kōṭukaḷum iḻuttuviṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu piṟimati tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cilavaṟṟiṉ mītu - tiṟampaṭat tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa caturaṅka oviyam kuṟippiṭat takkatu. oru kaṭṭam kaṟuppākavum maṟṟoṉṟu civappākavum aḷavu piṟaḻāmalum variyappaṭṭiruttal vaṉappuṭaṉ kāṭci yaḷikkiṉṟatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu mukkōṇam oṉṟākavum iraṇṭākavum mūṉṟākavum cērttu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

maṭpāṇṭa vakikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittuḷḷa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷil cila vēṟeṅkumē illātaviyāka iruttal kavaṉittataṟkuriyatu. avi cintup piratēcattiṟkē uriyavi eṉak kūṟal tavaṟākātu. nīr aruntap payaṉpaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, aṭiyil kumiḻ pōṉṟa kūriya vaṭivam peṟṟuk kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. avi nīr parukiya piṉṉark kaviḻttu vikkappaṭṭaṉ pōlum! cila pāttiraṅkaḷ aṭippuṟam taṭṭiyākavum mēṟpuṟam tiraṇṭu kūmpiya vaṭivattuṭaṉum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ivi palavakik kiṇṇaṅkaḷ, ciṟu taṇṭiṉmītu poruttappaṭṭa taṭṭukkaḷ, ciṟiya akalkaḷ, ciṟiya mūkkuṭiya ēṉaṅkaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭilkaḷ, paṭikkaṅkaḷ, kuṭaṅkaḷ, ciṟitu kuṭivāṉa pāttiraṅkaḷ, mēcimītu malar vikkap payaṉpaṭum nīṇṭa pāttiram pōṉṟavi, pampara uruvil aminta cīcākkaḷ, ikkālac cīcākkaḷip pōṉṟavi, pāṉikaḷ, caṭṭikaḷ, perun tāḻikaḷ, nīrt toṭṭikaḷ, palavaki muṭikaḷ, purimaṉikaḷ eṉap palavaki ākum.

pūcikkuriya maṭpāṇṭam

itaṉ uyaram 5 ce. mī. iḵtu aṭippuṟattil taṇṭu pōṉṟa vaṭivam uṭiyatu. itaṉ mēṟpuṟam vaṭṭil oṉṟu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvamippuṭaṉ irukkum ippāṇṭam pūcikkeṉṟē payaṉpaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya kalaṉkaḷ pāpilōṉiya nakaraṅkaḷāṉa, ‘kiṣ, ur, pārā’ eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭittiruttal kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu.

kaṉal caṭṭi

ituvum maṇṇāl iyaṉṟatēyām. iḵtu, ikkālattu marakkāl pōṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu niṟiyat tuḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭu mēl mūṭiyuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟatu. itaṟkuḷ neruppiṭṭu aṟikkuḷ kaṭṭilkaḷukku aṭiyil vippiṉ, tuḷikaḷ vaḻiyē aṉal veḷippaṭṭu aṟikkuḷ uḷḷa kuḷiri appuṟappaṭuttum aṟi cūṭāka irukkum. ikkaṉal caṭṭikaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ikkaṉal caṭṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttic cukavāḻvu vāḻak kaṟṟirunta appaṇṭip perumakkaḷ perumiyi eṉṉeṉpatu!

kumiḻkaḷ koṇṭa tāḻi

itu mikka viyappūṭṭum pāttiramākum. itaṉic ciṟappuṭip puti poruḷ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ uṭampil muḷ, pōṉṟa amippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa; aḵtāvatu, kumiḻkaḷ varici variciyāka vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.itaṉ aṭippuṟam kūmpi oru taṇṭu pōl amintuḷḷatu. ittakiya nūtaṉa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mikac cilavē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.ivi pōṉṟvi irākkiṉmēlpakutikaḷil uḷḷa ṭel asmar eṉṉum iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa eṉpatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu.

veṇkaṟ pāṉikaḷ

veṇ kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ cilavēṉum moheñco-tarōvil iruntaṉa eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttac cila citinta oṭukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.ivi nīlaṅ kalanta cāmpal niṟattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭinta oṭukaḷ nirampak kiṭikkāmiyiṉ, ippāttiraṅkaḷ moheñco-tarōvil arukiyē iruntaṉa eṉṟu niṉikkalām. āṉāl, ivi cumēriyāvil nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. palucistāṉ-īrāṉ ellip piratēcattil kaṟuppum nīlamum tīṭṭappaṭṭa ittakiya veṇkaṟ pāttira ōṭukaḷ palavaṟṟi ṭākṭar makkē kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar. eṉavē, ‘ivi, cintup piratēcattiṟku mēṟkē pēraḷavil payaṉpaṭṭaṉa; cintup piratēcattil mikakkuṟinta aḷavilēyē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa’, eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.

kippiṭi koṇṭa kalaṉkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta vāṇal caṭṭi oṉṟu irupuṟamum kippiṭi koṇṭatāka irukkiṉṟatu. periya tāḻikaḷiṉ kalayam pōṉṟa mēlmūṭikaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟattil maṭṭum kippiṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cila maṭkalaṉkaḷil kippiṭikkup patilākak kiviral nuḻiyat takkavāṟu tuḷikaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, harappāvil oru puṟam kippiṭi koṇṭa kalayamum irupuṟam kippiṭi koṇṭa kalayamum kiṭittaṉa. iraṭṭik kippiṭi uḷḷa pāttiraṅkaḷ pala irākkil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.[2] kippiṭi kaḷuṭaṉ ōviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ pala mālṭṭāvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.[3] kippiṭiyuḷḷa mūṭikaḷ jemṭeṭnasr eṉṉum iṭattil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa[4]

mikkūṭukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil āṭukaḷ pōlac ciṟiya kaṟkaḷil cetukkappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēṟpuṟam kuḻiyāka iruntatu. akkuḻi mi ūṟṟikkoḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭatām. ipporuḷkaḷ aṅku mikutiyākak kiṭikkavilli. āṉāl, ivi pōṉṟavi pala kirisi aṭutta ējiyaṉ tīvukaḷil akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavām. ivi ‘ur’ eṉṉum iṭattil ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil mik kūṭukaḷākavē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu sar ārtar ivāṉs aṟikiṉṟār. iḵtu uṇmiyāyiṉ, cintup piratēca makkaḷ 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē miyik koṇṭu eḻutum muṟiyik kiyāṭi vantaṉar eṉpatu aṟiyattakum.

purimaṇikaḷ

pala vaki maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi vikkap purimaṉikaḷ payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. avi maṇṇālum marattālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi uyaramākavum parumaṉākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil 20 ce.mī. uyaramuḷḷa purimaṇikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. periyatāḻikaḷi vikka marappurimaṉikaḷē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. cātāraṇa māṉa purimaṇi pōṉṟu karuṅkallik koṇṭu 30 ce.mī. uyarattil ceyyappaṭṭa purimaṇikaḷ pala moheñco-trōvil kiṭittaṉa. ivi iliṅkaṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil vikkattakka ‘yōṉikaḷ’, eṉṟu ivaṟṟik kaṇṭu piṭitta irāypahatūr tayārām cahaṉi kūṟiyuḷḷār. āyiṉ, ṭākṭar makkē, ivi tūṇkaṭku aṭiyil vikkattakka kal puri maṇikaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.

putikkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ

pala vīṭukaḷil tāṉiyaṅkaḷik koṭṭivikkap payaṉ paṭṭa periyatāḻikaḷ pātiyaḷavu nilattiṟ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēṟpākam mikka vaḻavaḻappāka amintuḷḷatu. elikaḷ ēṟātiruttaṟkeṉṟē ivvaḷavu vaḻavaḻappāka immēṟpakuti amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṉṟār. nilattiṟ putitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟṟāṉ nakikaḷum piṟa vili uyarnta poruḷkaḷum vittup pātukākkappaṭṭaṉa. ippaḻakkam cintup piratēcak kirāmaṅkaḷil iṉṟum iruppatāka aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

elip poṟikaḷ

elikaḷip piṭikkum poṟikaḷ cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇṇāl iyaṉṟavi. ivaṟṟil iraṇṭu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. poṟikkuḷ uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟi iriyāka vittu elikaḷ piṭikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.

piṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟi

cila periya tāḻikaḷiṉ veḷippuṟamum ciṟiya pāṇ ṭaṅkaḷum taṭṭukaḷum pīṅkāṉ pōlap paḷapaḷappākkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl, cintup piratēca makkaḷ appaṇṭik kālattilēyē pīṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟiyi oruvāṟu uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. ippiṅkāṉ pōṉṟa paḷapaḷappu uṇṭākkap pala poruḷkaḷic cērttu aritta kalaviyiyē payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. cintup piratēca makkaḷ akkalaviyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.āyiṉ, ippiṅkāṉ pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ ēlam, cumēr eṉṉum nāṭukaḷil irunta cavak kuḻikaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, ipporuḷkaḷ aṅkiruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭ ṭiruttalum kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar niṉikkiṉṟaṉar.

ammi, ēntiram, ural

cintup piratēca makkaḷ ammi, kuḻavi, ural ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. māvarikkum kal ēntiraṅkaḷ nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.ivi periya kaltaṭṭukaḷ mītu vikkappaṭṭē māvarikkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. arikkumpoḻutu mā citaṟik kīḻē viḻāmal iruppataṟkākavē kal taṭṭu ēntirattiṉ aṭiyil vikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. harappāvilum ittakiya ēntiraṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kalluralkaḷum harappāvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

palavaki viḷakkukaḷ

harappāvil muṭṭi vaṭivattil viḷakkoṉṟu kiṭittatu. itaṉ vāyppuṟam kuvintu, tiri iṭuvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivviḷakkiṟkuḷ oruvaki eṇṇey iṭṭut tiriyiṭṭuk koḷuttum vaḻakkam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. vēṟu palavaki viḷakkukaḷum kiṭittuḷa. moheñco-tarōvil oru vaki meḻukuvartti vikkum taṭṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ‘appaḻaṅkālattil meḻuku vattikaḷ upayōkattil iruntaṉa eṉpatu cuvi payakkum ceytiyē ākum’, eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē kūṟi viyakkiṉṟār.[5]

piṟa poruḷkaḷ

katāyutam pōṉṟu cāmpal niṟak kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru karuvi moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittatu. kal ūci, elumpu ūci, kaṟkōṭari, cempu arivāḷ, marakkaṭṭilkaḷ, ! nāṇaṟpāykaḷ, kōrippāykaḷ, mēcikaḷ, nāṟkāli pōṉṟa uyarnta marappīṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iruntamikkuriya kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, moheñco-tarōvil illattukkuriya ellāp poruḷkaḷum kuṟiviṉṟi oruvāṟu amintiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal poruntum

viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta kuḻantikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ palavākum: maṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, ūrvaṉa, nīrvāḻvaṉa, āṇpeṇ patumikaḷ, ciṟu ceppukkaḷ mutaliyaṉa nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kucci oṉṟil kayiṟu iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. akkayiṟṟi acittaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu akkucci mītu paṟavi oṉṟu ēṟuvatum iṟaṅkuvatumāka iruttaṟkuriya vacatiyōṭu kūṭiya karuvikaḷ cila kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. cintup piratēca vēṭkōvar ipporuḷkaḷic ceyvatil kāṭṭiyuḷḷa tiṟami peritum viyappukkuriyatākum. apperu makkaḷ 1.5 ce. mī. uyarattilum aḻaku mikunta poruḷkaḷic ceytuḷḷaṉar eṉiṉ, avartam paṇpaṭṭa toḻiṟ ciṟappi eṉṉeṉap pārāṭṭuvatu icciṟiya poruḷkaḷum oḻuṅkākavum veḷippuṟam miṉumiṉuppākavum eḻil mikuntu kāṇumāṟu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷami ikkālattār pārāṭṭutaṟku uriyatēyākum. toḻilāḷar caṅku tantam, kiḷiñcal oṭukaḷ, elumpukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟālum viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷic ceytuḷḷaṉar. ittakiya poruḷkaḷ cintup piratēcattil putiyuṇṭuḷḷa piṟa nakaraṅkaḷilum kiṭittalāl, iv vēlippāṭu appaḻaṅkālattil uyariya niliyil irun tiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu, kaḷimaṇ poruḷkaḷ palavum pala tiṟaṅkaḷ koṇṭavi.

iṉṟum ūtum ūtukuḻal

kaḷimaṇṇik kuṭivāṉa uruṇṭiyākac ceytu ataṉ uḷḷē ciṟitu kaṟkaḷi iṭṭuk kalakala eṉṟu olikkumāṟu ceyyappaṭṭa kilukiluppikaḷ pārkkattakkavi ākum. maṇ pantukaḷ, vālpuṟam tuḷiyuṭiya kōḻi, kuruvi pōṉṟa ūtu kuḻalkaḷ kaṇṇik kavarvaṉavākum. ‘yāṉ ōr ūtukuḻali eṭuttu ūtiṉēṉ. atu naṉṟāka ūtiyatu. 5000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ payaṉpaṭṭa ūtukuḻal iṉṟum aṅṅaṉamē payaṉpaṭal viyattaṟkuriyatu aṉṟō!’ eṉṟu aṟiñar ci.ār.rāy kūṟiyuḷḷatu viyappūṭṭum ceytiyākum. oru paṟavi vaṭivam aminta ūtukuḻal palavaki ocikaḷ uṇṭākumāṟu tiṟampaṭac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

tali acikkum erutu

vēṭkōvar, kaḷimaṇṇikkoṇṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ talivēṟu-uṭalvēṟākac ceytu, piṟaku iraṇṭiyum iṇippataṟku iyaṉṟavāṟu tuḷikaḷ iṭṭuc cūḷi yiṭṭaṉar. piṉṉar iraṇṭiyum kayiṟukoṇṭu piṇittu viṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam ceyyappaṭṭa vilaṅkup patumikaḷ kāṟṟuppaṭiṉum kayiṟṟi iḻuppiṉum taliyi acittuk koṇṭē irukkum. ivvāṟu tali acikkum vilaṅkup patumikaḷuḷ erutukaḷē palavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kikaḷi acikkum kuraṅkup patumikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷami kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

vaṇṭikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kaḷimaṇ (viḷiyāṭṭu) vaṇṭikaḷ cil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi mikka vaṉappuṭiyavi: naṉku uruṇṭu oṭakkūṭiyavāṟu amintuḷḷavi. ivi, ki.mu.3200 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ‘ur’ eṉṉum nakarattiṟ kiṭitta kalliṟ cetukkappaṭṭa tēr oṉṟiṉip peritum ottuḷḷaṉavām. ittakiya vaṇṭikaḷ kaḷimaṇṇālum veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. veṇkala vaṇṭip patumikaḷiliruntu, akkālattavar vaṇṭikku mēṟkūriyi amittup palar uḷḷē amarntu celluvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟē vaṇṭikaḷi amittup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām: avvaṇṭikaḷip pōṉṟaviyē iṉṟum cintup piratēcak kirāmaṅkaḷil iruppavi ākum. maṇ vilaṅkukaḷi maṇ taṭṭukaḷil niṟuttic cakkaram amittuk kuḻantikaḷ iḻuppu vaṇṭikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatum aṟiyattakkatu.

cokkaṭṭāṉ karuvikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kaḷimaṇṇālum kaṟkaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭa pala tiṟappaṭṭa cokkaṭṭāṉ kāykaḷ, kavaṟukaḷ, tāyakkaṭṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. kavaṟukaḷ āṟu paṭṭikaḷi uṭiyaṉavākak kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru paṭṭiyilum vaṭṭappuḷḷikaḷ iṭṭu, vevvēṟuvita eṇkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tantattāl ceyyappaṭṭapāyccikikaḷ ikkālattaṉa pōlavē amintuḷḷaṉa. ivi nimittam pārppōrāl kuṟi kūṟuvataṟkākap payaṉpaṭṭavi. ivaṟṟiṉ mītu puḷḷikaḷ illi; oviyaṅkaḷum nīlakkōṭukaḷum variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ, ivi cokkaṭṭāṉ āṭap payaṉpaṭṭavi alla eṉṟu oru cār aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ivi allāmal, cokkaṭṭāṉ kāykaḷ pala kallālum kaḷimaṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭu aḻakiya niṟaṅkaḷ pala pūcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ārāycciyāḷar cilar ivaṟṟic civaliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ mātirikaḷ cila ceṉṉip poruṭkāṭcic cāliyil vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi ikkālattil caturaṅkam āṭuvōr payaṉpaṭuttum kāykaḷ (Pawns) pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa.



↑ Drr Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.142

↑ ‘Iraq’ Vols. 1-4 published by the British School of Archeology

↑ M.A.Murray’s ‘Excavations in Malta’, part II, pp. 26-28.

↑ Dr.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.151.

↑ In any case, it is extremely interesting to discover that candles were also in use at such an early date’. Dr.Macky’ The Indus Civilization’, p. 137





* * *





﻿8. kaṇip poruḷkaḷ

payaṉpaṭṭi kaṇipporuḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta paṇṭi makkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, veḷḷīyam, kārīyam ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttippala aṇikaḷum poruḷkaḷum ceytu, koṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, avarkaḷ mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttiya ulōkaṅkaḷ cempum veṇkalamumē ākum.avarkaḷ iraṇṭu ulōkaṅkaḷit takka muṟippaṭi cērttup putiya ulōkam ceyyavum aṟintiruntaṉar.

poṉṉum veḷḷiyum

poṉ ciṟappāka nakikaṭkē payaṉpaṭṭatu. appoṉṉil ciṟitaḷavu veḷḷi kalantuḷḷatu. ataṉāl appoṉ kōlār, aṉantappūr pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭu cellappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. moheñco-tarō makkaḷ poṉṉiyum veḷḷiyiyum kalantu ‘elakṭram’[1] eṉṉum putiya ulōkam oṉṟic ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ipputiya ulōkamum nakikaḷ ceyyavē payaṉ paṭṭatām. ammakkaḷ īyattiliruntu veḷḷi eṭukkak kaṟṟiruntaṉar; aṅkaṉam tām eṭutta veḷḷiyiyē payaṉpaṭuttivantaṉar veḷḷiyik koṇṭu kālaṇikaḷum cila pāttira vakikaḷumē ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.

cempil īyak kalavi

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta cempup poruḷ kaḷil īyak kalavi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. īyak kalavi koṇṭa cempuk kaṉikaḷ irājaputaṉam, pāracīkam, palucistāṉam eṉṉum mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭu. avi cintuviṟku aṇmiyil iruttalāl aṅkiruntē cempu koṇṭuvarappaṭ ṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.

cempil nikkal kalavi

cempil īyattōṭu nikkalum kalantiruppatāka aṟiñar cōtittu aṟintuḷḷaṉar. ivvāṟē cumēriyāvil kiṭitta cempilum nikkal kalantuḷḷatu. innikkal kalappuṭiya cempuk kaṉikaḷ arēpiyāvil uḷḷa ‘ummāṉ’[2] eṉṉum iṭattiṟṟāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, cumēriyarum cintup piratēca makkaḷum avviṭattuc cempiyē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu cilar karutu kiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, intiyāvilēyē cōṭṭā nākapuriyil uḷḷa cempuk kaṉikaḷil nikkal kalavi iṉṟum kāṇappaṭuvatāl, ikkaṉikaḷiliruntē cintup piratēca makkaḷ cempik koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar makkē uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟaṉar.[3] ituvē poruttamāṉatākavum tōṟṟukiṟatu.

cempu kalanta maṇ

cempu kalanta maṇ cintup piratēcattilēyē kiṭittiruttal kūṭum eṉṟu eṇṇutaṟkuriyavāṟu maṇṇiliruntu cempi vēṟākap pirittamikkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ceṅkal koṇṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭa toṭṭikaḷil cempu kalanta maṇ kuviyalkaḷum urukkappaṭṭa cempup pāḷaṅkaḷum taṭṭikaḷum kiṭittṉa āṉāl, ivi urukkappaṭṭa muṟi uṇarumāṟu illi. ātaliṉ, potuvāka nilattiṟ kuḻi ceytu atil nilak kariyum cempum maṇṇum iṭṭut tīppaṟṟa vittu, turutti mūlam cūṭṭi mikuttuc cempi urukac ceyyum eḷiya muṟiyiyē appaṇṭi makkaḷ kiyāṇṭaṉar; iṅṅaṉam urukiya cempit tariyil veṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟu kālvāyil pāyccip pāḷam pāḷamāka eṭuttu vantaṉar. immuṟiyiṟ kiṭitta cempup pāḷam oṉṟiṉ niṟi 2½ irāttal iruntatu. itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, moheñco-tarō makkaḷ cintup piratēcattilēyē kiṭitta maṇṇuṭaṉ kalanta cempiyum veḷiyiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa cempiyum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. eṉpati aṟiyalām.

veṇkalam

ap paṇṭikkāla aṟiñar veḷḷīyattiyum cempiyum kalantu veṇkalam ākkak kaṟṟiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ toṭakkattil cempiyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; āṉāl, cempiviṭa veṇkalam uṟuti uṭiyatu eṉpati aṟintatum, veṇkalattiyum mikutiyāy payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar; potuvākac cempil 100kku 9 mutal 12 paṅku veḷḷiyattic cērppativiṭṭu, 100kku 22 mutal 26 paṅku vari veḷḷiyattic cērttu veṇkalamākkiyuḷḷaṉar. atē kālattil cumēriyāvilum im muṟi kiyāḷappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ṭākṭar pīrāṅkpōrṭ, ṭākṭar ulli pōṉṟa aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, atu tavaṟu; cumēriyarkku veṇkalam ceyyat terintilatu. avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya veṇkalam piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntē varavaḻikkappaṭṭatu; cumēriyar cempip payaṉpaṭuttavē aṟintiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, atē kālattil cintup piratēcamakkaḷ cempiyum veṇkalattiyum payaṉpaṭutta naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṟu ōr aṟiñar[4] aṟikiṉṟaṉar. cintup piratēca makkaḷ veṇkalam ceyta muṟi mikka paṇpuṭiyatāka iruntatāl, piṟkālattil acōkaṉ āṭciyil nālantāvil itē muṟi ciṟitum māṟṟamiṉṟik kik koḷḷappaṭṭatu.[5]

veḷḷiyam

moheñco-tarōvil cempoṭu kalappataṟkē veḷḷiyam mikutiyāka vēṇṭiyiruntatāl, veḷḷiyattāṟ poruḷkaḷ palavum ceyyakkūṭavilli. ataṉāl, ammakkaḷ pampāy, pīhār, orissā, mutaliya piṟa maṇṭilaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷḷīyatti varavaḻittiruttal vēṇṭum.

kārīyam

cintup piratēca makkaḷ kārīyatti mikutiyāyp payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu kūṟutal iyalātu. aṅku itukāṟum kiṭitta poruḷkaḷuḷ taṭṭu oṉṟē kārīyattāl ceyyappaṭṭatāka uḷḷatu. inta ulōkattāl ceyyappaṭṭa vēṟu poruḷkaḷ kiṭikkavilli.

makkaḷiṉ mati nuṭpam

orē iṭattil ellāp poruḷkaḷum kiṭippatu iyaṟki yaṉṟu. ovvoru poruḷ ovvōr iṭattiṟ kiṭippatē iyaṟki. āyiṉum, nām namakkut tēvippaṭum pala poruḷkaḷip palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varavaḻittukkoḷkiṟōm allavā? nammip pōṉṟē appaṇṭikkāla makkaḷum taṅkaḷ mati nuṭpattāl avi avi kiṭikkakkūṭiya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varavaḻittu, avaṟṟik kūrtta matiyuṭaṉ payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpatu mēṟcoṉṉa ceytikaḷiliruntu aṟiyalām.acceytikaḷ uṇmiyāyiṉ, ammakkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil kōlār vari irunta nāṭṭi naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar; teṉṉāṭṭavaruṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytaṉar eṉpaṉa aṟiyalām. maṇikaḷum piṟavum ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭa oruvakip paccikkal (amejāṉ kal) nīlakiriyil uḷḷa ‘toṭṭa peṭṭā’ eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu moheñco-tarōvukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟutal uṇmiyāyiṉ, accintup piratēcamakkaḷ nīlakiriyiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyām. ammakkaṭkuc caṅkum muttum tamiḻakattiliruntu pōyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. aḵtāyiṉ, avarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭuṭaṉum vāṇipat toṭarpu koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉal poruntum. iṅṅaṉam ammakkaḷ appaḻaṅkālattil carittira kālattiṟkum pala āyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar intiyā muḻuvatilum vāṇipap paḻakkam koṇṭiruntaṉar - āṅkāṅkuk kiṭitta poruḷkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉpaṭuttic cukavāḻkki vāḻntaṉar eṉṉum ariya ceytikaḷi aṟiya evartām viyappuṟār!

vēli muṟi

avvaṟiñarkaḷ cemmiyum veṇkalattiyum urukki vārppaṭamākkiṉar; takaṭukaḷākat taṭṭi āṇi koṇṭu poruttiṉar; ivviraṇṭu muṟikaḷāṟṟāṉ ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum ceytukoṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, avarkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi ivaṟṟāṟ ceyta poruḷkaḷukkē poṭi vittup paṟṟavikkum muṟiyik kiyāṇṭaṉar.

cempu-veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta cempu veṇkalak kaṉṉārkaḷ katti, vāḷ, īṭṭi, ampu mutaliya karuvikḷiyum, palvēṟu aḷavukaḷiyuṭiya kōppikaḷ nīrccāṭikaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, tāḻikaḷ.ivaṟṟiṉ mūṭikaḷ mutaliya vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉar; ciṟiya poruḷkaḷi urukki vārppaṭamāka vārttuc ceytuḷḷaṉar; periya poruḷkaḷit takaṭukaḷāka aṭittup poruttic ceytuḷḷaṉar. ivaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉa poruḷkaḷukkuk kippiṭiyē illi. iṅṅaṉamē amrī, cāṉhātarō iv viṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta cempu - veṇkalap poruḷkaḷukkum kippiṭi illātatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

īṭṭikaḷ

īṭṭi nīṇṭu akaṉṟa ilivaṭivil āṉatu kūriya nuṉi uṭiyatu; pakkaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum kūrākkappaṭṭavi. iṅkaṉam amintuḷḷa īṭṭi marappiṭi uṭiyatu. ilipōṉṟa pakuti mikavum meṉmiyāka iruppatāl oru muṟi eṟiyappaṭṭatum vaḷintu keṭum iyalpuṭiyatu. iti nōkka, ammakkaḷ pakivari etirnōkkip pōrkkaruvikaḷic ceytavar allar eṉpatum, vēṭṭikkē akkaruvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatum uṇaralām.

uṭi vāḷkaḷ

itu naṭuppakuti taṭittu, muṉi maḻuṅki, iru pakkamum kūrmi ākkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.itaṉ muṉimaḻuṅki iruppatāl, itu māṟṟārik kuttum poruṭṭuc ceyyappaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpati aiyamaṟa aṟiyalām. moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta ittakiya uṇṭavāḷkaḷil iraṇṭē kuṟippiṭattakkavi; oṉṟu 4.5 ce. mī. nīḷamum, 5.5 ce. mī. akalamum 1 ce. mī. kaṉamum uṭiyatu. maṟṟoṉṟu aḷaviṟ ciṟiyatu. ittakiya vāḷkaḷ cumēriyāvil kiṭittila; āyiṉ, ekiptu, cipras, ciriyā mutaliya nāṭukḷil kiṭittaṉa. ivi yāvum marak kippiṭi uṭiyaṉavē ākum.

iṭivāḷkaḷum kattikaḷum

iṭuppil ceruki vikkattakka iṭivāḷkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cila nīṇṭavi; cila kuṭṭi yāṉavi; cila ilipōṉṟa vaṭivuṭiyavi; cila oru puṟam kūriyavi.pala irupuṟam kūriyavi. ivi aṉṟic ciṟiya kattikaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. katti pōlak kallāl āṉa karuvikaḷ cilavum kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa.

vēlkaḷ

veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa vēlkaḷē palavākum. avi kūriya muṉiyuṭaṉ eḷitil pāyntōṭak kūṭiyavāṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.avaṟṟuṭ periyavi37.5 ce.mī.nīḷamum 12.5 ce. mī. akalamum palvēṟu vaṭivamum koṇṭaviyāka uḷḷaṉa. ciṟiyaviyum kūrmi poruntik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ampu muṉikaḷ

ivi melliya veṇkalat takaṭu koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭavi. iviyē nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi vēṭṭiyāṭuvōrāl peritum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivaṟṟuṭ pala irupuṟamum irampattiṉ amippi uṭiyavi. īṭṭi muṉikaḷip pōlavē iviyum marak kippiṭi uṭiyaṉavākum.

irampam

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta irampaṅkaḷ pala tiṟappaṭṭavi. oṉṟu ari vaṭṭa vaṭivattil kūriya paṟkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veṇkala irampam oṉṟu 38, 5 ce. mī. nīḷamum, 8.5 ce.mī. akalamum, 1 ce.mī. kaṉamum uṭiyatu.itaṟku akaṉṟa marakkippiṭi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. kip piṭiyi irampattōṭu poruttutaṟkeṉṟu mūṉṟu tuḷikaḷ irampattil amintuḷḷaṉa. itaṉ paṟkaḷ viyappuṟu muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya irampam cumēriyāvilum ekiptilum kāṇappaṭavilli. itu cintup piratēcattiṟkē taṉipperumi taravallatāka irukkiṉṟatu. ciṟiya irampaṅkaḷ caṅkaṟukkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa pōlum! eṉṉi? iṉṟum vaṅkāḷattiṟ caṅkaṟuppavar ittakiya ciṟiya irampaṅ kaḷiyē payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ātaliṉ eṉka. ‘caṅkaṟuppa teṅkaḷ kulam, caṅkaraṉārk kētukulam’ eṉṟu pāṭiya nakkīrar piṟanta tamiḻakattilum caṅkaṟukka ittakiya irampaṅkaḷiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal kūṭiyatē aṉṟō?

uḷikaḷ

ivi pala vakippaṭṭavi: periyavi 12, 5 ce. mī. uyarattiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭavi; ciṟiyavi 4.5 ce. mī. uyaramuṭiyavi. ivi irupuṟamum kūrmi uṭiyavi. pala uruṭṭu vaṭivil amintavi; oṉṟu maṭṭum paṭṭi vaṭivil amintatu. ippaṭṭi vaṭiva uḷi cumēriyā mutaliya ayal nāṭukaḷil kiṭittilatu. ivvuḷikaḷ kalliyum marattiyum cetukkap payaṉpaṭṭaviyākum; muttirikaḷic cetukku vataṟkeṉṟē mika nuṇṇiya muṉiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ciṟṟuḷikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

tōl cīvum uḷikaḷ

tōlik koṇṭu toḻil purintavar payaṉpaṭuttiya uḷi taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil amintuḷḷatu. akkālattavar ceruppukaḷi aṇintu iruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ itukāṟum kiṭittila. āyiṉum, mirutaṅkam pōṉṟa cila vāttiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, tōl vēli akkālattil iruntatu eṉpati uṇaralām. attōl-toḻilāḷar mēṟcoṉṉa uḷiyip payaṉ paṭuttiṉar pōlum! atu katti pōṉṟa takaṭu koṇṭu uḷi pōla muṉiyil maṭṭum kūrmi uṭiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. or uḷi marappiṭiyuṭaṉ kiṭittuḷḷatu. ammarappiṭi, ikkālattuk kattip piṭikaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttum maraṅkoṇṭē ceyyappaṭṭiruttal mikka viyappūṭṭuvatākum.

kōṭarikaḷ

kōṭarikaḷ nīṇṭum kuṟukiyum akaṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. ivi, accuc ceytu atil ulōkatti urukki vārttuc ceyyappaṭṭavi eṉṟu aṟiñar matikkiṉṟaṉar; vārppaṭam vārtta piṟaku eṭuttuc cammaṭṭi koṇṭu aṭittuc carippaṭutti oḻuṅkāṉa kōṭariyākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru kōpari 27.5 ce.mī nīḷamum: pavuṇṭ 3 avuṉs niṟiyum uṭiyatākum. ikkōṭarikaḷ naṭuvil marakkāmpi tuḻikkat tuḷikaḷ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

vāycci

moheñco-tarōvil veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa vāycci oṉṟu kiṭittatu. atu mikunta vēlippāṭu koṇṭatu. ataṉ naṭuvil marakkāmpu nuḻiya iṭam viṭappṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ nīḷam 25 ce.mī. ākum. atu, kākasas malip piratēcattil kūpaṉ āṟṟup paṭukiyiṟ kiṭitta vāycciyi ottuḷḷatu viyappukkuriyatu.itaṉ kālam iṉṉatu eṉpati iṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟal iyalavilli.

maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil nāṉku vitamāṉa maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa: (1) ari vaṭṭa vaṭivil nīṇṭa kippiṭi koṇṭavi. iviyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭṭavi. (2) ‘ṭa’ pōṉṟa vaṭivil amintuḷḷavi. (3) vaḷintirukkum katti. itaṉ kippiṭi katti vaḷivukku ēṟṟavāṟu piṉpuṟam vaḷintu virittut talipōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itupōṉṟavi, ekiptil ‘pārō maṉṉar’ tam 18ām talimuṟi āṭciyil vaḻakkil iruntaṉavām. (4) nāṉkām vaki maḻittaṟ katti nīṇṭu nērāka muṉiyil maṭṭum vaḷivuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu melliya takaṭṭil mikka kūrmiyākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkatti piṟavaṟṟiṉum arukik kiṭittiruttalāl, matippuṭiya vakiyākak karutappaṭṭiruttal kūṭiyatēyām. innāṉku vaki yaṉṟic ciṟu maḻittaṟ kattikaḷum pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi mukattil maṭṭum iṉṟi uṭalilum mayir kaḷiyap payaṉpaṭṭaviyākum. ivi mikutiyākak kiṭittalāl ivaṟṟi ‘irupālarum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar pōlum!’[6] eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṉṟār.

uḻu karuvikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta oruvakik karuvikaḷ kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi 27.5 ce.mī. nīḷamum 8.5 ce. mī. akalamum, 7.5 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭiyaṉa. avi irupuṟamum kūrākkappaṭṭavi. avi amintuḷḷa muṟiyiliruntum, mikka kaṉamāka iruppatiliruntum kalappik karuvikaḷāka iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar apippirāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivi pōlvaṉa cempilum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

tūṇṭil muṭkaḷ

cintu yāṟu mīṉkaḷ niṟintatātaliṉ, akkāla makkaḷ mīṉ piṭikkuntoḻilil pērārvam kāṭṭiṉar eṉpatimeyppikkak kaṇakkaṟṟa tūṇṭil muṭkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi veṇkalattāl iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. vaḷinta muṭkaḷi uṭiyaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mēlmuṉiyil nūl nuḻiyat tuḷi uḷḷatu. attuḷiyil uṟutiyāṉa kayiṟṟi kaṭṭi mīṉ piṭittaṉar.

piṟa karuvikaḷ

mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkāta taccarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya koṭṭāppuḷikaḷ, iḻippuḷikaḷ kattikaḷ, tuḷiyiṭum karuvikaḷ, necavāḷar payaṉpaṭuttiya karuvikaḷ, ūcikaḷ mutaliyaṉa palavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. tuṇi tikkum ūcikaḷ 5 ce.mī nīḷamuṭiyavi. avi cempālum veṇkalat tālum ceyyappaṭṭavi. iviyaṉṟit tantattāl iyaṉṟa taṭikaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa. ivi 20 ce. mī. nīḷam uṭiyavi; naṉku vaḻavaḻappākkappaṭṭu irupuṟamum kaṟuppu niṟam pūcappaṭṭavi. ivaṟṟuḷ ciṟiyaviyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi uṭikaḷi māṭṭi vikkac cuvarkaḷil cerukap payaṉpaṭṭavi (Coal Stand) ākalām. paḷiṅkuk kal, cuṇṇāmpukkal, kaṟutta paccik kal pōṉṟa kaṭiṉa poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa katāyutam pōṉṟavi kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. koṇṭi ūcikaḷ, pottāṉkaḷ, mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭikaḷ, kaṇṇukku mi tīṭṭum ūcikaḷ, patumikaḷ, maṇikaḷ, pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum cempālum veṇkalattālum ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa koṇṭi ūcikaḷ mūṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. pottāṉkaḷ elumpukaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalm, uyartarakkal mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, kārīyam, kal, mīṇ mutaliyavaṟṟāl pāttiraṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, cintup piratēca makkaḷ inta ulōkaṅkaḷik koṇṭu: tam vīṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya pāttiraṅkaḷiyum, tamakku vēṇṭiya aṇikalaṉ kaḷiyum, karuvikaḷiyum ceytu nākarika vāḻkki naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpatu teḷivātal kāṇka.

cāṇik kal

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ kattikaḷiyum piṟakaruvi kaḷiyum tīṭṭik kūrmi ākkuvataṟkuṅ potuvākac ceṅkaṟkaḷiyē cāṇik kaṟkaḷākap peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; ciṟappākak karuṅkal, vaḻavaḻappuḷḷa kal mutaliyavaṟṟiyum cāṇik kaṟkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. vēṟu cila kaṟkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷi merukiṭutaṟkeṉṟē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

eṇ iṭappaṭṭa karuvikaḷ

innakarattiṟ kiṭitta cempuk karuvikaḷiṉ kuviyal oṉṟil irunta pala karuvikaḷ cittirak kuṟikaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakiyak kuṟiyuḷḷavi harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. “ivi aracāṅkattārkku uriyavi yākalām. ataṉāṟṟāṉ. kaḷavu pōkātirukka eṇ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam karuvikaḷ mītu eṇ iṭum paḻakkam paṇṭi ekiptilum kiyāḷappaṭṭatu”, eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.
