﻿9. vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavikaḷum

maṉitaṉukku muṉ tōṉṟiya vilaṅkukaḷ

ulakam tōṉṟiyapōtu maṉitaṉukku muṉ tōṉṟiyavi vilaṅkukaḷē eṉpatu uyirnūṟ pulavar muṭipu. mutalil tōṉṟiya maṉitaṉ ivvilaṅkukaṭkup payantē malik kukikaḷil vāḻntu vantāṉ; avaṟṟik kolla villiyum kaṟkaruvikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ; tāṉ vacatiyōṭu kuṭicikaḷik kaṭṭi vāḻa avvilaṅkukaḷi oḻikka vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. avpoḻutu avaṉ vēṭṭiyāṭali mēṟkoṇṭāṉ; koṭumiyaṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷi uṇavākak koṇṭāṉ; iṅṅaṉam maṉitaṉ vilaṅkukaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiyatāṟṟāṉ ‘vēṭṭuvaṉ, vēṭaṉ’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷip peṟṟāṉ. avaṉē maṉita nākarika varalāṟṟiṉ mutaṟ pakutikku uriyavaṉ.

kālattiṟkēṟṟa māṟupāṭu

ivvāṟu mutal maṉitaṉ kaṇṭu añciyavilaṅkukaḷ pala iṉṟu illi. avi pala lakṣakkaṇakkāṉa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iṟantu viṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷ maṇṇuḷ maṟintiruntaṉa. uyirnūl aṟiñar avaṟṟic cōtittup pala ariya ceytikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. cintup piratēca nākarika kālattil irunta vilaṅkukaḷ, cila iṉṟu illi. ataṟku muṉ irunta vilaṅkukaḷ, cila cintup piratēca kālattil illi. kuḻantikaḷiyum maṉitariyum tūkkic cellum peruṅ kaḻukukaḷ ippoḻutu illi allavā? mikap paḻiya kālattil avi iruntaṉa eṉṟu uyir nūṟ pulavar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avi iruntaṉa eṉpati irāmāyaṇam - pāratam, piruhat kati pōṉṟa perunūlkaḷil uḷḷa ceytikaḷum meyppikkiṉṟaṉa. ivi niṟka, cintup piratēca vilaṅkukaḷik kāṇpōm.

cintup piratēca vilaṅkukaḷ

ārāycciyiṟ kiṭitta cila vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷi aṟiñar ārāyntuḷḷaṉar; viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷuḷ, pala vilaṅkuppatumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa muttirikaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. im mūṉṟikkoṇṭum cintup piratēcavilaṅkukaḷ iṉṉavitām eṉpati ārāycciyāḷar muṭivu ceytuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟi oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka iṅkuk kāṇpōm:

yāṉi

cintup piratēcattiṉ mēṟkelli kīrtar, intukuṣ, culimāṉ mutaliya malit toṭarkaḷum avaṟṟic cērnta kāṭukaḷumē ātaliṉ, kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷākiya yāṉi, puli, karaṭi, kāṇṭāmirukam, kāṭṭu erumi mutaliyavaṟṟic cintup piratēca makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ yāṉi mutaṉmi peṟṟatākum. vilaṅkiṉattil iṇiyaṟṟa paruvuṭal peṟṟuḷḷa ivvilaṅku, ataṉ tantam paṟṟip peru matippup peṟṟiruntatu. tantattāl iyaṉṟa cila poruḷkaḷ moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittuḷḷamiyāl, cintup piratēca makkaḷ yāṉikaḷi vaḷarkkavum vēṭṭiyāṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṉal tavaṟākātu.

erutukaḷ

erutu mikap paḻiya kālam mutalē payirt toḻiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭa vilaṅkākum. ēlam, cumēr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvilaṅku uḻutoḻiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭatu eṉpati avvan nāṭṭu muttirikaḷ vāyilāka uṇaralām. itu vaṇṭiyiḻukkavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. itaṉ pērutavi kaṇṭē cintup piratēca makkaḷ itaṉit teyvattaṉmi vāyntatāka niṉittaṉar. civapirāṉ yōkattil iruppatupōlavum, avaric cuṟṟilum cila vilaṅkukaḷ niṟṟal pōlavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa muttiriyil erutum oṉṟāka uḷḷatu. civapirāṉ pacu (vilaṅkukaṭku pati (talivaṉ) eṉappaṭuvatiyē ammuttiri kuṟittatātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. appaḻaṅkāla mutalē erutu ciṟappuṭi vilaṅkākak karutappaṭṭamiyāṟṟāṉ pōlum, atu civaṉārkkuriya ūrtiyākap piṟṟi nāḷil karutappaṭṭatu; ‘nanti’ eṉṉum peyar iṭappaṭṭatu.

vēṟōr iṉattic cērnta erutum[1] cintup piratēcattil iruntatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl, aḵtu iṉṟiya airōppiya erutukaṭkut tanti eṉṟu aṟiñar tīkṣat karutukiṉṟār; ‘ivverutu uruvaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākac cumēriyar muttirikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.ivaṟṟic cintup piratēca makkaḷ naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar; ivi intiyāvilum iruntaṉa’, eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.

nāykaḷ

vīṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷuḷ nāyum oṉṟu aṉṟō? nāy illāta ūr ētu? nakaram ētu? ceṅkallik kāyavittirunta pōtu ataṉmītunāykaḷ naṭantuceṉṟamiyāl uṇṭāṉaaṭiccuvaṭukaḷ appaṭiyē irukka, akkaṟkaḷ cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭu, vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭap payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akkaṟkaḷil iṉṟum kāṇappaṭum aṭiccuvaṭu kaḷik koṇṭum patumikaḷik koṇṭum aṟiñar, moheñco-tarōvilnāykaḷ iruntiruttalvēṇṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. cātāraṇa nāykaḷ aṉṟi vēṭṭi nāykaḷum, ikkālattup ‘pul-ṭāk’ (Bull-dog) pōṉṟa nāykaḷum aṅku iruntaṉavām. ōriṉa nāykaḷ kuṭṭi kālkaḷuṭaṉum curuṇṭa vāluṭaṉum iruntaṉa. maṟṟōr iṉa nāykaḷ nīṇṭa akaṉṟa kātukaḷuṭaṉum nīṇṭa vāluṭaṉum iruntaṉa. cila nāykaḷ kaḻuttil paṭṭikaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟu iruntaṉa. “iṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nāykaḷ, intiya ōnāy iṉattiliruntu tōṉṟiyavi; cātāraṇa nāykaḷ intiya nari iṉattiliruntu tōṉṟiyavi’, eṉṟu aṟiñar payṉi piracāt aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya nāykaḷiṉa elumpukaḷ harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. intiya nāykaḷ marakkalaṅkaḷ mūlam pāpilōṉiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatiliruntu, nāykaḷi vaḷarkkum vaḻakkam, paṇṭikkāla mutalē intiyāvil iruntateṉpatu teḷivām.

pūṉikaḷ

cintup piratēcattil nāykaḷip pōlap pūṉikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu kūṟakkūṭavilli. eṉṉi? cila elumpukaḷē pūṉikaḷik kuṟippaṉavākak kiṭittiruttalāl eṉka. mēlum, pūṉikaḷ apicīṉiyāviṟṟām tōṟṟam eṭuttavi. ātaliṉ, appaṇṭik kālattil intiyāvil mikac cilavē koṇṭuvarappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. piṉṉar nāḷaṭivil pūṉikaḷ intiyāviṟ perukiviṭṭaṉa.[2]

paṉṟikaḷ

paṉṟikaḷiṉ elumpukaḷ moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi akkālattil mikkiruntaṉa. ātaliṉ, ammakkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyi uṇṭu vantaṉar. ivi aṉṟik kāṭṭup paṉṟikaḷum uṇavukkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

āṭukaḷ

maliyāṭukaḷ, veḷḷāṭukaḷ eṉpavaṟṟik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ palavākum. veḷḷāṭukaḷ cintup piratēcattil mikkiruntaṉa. kāṣmīrap pakutikaḷil iṉṟum maliyāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟippōṉṟa āṭukaḷiṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷē moheñco - tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avvāṭukaḷ cintup piratēcattiṉ mēṟkumalip piratēcattuk kāṭukaḷil ērāḷamāka iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. maliyāṭukaḷip pōṉṟē oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cintup piratēcam mutal cumēriyā variyuḷḷa ciṟappāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, aḷvariyāṭukaḷiṉ iṟicci cintup piratēca makkaḷiṉ uṇavup poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟākac ciṟantiruntatu eṉpatil aiyamilli.

kaḻutikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kaḻutikaḷ iruntaṉavākat teriyavilli; āṉāl, harappāvil iruntaṉa. eṉṉi? aṅkuk kaḻutikaḷiṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷ kiṭittaṉa ātaliṉ eṉka. āyiṉum, cintup piratēcattuk kaḻutikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭa vitam uṇarumāṟilli. eṉṉi? poti cumakkavum vaṇṭi iḻukkavum erutukaḷē peritum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. ātaliṉ eṉka. cumēriyāvilum iṅṅaṉamē erutukaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

māṉkaḷ

palavaki māṉkaḷiṉ kompukaḷum, elumpukaḷum cintup piratēcattiṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. aṅkup puḷḷimāṉkaḷum kalimāṉkaḷumē mikkiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kompukaṭkum iṟiccikkumē cintup piratēcattavar ivaṟṟi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. vēṭṭiyāṭi vantaṉar. māṉ kompum kuḷampum maruntukkum payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.

erumikaḷ

maṇṇālum cempālum iyaṉṟa erumip patumikaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷamiyāl, cintup piratēcattil erumikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉak kūṟalām. āyiṉ, cintup piratēcat taṭpa veppa nilikaḷ kāraṇamāka, aṅku avi nīṇṭanāḷ uyirvāḻak kūṭavilli eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟivikkiṉṟaṉar.

oṭṭakam

mikac cila oṭṭaka elumpukkūṭukaḷē cintup piratēcattiṟ kiṭittaṉa. eṉavē, avi aṅkuppalavāka iruntila eṉak kūṟal mikiyākātu. mēlum ivi arēyiyāviliruntē ap paḻaṅkālattil koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟutal poruttamāṉatu.

muyalkaḷ

muyal mikap paḻiyakāla vilaṅku eṉpatu uyir nūṟ pulavar muṭipu. muyalkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷ pala cintup piratēcattiṟ kiṭittaṉa ātaliṉ, muyal uṇavu ciṟanta uṇavākak karutappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

āmi mutaliyaṉa

āmi oṭukaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. āmi iṟicci uṇavākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āmi oṭukaḷ vēṟu kāriyaṅkaṭkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. aṅku mutali iṟicciyum payaṉpaṭṭatu. aṅkuk kīri, kuraṅku, aṇil, eli ivi mikkiruntaṉa eṉpatu viḷiyāṭṭup patumikaḷ mūlam uṇarakkiṭakkiṟatu. elippoṟikaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, aṅku elikaḷ tuṉpam poṟukka muṭiyātapaṭi, iruntatu pulaṉākiṟatu.

paṟavikaḷ

ivaṟṟuḷ moheñco-tarō makkaḷ peru viruppamuṟṟu vaḷarttavi kiḷikaḷē ākum. eṉṉi? kiḷikaḷ pōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷa patumikaḷ aṅkup palavākak kiṭittuḷḷamiyāl eṉka. pala patumikaḷum ūtu kuḻalkaḷum kērḻi vaṭivattil ceyyappaṭṭu uḷḷamiyāl kōḻikaḷum palavāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷiyalām. kōḻic caṇṭi[3] akkālattil iruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē caṇṭikku eṉṟē kōḻikaḷ tayārākkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum aṟiyattakka ceytiyē ākum, harappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ mītu mayilē ciṟappuṟat tiṭṭappaṭṭiruttalāl, paṇṭi makkaḷ mayili naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivām. puṟākkaḷiṉ patumikaḷik koṇṭu cintup piratēcattil puṟākkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpati aṟiyalām. ippatumikaḷ mecopoṭṭēmiyāvil kiṭitta paṟavip patumikaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. oru peṇ patumiyiṉ tali mītu iraṇṭu puṟākkaḷ iruppatupōlac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉṟi miṉākkaḷ, paruntukaḷ pōṉṟaviyum aṅku iruntaṉa eṉṉalām.

nākaṅkaḷ

harappāvil pāmpup patumikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; cila muttirikaḷ pāmpu paṭam virittāṟ pōlac cetukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītum pāmpu uruvam tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. akkāla makkaḷ pāmpi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar; pāmpiṟkup pāl vārttaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇutaṟkuriya kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.



↑ Bosturas (parent of the modern European cattle) K. N. Dikshit in his ‘Pre - historic Civilization of the Indus Valley’, p.39.

↑ Pre-historic Civilization of the Indus Valley, p.41.

↑ kōḻiccaṇṭi mikap paḻiya kālamutalē tamiḻakattil uṇṭu eṉpatip puṟapporuḷ nūlkaḷāl uṇarka.





* * *





﻿10. uṇavum uṭiyum

viḷiporuḷkaḷ

cintu āṟṟuveḷi paruva maḻiyum āṟṟup pāyccalum ciṟappuṟap peṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatākum. ātaliṉ, aṅku irunta nilaṅkaḷ ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉa; vaṇṭal maṇ paṭintiruntaṉa: ciṟanta marutanilaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ātaliṉ, aṅkuk kōtumi, nel, vāṟ kōtumi (pārli), parutti, paṭṭāṇi, eḷ, pērintu, mulāmpaḻam mutaliyaṉa ērāḷamākap payirākkappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟum appakutikaḷil nellum kōtumiyum ciṟappuṭi viḷi poruḷkaḷāka iruttal kavaṉikkat takkatu. ippalavaki viḷiporuḷkaḷi appaṇṭi makkaḷ tāḻikaḷiṟ cēmittu vittiruntaṉar.attāḻikaḷ nilattil pātiyaḷavu putikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.

maruta nilamum nakar vaḷamum

iṅṅaṉam maruta nilappaṇpu mikutippaṭṭa cintuveḷiyil, nakaraṅkaḷ ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉa eṉpatil viyappoṉṟum illi aṉṟō? āṟṟuppāyccal peṟṟa maruta nilamē, makkaḷ nākarika vāḻkki naṭatta ēṟṟateṉat tamiḻnūlkaḷum cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuttāṉ aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷum kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅkaḷum amikkappaṭutal peruvaḻakku āriyar, cintu-kaṅkic camaveḷikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa anāriyar nakaraṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu rik vētattil kūṟiyiruttaliyum cintu veḷiyil cumār 3200 ki. mī. tolivu cuṟṟip pala paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ maṇ mūṭu paṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟit tōṇṭi ārāycci naṭttutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar pāṉarjī tam aṟikkiyiṟ kūṟiyiruttaliyum nōkka, cintup piratēcattiṉ vaḷamum avvaḷappaṅ kāraṇamākap pala nakaraṅkaḷ aṅku amikkappaṭṭamiyum naṉku viḷaṅkum.

pulāl uṇṭa makkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa terukkaḷilum illaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷippaṭum kālvāykaḷilum vaṭikāl kaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum elumput tuṇṭukaḷ pala citaṟik kiṭantaṉa. avaṟṟip pārviyiṭṭa ārāycciyāḷar, ‘potuvāka innakara makkaḷ pulāl uṇṇum vaḻakkattiṉar eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. ikkālattil cōṟṟilēyē iṟicciyik kalantu tayārikkum ‘pulavu’ (pulāl)[1] eṉṉum oruvaki uṇavu akkālattilum camikkappaṭṭu vantatu. ammakkaḷ cōṟṟil āṭu māṭu, paṉṟi, āmi, mutali ivaṟṟiṉ iṟiccit tuṇṭaṅkaḷik kalantu ‘pulavu’ ceytaṉar; palaviki mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷi uṇṭu vantaṉar; palatiṟappaṭṭa paṟavikaḷi uṇṭu vantaṉar; piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntu kalaṉkaḷ mūlam koṇṭuvarap paṭṭa ularnta mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷum[2] iṟicci vakikaḷum uṇṭaṉar; iṟiccit tuṇṭaṅkaḷi ularttip pakkuvappaṭuttip periya tāḻikaḷil aṭittu avvappōtu payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. ‘ittāḻikaḷil veḷḷāṭu, erutu, cemmaṟiyāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ elumpukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, ivvilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟiccikaḷ patappaṭutti aṭittu vikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu uṇmi. āṉāl, ippatappaṭuttappaṭṭa iṟiccittuṇṭaṅkaḷ tiṉantōṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟō, vicēṭa kālaṅkaḷiṟṟām payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟō ippōtu tiṭṭamākak kūṟutal iyalātu’.[3]

piṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ

appaṇṭi makkaḷ kāykaṟikaḷiyum pāl, veṇṇey, tayir, mōr, ney mutaliyavaṟṟiyum uṇavupporuḷkaḷākak koṇṭiruntaṉar; palavakip paḻaṅkaḷiyum uṇṭu vantaṉar.

camiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ

avarkaḷ māvarittup palavaki uṇavup poruḷkaḷ ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉar. vāṇal caṭṭi, āṭṭukkal, iṭṭali ūṟṟum caṭṭi ivi kiṭittuḷḷamiyē immuṭipukkuriya cāṉṟākum. kuḻampu kūṭṭuvataṟkuriya vēṟu kaṟikaḷ ceyvataṟkuriya macālip poruḷkaḷi iṭittu, avaṟṟip pala aṟi (macāli)p peṭṭiyil vittiruntaṉar. aṅkuk kiṭitta ammi - kuḻavi, kal ural ivaṟṟāl macālip poruḷkaḷ arikkap paṭṭaṉa eṉpatiyum, iṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatiyum naṉkuṇaralām.

uṇavu koṇṭa muṟi

akkālattavaruḷ palar pāykaḷmītu amarntu uṇavup poruḷkaḷi etiril - vittukkoṇṭu uṇṭaṉar. paṇakkārar nāṟkālikaḷil amarntu uṇṭaṉar eṉṟu muttirikaḷik koṇṭu kūṟalām. eṉiṉum, itu orōvaḻik koṇṭirunta vaḻakkamākak kōṭalē poruntuvatākum. eṉṉi? iṉṉum perumpālāṉa intiyar vīṭukaḷil nāṟkāli - mējikaḷi uṇpataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttāmiyiṉ eṉka. maram, cippi, caṅku, kaḷimaṇ, cempu, veṇkalam ivaṟṟālāya karaṇṭikaḷ, kaṟikaḷiyum kuḻampu, vakikaḷiyum cōṟṟiyum eṭukkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. uṇṇaṟkutavum karaṇṭikaḷō muṭkaḷō kiṭikkāmiyiṉ, avarkaḷ kikaḷālēyē uṇavu picintu uṇṭaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu.

uṭikaḷ

appaḻiya makkaḷ ettakiya uṭikaḷi evvevvāṟu uṭuttiruntaṉar eṉpati iṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttik kūṟutaṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittila. eṉiṉum, avarkaḷ viṭṭuppōṉa oviyaṅkaḷ, patumikaḷ, muttirikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum vivaraṅkaḷik koṇṭu, avarkaḷ uṭikaḷi uṭuttivanta muṟikaḷiyum uṭi vicēṭaṅkaḷiyum oruvāṟu uṇartal kūṭum. cintu veḷiyil parutti mikutiyākap payirāṉatālum, necavukkuriya karuvikaḷ aṅkuk kiṭittamiyālum ammakkaḷ nūl nūṟṟu āṭikaḷi neytu uṭuttu vantaṉar eṉpatu aiyamaṟat terikiṟatu. eḷiyavar cātāraṇap parutti āṭikaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar. celvar pū vēlippāṭu poruntiya parutti āṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; cilar kittāṉ pōṉṟa muraṭṭu āṭikaḷi uṭuttiṉar; cilar nārāl āṉa āṭikaḷiyum uṭuttiruntaṉar[4] moheñco-tarōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa vaṭivam oṉṟil pū vēlippāṭu aminta pōrvi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atu ciṟanta vēlippāṭu uṭiyatu. attakiya pōrvikaḷic celvarē payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉal takum. avarkaḷ tam iṭakkiyum mārpum maṟiyumāṟu pōrvi pōrttiṉarātal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, eḷiya makkaḷ iṭi āṭiyuṭaṉē tirupti aṭintiruntaṉarō - eḷiya viliyiṟ kiṭitta pōrvikaḷi aṇintiruttaṉarō-teriyavilli. moheñco-tarōvil palavakip pottāṉkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ātaliṉ, annakara makkaḷ caṭṭikaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali. kaḻuttup paṭṭikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa.

aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ āṭic ciṟappu

paṇṭic cintuveḷip peṇmaṇikaḷ pāvāṭikaḷip peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpati muttirikaḷikkoṇṭu kūṟalām. avi muṉpuṟam oraḷavu kuṭṭiyākavum piṉpuṟam nīṇṭum iruntirukkalām; nāṭākkaḷik koṇṭaṉavāka irukkalām. pāvāṭikaḷi iṟukka, maṇikaḷ cērttuc ceyyap paṭṭa (oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟa) arikkaccikaḷi ammakaḷir payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. akkaccikaḷuṭ cila muṉpuṟam mukappu vikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. celvar vīṭṭu makaḷir melliya mēlāṭikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. eḷiya makaḷir mēlāṭikaḷippaṟṟi oṉṟum kūṟakkūṭavilli. moheñco-tarōvil iṉṉum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭāta pakuti pattil oṉpatu paṅku. ātaliṉ, iṉṟuḷḷa nilimiyil etaṉiyum uṟutiyākak kūṟak kūṭavilli eṉpatu iṅku niṉivil iruttat takkatu.

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ paṭivaṅkaḷuḷ āṇ uruvaṅkaḷ perumpālāṉa āṭi iṉṟiyē kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ aṉittum āṭiyuṭaṉē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, ivaṟṟik koṇṭu, ‘moheñco-tarō āṭavar paṇṭi ekiptiyaruṭ perumpālarip pōl āṭiyiṉṟiyē iruntaṉar’ eṉak kūṟutal iyalātu eṉṟu aṟiñar tīkṣat aṟivikkiṉṟār.[5] im muṭiyē poruttamāṉatu.

muṇṭācu kaṭṭiya makaḷir

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭpala, taliyil oruvaki muṇṭācuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila āṇ paṭivaṅkaḷilum ittakiya muṇṭācukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam taliyil muṇṭācu koṇṭa paṭivaṅkaḷ, āciyā miṉaril uḷḷa aṭaliyā (Adalia) eṉṉum iṭattilum, ṭel-asmar eṉ ṉum iṭattilum, ciriyāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. it tali muṇṭācukaḷ paruttiyāl āṉavi; nimirntu viciṟipōla virippuṭaṉ irukkumāṟu ōraṅkaḷil paṭṭi vittut tikkappaṭṭavi; kaṉamāṉavi. ittakiya muṇṭācukaḷi maṅkōliya makaḷir iṉṟum taliyil aṇikiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu naṉku kavaṉittat takkatu.[6]

kāl caṭṭiyō?

teyvaṅkaḷ eṉak karutappaṭum āṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cila, muḻaṅkāl aḷavuvari tuṇṭukaḷi uṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa pōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ oru paṭivam maṭṭumē muḻaṅkālukkuk kīḻē toṅkumāṟu vēṭṭikaṭṭiyuḷḷatupōlat tōṟṟukiṟatu. uṭkārntirukkum maṟṟōr āṇ uruvam nīṇṭa vēṭṭiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. harappāvil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭattiṉ mītuḷḷa oviyam oṉṟil kāṇappaṭum maṉita uruvam kāl caṭṭi aṇintiruppatu pōlat tōṟṟukiṟatu. āyiṉ, atu kālcaṭṭi aṉṟu nīṇṭavēṭṭioṉṟu kāṟṟil kāliccuṟṟik koṇṭapōtu kāṇappaṭum niliyiṉiyē atu kuṟippa tākum’, eṉṟu cilar ceppukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmi uṇarak kūṭavilli.

kūtta makaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta paṭivaṅkaḷuḷ veṇkalatti urukki vārttuc ceyyappaṭṭa kūtta makaḷ ciliyē mikac ciṟantatākum. ivvoṉṟutāṉ āṭiyē iṉṟik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itippōlavē harappāvilum veṇkalap paṭivam oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. appeṇ paṭivaṅkaḷ nīkrō iṉappeṇṇik kuṟippaṉavākavē tōṟṟu kiṉṟaṉavām. appatumikaḷ iraṇṭum naṭaṉac ceyalik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. moheñco- tarōviṟ kiṭitta ciliyiṉ iṭakki niṟiya vaḷiyalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; kūntal caṭiyākap piṉṉit taliyiṉ piṉpuṟam otukkik kaṭṭappaṭṭu valappuṟat tōḷ mītu paṭintuḷḷatu. valakki iṭuppiṉmītu irukkiṟatu. paṇṭi ekiptil naṭaṉamātar āṭiyiṉḷic cila vēḷikaḷil naṭippatu vaḻakkamām.[7] appaḻakkam cintu nāṭṭilum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.





* * *





↑ paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷuṭ cilar tirumaṇa viruntukaḷilum ‘pulavu’ ceytu uṇṭu vantaṉar. eṉpatu akanāṉūṟṟu 186ām ceyyuḷāl aṟika.

↑ paṇṭit tamiḻar ēṟṟumatip poruḷkaḷil uppuppaṭuttiya mīṉum oṉṟākum eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakkatu.

↑ Dr.E.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.185.

↑ nārāṭi maravuri pōṉṟatu. iḵtu appaḻaṅkālattil upayōkikkappaṭṭati aṟivippataṟkē pōlum, iṉṟiya makkaḷ nārppaṭṭi vicēṭa kālaṅkaḷil aṇikiṉṟaṉar. PTS. Iyengar’s ‘The Stone Age in India’, p.38,

↑ ‘Pre-historic Civilization of the Indus Valley’, p.26.

↑ Dr.E. Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p. 135.

↑ paṇṭit tamiḻakattil viṟaliyar (oruvaki naṭaṉa mātar) vicēṭa kālaṅkaḷil āṭiyiṉṟit taḻiyi ariyiṟ kaṭṭikkoṇṭu āṭutal marapu - naṟṟiṇi, 170.





* * *





﻿11. aṇikalaṉkaḷ

aṇikalaṉkaḷ

cintup piratēca makkaḷiṉ uṭi vakikaḷi oviyaṅkaḷ, ciṟpaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭē aṟiya vēṇṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, avarkaḷ makiḻantēṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ nirampak kiṭittuḷḷamiyāl, avaṟṟip paṟṟit teḷivāṉa uṇmikaḷi uṇara iṭam ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. ammakkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi[1], veṇpoṉ[2], cempu, veṇkalaṉ, tantam, elumpu, mākkal, palavaki irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ, kaḷimaṇ, caṅku mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu tattam takutikkēṟṟa aṇikaḷivitam vitamākac ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. eḷiya makkaḷ kaṟuppu-civappuk kaḷimaṇṇāl nakikaḷ ceytu cūḷiyiṭṭu, aṇintu vantaṉar. cilar oḷikkaṟkaḷāl nakikaḷ ceytu merukiṭṭu, avaṟṟil, vita vitamāṉa niṟaṅkaḷit tīṭṭi oḷi vīcac ceytu aṇintuvantaṉar. cempu, calavikkal, oruvakik karuṅkal, caṅku elumpu, kaḷimaṇ ivaṟṟāl āṉa vaḷiyalkaḷ mikap pala kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. avi ikkāla vaṭuka makaḷir kālkaḷil aṇiyum veḷḷik kāppukaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. maṇ, elumpu, caṅku, civappukkal ivaṟṟāl āṉa maṇikaḷ pala kōkkappaṭṭu mālikaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ammaṇikaḷ puṉṉikkāy aḷaviliruntu cuṇṭikkāy aḷavu vari ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila taṭṭiyākavum, cila vaṭṭamākavum, cila nīḷamākavum uḷḷaṉa. ellā maṇikaḷum iyaṉṟavariyiṟ paḷapaḷappākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

putikkappaṭṭa nakikaḷ

veḷḷi, cempu mutaliya kalaṉkaḷil innakikaḷi vittu, nilattiṉ aṭiyilum kavarkaḷilum appaṇṭi makkaḷ oḷittu vittiruntaṉar. itaṉāl, ammakkaḷ taṅkaḷ nakarattiṟku uṇṭāka irunta aḻivi eṇṇi iṅkiṉam aṇikaḷi maṟittu vittaṉar eṉpatum, accam nīṅkiya piṉṉar vantu avaṟṟi eṭuttuk koḷḷalām eṉa eṇṇiṉar eṉpatum, veḷiyām. āṉāl, avarkaḷ eṇṇiyapaṭi tirumpivarak kūṭavilli pōlum!

putiyuṇṭa veḷḷik kalaṉkaḷ

oru vīṭṭiṉ aṭiyil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa aṇikaḷuḷ veḷḷip pāttiram eṉṟu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ataṉuḷ kaḻuttu mālikaḷ palavum, kaṭiṉamāṉa uyartara maṇikaḷ palavum iruntaṉa. avveḷḷik kalaṉ putiyuṇṭirunta iṭattil vēṟupala aṇikaḷ maṇṇuḷ putiyuṇṭu kiṭantaṉa. avaṟṟi eṭutta irāypakatur tayārām cahṉi eṉpavar, inta veḷḷik kalaṉum itic cuṟṟip pūmiyiṟ putiyuṇṭu kiṭanta aṇikaḷum oru paruttit tuṇiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭup putikkap paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. attuṇi aḻinta piṉṉar ivi iṅṅaṉam maṇṇuḷ putiyuṇṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.

piṟitōr vīṭṭiṉ aṭiyil irāypahatūr tīkṣajit eṉpār veḷḷik kalaṉ oṉṟik kaṇṭeṭuttār. ataṉuḷ kaḻuttu mālikaḷum paḷapaḷappuḷḷa kalmaṇikaḷum ulōkattuṇṭukaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatali nāṭākkaḷum iruntaṉa.

putiyuṇṭa cempuk kalaṉ

vēṟōr iṭattil cempk kalaṉ oṉṟu putiyuṇṭu kiṭantatu.ataṟkuḷ taṅkakkoṇṭiūcikaḷ, periya veḷḷik kātaṇikaḷ, civappuk kal maṇikaḷ, piṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntaṉa. antac cempup pāttirattiṟku uriya muṭiyum akappaṭṭatu. ammūṭi kippiṭi koṇṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.harappāvil ārāycci naṭattiya aṟiñar vāṭs eṉpār pala nakikaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttār. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉa moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittavaṟṟi ottiruntaṉa. cila eṅkumē kāṇappaṭāta vēlippāṭu poruntiyaviyāka uḷḷaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta nakikaḷum tariyiṉ aṭiyil putiyuṇṭu kiṭantaṉavē ākum.

kāṭcikkiṉiya kaḻuttumāli

veḷḷip pāttiram oṉṟil vikkappaṭṭirunta nakikaḷuḷ kaḻuttu māli oṉṟē ciṟappittuk kūṟattakka vēlippāṭu koṇṭatu. atu poṉṉālum paccik kaṟkaḷālum ceyta maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭu nīṇṭa aṇivaṭamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu. taṅka maṇikaḷ taṭṭiyākac ceyyappaṭṭu avaṟṟiṉmītu kumiḻkaḷ paṟṟavikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa kaṭiṉamāṉa paccik kaṟkaḷ pīppāy vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. oru poṉmaṇiyum aṭuttu oru pacci maṇiyumāka ammāli kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ naṭuvil ‘kaṭuttakkal’ eṉṉum oruvaki viṭuriyattālum, cūriya kāntam’ eṉappaṭum iravik kallālum ceyyappaṭṭa ēḻu patakkaṅkaḷ kōttut toṅkaviṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. atil uḷḷa paccikkal vaṭa parmāvilum tipēttilum kiṭittalāl, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiliruntē koṇṭuvarappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.[3]

maṟṟum iru kaḻuttu mālikaḷ

vēṟoru kaḻuttu māliyil kuḻavi vaṭivattilum uruṇṭi vaṭivattilum ceyyappaṭṭa taṅka maṇikaḷum, nīlap paḷiṅkuk kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appaḷiṅku maṇikaḷuṭ cila iṉṟum niṟaṅkuṉṟāmal paḷapaḷappākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoru māliyil, taṅkattāl taṭṭiyākac ceyyappaṭṭu iraṇṭu maṇikaḷit cērttup paṟṟavittuḷḷavi nīḷamākak kōkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ pāpilōṉiyā, ekiptu, ṭrāy mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭittaṉa. ivveḻil mikka aṇivaṭaṅkaḷiṉ vēlippāṭṭik koṇṭu akkāla makkaḷiṉ ciṟanta aṟivi naṉkuṇaralām.

harappāvil kiṭitta kaḻuttu mālikaḷ

harappāvil kiṭitta mālikaḷ palavākum. avi aṉittum viyattaku vēlippāṭu koṇṭavi. 240 maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa nāṉku cāraṅkaḷ koṇṭa māli, 27 taṅka maṇikaḷ paṟpala vaṭiviṟceytu kōkkappaṭṭamāli, palavaki vaṭiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa 70 taṅkamaṇikaḷ koṇṭa māli, palavakik kalmaṇikaḷum poṇmaṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭa māli eṉap palaki mālikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi iṭiyiṭiyē 7 patakkaṅkaḷ mutal 13 patakkaṅkaḷ vari kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. irattiṉak kaṟkaḷāl āṉa mūṉṟu mālikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, ittuṇi vitavitamāṉa mālikaḷ moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittila. kaḻutti iṟukkiya muṟiyil aṇiyappaṭum aṭṭikiyum akkālattil iruntatu. āṭavarum kaḻuttumālikaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.[4]

aḻakoḻukum iṭippaṭṭikaḷ

piṟa nakikaḷil iṭuppil aṇiyap payaṉpaṭum iṭippaṭṭikaḷē (oṭṭiyāṇaṅkaḷē) ciṟappāṉavi. ivi paṇpaṭṭa vēlippāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiyūvi. ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭēkuṟippiṭattakkavi; ovvoṉṟum āṟu caraṅkaḷik koṇṭatu. ovvoru carattilum pīppāy pōlac cetukkap paṭṭa civappukkal maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru maṇiyum 12 ce. mī. nīḷam irukkiṟatu. iccivappu maṇikaṭ kiṭiyē veṇkalamaṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi uruṇṭiyāyum malar moṭṭukaḷ pōlak kumiḻākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iviyaṉṟi veṇkalac cataṅkikaḷum muttukkaḷum kōtta i aip paṭ aikaḷum cila kiṭittaṉa. ivi cennīla niṟattuṭaṉum kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaṉum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭṭikaḷiṉ muṭivil mukappukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu, āṟu caraṅkaḷik kōttaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu āṟu tuḷikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviṭip paṭṭikaḷ kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum iṭippaṭṭikaḷip peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. ovvoṉṟum 100 ce. mī. nīḷam irukkiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟil uḷḷa kaṭiṉamāṉa civappuk kaṟkaḷ naṉku merukiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi ‘kuruntak kal’ koṇṭu merukiṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.

tuḷi iṭṭa pēraṟivu

merukiṭappaṭṭamaṇikaḷiṉ naṭuvil caraṭu nuḻivataṟkākat tuḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attuḷikaḷ mikka oḻuṅkuṭaṉ amintuḷḷaṉa. maṇikaḷiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷum tuḷiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ellāt tuḷikaḷum orē aḷavil amin tiruttalē viyappukkuriyatu. iṅṅaṉam tiruttamāṉa muṟiyil tuḷiyiṭuvataṟkeṉṟu appēraṟiñar payaṉpaṭuttiya nuṭpamāṉa karuvi yāteṉpatu viḷaṅkavilli. aṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷa cempālāṉa nuṭpamāṉa tuḷiyiṭum karuvikaḷē ivvaṟputa vēlikkup payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. ivvaṟputa vēlippāṭu avvaṟiñar tam nuṇṇaṟivi naṉku viḷakkuvatākum.

āṟu caraṅkoṇṭa aḻakiya kiyaṇi

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta kāppukaḷ, vaḷiyalkaḷ mutaliya kiyaṇikaḷuḷ ciṟappittuk kūṟattakuvaṉa cila uḷa. aviyē uruṇṭiyāṉa taṅkamaṇikaḷi āṟu caraṅkaḷākak kōttuc ceyyappaṭṭa kiyaṇikaḷ ākum. uruṇṭi maṇikaṭku iṭiyē taṭṭiyāṉa taṅka maṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caraṅkaḷ muṭiyum iṭattil arivaṭṭa vaṭivil taṅkattakaṭṭāl āṉa mukappukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi pōṉṟavi harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

uḷḷē arakkiṭṭa kāppukaḷ

kiyaṇikaḷil palavaki irukkiṉṟaṉa. taṅkam, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, paḷiṅku veṇkal, kaṟuppukkal, caṅku, cippi, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaṟṟāl kiyaṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila kāppukaḷil uṭpuṟam arakkum arakkuppōṉṟa vēṟoru poruḷum urukki ūṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. harappāvil pala kāppukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu muṟṟum cempiṉāl āṉatu oṉṟu uḷḷē arakkiṭattakkatāka veḷḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. harappāvil taṅkak kaṭakaṅkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

palavakippaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ

cempālum veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭa kiyaṇikaḷ, ippōtu ceyyappaṭum taṅka vaḷiyalkaḷ pōlak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila uruṇṭiyākavum cila taṭṭiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila vaḷiyalkaḷil iraṇṭu muṉikaḷum poruntiyuḷḷaṉa; cilavaṟṟuḷ vāy virintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cippiyāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ akalamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cippit tuṇṭukaḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nūlāl iṇikkappaṭṭuk kikaḷil aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. paḷiṅku vaḷiyalkaḷ cilavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi ciṟiya paṭṭāṭiyākavum, periya paṭṭāṭiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇ vaḷiyalkaḷ mikka kavaṉattuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭuc ciṟanta muṟiyil cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟic ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ mikavum uyarntatākum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.

oviyam tiṭṭappaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ

kaḷimaṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ vevvēṟu niṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum oviyaṅkaḷuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila vaḷiyalkaḷ mītu puḷḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya kaḷimaṇ vaḷiyalkaḷi ēḻip peṇmaṇikaḷē aṇintiruntaṉar pōlum! ikkālattum ēḻi mātar cempu, veṇkalam, pittaḷi, kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ceyyappaṭum vaḷiyalkaḷi aṇintuḷḷaṉar allavā? ivarkaḷip pōlavē, akkāla ēḻi makaḷirum ivaṟṟālāya aṇikaḷi aṇintaṉar eṉṟu kōṭal poruttamākum.

kāl kāppukaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta kāppu vakikaḷiṟ kikkup payaṉpaṭṭavi ivi, kāliṟkup payaṉpaṭṭavi ivi, eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟutaṟku illi. āyiṉum, cila patumikaḷiṉ kālkaḷil vaḷinta kōṇal kāppukaḷ iruppatākat terivatāl, appaḻaṅkālap peṇṭir kāl kāppukaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutal takum. ittakiya kālkāppukaḷiyē cimlāviccūḻavuḷḷa malippiratēcattil vāḻum peṇṭir aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum kirīṭ tīvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṅkiya uruvam oṉṟiṉ kālkaḷilum ittakiya kōṇaṟkāppukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[5] ivi pōṉṟa kāppukaḷiyē iṉṟum vaṭuka makaḷir kālkaḷil aṇikiṉṟaṉar.

neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ

vaṭṭamāka mukkōṇa vaṭivattiṟ kūmpiyaṉavākac ceyyapṭṭuḷḷa nakikaḷ cila moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.ivikuḻantikaḷiṉ neṟṟiyil toṅkaviṭappaṭum patakkaṅkaḷ pōlak kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ivi, pañcāpilum mārvārilum uḷḷa peṇkaḷ taliyic cuṟṟip poṉ nāṭā oṉṟik kaṭṭi, atiṟ kōttu aṇiyum cuṭṭi pōlavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iccuṭṭikaḷ 5 ce. mī. cuṟṟaḷavuṭaṉum 6.10 ce. mī. uyarttuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, pīṅkāṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi talimayiruṭaṉ iṇippataṟkum nāṭāviṟ kōppataṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu tuḷiyuṭaṉ kūṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa. cippikaḷil mūṉṟu nāṉku aḻakiya tuṇṭukaḷip - poruttiyum ittakiya cuṭṭikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iccuṭṭikaḷik koṇṭa kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷ nirampak kiṭittamiyāl, ivi taliyaṇikaḷē eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi mali. ikkālattil ‘neṟṟiccuṭṭi’ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatum nam peṇkaḷāl taliyil aṇiyappaṭuvatumākiya tali yaṇiyip pōṉṟaṉavē iccuṭṭikaḷ eṉal oruvāṟu poruntum. immātiriyuḷḷa cuṭṭikaḷ pala harappāvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi neṟṟi mutal uccittali vari oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka vittuk kūntalmītu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa niliyil cila patumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iccuṭṭikaḷ pala poṉṉāl iyaṉṟavi: aṭippuṟam kūntal iḻikaḷ nuḻiyat takkapaṭi veḷḷi iṇippuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟavi. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu 3 ce. mī. uyaramum aṭippākattil 4 ce.mī. akalamum uṭiyatu.[6]

kātaṇikaḷ

cila kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷ kātaṇikaḷuṭaṉ iruk kiṉṟaṉa.avaṟṟāl, akkāla makaḷir palavakiyāṉa kātaṇikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām. cila kātaṇikaḷ, taṅkac curuḷ pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila ciṟu kuḻāykaḷiyuṭiya kumiḻpōlac ceyyappaṭṭavi. avi, kātukaḷil uḷḷa tuḷikaḷiṟ celuttappaṭṭa piṉṉar maṟṟoru tuṇṭu vittut tirukāṇi pōlap poruttiviṭat takkavāṟu amintuḷḷaṉa. ivi taṅkattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi; ciṟitu kūmpiya kōpura. vaṭivuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkūmpiya pakutiyiṟṟāṉ kuḻāy vittup paṟṟavikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēṟu cila kātaṇikaḷ taṅkattāl vaḷiyaṅkaḷākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi, tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir vaṭikātukaḷil aṇintu koḷḷum vaḷiyaṅkaḷākak kāṭci aḷittal kavaṉikkat takkatu.

mūkkaṇikaḷ

oraṅkaḷiṟ paṟkaḷik koṇṭa cakkaram pōṉṟa taṅkattakaṭukaḷum piṟavum akkāla mūkkaṇikaḷākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, tīkṣit eṉpavar, ‘mūkkaṇikaḷ appaṇṭik kālattil iruntaṉa eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟakkūṭavilli. eṉṉi? kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷuḷ oṉṟilēṉum mūkkaṇikku uriya aṭiyāḷam iṉmiyiṉ eṉka. mēlum ki.pi.1200-kkup piṉṉarē mūkkil tuḷiyiṭum vaḻakkam muslimkaḷāl innāṭṭiṟ pukuttappaṭṭatu’[7] eṉṟu aṟivikkiṟār. āṉāl, aṟiñar ci. ār. irāy eṉpār, ‘cintup piratēca makaḷir mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar. avvaṇikaḷ kātil uḷḷa tōṭukaḷuṭaṉ poṟcaṅkiliyāl irupuṟamum iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa’,[8] eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ‘cintup piratēca makaḷir nīlak kal patitta mukkaṇikaḷi aṇintiruntaṉar eṉak kūṟalām’ eṉṟu pāṭrik kārliṭaṉ eṉpār aṟivikkiṟār.[9] cintuppiratēca ārāyccikkeṉṟē ciṟappāṉa ārāycciyāḷarāka intiya aracāṅkattāl niyamikkappaṭṭa ṭākṭar makkē eṉpavar, ‘akkāla mātar mūkkaṇikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; nīla niṟamuṭiya pīṅkāṉ tuṇṭukaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; oruvaki nīla arakkil mēṟpuṟam taṭṭiyākac ceytō, kaṟkaḷ, ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷ, oviyaṅkoṇṭa tuṇṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip poruttiyō mūkkaṇikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉarātal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu tuṇukkamāka ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟār.[10] ‘harappā nakarattu makaḷir, cuṟṟilum paṟkaḷik koṇṭa cakkaram pōṉṟa aḻakiya ciṟiya takaṭukaḷi mūkkaṇikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar vāṭs varintuḷḷār.[11]

mōtiraṅkaḷ

mōtiraṅkaḷ palavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cila, kampikaḷākac ceytu paṭṭiyākat taṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala, ulōkattic curuṭṭi vittu mōtiramāka aṭittuc ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. perumpālāṉa mōtiraṅkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam, caṅku cippi ivaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil taṅkamōtiram oṉṟum veḷḷi mōtiram oṉṟum kiṭittaṉa. oru mōtiram catura mukappu vittuc ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ammukappiṉ mītu kuṟukkum neṭukkumākak kōṭukaḷ variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pīṅkāṉ mōtiram oṉṟum harappāvil kiṭittatu.

pottāṉkaḷ

cempu, veṇkalam, pīṅkāṉ, mākkal mutaliyavaṟṟāl vaṭṭa vaṭivattil ceyyappaṭṭa pottāṉkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi potuvāka, mālṭṭā, pōrccukal, teṉ pirāṉs mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭitta paṇṭik kālap pottāṉkaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. avi taṭṭiyākac ceytu caṭṭi mītu poruttit tippataṟkēṟṟavāṟu iraṇṭu tuḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulōkattāl āṉa pottāṉkaḷ muṉpuṟam kumiḻākac ceyyappaṭṭu, aṭiyil iraṇṭu tuḷikaḷ iṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pottāṉkaḷ piṟi vaṭivattil ceyyap paṭṭut tuṇiyil iṇittut tittuk koḷḷattakka vacatiyuṭṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. harappāvil veḷḷip pottāṉkaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ naṭuvē cippit tuṇṭukaḷ aḻakāka vittup patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi pārvikku eḻil mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

tali nāṭākkaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta cila kaḷimaṇ patumikaḷ vāyilāka, akkāla makkaḷ taliyil oru vaki nāṭāvic cuṟṟik kaṭṭikkoṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷi yākiṟatu. akkāla āṭavarum peṇṭirum, annāṭākkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ippaḻakkam cumēriyariṭiyum mikuntiruntatu. annāṭākkaḷ curuṇikaḷākac cuṟṟi vikkappaṭṭu, nakik kuviyalkaḷuṭaṉ kiṭittaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cila maṅkiviṭāmalum uṟuti iḻavāmalum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi 1 ce. mī. akalattil melliya taṅkat takaṭṭāl carikipōlac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila nāṭākkaḷ 40 ce mī nīḷamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. anta nāṭākkaḷiṉ irumuṉikaḷum tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭu, iḻuttuk kaṭṭa vacati uṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟil orupuṟam muḻuvatum variciyākat tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittuḷikaḷil piṟa aṇikaḷ ētēṉum māṭṭit toṅkaviṭappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[12] cumēriyar ittakiya nāṭākkaḷ mītu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ cittiraṅ kaḷiyum, veṟum puḷḷikaḷiyum tīṭṭip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.

koṇṭi ūcikaḷ

āṭavarum peṇṭirum kūntalik kōtik koṇṭi iṭuvutum curuṭṭik kaṭṭik koḷvatum vaḻakkam. akkaṭṭukaḷ nekiḻṉamal irukkak koṇṭiūcikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta paṭivaṅkaḷik kāṇkiyil, ammakkaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭa koṇṭi ūcikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu teḷivām. citaṟuṇṭa ūcikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟap puṭiyatu veṇkalak koṇṭi ūci oṉṟē ākum. atu 11 ce. mī. nīḷam irukkiṟatu. ataṉ ucciyil iraṇṭu kaṟutta kalimāṉ talikaḷ vevvēṟu ticiyi nōkkuvaṉavāy amintuḷḷaṉa. akkalimāṉkaḷiṉ kompukaḷ aḻakuṟa muṟukkik koṇṭiruppaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟoru veṇkala ūciyil curuṇṭa kompuṭiya tali oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvaki ūcikaḷ cumēriyar, ekiptu, kākasas, naṭu airōppā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta ūcikaḷip pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. tantam, elumpu mutaliyavaṟṟāl āṉa koṇṭi ūcikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.cila ūcikaḷ talippuṟam maṭṭum ulōkattālum, kuttikkoḷḷum pakuti marattālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ talippakutiyē kiṭittatu. marappakuti aḻintuviṭṭatu. ivvāṟu kiṭitta talippakuti oṉṟu 1 ce. mī. uyaram irukkiṉṟatu; ataṉmītu mūṉṟu kuraṅkukaḷ tōḷkaḷiṟ kikōttuk koṇṭu cuṟṟi uṭkārntiruppaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟoṉṟiṉ talippuṟam veṇkallāl āṉatu. aḵtu, iraṇṭu kuraṅkukaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟu aṇittukkoṇ ṭiruppaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṟṟuñ cila talip puṟaṅkaḷ tāmari vittukaḷ pōlavum, vēṟu palavakiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

camayat toṭarpuḷḷa patakkam

kaḻuttu mālikaḷil kōkkappaṭṭaṉa pōla iṉṟit taṉippaṭṭa vēlippāṭu koṇṭa patakkam oṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷatu. itu 7 ce. mī. nīḷamum 6 ce.mī akalamum uḷḷatu; vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉatu. itu veṇmi kalanta mañcaḷ niṟa mākkallil ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mītu piṟi pōlac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ippiṟiyil, oṟṟik kompuṭiya erutiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ etiril toṭṭi oṉṟu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; piṟiyiṉ muṉikaḷ cērumiṭattil ētō oṉṟu toṅkutal pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ippatakkattil cetukku vēli nirampiyuḷḷatu. itaṉuḷ palaniṟac cāntukaḷ appiyiruttal kūṭiyatē. ippatakkam taṅkat takaṭṭil vittup patikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. attakaṭṭiṉ talippuṟattil māṭṭikkoḷḷat takkapaṭi vaḷiyam iṇikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. itu camayat toṭarpuṭiya patakkamāka iruttal vēṇṭum.[13]

palavakik kaṟkaḷ

cintuppiratēcattiṟ kiṭitta palavakik kiyaṇikaḷ, kaḻuttu mālikaḷ, iṭippaṭṭikaḷ, mōtiraṅkaḷ iṉṉapiṟa aṇikaḷil patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaviyum taṉiyē kiṭittuḷḷavi yumāṉa palavaki viyattaku maṇikaḷ yāvi? avi iravik kaṟkaḷ, civappu, mañcaḷ, nīlam, cūriya kāntak kaṟkaḷ, viṭuriyam, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ, tūymi ceyyappaṭāta viṭūriyam, kōmētakam, irattam pōṉṟa cenniṟamuṭiya māṇikkam, vayiram, pāmpukkal, kūriya oruvakik kaṟkaḷ, ‘amesāṉ’ eṉappaṭum oruvakip paccik kal, vēṟu (palvēṟupaṭṭa) kaṟkaḷ,[14] spaṭikam, ulōkak kalavikkal, paḷiṅkukkal mutaliyaṉa ākum. nīlam oṉṟit tavira, piṟa kal vakikaḷ aṉittum āpkāṉistāṉam, kāṣmīr, tipēt, vaṭaparmā, piṟa intiya maṇṭilaṅkaḷ ākiya pala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntē varavaḻikkappaṭṭavi.

ik kalmaṇikaḷ iṉṟic caṅku, cippi, pīṅkāṉ, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaṟṟālum maṇikaḷ ceytu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.

maṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa vitam

cintup piratēca makkaḷ kaṟkaḷic cōtittu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmikkēṟṟa muṟiyil uruvākki vaḻavaḻappākat tēytatu merukiṭṭuḷḷaṉar ivvēlippāṭṭiṟkut tēvippaṭṭa kaṭiccal iyantiraṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṭam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. avi iṉṟēl, ivvēlippāṭukaḷ kaṟkaḷil kāṇappaṭā. kaṟkaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭa piṉṉarē nuṇṇiya kūrmiyāṉa karuvikaḷik koṇṭu tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta pala maṇikaḷ tuḷiyiṭappaṭāmal iruppatikkoṇṭu iṅṅaṉam karutappaṭukiṟatu. attuḷiyiṭappaṭāta kaṟkaḷ moheñco-tarōviṉ iṟutik kālattiṉavātal vēṇṭum. vēli muṭivataṟkuḷ nakaram tuṟakkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ammakkaḷ, cila cantarppaṅkaḷil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa kaṟkaḷi oṭṭa vittu, avaṟṟiṉ maṇikaḷik kaṭintirukkiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya maṇikaḷē mikka vēlippāṭṭuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu pīppāy pōṉṟa 1ce.mī nīḷattil uḷḷa maṇi oṉṟu civappu, veḷḷi, nīlam mutaliya niṟaṅkaḷik koṇṭa aintuvitak kaṟkaḷi oṭṭavittucceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, immaṇiyip pārttavuṭaṉ itaṉ kalappi uṇarntu kūṟutal iyalātu eṉiṉ, itaṉ vēlippāṭṭi eṉṉeṉpatu! immaṇi ippoḻutu uṭintu kiṭappatāl itaṉ marmam veḷippaṭṭatu. kōmētakamō eṉṟu aiyuṟattakkavāṟu oru maṇi kiṭittuḷḷatu. aḵtu orupuṟam cippi, maṟṟoru puṟam caṟṟuc civanta cuṇṇakkal ākiyavi koṇṭu pakkuvapṭuttip pōlik kōmētaka maṇiyākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu viyattaṟ kuriyatē ittakiya pōli maṇikaḷ maṟṟa maṇikaḷiviṭac ciṟantaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi pōṉṟa pōli maṇikaḷ ‘niṉaveh’ eṉṉum iṭattilum kiṭittaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar kām pel tāmcaṉ kūṟukiṉṟār.

oviyam aminta maṇikaḷ

aṟiñar, oviyaṅkoṇṭa maṇikaḷē mikka ciṟap puṭiyavi eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. civappuk kallil paṭṭi maṇikaḷ, ceytu, avaṟṟiṉmītu veḷḷi niṟaṅkoṇṭa cittiraṅkaḷit tīṭṭit tīyiliṭṭu, accittiraṅkaḷip patiyac ceyyum muṟi kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. ittakiya maṇikaḷ pala moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivipōṉṟa pala, ur nakarattu araca parmpariyiṉar cavakkuḻikaḷiliruntu eṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. avi aḷavilum uruvilum moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta maṇikaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, immaṇikaḷ cintu veḷiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavā? cumēriyāviṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavā? aṉṟi vēṟeṅkēṉum ceyyappaṭṭaṉavā? tuṇintu kūṟutaṟku iyalavilli. āyiṉ, immaṇikaḷ cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭit tiruppatāṟṟāṉ ivaṟṟiṉ kālamum cumēriyariṉ uyar nākarikak kālamum oṉṟeṉak kūṟavum, akkālam ki.mu. 3250 - ki.mu. 2750 eṉa muṭivu kaṭṭavum vacati ēṟpaṭṭatu. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta immaṇikaḷ pōṉṟaṉavum, 8 pōṉṟa amippuṭiya civappu maṇikaḷ cilavum cāṉhu-tarōvil kiṭittaṉa. piṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ veṇṇiṟa oviyaṅkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru maṇiyum 2 ce. mī. uyaram irukkiṉṟatu. aṅku, veṇ maṇikaḷ mītu cenniṟam tīṭṭi, veṇṇiṟa oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa maṇikaḷum cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

paṭṭi veṭṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum paṭṭi veṭṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya maṇikaḷ ekiptit tavira vēṟu ennāṭṭilum kiṭittila; ekiptilum piṟkāla urōmar tam kallaṟikaḷilēyē kiṭittaṉa. ivi oviya maṇikaḷ alla; aṟukōṇam, eṇkōṇam eṉṉum muṟiyil paṭṭikaḷāka amintavi.

kaṟkaḷiṟ pulami

cintu veḷiyiṉar, iṅṅaṉam pala tiṟappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷic cōtittu, avaṟṟiṉ eḻil kuṉṟāmal irukkumpaṭi ariya vēlippāṭukaḷ ceytu, palavaki maṇikaḷākki mālikaḷākac ceytum, vēṟu pala ulōkaṅkaḷālāṉa maṇikaḷuṭaṉ cērttum, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliyavaṟṟāl āṉa palavaki aṇikaḷiṟ poruttamuṟap patittum aṇintu vantaṉar. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa appaḻaṅkālattil - iṉṟikku 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar - apperu makkaḷ kaṟkaḷilum pulami cāṉṟavarāka iruntaṉar eṉiṉ, ammamma! avartam uyariya nākarikatti eṉṉeṉap pukaḻa vallēm![15]

'taṅkak kavacam koṇṭa maṇikaḷ'

immaṇikaḷuṭ cila māliyākak kōkkappaṭṭa pōtu, maṇikaḷiṉ irupuṟattum taṅka villikaḷ kavacam pōla oṭṭavikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟu nammavar palar immuṟippaṭi uruttirākka maṇikaḷik kōttu aṇintuḷḷaṉar. ippaḻakkam harappāvil mikutiyāka iruntateṉṟu aṟiñar vāṭs aṟivikkiṟār. itu cumēriyariṭattum iruntatām.

cīppukaḷ

appaṇṭi makkaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭa cīppukaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; avi tantam, māṭṭukkompu, elumpu, maram eṉpaṉavaṟṟāl āṉavi; pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil ceyyap paṭṭavi. avaṟṟuḷ cila taliyil cerukik koḷḷavum payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam makaḷir cīppukaḷik kūntaliṟ cerukik koḷḷum paḻakkam iṉṟu parmā nāṭṭil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. mēṉāṭṭu mātar cilar melliya vaḷinta cīppukaḷi aṇikiṉṟaṉar. oru kallaṟiyil iḷam peṇ oruttiyiṉ maṇṭi oṭṭiṉ arukil irupuṟamum paṟkaḷik koṇṭa aḻakiya cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa tantac cīppu oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. ‘V’ pōṉṟa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭa cīppoṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittatu. itu nīṇṭa kūntalic cikkiṉṟik kōta utaviyatākum. iccīppu neruṅkiya paṟkaḷiyuṭiyatāy aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭiruntamiyāl, taliyic cīvavum taliyil vittuk koḷḷavum payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. itupōṉṟa cīppu ekiptil ‘paṭari’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟṟāṉ kiṭittatu vēṟu eṅkum akappaṭavilli.

kaṇṇāṭikaḷ

cintu veḷi makkaḷ mukam pārkkak kaṇṇāṭikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiyavi yāvi? avarkaḷ ceppu-veṇkalat takaṭukaḷi naṉku merukiṭṭup paḷapaḷappākkik kaṇṇāṭika ḷākap payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭaṉar; avaṟṟiṉ paḷapaḷappu maṅkiviṭāmal irukka, oraṅkaḷi maṭakki araṇ ceytiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟivuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ pala aḷavukaḷi uṭiyavi; marak kippiṭikaḷik koṇṭavi. moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭitta cila kaṇṇāṭikaḷ muṭṭi vaṭivil amintuḷḷaṉa! cila vaṭṭa vaṭivil amintuḷḷaṉa. oru kaṇṇāṭi 22 ce. mī. uyaram irukkiṟatu.ittakiya kaṇṇāṭikaḷ ekiptilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

makaḷir kūntal oppaṉi

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta peṇ paṭivaṅkaḷik koṇṭu, akkāla makaḷir taṅkaḷ kūntali evvem muṟiyil oppaṉi ceytukoṇṭaṉar eṉpati oruvāṟu uṇaralām. ammakaḷir kūntali naṉṟākac cīppiṭṭu vārip piṉṉi viṭṭiruntaṉar. kōtic curuṭṭi nāṭāvāl kaṭṭi iṭappuṟam cāyttuk koṇṭipōlamuṭintiruntaṉar. kūntalippiṉṉināṭāvik koṇṭu muṭintiruntaṉar; kūntalip piṉṉi nāṭāvik koṇṭu pūppōla muṭippiṭṭu, vitavitamāka (kaṇṇaṉ koṇṭi pōṭṭiruppatupōla) mēlē tūkki muṭintu, ammuṭippukaḷil perṉ, veḷḷi, tantam, elumpu mutaliya vaṟṟāl āṉa koṇṭi ūcikaḷit tattam takutik kēṟṟavāṟu cerukik koṇṭaṉar; palavaki naṟumalarkaḷic cūṭivantaṉar. mintar kūntal oppaṉi

āṭavar cilar tam cikiyik kuṟukalāka veṭṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar; cilar nīḷamāka vaḷarttuc curuṭṭip piṉpuṟam muṭintiruntaṉar; atu carintuviṭāmal irukkak koṇṭi ūcikaḷiyum nāṭākkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; cilar tali naṭuvil vaṭu eṭuttumayiri ali aliyākac cīviviṭṭiruntaṉar. vēṟu cilar talimayirik kōtic curuṭṭi ucciyiṟ kaṭṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kūntali muṭital cumēriyāvilum paḻakkamāka iruntatu. potuvākac cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ kūntal ali aliyākavum curuṇṭu curuṇṭum toṅkikkoṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu pala patumikaḷ vāyilāka aṟiyalām. cilar kūntalip piṉṉip pakkaṅkaḷil viṭṭiruntaṉar; ippaḻakkam pāpilōṉiyariṭamum iruntatēyākum.[16]

mīci illā āṭavar

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭittuḷḷa āṇ patumikaḷ perumpālāṉa teyvaṅkaḷiyō - teyvamaṉṉa yōkiyariyō kuṟippaṉa. eṉiṉum, maṉitaṉ taṉṉi uḷḷattiṟkoṇṭē teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷic camippatu iyalpu. anniliyi niṉintu ārāyiṉ, paṇṭic cintu veḷi makkaḷ mīciyi vittilar tāṭi oṉṟiyē vittiruntaṉar eṉpati aḻuttamākak kūṟalām. ittāṭiyum pala vakiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kātu mutal vaḷarntuḷḷa mayirum kīḻ utaṭṭaṭiyil vaḷarntuḷḷa mayirum oṉṟākat tōṟṟamaḷikkumāṟu tāṭi vittiruttal oru muṟi; ati oḻuṅkupeṟak kattirittiruttal oru muṟi: mukavāyk kaṭṭiyiṉ aṭiyilmaṭṭum ciṟitaḷavu tāṭivaḷarttal mūṉṟām muṟi. attāṭiyiṉ nuṉi uḷnōkki vaḷintirukkum paṭi viṭutal nāṉkām muṟi. immuṟippaṭiyē cumēriyarum tāṭikaḷi vittiruntaṉar; āṉāl palar nīḷa vaḷarttuviṭṭiruntaṉar. mīci vaḷarntu mēl utaṭṭil toṭutalāl uṭkoḷḷum uṇavil mācupaṭum eṉṟu eṇṇi, avvaṟiñar mīciyi aṭiyōṭu cirittu vantaṉar eṉṟē karutavēṇṭum. cukātāra muṟiyil uṟiviṭaṅkaḷi amittu vāḻnta appēraṟiñarkaḷ, uṇavilum cukātāra muṟiyik kiyāṇṭu mīciyi aṟavē oḻittaṉar pōlum!

inta aḷavōṭēṉum avarkaḷ niṉṟaṉarō? illi! illi! āṭavarum peṇṭirum uṭaliṉ piṟa pakutikaḷil vaḷarum mayiriyum kaḷintu vantaṉar eṉpatu cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta kaṇakkaṟṟa maḻittaṟ kattikaḷik koṇṭum kūrmiyāṉa ciṟiya takaṭukaḷik koṇṭum kūṟalām’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē urikkiṉṟār.[17]

kaṇṇukku mi

appaṇṭik kāla makaḷir evvakiyilum iṉṟiya makaḷirkkup piṉṉiṭintavarākat teriyavilli. avarkaḷ kaṇkaḷil kaṟuppu mi tīṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. peṇṭir maṭṭum illi. āṭavarum mi iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. irupālarum kaṇṇukku mi iṭac ceppuk kuccikaḷiyum piṟa melliya kuccikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. makaḷir ciṟiya natti oṭukaḷil kuṅkumam pōṉṟa ceṅkāvip poṭikaḷi vittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟi neṟṟiyil poṭṭiṭap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ivi pōṉṟa natti oṭukaḷic cumēriya makaḷir vittiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, avarkaḷ avvōṭukaḷil mukattiṟ pūcum poṭi vakikaḷiyē vittiruntaṉar.

mukattiṟkup poṭi,

cintu veḷi makaḷir oru vaki veṇpoṭiyit taṅkaḷ mukattiṟ pūci vantaṉar. ippoṭi moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷatu. itē poruḷ kaṇṇukku iṉiya maruntākavum payaṉ paṭṭirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. ‘aritāram pōṉṟa oruvakip poṭiyum mukattiṟ pūcik koḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭatu. oruvakip pacci maṇṇum naṉṟāyp poṭi ceyyappaṭṭu mukattiṟ pūcap payaṉpaṭṭatām. ippacci maṇ kaṭṭi kaṭṭiyāka moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷatu. itu kaṇṇukku miyākavum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu pacci niṟam pūcavum payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār. itupōṉṟa pacci niṟapporuḷip paṇṭi ekiptiyar kaṇṇukkē payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. ivvāṟu mukattiṟ poṭi pūcik koḷḷum paḻakkam paṇṭik kiris, cīṉam ākiya nāṭukaḷilum iruntaṉa.



↑ veḷḷi eṉpatu āpkāṉistāṉattiliruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭa īyattiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatātaliṉ, avvariya poruḷāl āṉa kalaṉkaḷ arukiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

↑ veṇ poṉ (Electrum) eṉpatu nam nāṭṭil kiṭikkiṟatu. itil veḷḷiyē mikutiyākak kalantirukkum. itiṉiṉṟum poṉṉip pirikka vaki aṟiyāta ammakkaḷ, appaṭiyē nakikaḷ ceytukoṇṭaṉar.

﻿12. vāṇipam

uḷ nāṭṭu vāṇipam

cintu veḷi makkaḷ koṇṭirunta vāṇipam uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam, veḷināṭṭu vāṇipam eṉa iraṇṭākak kūṟalām. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta cempu mutaliya kaṇip poruḷkaḷ irājaputaṉam, mattiya mākāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiviruntum, māṉ kompukaḷ kāṣmīriliruntum, viramum veḷḷi kalanta īyamum āpkāṉistāṉattiliruntum, oruvaki uyartarap paccikkal vaṭaparmāviliruntum allatu tipēttiliruntum, poṉ kōlār, aṉantappūr eṉṉum ceṉṉi mākāṇa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum, oruvakip paccikkal micūriliruntum, uyartara ‘amesāṉ’ eṉṉum paccikkal nīlakiriyiliruntum, caṅku muttu mutaliyaṉa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum koṇṭu cellappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatāl, appaṇṭikkālattil intiya uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam ciṟappuṟa naṭantu vantatu eṉpatiyum, cintu veḷi makkaḷ intiyā muḻuvatiyum cuṟṟuppuṟa nāṭukaḷiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum oruvāṟu uṇaralām. hitarāpātil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa cavakkuḻikaḷ mītum, tirunelvēlik kōṭṭattiṟ kiṭitta nāṇayaṅkaḷ mītum, moheñco-tarōvil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu. kāṇappaṭum cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷ pōṉṟavi kāṇappaṭṭami iṅkuk karutat takkatu.[1] iṅṅaṉam ‘teṉṉintiyāviṉ kaṭaṟkari,kattiyavār, vaṭamēṟku maṇṭilam, cintu-pañcāp maṇṭilaṅkaḷ, kaṅkic cama veḷiyiṉ vaṭapakuti, irājaputaṉam mutaliya intiyap pakutikaḷ ak kāla nākarikattiṟku uriyaviyākalām'[2] eṉṟu tīkṣat avarkaḷ kūṟutalāl, akkāla uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipacciṟappi oruvāṟu uṇaralām.

veḷi nāṭṭu vāṇipam

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitti eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ, muttirikaḷ, oviyaṅkaḷ,karuvikaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi ārāynta nipuṇar avaṟṟukkum ēlam, cumēr mocopoṭṭēmiyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpi naṉku aṟintu viyantuḷḷaṉar. ipporuḷkaḷiṉ orumippāṭṭālum ēṟṟumati iṟakkumatip poruḷkaḷālum, nām, cintu nāṭṭavar ceytuvanta veḷināṭṭu vāṇipatti oruvāṟu uṇaralām.

(1) moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta uyarnta calavikkal muttirikaḷip pōṉṟavi mikap palavāka ‘ēlam’ eṉṉum nāṭṭiṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. calavikkal poruḷkaḷ mikutiyākac cintu nāṭṭiṟ kiṭikkāmiyāṉum, avaṟṟiṉ mītu amintuḷḷa vēlippāṭu annāṭukaṭkē uriyavi ātalālum, avi ēlam-cumēriyanāṭukaḷil mikutiyākak kiṭit talālum, avi annāṭukaḷiliruntē cintuviṟkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi mali.[3]

(2) cila intiya muttirikaḷ paḻiya ur, kiṣ, ṭel asmar eṉṉum mocopoṭṭēmiyā nakaraṅkaḷil kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. aṅku irunta piṟa poruḷkaḷum, annakaraṅkaḷiṉ nākarika kālamum ki.mu.3000 ki.mu.2500kku uṭpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, cintu nākarikamum veḷināṭṭu vāṇipamum mēṟcoṉṉakālattaṉavēeṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivākum. ṭelasmaril ṭākṭar pirāṅkpōrṭ eṉṉum aṟiñar kaṇṭeṭutta nīṇṭa uruṇṭiyāṉa muttiri cintu veḷiyiṉatē ākum. atil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa yāṉi, kāṇṭā mirukam, mutaliya ākiya mūṉṟum cumēriyāvil illātavi.

(3) atē nakarattil, uṭalil tuvāramuḷḷa muṭkaḷip pōṉṟa amippik koṇṭa maṇ pāttiraṅkaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa. avi irākkil ki.mu.2800-ki.mu.2500kku uṭpaṭṭa kālattup poruḷkaḷōṭu kalantiruntaṉa. avi intiyāviṟkē uriyavi eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.[4]

(4) oruvaki veḷiṟiya paccik kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāttiram oṉṟu pāy vaṭiva vēlippāṭu koṇṭatāka moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittatu. attakiya pāttiraṅkaḷ pala ṭel asmar, kiṣ, cucā mutaliya paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

nanti vaḻipāṭu

(5) 1936 il ṭel akrap (Tel Agrab) eṉṉum iṭam putitākat tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. aṅkuk ki.mu. 2800 kku uriya kōvil oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉuḷ pūcikkuriya pāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. oruvakip paccik kallāl āṉa pāttirattiṉ mītu toḻuvattiṉ muṉ periya intiya pirāmaṉi erutu niṟpatākavum, atu niṉṟuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭattiṉ muṉpuṟam cumēriya maṉitaṉ uṭkārntiruppatu pōlavum cittiram tīṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ippaṭattiṉāl, appaṇṭik kālattilēyē nanti vaḻipāṭu (Bull Cult) cintuviliruntu cumēriyāvukkuc ceṉṟiruttal viḷaṅkukiṟatu aṉṟō!

(6) muttirikaḷum piṟa pāttiraṅkaḷum ceytaṟkuriya uyartarak kaṟkaḷ cumēriyāviṟku aṉuppappaṭṭuc cemmip paṭuttappaṭṭa piṟaku koṇṭuvarappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṉi? uyartarap paccik kaṟkaḷum vēṟu kaṟkaḷum koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭa maṇikaḷ moheñco-tarōviviṭa ‘ur’ nakaril (cumēriyāvil) mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷamiyāl eṉka.

(7) naṉṟāka vaḷintu muṟukkuṇṭa kompukaḷiyuṭiya cemmaṟiyāṭukaḷik kuṟikkum cittiraṅkaḷ cila moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi morēviyā, kirīṭ tīvu, aṉaṭōliyā, ekiptu cumēriyā, ēlam eṉṉum paṇṭi nāṭukaḷil mikkiruntaṉa.

(8) moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta putuvakik kōṭāri oṉṟu cumēriyāviliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉṉi? attakiya pala kōṭarikaḷ aṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ eṉka.

(9) mīṉ piṭikkap payaṉpaṭṭa valiyiṉ oraṅkaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭirunta maṇ kuṇṭukaḷ iru nāṭukaḷilum oru vitamākavē kiṭittaṉa.[5]

(10) ‘ur’ nakarattil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa aracar tam kallaṟikaḷil tīṭṭappaṭṭa cittiraṅkaḷuṭ kuraṅku oṉṟākum. aḵtu annāṭṭu makkaṭkut teriyāta vilaṅku. eṉavē, an nāṭṭavar nerukkamāṉa vāṇipattoṭarpukoṇṭirunta cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭamiruntē kuraṅkiṉip peṟṟavarātal vēṇṭum.

(11) mēṟ collappaṭṭa kallaṟikaḷil ciṟanta vēlippāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiya irattiṉa maṇikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. aviyum cintuveḷiyiliruntē ceṉṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum.

(12) kūntalit taliyic cuṟṟilum vāric cerukip piṉpuṟam kaṟṟiyāka muṭitalum, vevvēṟu muṟikaḷil muṭitalum avaṟṟiṉmītu koṇṭi ūcikaḷic cerukutalum iru nāṭṭārum potuvākak koṇṭirunta paḻakkamākum.[6]

(13) cintu veḷi makkaḷ yāṉit tantam, yāṉit tantattālāṉa cippukaḷ, caturaṅka viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvi kaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟic cumēriyāvukku ēṟṟumati ceytiruk kiṉṟaṉar.[7]

(14) cintu veḷi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu pūcappaṭṭa ceṅ kāvippoṭi intiyāvil iṉṉa iṭattiliruntu koṇṭu cellappaṭṭateṉpatu tuṇitaṟkilli. āyiṉ, pāracīka vaḷikuṭāvi aṭuttuḷḷa hormus (Hormuz) eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu uyartarac ceṅkāvip poṭi iṉṟum intiyāvil iṟakkumati āki varuvatāl, avviṭattiliruntē appaḻaṅkālattilum vantirukkalām.

(16) vaṭanāṭṭu miṭṭāy vakikaḷit taṭṭukaḷil vittu terukkaḷil viṟkum vaṭanāṭṭavar, attaṭṭukaḷit tāṅka mūṅkiṟ kuccikaḷāl ākiya taṇṭukaḷi eṭuttu varutal kāṇkiṟōm. attakiya nāṇalālāṉa taṇṭukaḷ palavaṟṟi moheñco-tarō makkaḷ payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.atip pōṉṟa taṇṭukaḷ cumēriyā, ekiptu, ēlam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kallālum, maṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum, immūṉṟu nāṭṭavarum cintuveḷi makkaḷiṭamiruntē ittaṇṭukaḷ ceytalik kaṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.

(16) irutaya vaṭivattil elumpukaḷik koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭa nuṇukka vēlippāṭu aminta vēṟu aṇikaḷil patikkattakka ciṟiya aḻakiya poruḷkaḷ ṭelasmaril kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta cippikaḷāl āṉa poruḷkaḷip peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. ivi iruvaki yiṉavum orē kālattaṉa.

(17) kirēkka ciluvi (Greek Cross) vaṭivam poṟikkap paṭṭa muttiri oṉṟu vaṭa kiḻakkuk kirīsil kiṭittatu. atu putiya kaṟkālattiṟku uriyatu. atupōṉṟavi pala moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, avi cintu veḷikkē uriyaṉa eṉak kūṟutal poruntum. avaṟṟiṉ amippu muṟi harappāviṟ kiṭittavaṟṟiliruntu naṉku aṟiyak kiṭakkiṟatu.[8] avi cumēriyāvilum, kīrīṭ tīvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

(18) moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta paṭivaṅkaḷil uḷḷapaṭi mīciyik cirittut tāṭiyip palavāṟu vittukkoḷḷum muṟi cumēriyāvilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

(19) pūcaṉikkuriya taṭṭukaḷit tāṅkum taṇṭukaḷ pala cintu veḷiyilum cumēriyāvilum oṉṟu pōlavē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

(20) eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭiṉum aṭippaṭi oṉṟākavē iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[9]

(21) cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta kōṭarikaḷip pōṉṟa veṇkalak kōṭari oṉṟu kākasas malināṭṭic cērnta ‘kūpaṉ’ āṟṟup paṭukiyiṟ kiṭittatu.

(22) viciṟipōṉṟa talimuṇṭācu turukkiyil uḷḷa ‘aṭaliyā’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kiṭitta patumikaḷilum, ciriyāviṟ kiṭitta cittiram oṉṟilum cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta cittiraṅkaḷilum ottē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

(23) nāṇaṟ pēṉā oṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittatu. atu pōṉṟa eḻutukōl kirīṭ tīvil pāttiraṅkaḷ mītu eḻutap payaṉpaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. appēṉar urōmarkaḷ kālam vari ekiptiyarukkut teriyātatākum.[10]

(24) akkēṭiyar nakaramāṉa ‘eṣṇaṉṉā’vil kaṇṭaṟiyap paṭṭa araṇmaṉiyuḷ nīrāṭum aṟi amikkappaṭṭatu, akkēṭiyar cintu veḷi makkaḷōṭu koṇṭirunta kūṭṭuṟaviṉālēyām eṉṉi vēṟu en nāṭṭilum nīrāṭum aṟikaḷ appaḻaṅkālattil amikkap paṭāmiyiṉ eṉka.[11]

ekiptiyaruṭaṉ vāṇipam

cintu veḷi makkaḷ ekiptiyaruṭaṉ nērākavō aṉṟic cumēriyar mūlamākavō vāṇipam ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu kīḻvarum uṇmikaḷāl uṇaralām:

(1) cintu veḷiyilum ekiptilum kiṭitta kaḻuttu mālikaḷ vaṭṭamākavē amintuḷḷaṉa.

(2) ekiptil kiṭitta pīṭaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ kālkaḷ erutiṉ kālkaḷip pōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṭṭil kālkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē amintuḷḷaṉa. cintu veḷiyil attakiya pīṭam oṉṟiṉmītu civaṉār uruvam amarn tiruppatāka uḷḷa muttiri kiṭittuḷḷatu.[12]

(3) ekiptil uḷḷa piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷākiya aracar cavakkuḻikaḷil, peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cetukkappaṭṭa naṉmaṇikaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. avi iṟantavariṉ maṉiviyarik kuṟippaṉa; ammaṉiviyar maṟupiṟappil tam kaṇavarik kūṭi vāḻvar eṉpatu appaṇṭikkāla ekiptiyar eṇṇam. avveṇṇam cintu veḷiyilum cumēriyāvilum iruntatākat terikiṟatu.

(4) tāy kuḻantikkup pāl koṭuttal pōṉṟa cittiraṅkaḷ pala ekiptil kiṭittaṉa. āṉāl, avi cumēriyāvil kāṇappaṭavilli. moheñco-tarōvil iraṇṭoru cittiraṅkaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

(5) pala mukappukaḷik koṇṭa uyartara maṇikaḷ moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi ekiptit tavira vēṟu entap paṇṭi nāṭṭilum kiṭittila; ekiptilum, piṟkāla rōmarkaḷiṉ kallaṟikaḷiṟṟām kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, avi intiyāviṟkē uriyavi eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.

(6) ī vaṭivattil ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ ekiptilum moheñco-tarōvilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi, cuṟucuṟuppiyum toḻil muṟukkiyum aṟivikkum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

(7) ekiptil irunta 13, 17 ām araca talimuṟiyiṉar kālattavi ākiya cittira eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭa mūṉṟu cemput takaṭukaḷ, moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittavaṟṟiyē peritum ottuḷḷaṉa.

(8) meḻuku varttiyit tāṅkum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum ēṟakkuṟiya oṉṟākavē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.[13]

- (9) kaṟpalakikoṇṭu cippi vaṭiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa karaṇṭikaḷ pala ekiptil kiṭittaṉa. cintu veḷiyilum attakiyaṉa kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi kaḷimaṇṇālum cempiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭavi. ovvoṉṟaṉ nuṉiyilum kippiṭi iṇippukku uriya tuḷi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[14] iṉṉa piṟa cāṉṟukaḷāl, appaḻaṅkālac cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ vāṇipa ulakam, ekiptuvari paravi iruntatu eṉpati naṉkaṟiyalām aṉṟō?

nīrvaḻi vāṇipam

uḷ nāṭṭu vāṇipattiṉ oru pakuti cintu āṟṟiṉ mūlamākavē naṭantatu. appaṇṭik kālattil orē vita nākarikattil irunta cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷ pala cintu āṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē tamakkuḷ vāṇipam naṭattik koṇṭaṉa; arapik kaṭal tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaṭkut tam poruḷkaḷip paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟic cintu āṟṟiṉ mūlam aṉuppiṉa: mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷi aṭinta poruḷkaḷi muṉcoṉṉa paṭakukaḷ mūlamē peṟṟukkoṇṭaṉa.

paṇṭi makkaḷ, iṅṅaṉam cintuyāṟṟu vaḻiyē vanta poruḷkaḷik kappalkaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu tuṟimukaṅkaḷi viṭṭuk kari ōramākavē teṉ palucistāṉam, teṉ pāracīkam, irāk, apicīṉiyā, ekiptu eṉṉum nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytaṉarātal vēṇṭum. appaṇṭi makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya muttiri oṉṟil kappal paṭam oṉṟum, pāṉi mītu ōṭattiṉ paṭam oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭaṅkaḷāl, avarkaḷ kaṭaliṟ cellum kappalkaḷiyum āṟṟiṟ cellum oṭaṅ kaḷiyum naṉku aṟintu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām.

kāmpē vaḷikuṭāvic cērtta purōc(Broach) kāmpē (Combay) eṉṉum tuṟimukaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum cintuveḷi nākarika kālattil ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. attuṟi mukaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē cenniṟakkal maṇikaḷip paṟṟiya vāṇipam uyariya niliyil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum narumati yāṟṟup paḷḷat tākkukaḷil oḻuṅkāṉa ārāycci naṭipeṟumāyiṉ, pala uṇmikaḷ veḷiyākum.[15]

nilavaḻi vāṇipam

cintu veḷikku jēṭ (lade) eṉṉum oruvakik kal naṭu āciyāviliruntu vantukoṇṭiruntatu. akkal cintu veḷi mūlam teṉ pāracīkam, irark, cumēriyā, ciriyā, ekiptu ākiya nāṭukaṭku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, akkālat tarivaḻi vāṇipam ēṟakkuṟiya 4800 ki.mī. tolivil naṭantu vantatu eṉpatu aṟiyattakum.[16]

ittari vaḻi vāṇipattiṟku appaṇṭi makkaḷ erutukaḷiyum kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avaṟṟuḷḷum erutukaḷē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, erutukaḷē appaṇṭikkāla vāṇipatti vaḷarttaṉa eṉṉalām. intak kāraṇam oṉṟik koṇṭē appaṇṭimakkaḷ eruti uyarvāka matittu aṉpu kāṭṭiṉar eṉpatum mikiyākātu.

paṇṭikkālap palucistāṉam

appaḻaṅkālattil ւlucistāṉam ceḻumiyuṭiyatāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum makkaṭ perukkam uṭiyatākavum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. avaruṭ cilarēṉum mēṟcoṉṉa tarivaḻi vāṇipattiṟ paṅkeṭuttaṉarātal vēṇṭum cintu veḷi makkaḷōṭu neruṅkiya uṟavāṉavarākavum iruttal vēṇṭum; ammakkaḷatu mēṟkuppuṟa vāṇipattiṟkup pērutavi purintiṉarātal vēṇṭum.[17]

cintuveḷi makkaṭkup piṉ

iṅṅaṉam peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ naṭipeṟṟu vanta mēṟkuppuṟa vāṇipam, āriyar vantavuṭaṉ tiṭīreṉa niṉṟu viṭṭatu. āriyar pañcāpiliruntu, piṉ kaṅkic camaveḷiyiṟ kuṭi pukkaṉar. avarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam uḷnāṭṭil taṅkiviṭṭatālum cintuveḷi makkaḷ nili kulintu viṭṭaṉar ātalālum avvāṇipam aṭiyōṭu niṉṟuviṭṭatu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ, cintu veḷinākarika kālattiṟkuppiṟpaṭṭa ārampa aciriyap pēraraciṉ kālattilum, ekiptiyar iṭi-kaṭi āṭcik kālaṅkaḷilum avviru nāṭukaḷilum intiyap poruḷkaḷē kiṭittila pōlum![18]

niṟik kaṟkaḷ

kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa niṟik kaṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi aṉittum vaḻavaḻappuṭaṉum paḷapaḷappuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīḷam, akalam, uyaram ākiya mūṉṟu aḷavukaḷum orē muṟiyil irukkumpaṭi ceytuḷḷa kaṟkaḷē palavākum. ivi mikac ciṟiya aḷavu mutal 274938 kirām (gramme) vari palvēṟu niṟikaḷik kuṟippaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa, ucciyilum aṭiyilum taṭṭiyākkappaṭṭa uruṇṭi niṟikaḷ aṭuttāṟpōl mikutiyāka iruppavi ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ cila viyattaku pēraḷavik kuṟippaṉa. mūṉṟām vakiyāṉa niṟikaḷ uruṇṭu nīṇṭaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. immātiri niṟikaḷ cumēriyā, ēlam, ekiptu ākiya nāṭukaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cila niṟikaḷ iṉṟiya viṟaku viṟkum kaṭikaḷil iruppavi pōlap periyaṉavum ucciyil vaḷiyattiṟku aminta tuḷi uṭiyaṉavumākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi kūmpiya ucciyi uṭiyaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu 25 kilōvukku mēṟpaṭṭa niṟiyuṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu. periya viyāpārattiṟkē akkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. in nālvakik kaṟkaḷum karuṅkal, paḷiṅkukkal, paccikkal, maṅkiya civappukkal mutaliya palatiṟappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷāl aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cintu veḷiyiṉar. ikkaṟkaḷi vēṇṭiya vaṭivattil veṭṭi, cāṉikkal pōṉṟa kaṟpalakimītu naṉṟākat tēyttu, iṟutiyil meruku koṭuttu vantaṉar. oru niṟikkallilēṉum ‘eṇ’ iṭappaṭātatu viyappukku uriyatu. ciṟu kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum niṟikaḷākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

niṟi aḷavukaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta niṟikaḷiyum harappāviṟ kiṭittavaṟṟiyum cevviyākac cōtitta aṟiñar ē.es.hemmi eṉpavar. avar, ciṟiya niṟikaḷ iraṭṭi eṇkaḷik kuṟippaṉa. periyavi tacāmca piṉṉa aḷavi uṭiyaṉa. avi 1, 2, ⅓x8, 4, 8, 16, 32, 54, 160, 200, 320, 640, 1500, 3200, 5400, 8000, 12800 ākiya eṇkaḷik kuṟippaṉa. ‘oṉṟu’ (kuṟippiṭṭa oru nilitta niṟi - ‘unit') eṉpatu 0.8565 kirām ākum. ivi muṟṟum cintu veḷikkē uriya niṟikaḷ ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭu piṟa nāṭṭu niṟikaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭat takkaṉa āyiṉum, inniṟikaḷ iyalpākavē ceyyappaṭṭaṉavē yām’, eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. in niṟik kaṟkaḷ aḷaviṟantaṉavāka iruttalik kāṇiṉ, moheñco-tarō ciṟanta vāṇipap perukkamuṭiya nakaramāka iruntatu eṉpatu aṅkik kaṉipōl aiyamaṟa viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

tarācukaḷ

taṭṭukaḷ cempālum taṇṭu veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭa tarācu oṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittatu. ataṉ tōṟṟam uyariyatāka illi. eṉiṉum, atik koṇṭu annakarat tarācukaḷ eḷiyavi eṉak kūṟutal tavaṟu. niṟikaḷi nōkka tarācukaḷ vaṉmiyuṭaṉ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivākum. nam nāṭṭu maṇṭik kaṭikaḷilum viṟakuk kaṭikaḷilum payaṉpaṭum periyatarācukaḷ pala akkālattil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār.

aḷavu kōl

akkālattil aḷavu kōlkaḷum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum kiṭittilatu. oru cippiyiṉ uṭinta pakutiyil cila aḷavuk kuṟikaḷ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appakuti nīḷappakuti oṉṟiṉ tuṇṭākum. atil iraṇṭu vita aḷavukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi tacāmca piṉṉattik kuṟippaṉavākum. akkālattavar, icciṟpip pakutikaḷ mītu tacāmca piṉṉa aḷavukaḷik kuṟippiṭṭu, appakutikaḷi ulōkat takaṭṭil poruttip patintirukkumāṟu ceytu, nīṭṭal aḷavikkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar pōlum! intat tacāmca piṉṉa aḷavic cintu veḷi nākarika kālattilum ataṟkucciṟitu muṉṉarum ēlam, mecopoṭṭēmiyāvilum vaḻakkil iruntatu. eṉṉi? inta aḷavi paḻiya elamiṭ cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum, mecopoṭṭēmiyāvil uḷḷa jemṭeṭ nasr (Jemdet Nasr) eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kiṭitta paṇṭic cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭalām eṉka.[19]

muttiri patittal

cintu veḷi makkaḷ viṟpaṉiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ mītum, poruḷkaḷ niṟinta tāḻikaḷ vāyppuṟattil uḷḷa mūṭi mītum pūṭṭappeṟṟa vīṭṭuk katavil iruntapūṭṭukaḷ mītum muttiriyiṭṭu vantaṉar.[20] ivvaḻakkam iṉṉum iruntu varukiṟataṉṟō? ittakiy muttirikaḷum piṟa ilacciṉi patitta tāyittukaḷum piṟa paṇṭi nāṭukaḷil kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āṉāl cintu veḷiyil avi cempālum kaḷimaṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇ tuṇṭu oṉṟil, neṟṟi naṭuvē nīṇṭa oṟṟik kompuṭiya kutiripōṉṟa nūtaṉa vilaṅkiṉ uruvam oru puṟam patintiruntatu. maṟupuṟam nāṇaṟkaṭṭu oṉṟu kayiṟukoṇṭu iṟukkik kaṭṭappaṭṭu iruppatu pōṉṟa uruvam patintuḷḷatu. ākavē, inta muttiri, nāṇalāl ceyyappaṭṭa peṭṭikaṭkō katavukaṭkō poruttum taṉic ciṟappuṭiyatāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīkṣat karutukiṉṟār. ittakiya nūtaṉa vilaṅku patikkappaṭṭa muttirikaḷ pala, moheñcotrōvilum harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cuṭṭa maṇṇālāṉa muttirikaḷ cila kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷkaḷukku maṭṭumē muttiriyiṭap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.[21]
