* * *





﻿13. viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ - toḻilkaḷ - kalikaḷ

palavaki viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷil kōlikaḷē mikkuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, annakarac ciṟār kōli viḷiyāṭṭil peru viruppam koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu tēṟṟam kaḷimaṇ pantukaḷ pala mātirikkākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟāl, ciṟār pantu viḷiyāṭṭilum paṇpaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu. periyavarkaḷ caturaṅkam cokkaṭṭāṉ mutaliya āṭṭaṅkaḷi āṭivantaṉar. pāyccikaḷ ikkālattaṉa pōlavē puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caturaṅkam āṭutaṟkuriya kāykaḷ maṇṇālum uṟutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷālum aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi pala niṟaṅkaḷip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi vittu āṭutaṟkuriya caturaṅkappalakikaḷ marattāl ceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum; nāḷaṭivil avi aḻintu pōyiruttal vēṇṭum! appalkikaḷil caturac cippikaḷ patittuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷ pirikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iccaturac cippikaḷ pala innakara ārāycciyiṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam caturaṅka viḷiyāṭṭiṟkuriya cātaṉaṅkaḷ pala ‘ur’ nakarattilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta oru ceṅkal mītu nīḷ caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷ nāṉku orē variciyil iruppaṉa pōla mūṉṟu varicikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkal maṟṟoru vakic caturaṅkappalakiyākap payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. ataṉ mītuḷḷa kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu kuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷuṭaṉ (ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭum ‘mali’ pōlak) kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. piṟitoru kalmītu tāya viḷiyāṭṭukku uriya kōṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ pala ur, ekiptu ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum paḻakkattil iruntaṉa.

vēṭṭiyāṭal

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta muttirikaḷ cilavaṟṟil uḷḷa aṭiyāṅkaḷālum aṅkuk kiṭitta māṉ kompukaḷālum vēṭṭi nāyp patumikaḷālum am mānakarattār vēṭṭiyil viruppaṅ koṇṭaṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām. oru muttiriyil, oru marakkiḷiyil iraṇṭu maliyāṭukaḷiṉ talikaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruttalik kāṇalām. maliyāṭukaḷ kīrtarmalit toṭaril mikkirun taṉavātal vēṇṭum. vēṟoru muttiriyil, oruvaṉ kiyil villum ampum koṇṭu niṟpatu pōlavum, avaṉ arukē nāyoṉṟu iru vilaṅkiṉ vālip paṟṟi iḻuppatu pōlavum cittiram cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila muttirikaḷil maṉitar māṉiyum maliyāṭṭiyum vēṭṭiyāṭuvatu pōlavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvēṭṭikku uriya cempu veṇkala ampu muṉikaḷ akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavikaḷi vēṭṭi āṭuvōr kaḷimaṇ uruṇṭikaḷik kavaṇil vittu eṟintamikkuriya cāṉṟukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

kōḻi keḷatāric caṇṭikaḷ

oru muttiriyil, iraṇṭu kāṭṭuk kōḻikaḷ caṇṭi iṭutal pōlap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, ikkālattil naṭipeṟṟu varum māṭṭuccaṇṭi kōḻic caṇṭi mutaliyaṉa akkālattil naṭipeṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu karututal takum. keḷatārikaḷi vaḷarppavaruṭiya vīṭukaḷil iruntaṉavākac cila kaṇimaṇ kūṇṭukaḷ kiṭittaṉa. vēṟoru maṇkūṇṭil keḷatāri nuḻivatu kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘iṉṟu moheñco-tarōvik kāṇa varum cilar, tām vaḷarkkum keḷatārikaḷik kūṇṭukaḷil vittukkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. tiruṣṭi tōṣātikaḷ tākkā tiruppataṟkāka eṉṟē akkūṇṭukaḷ nīlaniṟa maṇikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. contakkārar avaṟṟi aḻippatum, avi avarkaṭku maṟu kural koṭuppatum moheñco-tarōvil irunta paḻiya kavutāri vaḷarppi niṉip pūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.[1]

kottuvēli

cintu veḷiyil vāḻnta kottarkaḷ tam toḻiliṟ paṇpaṭṭa pulami uṭiyavar āvar. avarkaḷ ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷiyum cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷiyum aṭukki, oḻuṅkāṉa cuvarkaḷiyum vīṭukaḷiyum māḷikikaḷiyum potu iṭaṅkaḷiyum kōṭṭikaḷiyum appaṇṭik kālattilēyē paṇpaṭa amikkak kaṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu viyappūṭṭuñ ceytiyē ākum. avarkaḷ oru māḷikiyil 2700 ce.mī. kūṭam amittu, ataṉ mēl aṭukkit tāṅka 20 tuṇkaḷi naṉ muṟiyil niṟuttiyuḷḷaṉar; vīṭṭut tariyi vaḻavaḻappākki, atil vaṭṭak kōṭukaḷum caturak kōṭukaḷum vakiyuṟa iḻuttuḷḷaṉar; maḻiyil naṉiyattakka vīṭṭuppuṟac cuvarkaḷic cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālum uṭpuṟaccuvarkaḷi ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷālum kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. iḵtoṉṟē avarkaḷ aṟiviyum aṉupavattiyum naṉku viḷakka vallatu; kiṇaṟṟiṉ uṭpuṟattiṟkeṉṟē taṉippaṭṭa kaṟkaḷ ceytuḷḷami viyattaṟku uriyatu. cuvarkaḷukkuḷḷum kaḻinīrk kuḻikaḷi amittu vīṭu kaṭṭum āṟṟal peṟṟa apperumakkaḷi evvāṟu pukaḻa vallēm! vīṭu - kiṇaṟu - kuḷam paṟṟiya pakutiyil pala viyattaku uṇmikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭamiyāl, iṅku, avaṟṟi mīṭṭum kūṟal kūṟiyatu kūṟalā’kum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, 5000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṉ irunta cintu veḷik kottarkaḷ, tam toḻil muṟiyi aṟivu koṇṭu ceytu vantaṉar: toḻili vayiṟṟukkākacceyyavilli; kalikkākac ceytu vantaṉar eṉṉalām. ivvuṇmiyi avarkaḷ kaṭṭiyuḷḷa nīrāṭum kuḷattiṉ nallamippik koṇṭum harappāvil kaṭṭiyuḷḷa mikapperiya kaḷañciya vēlippāṭṭik koṇṭum naṉkaṟiyalām. itaṉi mēlum virittal vēṇṭā.

maṭpāṇṭat toḻil

cintuveḷi nākarikattil ciṟappiṭam peṟattakkavi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē ākum. ākavē, avaṟṟic ceyta vēṭkōvar tiṟamiyē tiṟami! avartam perumiyē perumi! avarkaḷē avveḷiyil mutal iṭampeṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṉal mikiyākātu. viyappūṭṭum vitavitavitamāṉa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭu koṇṭa mikac ciṟiya poruḷkaḷ, elumpukaḷiyum cāmpaliyum piṟavaṟṟiyum putittu aṭakkam ceyvataṟku aminta tāḻikaḷ,[2] palavaki oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, kaḻinīrk kuḻikaḷ, oli perukkik kuḻikaḷ, poruttamāṉa aḷavukaḷ aminta palavakic ceṅkaṟkaḷ, vīṭṭu vēlikaṭku uriya palvakip poruḷkaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum vēṇṭiyōr vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceytu tanta vēṭkōvar pērāṟṟali eṉṉeṉpatu!

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta vēṭkōvari viṭa harappā vēṭkōvar piṉṉum uyarntavarāka iruttal kūṭumō eṉṟu aṟiñar aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. aṅku or iṭattil 16 kāḷavāykaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. avi aḷaviṟ ciṟiyavi: āṉāl, virivil cūṭēṟattakka muṟiyil amikkappaṭṭavi: kaḷimaṇ ceppukaḷ, patumikaḷ, maṇikaḷ nakikaḷ ākiya vaṟṟic cūḷiyiṭavum, kal vaḷiyalkaḷ, paṭṭukkalkaḷimaṇ muttirikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟic cuṭṭu merukiṭvum, civappu maṇikaḷic ceyyavum payaṉpaṭṭavi. vēṇṭiya aḷavu veppattiyum kuḷircciyiyum ūṭṭi, vitavitamāṉa niṟaṅkaḷi ēṟṟutaṟku vacatiyāka iruntavi. moheñco-tarōvil oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vēlippāṭu kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭiyatu. 1 ce. mī. uyarattil aḻakiya patumikaḷ ceyyak kaṟṟirunta avvēṭkōvar tiṟamiyi paṇpaṭṭa kaliyaṟivi iṉṟu eṇṇi eṇṇi viyavāta aṟiñar illi!

kaltaccar toḻil

cintu veḷiyiṟ kaṟ caṭṭikaḷ, nīr parukum paccik kal ēṉaṅkaḷ, ammikaḷ, kuḻavikaḷ, uralkaḷ, entiraṅkaḷ, āṭṭukkaṟkaḷ, tūṇkaḷit tāṅkum kuḻiyaminta kaṟkaḷ, ciṟṟurukkaḷ, liṅkaṅkaḷ, yōṉikaḷ, muttirikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟik koṇṭu, cintu veḷiyil kal taccarkaḷ iruntaṉar eṉpatum kal vēli naṭipeṟṟatu eṉpatum naṉkaṟiyalām. harappāvil aḷavaṟṟa liṅkaṅkaḷ pala vaṭiviṉavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. maṉita urukkaḷ (cilikaḷ) ceyyappaṭṭaṭuḷḷaṉa. avvēlippāṭu viyakkat takkatāka uḷḷateṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟi viyantuḷḷaṉar.

marattaccar toḻil

marapporuḷkaḷ virivil aḻiyum iyalpiṉa. ātaliṉ, cintu veḷiyil iṉṟu kiṭittila eṉiṉum, aṅku marattāl iyaṉṟa kaṭṭilkaḷ, nāṟkālikaḷ, mēcikaḷ, mukkālikaḷ, pīṭaṅkaḷ, maṉikaḷ, katavukaḷ, peṭṭikaḷ, tūṇkaḷ, palavakik karuvikaḷiṉ kippiṭikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. erutiṉ kālkaḷip pōlac ceyyappaṭṭa kālkaḷik koṇṭamarattāl āṉa mukkālippīṭaṅkaḷum erumik kālkaḷ vitta cāy maṉikaḷum iruntaṉa eṉpati muttirikaḷik koṇṭu aṟiyalām. ammarattaccarkaḷ kāṭṭiya paḻakkattiliruntē, ciṅkak kālikaḷik koṇṭa pīṭaṅkaḷ piṟkālattil tōṟṟameṭuttaṉa pōlum!

kaṉṉāra vēli

cempu, veṇkalakkaruvikaḷum piṟaporuḷkaḷum nirampak kiṭittami koṇṭu, cintu veḷiyil kaṉṉāra vēliyum ciṟappāka naṭipeṟṟami aṟiyalām. ikkāla aṟiñarkaḷ peru viyappukkoḷḷumpaṭi urukki vārkkappaṭṭa ciṟanta vēlippaṭāminta veṇkala (naṭaṉamātiṉ) uruvaccili oṉṟē akkaṉṉārap perumakkaḷiṉ kittoḻiṟ ciṟappi uṇarttap pōtumāṉatu! melliya takaṭukaḷāka amintuḷḷa īṭṭikaḷum, ciṟiya aḻakiya kūrmiyāṉa paṟkaḷik koṇṭa irampaṅkaḷum, kōṭarikaḷum, arivāḷ maṇikaḷum. iṭi vāḷkaḷum, nīṇṭa vāḷkaḷum, ampukaḷum piṟavum avar tam toḻiṟ pulamiyit teṟṟeṉat terivippaṉavākum. mikap palavāka-vakivakiyākak kiṭitta maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ, maḻittal takaṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik kāṇum ikkāla ārāycci aṟiñar, akkālak kaṉṉāratu tiṟamiyi eṇṇip peruviyappuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar eṉiṉ, avartam paṇpaṭṭa toḻil muṟiyi eṉṉeṉap pukaḻvatu!

araṇmaṉiyuḷ kāḷavāykaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil araṇmaṉi eṉṟu karutappaṭṭa periya kaṭṭiṭattiṉ tiṟantaveḷiyil ciṟiyavaṭivil cila kāḷavāykaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. avi aracāṅkattāruṭiya paṭikkalaṅkaḷic ceyyavum, paḻutu pārkkavum payaṉpaṭṭa kāḷavāykaḷāka irukkalām. avi vaṭṭamāka amintavi. - ittakiya kāḷavāykaḷ ‘kiṣ’ nakarattu araṇmaṉiyuḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avi nīḷ caturamāka amintavi. avaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.mu.2800 ākum.[3]

poṟkollar toḻil

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭittuḷḷa poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikaḷil amintuḷḷa vēlippāṭu ciṟappuṭiyatākum. kāppukaḷ, vaḷiyalkaḷ, aṭṭikikaḷ, kaḻuttu mālikaḷ, am mālikaḷil kōkkappaṭṭa palavakip poṉmaṇikaḷ, pū vēlippāṭu aminta maṇikaḷ, pala uruvaṅkoṇṭamaṇikaḷ, nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭaminta talic cāmāṉkaḷ, cuṭṭikaḷ, kātaṇikaḷ, kaṭakaṅkaḷ, mōtiraṅkaḷum, iṉṉapiṟavum akkālap poṟkollar vēlippāṭṭi viḷakkuvaṉa ākum. ivvaṇikaḷil iḻippu vēlippāṭu ciṟantu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. moheñco-tarōvil kiṭittavaṟṟiviṭa harappāvilēyē poṉ aṇikaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

irattiṉakkal cōtaṉi

aṇikaḷ ceytaṟkaminta pala vaki irattiṉak kaṟkaḷiyum, vēṟu pala niṟak kaṟkaḷiyum cōtippataṟkeṉṟē palar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉṉi? ‘navarattiṉaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟu collappaṭum maṇikaḷ aṉittum cintuveḷi makkaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: paṇpaṭṭa vēlippāṭu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa; nīlakiri mutaliya neṭun tolivil uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum kaṟkaḷ koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa eṉṉum vivaraṅkaḷāl eṉka.

cetukku vēli

cintu veḷiyil ituvari 1000kku mēṟpaṭṭa palavaki muttirikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. muttiriyiṉ oru puṟam ētēṉum or uruvamum cittira eḻuttukaḷum maṟupuṟam vēṟu kuṟiyīṭukaḷ palavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ceyyappaṭṭu 5000 āṇṭu kaṭku mēlākiyum, iṉṟum puttam putiyaṉavākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa eṉiṉ, ivaṟṟic ceytavar toḻiṟ pulamiyi eṉṉeṉpatu? cempu nāṇayaṅkaḷ mītum muttirikaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa[4]; eḻuttukkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. immuttirikaḷil kāṇappaṭum kuṟippukaḷikkoṇṭē appaṇṭi makkaḷ moḻiyiyum eḻuttukkaḷiyum, camayac ceytikaḷiyum, ōviyam, ciṟpam, cetukku vēli iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum aṟiya iṭam ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉiṉ, ivaṟṟit tayāritta pēraṟiñar nam pōṟṟutaṟkuriyarē yāvar allarō? pala muttirikaḷ veṇkal koṇṭu ceytu cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭu merukiṭappaṭṭavi. ivi 1 ce. mī. mutal 6 ce. mī. vari caturamāka uḷḷaṉa. cila 2 ce. mī. mutal 3 ce.mī. vari caturamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cittiraṅkaḷ maṭṭumē koṇṭavi cila; cittiraṅkaḷōṭu eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ koṇṭavi pala. ivi aṉittum irampam koṇṭu oḻuṅkāka aṟukkappaṭṭavi; ciṟṟuḷiyālō - kattiyālō oḻuṅkupeṟac cetukkappaṭṭavi; piṉṉarē merukiṭappaṭṭavi. ivi ivvāṟu tayārikkap paṭṭa piṟakē, ivaṟṟiṉmītu uruvaṅkaḷum eḻuttukkaḷum cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉṉar ivi, ētō oruvkip poruḷ mēlē pūcappaṭṭuc cuṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.appaṇṭikkālattil ittuṇi muṟikaḷum oru muttiri ceyyak kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉiṉ, muttirit toḻil īṭupaṭṭirunta māntar tam toḻil aṟiviyum kaliyuṇarviyum eṉṉeṉpatu!

caṅkut toḻil

akkālattavar caṅkikkoṇṭu palavaki nakikaḷiyum, nakikaḷiṟpatikkum ciṟu patakkaṅkaḷiyum piṟa poruḷkaḷiyum ceytukoṇṭaṉar caṅki aṟuttu, aram cetukkum karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟikkoṇṭu palcakkaram, caturattuṇṭu, mukkōṇat tuṇṭu. irutayam pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟic ceytaṉar; ivaṟṟip poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu ivaṟṟāl āṉa nakikaḷiṟ patittu vantaṉar. ivvāṟu ceyyappaṭṭu vanta vēli viyappūṭṭuvatēyākum.

mīṉ piṭittal

moheñco-tarō nakaram cintu yāṟṟiṉ kari mītē irunta nakaramātalālum, harappā rāviyāṟṟiṉ aṇmiyil iruntatātalālum, avveḷikaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷ mīṉ piṭikkun toḻiliyum ciṟappāka mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu tavaṟākātu.aṅku mīṉ piṭikkap payaṉpaṭṭacempu-veṇkalat tūṇṭilkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. mīṉ piṭikkum valikaḷiṉ oraṅkaḷiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa maṇ uruṇṭikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ‘moheñco-tarō makkaḷ mīṉ uṇavip peritum payaṉ paṭuttiṉar eṉṟu kūṟalām’ eṉṟu makkē pōṉṟa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatiliruntu, mīṉ piṭikkun toḻil akkālattil ciṟappuṭiyatākak karutappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpatu veḷiyām.[5]

vaṇṭi oṭṭutal .

cintu veḷiyil perumpālum māṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷē mikkiruntaṉa. avaṟṟi oṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟē vaṇṭikkārar palar iruntaṉar. ‘cāṭṭi’ iruntatu. vaṇṭi kūṇṭu uṭiyatu. ikkuṟippukaḷi uṇarttum viḷiyāṭṭu maṇ vaṇṭi oṉṟu harappāvil kiṭittatu. harappāvil kiṭitta viḷiyāṭṭu vaṇṭi cempāṟceyyappaṭṭatu. vaṇṭi oṭṭiyiṉ mukam vaṭṭamāṉatu: mūkkut taṭṭiyākavum oraḷavu uyarntum irukkiṟatu; kūntal civip piṉpuṟam muṭiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cintuveḷi nākarikattu vaṇṭikaḷē ulakiṟ paḻimiyāṉavi eṉṉalām. cintu veḷi nākarika kālattiṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarē ekiptil vaṇṭikaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. ṭākṭar makkē cāṉhu-tarōvil iraṇṭu maṇ vaṇṭikaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttār. oṉṟu naṉṉiliyil iruntatu; maṟṟatu cakkaraṅkaḷ illāmal iruntatu.

harappāviṟ kiṭitta vaṇṭikaḷ (1) iṉṟiya vaṭa intiya vaṇṭikaḷip pōluḷḷa catura vaṇṭikaḷ, (2) mōṭṭār lāriyip pōṉṟa cāmāṉ ēṟṟic cellum vaṇṭikaḷ, (3) ik kālattuc cāraṭṭu vaṇṭiyip pōla etiretir ācaṉaṅkaḷik koṇṭa vaṇṭikaḷ, (4) paṭaku pōṉṟa vaṇṭikaḷ eṉa nāl vakippaṭum. iviyaṉṟi iraṇṭu uruḷikaḷik koṇṭa irataṅkaḷ eṉpaṉavum iruntaṉa. ivi marattāl ceyyap paṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. ittakiya viḷiyāṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷ iṉṉum pañcāppil hoṣiyarp pūr eṉṉum iṭattiṟ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[6]

nāvitat toḻil

kaṇakkaṟṟa cuvarak kattikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷamiyāl cintu veḷi makkaḷ kṣavarattil mikka akkaṟikoṇṭu iruntaṉar eṉpatum, attoḻilāḷar palar iruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatum kūṟāmalē viḷaṅkum ceytikaḷām.

tōṭṭi vēli

‘nakara cukātārattik kavaṉitta perumi, appaṇṭikkāla nākarika nakaraṅkaḷ aṉittiyum viṭa moheñco - tarōvukkē uriyatākum’ eṉṟu sar jāṉ mārṣal kūṟutalāl, annakarāṇmik kaḻakattiṉar ṉar tōṭṭikaḷi vittiruntaṉar eṉpati naṉku uṇaralām.

kāval toḻil

periya māḷikikaḷiṉ muṉpuṟa aṟikaḷum teruk kōṭikaḷil irunta aṟikaḷum kāvaṟkārarkku uriyavi eṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa taṉṟō? moheñco-tarō, harappā eṉṉum periya nakaraṅkaḷ vāṇipap perukkuṭiyaṉa - ātaliṉ, avaṟṟikkākka vēṇṭiya poṟuppu annakarak kaḻakat tāriyō-aracariyō cārntatākum. avarkaḷāl amarttap paṭṭa pātukāvalar palar nakaraṅkaḷip pātukāttu vantaṉar.[7]

kappal toḻil

cintu veḷi makkaḷ āṟṟu vāṇipattilum kaṭal vāṇipattilum ciṟantiruntaṉar. ātaliṉ, āṟukaḷukku vēṇṭiya paṭakukaḷiyum kaṭaṟ celavukkuriya kappalkaḷiyum kaṭṭat terintiruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta muttiri oṉṟil paṭakiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atiṟ pāymaram illi. cukkāṉum, paṭakiṉ naṭuvil or aṟiyum, mīkāmaṉ irukka iṭamum uḷḷaṉa. appaṭakiṉ amippik koṇṭu, akkālap paṭakukaḷi nām oruvāṟu aṟintukoḷḷalām. avi nāṇaṟkōlkaḷ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceyyappaṭṭa paṭakukaḷ paṇṭi ekiptilum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṭpāṇṭa oṭṭil tīṭṭappaṭṭirunta oviyam oṉṟu paṭakiṉum vēṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷum vaḷintu uyarntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. naṭuvilaṟiyē illi; 180 ce.mī. uyarattil pāy maram irukkiṟatu. cukkāṉum uḷatu. appaṭaku āṟṟilum kaṭalilum payaṉpaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum.[8] eṉavē, cintu veḷi makkaḷ paṭakukaḷik kaṭṭavum vēṟu vakik kappalkaḷik kaṭṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivām.

payirt toḻil

nel, kōtumi, vāṟkōtumi, eḷ, parutti mutaliyaṉa payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, uḻavu ciṟappuṟṟa toḻilākavē iruntuvanta teṉṉalām. vayalkaṭku vēṇṭiya nīric cintu yāṟē tantu vantatu. ammakkaḷ cintu yāṟṟunīrik kālvāykaḷ mūlam koṇṭu ceṉṟu vayalkaḷiṟ pāyac ceytiruttal vēṇṭum. kalappikkārarkaḷum arivāḷkaḷum kiṭittami koṇṭum ciṟitu kōtumi iruntatik koṇṭum payirttoḻil ciṟappuṭiya toḻilākak karutappaṭṭateṉpatu veḷiyākum.

necavut toḻil

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa vīṭukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ necavut toḻil naṭantu vantatu eṉpati aṟivippaṉ pōlak ‘katirkaḷ’ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi paḷiṅkālum caṅkiṉālum kaḷi maṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭavi. eṉavē, celvar mutal vaṟiyar īṟāka iruntavar aṉivarum nūl nūṟṟalil īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉa oruvāṟu kūṟalām. cintu veḷiyiṟ paruttiyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭṭatu. kampaḷa āṭikaḷ ‘iruntila’ eṉṟu cilarum, iruntirukkalām eṉṟu cilarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāriṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āṭikaḷ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu taruvikkappaṭṭaṉa. ‘kittāṉ’ pōṉṟa taṭitta tuṇiyum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. moheñco-tarōvil 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ irunta pañcu kiṭittuḷḷatu. aḵtu iṉṟiya intiyap pañcippōlavē irukkiṟatu. ippañcittāṉ pāpilōṉiyar cintu[9] eṉṟum, kirēkkar ciṉṭaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. celvarkaḷ cittira vēlippāṭaminta āṭikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar; pūt tiyalkaḷik koṇṭa mēlāṭikaḷip pōrttu vantaṉar eṉpatic cila vaṭivaṅkaḷik koṇṭē kūṟalām.

tiyalum piṉṉalum

ivi iraṇṭum aṅku iruntaṉa eṉpatu mēlē kūṟappaṭṭatu. ivvēlikaṭku uriya ūcikaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi ceppu, veṇkalam, elumpu, tantam, taṅkam ivaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭavi. avaṟṟuḷ cila nūl kōttut tippataṟku utavum ūcikaḷ; cila pūt tiyalkaḷ pōṭavum, cittiraṅkaḷ piṉṉavum payaṉpaṭṭaviyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

tanta vēli

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta poruḷkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu yāṉik kōṭukaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi. or aḻakiya pāttirattiṉ aṭippuṟattiṟkō aṉṟi mēṟpuṟattiṟkō payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa tantat takaṭu oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. atu cila aḻakiya vēlippāṭu kaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatu. tantap pāttiraṅkaḷ iruntirukkalām; periya pāttiraṅkaḷi aḻaku ceyyat tantat takaṭukaḷip payaṉpaṭutti irukkalām: tanta ūcikaḷ, tantak katirkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kiṭittuḷḷatik koṇṭum muttirikaḷ palavaṟṟil kōṭuḷḷa yāṉikaḷ kāṇappaṭalikkoṇṭum, cintuveḷiyil yāṉikaḷ putiyaṉa alla eṉpatum tantat toḻil naṭipeṟṟu irukkalām eṉṟum kūṟalām,[10] tantap poruḷkaḷ cila cumēriyāviṟ kāṇappaṭu talikkoṇṭu. avi cintu veḷiyiliruntē pōyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatāl, tanta vāṇipamum naṭipeṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭam uṇṭu.

maṇi ceyyun toḻil

paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu uyartarak kaṟkaḷi varavaḻittu, avaṟṟi aṟuttup paṟpalavita maṇikaḷākki merukiṭṭuc cevvippaṭuttum toḻil vallār palar iruntaṉar eṉpatu, cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭittuḷḷa aḷaviṟanta maṇikaḷik koṇṭu eḷitil aṟiyalām. ammaṇikaḷil orē aḷavuḷḷa vaḻavaḻap pāṉa tuḷikaḷ kiṭintuḷḷatik kaṇṭu ārāycciyāḷar viyappuṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉiṉ, attoḻil vallār vittirunta nuṇṇiya karuvikaḷiyum avartam āṟṟaliyum eṉṉeṉap pārāṭṭuvōm! curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, avvēli muṟiyē iṉṟuḷḷa maṇi vēlikaṭkup piṟappiṭam eṉṉal mikiyākātu.

pāy piṉṉutal

māṭaṅ kaṭṭiyavar payaṉpaṭuttiya kōrip pāy oṉṟu paḻutuṟṟa niliyiṟ kiṭittatu. cintu āṟṟup paṭukiyil kōriyum nāṇalum iyalpākak kiṭittamiyiṉ, cintu veḷi makkaḷ avaṟṟik koṇṭu pāykaḷip piṉṉik koṇṭaṉar; kūrikaḷi amittuk koṇṭaṉar. eṉavē, pāy piṉṉun toḻil aṅku iruntatu eṉpatu tōṟṟam.

eḻutak kaṟṟavar

cintu veḷi makkaḷ eḻutak kaṟṟavar āvar. itaṉi, avarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa cempu naḷṇayaṅkaḷālum 1000kku mēṟpaṭṭa muttirikaḷālum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷālum naṉkuṇaralām. avarkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu eḻuta oruvaki nāṇaṟ pēṉāvip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. attakiya pēṉākkaḷik kirīṭ tīviṉar payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avi nākarikam mikunta ekiptilum urōmarkaḷ kālattukku muṉ iruntatilli. eḻutap payaṉpaṭṭa civanta kaḷimaṇ palakikaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ nīḷam 10 ce.mī. akalam 8 ce.mī, kaṉam 180 ce. mī. avaṟṟiṉ mītu oruvakip poruḷ taṭavappaṭṭu, apporuḷmītu eḻutappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.[11] kaṇakkaṟṟa muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum cittira eḻuttukaḷik kāṉiṉ, cintu veḷi makkaḷatu eḻuttu muṟiyi naṉku aṟiyalām. avveḻuttukaḷ iṉṟu vacikkappaṭavilli. āyiṉum, ‘aviyē intiya mutal eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu itukāṟuṅ karutappaṭṭa pirāmi eḻuttukaṭkē piṟappiṭamākum’ eṉṟu lāṅṭaṉ (Langdon) haṉṭṭar pōṉṟa moḻillunar aṟintuḷḷaṉar. avveḻuttukaḷi ārāyntu pākupaṭutti haṉṭṭar eṉpavar ēṟakkuṟiya 250 pakkaṅkaḷ koṇṭa perunūl[12] oṉṟi 1934 il veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. hīrās eṉṉum pātiriyār oruvarum ittakiya ārāyccip paṇiyil iṟaṅkiyuḷḷār.avarum virivil nūl oṉṟi veḷiyiṭuvār. iṅṅaṉam aṟiñar palar kavaṉattiyum īrttuḷḷa aṟputa eḻuttukkaḷip paṇṭic cintup piratēca makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.

vāṇipat toḻil

cintu makkaḷ toḻilkaḷiṟ ciṟantiruntavar eṉpataṟku avarkaḷatu vāṇipamē ciṟanta cāṉṟu pakarum, avarkaḷ ēṟakkuṟiya 4800 ki.mī. tolivil naṭu āciyāviliruntu ekiptu vari vāṇipam naṭatti vantaṉar. ikkālap puki vaṇṭiyō, vāṉa ūrtiyō, nīrāvik kappalō, mōṭṭār lārikaḷō illāta 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ avarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ paṇṭap potikaḷi erutukaḷ mītu ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟum, kappalkaḷ mūlam kariyōramākavē ceṉṟum kaṭal vāṇipam - ākiya iraṇṭiyum veṟṟi peṟa naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpatu muṟpakutiyil viḷakkamuṟak kūṟappaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum kaliyuṇarviyum piṟa nāṭṭiṉarkkuk koṭuttu, avar tam poruḷkaḷiyum kaliyuṇarviyum peṟṟu, vāṇipattiyum kaliyuṇarviyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar. ‘tirikaṭal oṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu’ eṉpataṟku apperumakkaḷē cāṉṟāvar. avarkaḷ intiyāviṉ pala pakutiyiliruntum tamakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷip peṟṟu uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipattiyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar.

ciṟpak kali

cintu veḷi makkaḷ ciṟpak kaliyit tōṟṟuvittavar eṉṉal tavaṟākātu. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta cuṇṇāmpukkal koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭirunta cili oṉṟu aṟiñar kavaṉatti īrttatu. avvuruvam yōkiyiṉ uruvam eṉṟum, camayat talivar uruvam eṉṟum aṟiñar palavāṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. avvuruvam cittirat tiyalikkoṇṭa pōrvi oṉṟip pōrttuḷḷatu; mīciyiṉṟit tāṭiyi maṭṭum vittuḷḷatu; attāṭi oḻuṅkupeṟa amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. talimayir oḻuṅkākac cīvappeṟṟu, vaṭṭap patakkattuṭaṉ kūṭiya taliyaṇiyāl aḻuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu.inta amippu muṟi kaṇkavar vaṉappiṉatu.

harappāvil kiṭittuḷḷa kaṟcilikaḷ cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ ciṟpakkali aṟivit tiṟampaṭak kāṭṭuvaṉavām. avi viyakkattakka muṟiyil ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kikaḷ taṉiyē ceytu iṇippataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu accilikaḷil tuḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; aṅṅaṉamē talikaḷum iṇikkappaṭum pōlum kaṟkaḷil iṇippu vēliyic ceyyat tuṇinta ammakkaḷiṉ kaliyaṟivi eṉṉeṉpatu! ivvāṭavar cilikaḷ maṇaṟ kallālum paḻuppu niṟak kallālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkaṟkaḷ cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭippaṉa alla; veḷi iṭaṅkaḷiliruntē koṇṭuvarap paṭṭavi. ikkaṟkaḷip piṟkāla intiyar iṉṟaḷavum cilikaṭkup payaṉpaṭutta aṟintilar eṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.

ciṟpak kali kirēkkariṭamiruntutāṉ intiyāviṟku vantatu eṉṟu itukāṟum kūṟi vantatu iṉṟu tavaṟākiviṭṭatu. avartam kali tōṉṟaṟku muṉṉarē cintuveḷi makkaḷ ciṟpak kaliyit tōṟṟuvittaṉarātal vēṇṭum. ‘intiyac ciṟpak kali intiyāviṟkē uriyatu’ eṉṟu carjāṉ mārṣal kūṟiyuḷḷatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.[13]

veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa naṭaṉamātiṉ uruvam oṉṟum cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ ciṟpa aṟivic ciṟappuṟak kāṭṭuvatākum. ataṉippaṟṟi viyantu eḻutāta aṟiñar ilar eṉiṉ, ataṉ vēlippāṭṭuc ciṟappi eṉṉeṉpatu! veṇkala vārppaṭaṅ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa avvuruvam vaḻavaḻappākavum oḻuṅkākavum amintuḷḷatu viyappukku uriyatēyākum. cila uruvac cilikaḷ naṭaṉam ceyvatṟku ēṟṟavāṟu niṟpaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvōr uruvamum iṭatu kālit tūkki valatu kālmītu niṉṟu naṭaṉam ceyvatu pōla amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iccilikaḷ, civaperumāṉatu piṟkāla naṭaṉattiṟku tōṟṟuvāy ākalām eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu.

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta vilaṅkup paṭivaṅkaḷik koṇṭum ammakkaḷatu ciṟpa aṟivi uṇaralām; oru kuraṅku taṉ kuṭṭiyuṭaṉ uṭkārntiruppatu pōlac ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. vēṟu oru kuraṅku taṉmuḻaṅkālkaḷ mītu kikaḷi ūṉṟikkoṇṭu uṭkārntiruppatu pōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam ceyyappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷil kaṟkaḷum cippikaḷum kaṇkaḷāka vittuppatikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. immuṟi ekiptilum cumēriyāvilum kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa erumi, kuraṅku, veḷḷāṭu eṉpaṉa pārkkat takkavi; ciṟanta muṟiyil amintuḷḷavi.

ōviyak kali

cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ oviyat tiṟami avar tam maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiliruntē aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅkuk kiṭittuḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oviyaṅkaḷ mikat teḷivāka akkāla niliyiyum nākarikattiyum uṇartta vallaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi varalāṟṟuk kālattu oviyaṅkaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ōviyak kaliyi aḷaviṭṭu aṟiyap pērutavi purikiṉṟaṉa. oru vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ pala vaṭṭaṅkaḷ iṭṭuḷḷa muṟi pōṟṟutaṟkuriyatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu maraṅkaḷ, ilikaḷ, ilikkottukaḷ, catūkkaṭṭaṅkaḷ, kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka uḷḷa mukkōṇaṅkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, pāmpukaḷ, mīṉkaḷ, kāṭṭu erumikaḷ, mali āṭukaḷ,mayilkaḷ, māṉkaḷ, kappalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ oviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

toṭarntu varumtoṉmi nākarikam

iviyaṉṟi, mikka vēlippāṭukaḷ aṟṟa cilikaḷum oviyaṅkaḷum pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi cintu kaṅki veḷikaḷilum paravi irukkiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ, cintu veḷip paṇṭi nākarikam kaṅki veḷiyilum paravi iruntami naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mēlum appaṇṭik kālac ciṟpak kaliyum ōviyak kaliyum varalāṟṟukkālam vari toṭarntu kik koḷḷappaṭṭu vantiruntami veḷiyākiṉṟatu. pāṭalipurattil akappaṭṭa mōriyar kālattu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum cintu veḷiyiṉ maṭpāṇṭat toṭarcciyākavē karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cintu veḷi nākarika kālattiṟkum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟkum - iṭiyil pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintirunta pōtilum vāḻkkiyil pala māṟutalkaḷ uṇṭāki iruppiṉum oviyak kaliyum ciṟpak kaliyum varalāṟṟuk kālamvari toṭarntē vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟāl, intiyac ciṟpak kalikkum oviyak kalikkum cintu veḷiyē tāyakamāṉatu eṉpatu teḷivākum aṉṟō?

iciyum naṭaṉamum

kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āṇ uruvam oṉṟaṉ kaḻuttil ‘tavul’ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa naṭaṉamātiṉ vaṭivam oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. cila kaṟcilikaḷ naṭaṉamuṟiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu muttirikaḷil ‘mirutaṅkam’ pōṉṟa tōṟ karuvikaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkālattu ‘vīṇi’ pōla oviyam oṉṟu tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkālattup pākavatarkaḷ kikaḷil vittuk koṇṭu tāḷamiṭum karuvikaḷ pōṉṟaviyum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉittiyum nōkka, cintu veḷi makkaḷ icik kaliyilum naṭaṉak kaliyilum ōraḷavu payiṟci uṭiyavar eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

kaṇitap pulami

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭittuḷḷa niṟik kaṟkaḷ mikappala. avi perumpālum ellā vīṭukaḷilum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟalām. niṟik kaṟkaḷip paṟṟiya viḷakkam ceṉṟa pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ īṇṭu virittal vēṇṭā. niṟiyiṉ mikac ciṟiya aḷavu tacāmca piṉṉattiṟ celkiṉṟatu; pēraḷavu 15 pavuṇṭ vari celkiṉṟatu eṉiṉ, akkāla makkaḷatu kaṇitap pulami teṟṟeṉat terikiṟataṉṟō? niṟikaḷ 64 vari iraṭṭi eṇkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉa aḷavik kuṟikkum niṟikaḷ oru kirāmiṉ 0.8565 aḷavu vari pōkiṉṟaṉa eṉiṉ ammakkaḷatu. kaṇitak kali vaḷarcciyi eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟi viyappatu. ‘inniṟik kaṟkaḷ mecopoṭṭēmiyā pōṉṟa akkāla nākarika nāṭukaḷil irunta niṟik kaṟkaḷi viṭa eṭiyil cariyāṉaviyākavum aḷavil oḻuṅkāṉaviyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟintu viyakkiṉṟaṉar.

maruttuvak kali

iṉṟu intiyā muḻutilum āyurvēta yūṉāṉi maruttuvap pulavar payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟa ‘cilā cittu’ eṉṉum maruntu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷatu. cintu veḷiyiṉar, campar (Sambur) māṉ kompukaḷip poṭiyākki maruntākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. māṉ kompukaḷum, māṭṭuk kompukaḷum pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. māṭṭukkompukaḷ cila kiṇṇam pōlak kuṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi maruntu vittuk koḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa ākum. nāṭṭu maruttuvattil ‘cilā cittu’ uyartara maruntākum. atu vayiṟṟil uṇṭākum kuṭal, nuriyīral mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya nōykaḷik kuṇappaṭuttum āṟṟal peṟṟatu. atu, imayamalip pāṟikaḷiliruntu kacintu varuvatu. ataṉi mali vāḻnar koṇṭuvantu iṉṟum uḷ nāṭukaḷil koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. cintu veḷiyilēyē moheñco-tarōvi otta nākarikanakaramāka irunta ‘otmañco-puti’ eṉṉum iṭattil maruntukkuriya oruvaki elumpukaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi kātu, kaṇ, toṇṭi tōl paṟṟiya nōykaḷik kuṇappaṭuttum vaṉmiyuṭiyaṉa. ‘moheñco-tarō makkaḷ viruntil viruppuṭiyar eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ātalāl, avarkaḷ acīraṇattāl tuṉpuṟṟaṉarātal iyalpē. avarkaḷik kuṇappaṭutta makaḷir cila maruttuva muṟikaḷi aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟal tavaṟākātu’.[14] ‘...iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟāl, iṉṟuḷḷa āyurvēta maruttuvak kali paṟṟiya mūla uṇarccikaḷ cintuveḷi maruttuvarkaḷiṭamiruntē tōṟṟamāyiṉa eṉṉal naṉku veḷiyām’.[15]

vāṉa nūṟ pulami

cintu veḷi makkaḷ vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭiyuḷḷa muṟiyiliruntu irāci kaṇaṅkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷik kavaṉittu vantaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu, muttirikaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷ irācikaḷit tām kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar; ammakkaḷatu āṇṭut toṭarcci ēṟakkuṟiyat ti mātattiliruntē toṭakkamāka iruntatu eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[16] āṉāl, avaṟṟit tiṭṭamākak kūṟakkūṭavilli eṉṟu irāv pakatūr tīkṣit pōṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[17] hirās pātiriyār, ‘cintu veḷi makkaḷ irāci maṇṭalatti naṉku aṟintavarkaḷ, avarkaḷ kālattil eṭṭu irācikaḷē iruntaṉa, cumēriyar kālattiṟ pattu irācikaḷ kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭaṉa. cintu makkaḷiṉ irāci maṇṭalak kuṟippik kaṇittup pārkkiyil, akkālam, ēṟakkuṟiya ki.mu. 5610 eṉak kūṟalām. itu, cumēriyar nākarikamē tōṉṟāta kālam ākum eṉṟu pēciyuḷḷār.[18]

uṭaṟ payiṟci

harappāviṟ kiṭitta muttiri oṉṟil or āṭavaṉ uṭaṟ payiṟci ceyvatākap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, cintuveḷi makkaḷ ōraḷavu uṭaṟpayiṟci muṟikaḷi aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutal tavaṟākātu.[19]

nakara makkaḷ

toḻilkaḷiyum kalikaḷiyum nōkka, cintu veḷi nakaraṅkaḷil moḻip pulavar, nimittaṅ kūṟuvōr, kālakkaṇitar, maruttuvar, icivāṇar, kūttap pulavar, kūtta makaḷir, araciyaṟ makaḷir, araciyaṟ paṇiyāḷar, nakarakkāvalar, camayak kurumār (purōkitr), vēḷāḷar, uṇavup poruḷ viṟpōr, kūla vāṇikar, poṟkollar, kaṉṉār, taccar, mīṉvāṇikar, appa vāṇikar, uppuvāṇikar, marakkalam oṭṭuvōr, vaṇṭi oṭṭuvōr, marakkalam kaṭṭuvōr, vēṭkōvar, irattiṉap paṇiyāḷar, cetukku vēliyāḷar, oviyan tīṭṭuvōr, ciṟpikaḷ, kāl naṭi vaḷarppōr, caṅku aṟuppōr, ciṟpa vēli ceypavar, veṇkal-mākkal mutaliya palavakik kalvēli ceyvōr, tantavēli ceypavar, viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ ceyvōr, nīrkoṇṭu varupavar, kottarkaḷ, muttiri ceypavar, nāvitar, tōṭṭikaḷ mutaliya pala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉpatit teḷivuṟa aṟiyalām. ivarkaḷ allāmal ekiptu, asiriyā, pāpilōṉiyā, ēlam, cumēriyā, pāracīkam naṭu āciyā, parmā, teṉ intiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭuk kuṭiyēṟiyirunta makkaḷ cilarāvar. ātaliṉ, moheñco-tarō ikkālattuk karācci nakarippōlap pala nāṭṭavarik koṇṭirunta vāṇikap perunakar ākum: paṇṭip pūmpukār , koṟki, muciṟi, toṇṭi pōṉṟa vāṇipa nakaram ākum eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu. itaṉāṟṟāṉ aṅkuk kiṭitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷum maṇṭi oṭukaḷum maṅkōliyar, alpiṉar, matyatarik kaṭaliṉar, āsṭrēliyā iṉattavar ākiya pal nāṭṭu makkaḷuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.



↑ Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’ pp.187, 188.

↑ paṇṭit tamiḻakattil attoḻil ciṟappuṟa naṭantu vantatu ularttip pakkuvapṭuttappaṭṭa ‘mīṉ uṇaṅkal’ veḷi nāṭukaṭku aṉuppappaṭṭa ceyti maturic kāñci mutaliya toṉṉūlkaḷāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṟatu.

﻿14. camayanili

cāṉṟukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceytu cuṭappeṟṟa ciṟiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum muttirikaḷum ulōkat takaṭukaḷum tāyittukaḷum nirampak kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mūlam akkālattavar tam iṟivaṇakkattiṟakuriya poruḷkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉavi eṉpati oruvāṟu aṟital kūṭum.perumpālum ovvoru vīṭṭilum kaṭavuḷ uruvam vikkap peṟṟu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟatu eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭavuḷ uruvam vīṭṭuc cuvaril irunta māṭaṅkaḷilēṉum cuvaril marappalaki aṭittu ataṉ mītēṉum vikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār.

tarip peṇ vaṇakkam

cintu veḷiyiṟ peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ mikap palavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi pōṉṟavi palucistāṉam mutal kirīs variyil uḷḷa ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, paṇṭi makkaḷ, tamakku vēṇṭiya ellā uṇavup poruḷkaḷiyum utavi vanta tariyip peṇ teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.[1] ittarip peṇ teyvam piṟa nāṭukaḷil piṟa kaṭavuḷaruṭaṉ cērttē vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. atteyvattiṟkuk kaṇavaṉ, makaṉ iraṇṭu teyvaṅkaḷ cērkkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. āṉāl, cintu veḷiyil tarip peṇ tēvati taṉiyākavē vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu eṉṉalām. at teyvam, amitiyāṉa mukat tōṟṟaṅ koṇṭatākavum, accamūṭṭum tōṟṟamuṭiyatākavum, kuḻantiyi vittiruppatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟaḷavum civaperumāṉ maṉiviyārāṉa umiyammiyār pārvati, turkki, kāḷi eṉap pala uruvaṅkaḷil vaḻipaṭappaṭutaliyum, piṉ iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷum accamūṭṭuvaṉavāka iruttaliyum cintu veḷip peṇ teyva uruvaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭuk kāṇa, tarippeṇ vaṇakkam nāḷaṭivil cakti vaṇakkamāka māṟiyateṉpati naṉku uṇaralām.[2]

iṉṟu kirāmantōṟum kāṇappaṭum kirāma tēvatikaḷ aṉittum tarip peṇṇiṉ piratinitikaḷē eṉṉal poruntum. itteyvaṅkaḷ aṉittum kaṉṉit taṉmi vāyntavi ākum. ivaṟṟuṭ pala piṟaku ekkāraṇam koṇṭō, āṇ teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. appoḻutu tāṉ ‘cakti vaṇakkam’ ēṟpaṭṭatu. tarip peṇ vaṇakkam iṉṟaḷavum koṇṭarkaḷiṭam iruttalum, avarkaḷ nilamakaḷi (pūmi tēviyi)t tarip peṇ[3] eṉṟē kūṟutalum kāṇa, itu vaṭa intiyāvil irunta paḻan tamiḻar vaḻipāṭu eṉpati naṉkuṇaralām. tarip peṇ nalla viḷiccali nalkun teyvam eṉpatu koṇṭar koḷki. akkoḷkiyē cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭattum iruntateṉpatu veḷiyām.

kaviṉ peṟu kaṟpaṉi

tariyit tāyāka uruvakappaṭutti, atu namakkāka aḷikkum poruḷkaḷik karuvuyirppatākak kāṭṭum cittiram oṉṟu cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭitta muttiri oṉṟil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. oru peṇ karuvuyirkkum niliyil talikīḻāy niṟkiṟāḷ; avaḷatu karuviliruntu ceṭi oṉṟu veḷippaṭukiṟatu. kuptar kālattiya ittakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ aikkiya maṇṭilattil uḷḷa ‘pītā’vil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.

narapali uṇṭā?

oru muttiriyil oruvaṉ vāḷēnti niṟpatupōlavum avaṉukku aṭiyil oru peṇ muḻaṅkāṟ paṭiyiṭṭu iruppatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭutali nōkki, ārāycciyāḷar, akkālattil tarip peṇṇukku narapali koṭukkum vaḻakkam iruntatō eṉṟu aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, ivvārāyccikku muṉṉarē aṟiñar palar, paṇṭi intiyāvil narapali iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tam ārāycci nūlkaḷil varintuḷḷaṉar.[4] itē paḻakkam āsṭrēliya iṉattavarāṉa koṇṭarkaḷiṭamum 80 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ vari iruntu vantatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. vēṟu oru muttiriyil, iṟivi muṉ oruvaṉ veḷḷāṭu oṉṟip piṭittukkoṇṭu niṟkiṟāṉ; palar variciyāka vaḻipaṭa niṟkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, akkāla makkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷip paliyiṭum paḻakkamuṭiyavr eṉpatu pulaṉākum.

taliyil vicirip pāki

tarip peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ aṉittum nakikaḷ nirampap pūṇṭaviyākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ talimītu viciṟi pōṉṟa pākikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ariyil mikac ciṟiya tuṇi cuṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

civa vaṇakkam

cintu veḷi makkaḷ liṅka vaṇakkattiṉar eṉpati, aṅkuk kiṭitta pala liṅkaṅkaḷik koṇṭu aḻuttamākak kūṟalām. iṭakkālit tūkki āṭum naṭarācaratu uruvam kiṭittuḷḷatu. oru muttiriyil, mūṉṟumukaṅkaḷik koṇṭamaṉita uruvam oṉṟu, kālkaḷi maṭakki iru kutikālkaḷum oṉṟu cērntu kāṟ peru viralkaḷ kīḻnōkki uḷḷavāṟu ācaṉamiṭṭu yōkattil amarntiruppatupōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mārpil mukkōṇa vaṭivap patakkaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kikaḷ niṟiyak kaṭakaṅkaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭuppil iraṭṭip paṭṭiyāka or arik kacci kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvuruvattiṉ valappuṟam yāṉiyum puliyum, iṭappuṟam erutum kāṇṭāmirukamum niṟkiṉṟaṉa. irukkiyiṉ aṭiyil iraṇṭu māṉkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.[5] yōkiyiṉ talimītu vaḷinta erumik kompukaḷ uḷa. naṭuvil taṭitta malark kottō ilik kottō nimirtti vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘itu civaṉik kuṟippatu’ eṉṟu car jāṉ mārṣal karutukiṟār. talimītuḷḷa kompukaḷum pūṅkottum, piṟkālattil tiricūlamāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. oru cārār ‘ik kompukaḷ cāñci stūpattiṉ vāyilil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa cūlattip pōṉṟuḷḷaṉa. yōkiyi aṭuttu vilaṅkukaḷ iruppatu civaṉ-pacupati eṉpatik kuṟikkiṉṟataṉṟō? mūṉṟu talikaḷ naṉku terivatāl, piṉpuṟam iraṇṭu talikaḷ iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu oru cārār, ‘mūṉṟu talikaḷum ākkal, aḷittal, aḻittal eṉṉum muttoḻilkaḷik kuṟippaṉa’ eṉpar. itupōṉṟa muttali uruvaṅkaḷ ‘āpu’ malikkarukil aḻintu kiṭakkum kōyilkaḷilum, vaṭa āṟkāṭu jillāvil kāvērip pākkatti aṭutta mēlic cēriyilum, pelkām kōṭṭattil uḷḷa kōkāk’ nīr vīḻccikku arukilum, utayapuri camastāṉattic cērnta ‘cittōrkār’ eṉṉum iṭattilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. āyiṉ, ivi varalāṟṟuk kālattil vaḻakkāṟu aṟṟuppōyiṉa.

liṅka vaḻipāṭu

periya liṅkaṅkaḷum ciṟiya liṅkaṅkaḷum mikap palavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil maṭṭum 600kku mēṟpaṭṭavi kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭi makkaḷ ciṟiya liṅkaṅkaḷit tāyittukaḷ pōlak kaḻuttilō kiyilō kaṭṭiyiruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum:[6] iviyaṉṟi muttirikaḷum, cempu villikaḷ pōṉṟaṉavum aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa.

puttar perumāṉā? kaṇṇapirāṉā?

muttiri oṉṟil, or uruvam uṭkārntiruppatu pōlavum itaṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷilum variciyāka makkaḷ niṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvatu pōlavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paktartam talimītu paṭam - virittāṭum pāmpu pōṉṟa taliyaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immuttiriyil uḷḷa kāṭci, ‘cāñci, parhat’ eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil puttar perumāṉi nāka vamcattu aracarkaḷ vaṇaṅkutal pōlak kāṇappaṭum oviyaṅkaḷi niṉippūṭṭukiṟatu.[7] kaṇṇapirāṉ, kaliyaṉ eṉṉum nāka aracaṉi veṉṟa piṉ, avaṉum avaṉic cērntavarum kaṇṇaṉi vaḻipaṭum kāṭciyi immuttirikaḷil uḷḷa cittiram kuṟikkiṟatu.[8]

kompuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ

pacupatiyiṉ taliyil kāṇappaṭṭa kompukaḷ pōla vēṟupala uruvaṅkaḷiṉ talikaḷil kompukaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēta kālattilum tāca, vētāḷam, cuṣṉē mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷ kompuṭiyaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. aracar purōkitar mutaliya uyarnili makkaḷ tam mēmpāṭu tōṉṟak kompukaḷiyuṭiya kavacaṅkaḷit taliyil aṇital paṇṭi marapu. cumēriyāvilum pāpilōṉiyāvilum ittakiya kavacaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. ittakiya kavacaṅkaḷ cintu veḷiyilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.[9]

nāṉku kit teyvaṅkaḷ

cintu veḷiyil nāṟki uruva oviyaṅkaḷ cila citintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ‘ittakiya nāṟki maṉita uruvaṅkaḷē, piṟkālattil āriyar pirammā, viṣṇu, ruttirarkaḷi nāṟkiyiṉarākak kuṟikkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa ākalām’ eṉṟu ār.pi. caṇṭā kūṟukiṟār.

camaṇa camayamum paṇṭiyatō?

cila muttirikaḷil aṟuvar niṉṟu yōkam ceytal pōlat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oviyaṅkaḷi nōkkic caṇṭā eṉpavar, “ivi camaṇa yōkikaḷippōla irukkiṉṟaṉa; vaṭamaturip poruṭkāṭciccāliyil uḷḷa ki.pi.iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta camaṇa tēvar cilikaḷi ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. niṟkum niliyil yōkam purital camaṇarkkē ciṟappāṉatu. erutu camaṇaratu aṭiyāḷakkuṟi ākum. cila muttirikaḷil yōkiyiṉ muṉpuṟam erutu iruttal kuṟippiṭat takkatu. ākavē, ivvuruvam riṣapa tēvarik kuṟippatākalām. aḵtāyiṉ, civa camayam pōlavē camaṇa camayamum mikavum paḻiya camayamākalām” eṉpar. vēṟu cilar, “yōkiyiṉ uruvam civaṉik kuṟippatu; erutu. nantiyik kuṟippatu”, eṉpar.

nanti vaḻipāṭu

cintuveḷi vilaṅku vaṇakkattil mutaliṭam peṟṟatu . erutē ākum. yōkikku muṉ timil parutta erutu niṟpatu pōlavum paṭuttiruppatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. oru kōvilukkuḷ uḷḷa ciṟu kōvil muṉ (mūlastāṉattiṉ muṉ) nanti niṟpatu oru muttiriyiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cintu veḷi makkaḷ vāṇipattiṟ pērutavi purintu vantatu erutē ākum. ātaliṉ, nāḷaṭivil atu vaṇakkattiṟku uriya vilaṅkāka māṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. nanti vaṇakkam mutalil taṉiyē iruntu, piṉṉarc civa vaṇakkattuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.

oṟṟik kompu erutu

erutukaḷil iruvakiyiṉa muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. oruvakiyiṉa timil parutta erutukaḷ, maṟṟoru vakiyiṉa timiliṉṟit taliyil oṟṟik kompuṭiyaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉarp piṉṉiya kiḷikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya maram oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. oṟṟik kompuṭiya erutukaḷ vaṇakkattiṉ poruṭṭuk kaṟpikkappaṭṭavi eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu ciṟappuṭiyatāka irukkalām eṉṟum aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

āṟu tali vilaṅku; katiravak kaṭavuḷ

oru muttiriyil, orē uṭalil āṟu vevvēṟu vilaṅkut talikaḷ tōṉṟi iruppatu pōṉṟa uruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. inta āṟu talikaḷuḷ mūṉṟu erutiṉ talikaḷ ākum. oṉṟu pulittali, maṟṟa iraṇṭu citintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi āṟum vaṭṭamākak katiravaṉ katirkaḷi ottirukkumāṟu oṭṭa vikkappaṭṭu irukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoru tāyittil or erututtali āṟu katirkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭuvatu katiravaṉik kuṟippatākum eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

kalappu uruvaṅkaḷ

cintu veḷi muttirikaḷil maṉita vilaṅkukaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōr uruvam maṉitat taliyuṭaṉum yāṉi uṭaluṭaṉum erutiṉ kompukaḷuṭaṉum puliyiṉ kālkaḷuṭaṉum vāluṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvuruvam, pala teyvaṅkaḷi - orē vilaṅku uruvamākac cērttuk kāṭṭac ceyyappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum. erutuk kompukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya maṉita uruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. immaṉitavuruviṉ kālkaḷ erutiṉ kālkaḷ pōl uḷa. piṉpuṟam erutiṉ vāl pōṉṟu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. immaṉita uruvam oṟṟik kompuṭiya puliyōṭu pōr purivatākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakiya vinōta uruvaṅkaḷ cumēriyāvil nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; immaṉitar ‘teyva cakti vāyntavar eṉpatiyum, ivar aṭikkaṭi kaṭavuḷarākak karutappaṭṭavaruṭaṉ pōr toṭuttuk koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum ivi terivikkiṉṟaṉapōlum! maṉitattali, ari uruvam āṭu, pāti uruvam erutu, pāti uruvam yāṉi pōṉṟa maṉita vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ pala muttirikaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru maṉitaṉ pulikaḷōṭu pōr purivatu cila muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ekiptilum cumēriyāvilum ciṅkaṅkaḷōṭu pōr purivatu kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. oru muttiriyil peṇṇum puliyum cērnta uruvam oṉṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu. puliyi orutēvatiyākak kuṟippatu pōlum[10] peṇṇuruvattiṉ mutukil taliyaṟṟa puliyuṭal poruttap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ittakiya uruvam tēvatiyik kuṟippatu eṉṟē aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

piṟa vilaṅkukaḷ

cintuveḷi makkaḷ nīṇṭa kompukaḷum parutta timilkaḷum uṭiya eruti vaṇaṅkiyatōṭu erumi, kāṭṭu erumi, yāṉi, vēṅki, karaṭi, māṉ, kāṇṭāmirukam, muyal, veḷḷiāṭu mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiyum vēṟu teyvaṅkaṭkup piratinitikaḷākavō aṭiyāḷamākavō eṇṇi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. harappāvil kiṭitta muttiri oṉṟil, oru vilaṅku uṭkārntirukkiṟatu; ataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟu or āṭavaṉ kūppiya karaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ataṉi vaḻipaṭukiṟāṉ. niṟka, cintuveḷi makkaḷ veḷḷāṭṭuk kompic ciṟappuṭiyatākak karutiṉar pōlum! āṇ teyvat tali mītum pulit tēvatikaḷ talimītum veḷḷāṭṭuk kompu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

nāka vaṇakkam

cintuveḷi nākarika kālattiliruntu iṉṟaḷavum nāka vaṇakkam iruntu varukiṟatu. kaḷimaṇ tāyittu oṉṟil, mukkāli oṉṟaṉmītu pāl kiṇṇam irukkiṟatu; ataṉ etiril nākam oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. ituvaṉṟic cila muttirikaḷil kompu muḷitta pāmpukaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

puṟā vaṇakkam

maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa puṟākkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi mecapoṭṭēmiyāvil kiṭittavaṟṟiyē peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. tarippeṇ paṭivattiṉ talimītu iraṇṭu puṟākkaḷ niṟpatupōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iccilikaḷ ekiptilum kirīṭtīvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, paṇṭi nākarika nāṭukaḷil ellām tarippeṇtēvatiyuṭaṉ puṟākkaḷ iṇikkap peṟṟu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu.

karuṭa vaṇakkam

tāyittu oṉṟil karuṭap paṟavi paṟappatu pōlap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ kālkaḷ maṉitar kālkaḷ pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuruvattik koṇṭa tāyittukaḷ moheñco-tarōvilum, harappāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.[11]

mara vaṇakkam

cintuveḷi makkaḷ maraṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟil uṟiyum teyvaṅkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu moheñco. tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭitta cila muttirikaḷiliruntum villikaḷiliruntum terikiṟatu. ippaḻakkam tamiḻakattil iṉṟum peruvaḻakku uṭiyatākum. araca maramē ciṟappuṭiya vaṇakkattiṟku uriyatu. āṉāl, aḵtu erutuṭaṉ iṇikkap paṭṭē muttirikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. cila muttirikaḷil vēppamaram kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻ makkaḷ araca, vēppa maraṅkaḷic cuṟṟutaliyum iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷiyum ciṟappuṭi yaṉavākak karututaliyum iṉṟaḷavum kāṇalām. marattaṭiyil mēṭiyiṭṭiruttalum akkāla muṟi eṉpatu cila tāyittukaḷiliruntu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṟatu. marattaṭiyil erumi oṉṟu niṟkiṟatu. ataṉ kompukaḷāl tākkuṇṭa oruvaṉ uṭpuṟam vīḻntu kiṭakkiṟāṉ. iṅṅaṉam erumikaḷōṭu viḷiyāṭal paṇṭi vaḻakkam ivvaḻakkam kirīṭ tīvilum iruntatām.

mara tēvatikaḷ

cila muttirikaḷil uḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷ vinōtamākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru marattiṉ iraṇṭu kiḷikaṭku iṭiyil āṭiyiṉṟip peṇ uruvam oṉṟu niṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉ kūntal aviḻntu toṅkukiṟatu. marattaṭiyil āṭiyaṟṟa maṟṟoru peṇṇuruvam maṇṭiyiṭṭuvaṇṅkukiṟatu. marattiṉmītu niṟkum peṇṇuruvattiṉ taliyil iraṇṭu kompukaḷum avaṟṟukku iṭiyil malarkkottum irukkiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kikaḷ nirampa vaḷiyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuruvaṅkaṭku etiril ciṟiya kuṭṭip pāvāṭikaḷ kaṭṭiya peṇṇuruvaṅkaḷ ēḻu variciyāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa. maṇṭiyiṭṭu vaṇaṅkum peṇṇuruviṟkup piṉpuṟam maṉitattaliyuṭaṉ ariyuruvam āṭākavum maṟupāti erutākavum uḷḷa vilaṅkuruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu, mara tēvatiyiṉ ūrtiyāka irukkalām

oru marattiṟku muṉṉar erutiṉ kompukaḷiyuṭiya puli oṉṟi, vāl - erutiṉ kālkaḷ - erutiṉ tali - maṉita uṭal ivi koṇṭa vinōta vilaṅkoṉṟu etirppatāka uḷḷa tōṟṟam oru muttiriyil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. vēṟoru muttiriyil iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷ mītu iruvar uṭkārntiruppatu pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avartam talikaḷil mara muṭi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅṅaṉam kuṟikkappaṭum palavaki uruvaṅkaḷ mara tēvatikaḷ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ‘maraṅkaḷil maṉita āvikaḷ taṅkutal uṇṭu’ eṉṉum koḷki akkālamakkaṭku uṇṭu pōlum! maratēvatikaḷuṭaṉ pulikaḷē toṭarpu paṭuttappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, mara tēvatikaṭkum pulikaṭkum ētō toṭarpu iruttal vēṇṭum. itaṉāl, mara vaṇakkam akkālattil ciṟappuṭiyatāka iruntatu eṉpatu naṉku veḷiyākiṉṟataṉṟō?

koṇṭar iravil maramacikka añcuvar; maratēvatikaḷ maraṅkaḷil uṟaṅkum, irāk kālattil maram acittalō paḻaṅkaḷip paṟittalō avaṟṟiṟku ākātu eṉpatu avar koḷkimaratti veṭṭupavar. mutalilmarattiṟkup pūci iṭṭa piṉṉarē veṭṭat toṭaṅkutal iṉṟaḷavum uḷḷa paḻakkamākum.cilaceṭikaṭku mantiram kūṟik kāppukkaṭṭiya piṉṉarē ili kaḷiyō, paḻaṅkaḷiyō paṟippatu vaḻakkam. cilar marattiṟku mutalil tāli kaṭṭiya piṟakē peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭutal vaḻakkam.

oru muttiriyil iru uruvaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷi naṭuvataṟku ēnti niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avikaṭku iṭiyil maratēvati uruvam oṉṟu niṟkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam maraṅkaḷi naṭutal tūya ceyal eṉṟu nammavar niṉittal neṭuṅkālap paḻakkamākum.

āṟṟu vaṇakkam

vāyil ciṟu mīṉkaḷi vittuḷḷa mutali, āmi pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷi āṟukaḷuṭaṉ cērttup poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa muttirikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl akkāla makkaḷ āṟṟu vaṇakkam[12] ceytu vantaṉar eṉpati naṉku aṟiyalām.

pali iṭum paḻakkam

cintuveḷi makkaḷ mara tēvatikaṭkum piṟa kaṭavuḷarkkum veḷḷāṭu, erumi mutaliyavaṟṟip pali yiṭṭaṉar. cintu veḷiyiṟ perumpālum veḷḷāṭē paliyiṭap paṭṭatām.[13]

tāyittu aṇital

maṇ tāyittukkaḷē makkaḷāl peritum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. avi tēyntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāyittukkaḷil kāṇappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷ camaya campantamāṉa pala nikaḻccikaḷik kūṟuvaṉavākalām. aṇil, cemmaṟiyāṭu, muyal, māṉ, nāy ākiya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ - kal, pīṅkāṉ, veṇkalam, cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ villikaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭu mēlē tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi kayiṟu koṇṭu kaṭṭik koḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. puṟāvaṭivilāṉa tāyittukkaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. talippil muṭiyiṭappeṟṟa cippiyālāṉa tāyittu oṉṟu mantira cakti uṭiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. iṅṅaṉam muṭiyiṭutal mantira vaṉmi uṭiyateṉṟu mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷilum appaḻaṅkālattil karutappaṭṭatām. attāyittiṉ naṭuvil iraṇṭu ciṟiya tuḷikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, atu pottāṉākavō tāyittākavō payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. cila tāyittukkaḷil aṭi-muṭi aṟṟa varivaṭivaṅkaḷ cumēriyar muttirikaḷi ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṇikaḷit tāyittuk kaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkamum cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭi iruntatu. ippaḻakkam mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷilum iruntatākum.

cila tāyittukkaḷil svastikā kirēkkac ciluvi pōṉṟa kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi iraṇṭum ēlam, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil irunta paṇṭik kuṟiyīṭukaḷē ākum. svastikā’ kuṟi yōkak kuṟiyāka innāṭṭil iṉṟaḷavum karutappaṭukiṟatu. ikkuṟikaḷ cintu veḷi illac cuvarkaḷ mītum iruntaṉa.

camayap patakkam

camaya campantamāṉa patakkaṅkaḷum paṇṭikkālac cintuveḷi makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa. vaṭṭappiḷavu pōṉṟa oru patakkattil, oṟṟik kompuṭiya. vilaṅkoṉṟu niṟkiṟatu. ataṉ etiril palipīṭam oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. piṟiyiṉ muṉikaḷ muṭiyum iṭattil nīḷ caturavaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ituvum vilaṅku vaṭivamum naṭuvil kuṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ tuḷikaḷil civappuc cāntu pūcappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. ap patakkam ulōka vaḷiyattil poruttap peṟṟuk kōvil atikārikaḷāl makkaṭku aḷikkappaṭum ciṟappuṭip poruḷāka iruntiruttal kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmi uṇarntavar yāvar?

kaṭavuḷ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ūrvalam

camayak kuṟikaḷ eṉak karutappaṭum erutu mutaliya vilaṅku kaḷi makkaḷ tūkkic celvatu iraṇṭu muttirikaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. oruvaṉ oru kampattil erutu uruvatti vittut tukkic celkiṟāṉ. avaṉ piṉṉar, vēṟu oruvaṉ palipīṭam allatu toṭṭi pōṉṟa oṉṟit tūkkiccelkiṟāṉ.mūṉṟām maṉitaṉ vēṟu or uruvattic cumantu celkiṟāṉ. ivvūrvalak kāṭci, iṉṟik kōvil viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil tūkki varappaṭum nanti vāhaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi niṉippūṭṭukiṉṟatu aṉṟō?



nērttik kaṭaṉ

karuppavatikaḷik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ, kik kuḻanti ēnti niṟkum tāyik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ, tavaḻntu cellum kuḻantikaḷik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum vilaṅkup patumikaḷum nērttik kaṭaṉ poruṭṭuk kōvilkaḷil vikkappeṟṟaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ip paḻakkam iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varukiṉṟatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. iṅṅaṉam celuttappaṭṭa nērttikkaṭaṉ poruḷkaḷ ellāk kōvilkaḷilum iruttalik kāṇalām.

iciyum naṭaṉamum

camayat toṭarpuṭiya kaḷimaṇ tāyittu oṉṟil cila uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oruvaṉ mattaḷam aṭikkiṟāṉ; cilar avvōcik kēṟpa naṭikkiṉṟaṉar. tāyittuc camayat toṭarpuṭiyatāka iruttaliṉ, atil kuṟikkappaṭṭa naṭaṉamum camayat toṭarpuṭiyatāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu poruttamē aṉṟō? harappāvil kiṭitta tāyittu oṉṟil, puli muṉ oruvaṉ mattaḷam aṭippatu pōlap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēṟoru muttiriyil erutiṉ muṉpu maṅki orutti kūttāṭutal kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nāṭṭiya makaḷik kuṟikkum veṇkalap paṭivam oṉṟu harappāvil kiṭittatu. ataṉ vēlip pāṭṭik kaṇṭu viyavāta ārāycciyāḷar illi. accili kōyil naṭaṉamātiṉ ciliyāka irukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[14]

paṭittal paḻakkam

kaṭavuḷar uruvaṅkaḷiṉ muṉ uṇavupporuḷkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum paṭittal akkāla makkaḷiṉ paḻakkamāka iruntatu eṉpatu cila tāyittukkaḷ koṇṭum muttirikaḷ koṇṭum kūṟalām; taṇṭiṉmītu poruttappeṟṟa taṭṭukaḷ (Offering Stands) pala kiṭittami koṇṭum kūṟalām; oru muttiriyil peṇkaḷ palar paṭikkut taṭṭukaḷi ēnti niṟpatākac cittiram tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[15] ‘harappāvil kōvil aṇikaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa; avi caṅku, kaḷimaṇ, kal, uyartarak kal mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyyappeṟṟavi. avi kōvilkaḷukku uriyaṉavākalām eṉṟu em.es.vāṭs eṉṉum aṟiñar aṟikiṉṟār.[16]

kōvil vaḻipāṭu

moheñco-tarōvil peḷatta stūpiyuḷḷa iṭattiṉ aṭiyil - iṉṟaḷavum tōṇṭip pārāta iṭattil cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ kōvil irukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ‘pala kōvilkaḷ aḻiyum poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu kaṭṭappeṟṟamiyāl, aḻintu viṭṭiruttal iyalpu. avi iṉṟu iṉmikoṇṭē kōvilkaḷ iruntila eṉak kūṟal iyalātu’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē pōṉṟa aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. uyaramāṉa kōvil kaḷik kaṭṭi vāḻnta cumēriyarōṭu vāṇipat toṭarpum piṟa pala tuṟikaḷil toṭarpum koṇṭirunta cintu veḷi makkaḷ kōvilkaḷ iṉṟi vāḻntaṉar eṉpatu poruntāk kūṟṟākum. eṉavē, kōvilkaḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttākum.

cintu veḷic camayam yātu?

cintu veḷi makkaḷ, (1) tarippeṇ vaṇakkam (2) liṅka-yōṉi vaṇakkam (3) civa vaṇakkam (4) nanti vaṇakkam (5) cūriya vaṇakkam (6) vilaṅku vaṇakkam (7) paṟavi vaṇakkam (8) nāka vaṇakkam (9) mara vaṇakkam (10) āṟṟu vaṇakkam (11) ciṟu teyva vaṇakkam (12) vinōta-teyva vaṇakkam (13) kaṇṇaṉ vaṇakkam ivaṟṟi uṭiyavar eṉṟu oruvāṟu kūṟalām; mēlum avarkaḷ, (1) camayat toṭarpāṉa tāyittukkaḷiyum muttirikaḷiyum patakkaṅkaḷiyum aṇintu vantārkaḷ, (2) paliyiṭṭu vantārkaḷ, (3) palavakip poruḷkaḷip paṭittu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ, (4) kaṭavuḷukku viḻākkaḷi naṭatti vantārkaḷ, (5) nērttik kaṭaṉ celutti vantārkaḷ, (6) camayat toṭarpāka iciyum naṭaṉamum vaḷarttārkaḷ, (7) mantira māyaṅkaḷil paḻaki iruntārkaḷ, (8) yōkap payiṟciyi naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟi oruvāṟu uṇaralām.

curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, iṉṟu hintu matattil uḷḷa perumpālāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum cintu veḷi makkaḷuṭiya camayattaṉavē eṉak kūṟi muṭittal tavaṟākātu.

civattiṉ paḻimi

ippala vaki vaḻipāṭukaḷ iṉṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, ivi aṉittiyum. ikkāla hintukkaḷ aṉivarum piṉpaṟṟutal illi aṉṟō? uyarntavar civaliṅkam, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya perun teyvaṅkaḷiyē vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. kirāmattavarum pāmara makkaḷumē maravaṇakkam mutaliyavaṟṟik kikkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē, appaṇṭik kālac cintu veḷi makkaḷuḷ kalvi kēḷvikaḷāl uyarnta perumakkaḷ peruṅ kaṭavuḷari vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar? tāḻnta niliyil iruntavarkaḷ palavakit teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉarātal vēṇṭum. iḵtu eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, ivvārāycciyāl aṟiyappaṭum uṇmi oṉṟuṇṭu. aḵtāvatu, civa camayam cintuveḷi nākarikattukkum (ki.mu.4000kku) muṟpaṭṭa paḻimiyuṭiyatu eṉpatē ākum. aḵtu iṉṉum evvaḷavu paḻimiyuṭiya teṉpati variyaṟukkakkūṭavilli. accamayam ulakattilēyē mikka paḻimi vāyntu, iṉṟaḷavum iruntuvarum camayam eṉpati maṟattal ākātu.[17]

↑ P.Mitra’s ‘Pre-historic India p 275. “māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟi yulakamum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. mulli nila makkaḷ kaṇṇaṉit teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. ivvaḻipāṭu putiya kaṟkāla kālattiliruntē paṇṭi makkaḷāl koḷḷappaṭṭatākum. pārata kālattuk kiruṣṇaṉukku muṉṉarē vēta kālattil yamuṉik kariyil āriyari etirtta kiruṣṇaṉ (kaṟuppaṉ) oruvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉic cērnta vīrar pallāyiravar āvar. kiruṣṇaṉukku jaṉma virōti āriyar kaṭavuḷāṉa intiraṉ. ivviruvar pakimiyē piṟkāla purāṇaṅkaḷil pala katikaḷiṉ vaṭivil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kiruṣṇaṉ āriyak kaṭavuḷ allaṉ, avaṉ vētamoḻikaḷiṭam veṟuppuk koṇṭavaṉ. vētattiṉ malarnta vārttikaṭku iṭaṅ koṭātē’ eṉṟu arccuṉaṉi eccarittal pakavat kitiyiṟ kāṇka” - PT.Srinivasa Iyengar’s ‘Stone Age in India’, p. 49-51.

↑ viyākrapātamuṉivar uruvam iṅkuc cintikkaṟ pālatu. āsṭrēliya iṉattavarāṉa koṇṭar, iṟantavaṉippaṟṟic ceyyum iraṇṭām nāṭ caṭaṅkil, avaṉi eritta iṭattil paṭiyal iṭṭu avaṉatu āviyi viḷittu. ‘nī picācākavō puliyākavō vantu eṅkaḷit tuṉpuṟuttalākātu’ eṉṟu vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟum vaḻakkam eṉpati aṟika. mēlum akkoṇṭarkaḷ. tām tam pakivari aḻikkum poruṭṭup puliyākavō pāmpāvō urumāṟum āṟṟal peṟṟavar eṉpati nampukiṉṟaṉar; irākkālaṅkaḷil tūṅkumpoḻutu āvi veḷic ceṉṟu cuṟṟuvatāka nampukiṉṟaṉar; atu puliyuruvil alivatāka nampukiṉṟaṉar; ciṟuttippuvi teyvattaṉmi yuṭiyateṉpati nampukiṉṟaṉar; pulittōl mītu ki aṭittuc cattiyam ceykiṉṟaṉar. ‘nāṉ ikkuṟṟam ceytavaṉāyiṉ. puliyāl kollap paṭuvēṉ ākuka’ eṉac cūḷ urikkiṉṟaṉar. kōtāvari jillāvil uḷḷa kōyikaḷ eṉṉum malivāṇar tam tēvarāṭṭi iravil pulimītu ēṟikkoṇṭu ēḻu kirāmaṅkaḷic cuṟṟuvatāka nampappaṭukiṟāḷ. mēlum, “avaḷ iravil puli vēṭaṅ koḷvāḷ; oru kāl maṉitar kālāka irukkum. anniliyil avaḷ ‘maruṭpuli’ eṉappaṭuvāḷ; iravil taṉiyē varum āḷmītu pāyntu accuṟuttikkolvāṉ” eṉṟu avaḷippaṟṟik kōyikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. -'Thurston’s ‘Castes and Tribes of S.India’, Vol.III pp.395, 405, 406, 407; and Vol. IV, pp. 69, 70. ivvivaraṅkaḷi nōkkac cintuveḷimakkaḷ puliyi vaṇaṅki vantamikkuriya karuttit teṟṟeṉat teḷiyalām aṉṟō?

↑ ikkaruṭavaṇakkam piṟkālattil tirumāl vaṇakkattōṭu iṇippuṇṭu viṭṭatu.eṉiṉum, hintukkaḷ karuṭaṉik kāṇumpoḻu tellām vaṇaṅkutali iṉṟum vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.

↑ āṟṟil putuveḷḷam varumpoḻutu āṇṭukkoru muṟi āṟṟi vaṇaṅkutal paṇṭit tamiḻar marapu eṉpatip paripāṭalāl aṟika.

↑ murukapperumāṉukku āṭṭippaliyiṭal paṇṭit tamiḻar marapu. itaṉit tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyiṟ kāṇka, kāḷi tēvikku erumip pali iṭutal iṉṟum kāṇalām.

P. vii.





* * *





﻿15. iṭutalum cuṭutalum

cuṭutal

cintuveḷi makkaḷ iṟantāri eṉ ceytaṉar eṉpatu moheñco-tarō ārāycciyi maṭṭuṅ koṇṭu tiṭṭamākak kūṟutaṟkilli. harappā ārāycciyiyum uḷaṅ koṇṭu nōkkiṉ, cila vivaraṅkaḷi aṟiyalām. eṉiṉum, it tuṟikkuriya ārāyccip poruḷkaḷ ekiptilum cumēriyāvilum kiṭittaṉa pōlap pēraḷavu cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭittila. moheñco-tarōvil camātiyō, iṭukāṭō kiṭittila, āyiṉ, cantukaḷil, vīṭukaṭku aṭiyil nīr aruntum kuvaḷikaṭkuḷ irunta eritta cāmpalum elumput tuṇṭukaḷum ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi nōkka, cintu veḷi makkaḷ iṟantār uṭali erittuc cāmpaliyum elumpukaḷiyum cēmittup putittu vantaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. ippaḻakkam iṉṟum hintukkaḷiṭi iruntu varuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

iṭutal

harappāvil kiṭitta cavak kuḻikaḷil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu aṭukkukaḷ uṇṭu. mikavum aṭiyil irunta cavakkuḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷi nērākap paṭukkavittuk kikaḷi mārpiṉ mītu maṭakkivittup putittu vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. nilattiṟkaṭiyil putikkappaṭṭa uṭalkaḷ mūṉṟu vakiyāka aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; (1) cila uṭalkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē iṭam viṭṭup putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; (2) cila nerukkamākap putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; (3) cila oṉṟiṉkīḻ oṉṟākap putikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu piṇaṅkaḷip putikkum paḻakkam palucistāṉattilum peruvaḻakkuṭiyatāka iruntatu. avvuṭalkaḷiṉ arukil nīruṭiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, kōppikaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, iṟantār payaṉpaṭuttiya piṟa poruḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa putikkappaṭṭaṉa. ippaḻakkam cumēriyāvilum ekiptilum ciṟappāka iruntu vantatu. itē paḻakkam, putukkōṭṭi, āticcanallūr mutaliya tamiḻnāṭṭup pakutikaḷiṟ kiṭitta tāḻikaḷik koṇṭu, tamiḻnāṭṭilum iruntateṉat tuṇintu kūṟalām.

īrāṉiyar paḻakkam

mēl aṭukkukaḷiṟ kiṭitta cavak kuḻiyil taṉittaṉi uṭaṟpakutikaḷum elumpukaḷum putikkappaṭṭaṉa pōlum! iṅṅaṉam uṭaṟpakutikaḷip putittuvantavar muṉ collap paṭṭavariṉum vēṟāṉavarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṟpakutikaḷ putikkappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ harappāvil nūṟṟukku mēlākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟil uḷḷa maṇṭi oṭukaḷiyum piṟa elumpukaḷiyum kāṇkiyil, uṭaṟ pakutikaḷik kaḻuku, nari mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup pōṭṭuc catippakutikaḷ tiṉṉappaṭṭapiṟaku, elumpukaḷi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil iṭṭup putittu vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ippaḻakkam pāracīkariṭamē celvākkup peṟṟatātaliṉ, īrāṉiyar harappāvil kuṭiyēṟi iruntaṉar; annakara makkaḷuḷ īrāṉiyarum oru pakutiyiṉarāka iruntaṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām.

tāḻikaḷ mītu oviyaṅkaḷ

ittāḻikaḷ ellām oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. ovvoṉṟilum vitavitamāṉa oviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru tāḻiyiṉ kaḻuttarukil karuṭap paṟavikaḷ[1] iraṇṭu varicikaḷil paṟappaṉa pōlat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvaricikaṭku iṭiyē ilikkottuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum toṭṭikaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṟa tāḻikaḷ mītu, cempaṭavaṉ vali pōṭṭu mīṉ piṭippatu pōlavum, mīṉ āmi paṟavikaḷ. ilikaḷ mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷip pōlavum cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

mayilkaḷ teyvikattaṉmi peṟṟaviyā?

akkāla makkaḷ mayilkaḷit teyvikac ciṟappuṭiya paṟavikaḷāka matittaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. immayilkaḷiṉ tuṇiyik koṇṭu maṉitar āvikaḷ mēl ulakam pōvaṉa eṉpati akkālattavar eṇṇi vantaṉar pōlum! aṉṟic cūkkum uṭalē mayilāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatō aṟiyōm. oru tāḻi mītuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷil mūṉṟu mayilkaḷ paṟappatu pōlat tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ammayilkaṭku iṭiyiṭiyē viṇmīṉkaḷ variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ, ‘mayilkaḷ cūkkuma uṭalkaḷik kuṟippaṉa: avi mēl ulakatti aṭivatākap paṇṭiyōr eṇṇiṉar’ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.[2] cila tāḻikaḷ mītu ciṟiya mayilkaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka iruppaṉapōla oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṉ mītu aintu mayilkaḷ paṟappaṉa pōlak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷ mītu kāṇappaṭum mayilkaḷiṉ tōki tiricūlam pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. cila mayilkaḷiṉ talimītu iraṇṭu kompukaḷum avaṟṟukku iṭiyil nimirnta malarkkottum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

paṟavi muka maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ

cila tāḻikaḷ mītu paṟavi mukkup pōṉṟa nīṇṭa mūkkukaḷiyuṭiya maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi, mayilkaḷ variyappaṭṭa karuttuk koṇṭē variyappaṭṭaṉavākalām: aḵtāvatu cūkkuma uṭampiccumantu cellum vaṉmi vāyntaviyākalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. oru tāḻimītu paṟavi mukkuṭiya maṉita uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avvuruvam iṭakkiyil ampum villum vittuḷḷatu. ataṉ irupuṟamum iraṇṭu erutukaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avverutukaḷik kayiṟu koṇṭu kaṭṭi, avaṟṟiṉ kayiṟṟi maṉita vuruvam taṉ valakkiyil piṭittuḷḷatu. iṭappuṟamuḷḷa eruti nāyoṉṟu turattivantu vālip piṭittuk kaṭittu iḻukkiṟatu,nāykkup piṉ, talimītu kompu muḷitta mayilkaḷ iraṇṭu paṟakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku arukil periya veḷḷāṭu oṉṟu niṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉ kompukaḷ eṭṭut tiricūlaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷāṭṭiṟkum piṟa uruvaṅkaṭkum iṭiyil ilikaḷum viṇmīṉkaḷum variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam pala uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa cittiram, ‘kālaṉ, uyirik kavarntu celvatik kuṟippatākalām’ eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. paṟavi mukattik koṇṭa maṉita uruvaṅkaḷit tīṭṭum paḻakkam paṇṭik kirēkkariṭamum mecopoṭṭēmiyariṭamum iruntatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.[3]

tāḻikaḷuṭ pala poruḷkaḷ

iṟantavar aṇintirunta aṇikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttiya pāṇṭaṅkaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum uṇavup poruḷkaḷum tāḻikaḷil iruntu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟi ellām iṟantavar cāmpaluṭaṉ tāḻikaḷil iṭṭup putittuviṭal appaṇṭi makkaḷ marapu eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu. tāḻikaḷil cūḷiyiṭap peṟṟa maṇ appaṅkaḷ kiṭittatik kuṟippatākak kūṟalām. pala tāḻikaḷil taṅkanakikaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa: navarattiṉaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. iṅṅaṉam tāḻikaḷil iṟantār aṇikaḷip putittal paṇṭi mēṟkuppuṟa nāṭukaḷilum iruntuvanta paḻakkamē ākum.

uṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam

oru piṇik kuḻiyiṟ kiṭitta muttiri oṉṟil, kaṭṭilmītu maṅki orutti cāyntukoṇṭu iruppatu pōla, uruvam cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl iṟanta āṭavaṉuṭakkuriya maṉivi kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam ak kālattil iruntirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar; ‘iṟantavar aṭutta vāḻkkiyil ippiṟaviyil iruntavāṟē irukka viḻintaṉar; avviḻivāṟṟāṉ maṉiviyum uṭaṉ iṟantavaḷātal vēṇṭum[4] eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

muṭivu

moheñco-tarō, harappā mutaliyaṉa ciṟanta nākarikamum celvap perukkamum uṭiya nakaraṅkaḷ; vāṇipap pukaḻpeṟṟa nakaraṅkaḷ. ātaliṉ, avaṟṟil pala nāṭṭu makkaḷ kuṭipukuntiruntaṉar. avaravar tattam nāṭṭup paḻakkattiṟkēṟpa iṟantār uṭalip putittum, erittum, nāy narikaṭku iṭṭum vantaṉar eṉpati harappāviṟ kiṭitta putikuḻi vivaraṅkaḷāl naṉku aṟiyalām. appalavaki aṭakka muṟikaḷ iṉṟum ceṉṉi, kalkattā, pampāp pōṉṟa vāṇipap perukkamuṭiya nakaraṅkaḷil palanāṭṭu makkaḷ uṟikiṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷil kāṇalām.[5] eṉiṉum, cintuveḷi makkaṭ keṉṟē ciṟappāka irunta paḻakkam iṭutalum cuṭutalumē eṉpati harappāvil kiṭitta muḻuvuṭal puti muṟiyālum (aviyē āḻattil putikkappaṭṭavi). moheñco-tarōvil vīṭukaṭkaṭiyil kiṭitta cāmpal koṇṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷālum naṉkaṟiyalām. mēlum, cintu veḷi makkaḷ maṟupiṟavi uṇarcci uṭiyaṉarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatum aṟiyattakkatu.[6]





* * *





↑ iṉṟum karuṭaṉi vaḻipaṭal kavaṉikkattakkatu. itu paṇṭik kālattiruntu iṉṟaḷavum toṭarntu varum vaḻipāṭākum.

↑ mayilēṟi viḷiyāṭu kukaṉē eṉa varum aṭiyum, mayil murukaṉit tāṅka muṭiyāta paṟaviyāka iruntum, karuṭaṉip pōla ūrtiyākak kūṟappaṭṭataṉ uṭkaruttup paṇṭiyōr nampikkiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatē pōlum! naṭuvīṭṭil āṇmayilippōla oviyam eḻuti ataṉi maṭṭum vaḻi paṭum vaḻakkam iṉṟun tamiḻnāṭṭil uṇṭu. ataṉi mayilēṟu viḻā eṉpar.

↑ M.P.Nelson’s The Minoan Mycenen Religion’, pp.320.321.

↑ 'cātal añcēṉ: añcuval cāvil

piṟappuppiṟi tākuva tāyiṉ

maṟakkuveṉ koleṉ kātalaṉ eṉavē





eṉa varum naṟṟiṇi aṭikaḷum,

‘immi māṟi maṟumi ākiṉum

nīyā kiyareṅ kaṇivaṉi:

yāṉā kiyarniṉ neñcunēr pavaḷē”





eṉa varum kuṟuntoki aṭikaḷum,

‘kātalar iṟappiṉ...’ eṉa varum maṇimēkali aṭikaḷum iṅkuc cintikkaṟpālaṉa.



↑ kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam vāṇikacciṟappuṭiyatu. pala pāṭi makkaḷik koṇṭatu. ātaliṉ, aṅku iṭutal, cuṭutal tāḻiyiṟ kavittal, kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭuk kalli mūṭiviṭal, nāy narikaṭku iriyāka viṭutal mutaliya palavaki muṟikaḷ kik koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati maṇimēkaliyāl aṟika.

↑ ‘No trace of the doctrine of Transmigration is found in the Rig Veda, and yet no other doctrine is so peculiarly Indian. It may have had its origin Non-Aryan animism, but became established among the Aryans quite early’, ... DR.S.K. Chatterji’s Origin and Development of the Bengali Language, Vol. I p.42 eṉpatu īṇṭuc cuvittiṟkuriyatu.





* * *





﻿16. cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ

eḻuttu ārāycciyāḷar

cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭitta muttirikaḷiṟ poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa cittirak kuṟikaḷē cintumakkaḷ moḻikku uriya eḻuttukaḷ ākum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. av veḻuttukaḷi vakipaṭutti, ‘cintu veḷiyiṉ paṇṭi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal’ eṉṟum,[1] paṇṭi intiya eḻuttukaḷiṉ amippu muṟi[2] eṉṟum, ‘cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷ’[3] eṉṟum aṟiñarkaḷ, tām aṟintavari veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. avarkaṭkup piṟaku ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar eṉṉum pēraṟiñar cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷip paṟṟi virivāka ārāyntu, aḻakiya nūl oṉṟi 1934 il veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷār.[4]

paṭikka muṭiyāta eḻuttukaḷ

inta eḻuttukaḷ paṭikka muṭiyāta niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi paṭikkappaṭṭa piṉṉarē cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ uṇmi varalāṟṟi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarakkūṭum. ittakiya eḻuttukaḷ cumēriyāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa: pacipik peruṅ kaṭalil uḷḷa īsṭar tīvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ekiptiyap paṇṭi eḻuttukaḷum, sipras tīvil kiṭitta paḻiya eḻuttukaḷum, lipiyāvil kiṭitta eḻuttukaḷum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭ ṭuṭaṉ peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. ‘ivi aṉittum oru potu eḻuttu muṟiyiliruntu neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉarē pirintaṉavātal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar karutukiṉṟār. ivi itukāṟum paṭikkak kūṭavilli.

eḻuttukaḷip peṟṟuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ

sṭeṭiṭ (Steatite) eṉṉum oruvakik kal mītu cuṇṇam taṭavic cuṭṭu, ataṉmītu eḻuttukaḷum vilaṅku mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷum poṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ikkaṟkaḷālāya poruḷkaḷ caturamākavum nīḷac caturamākavum nīṇṭu uruṇṭa vaṭivamākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. cila muccaturamāka amintuḷḷaṉa. avi cīṭṭukaḷ (iracītukaḷ) eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar karutukiṟār. ēṉiyavaṟṟuṭ cila kayiṟṟiṟ kōttuk kaḻuttil kaṭṭik koḷvaṉapōla amintuḷḷaṉa. cempālāya nīḷ caturat takaṭukaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi nāṇayaṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟil vilaṅku uruvam mēlum, eḻuttukaḷ kīḻumākap ṭoṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi aracartam peyarkaḷāka iruttal kūṭum. iraṇṭoru nīḷ caturak kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷ mītu cila eḻuttukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷik koṇṭa poruḷkaḷ oppantac cīṭṭukaḷāka iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

eḻutum muṟi

ic cintuveḷi makkaḷ, iṅkuk kiṭitta muttirikaḷ, nāṇayaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mītu eḻutiya aḷavōṭu niṉṟu viṭṭaṉar eṉak karututal tavaṟu. avarkaḷ, aḻiyum iyalpiṉavāṉa palvēṟu poruḷkaḷ mītum eḻutivantaṉar eṉak kōṭalē poruttamākum. avarkaḷ tōl, pāpiras, paṭṭu, ivaṟṟiyō, ivaṟṟil oṉṟiyō iraṇṭiyō eḻutap payaṉpaṭutti irukkalām. eḻuttukaḷ mēliruntu kīḻnōkki eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; nērāka amintuḷḷaṉa. inta eḻuttukaḷ akaravarici uṭiyaṉa alla; cittira eḻuttukaḷ pala; olikkuṟippu uṭiyaṉa pala. ivveḻuttukaḷ valam iṭamāka vācikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyavi; cila iṭaṅkaḷil iṭam valamāka vācikkat takkapaṭi amintuḷḷaṉa. ivi palvēṟu kālattu vaḷarcci yuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

eḻuttukaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa :

1. cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷ uccarippik kuṟippaṉa.

2. avi ovikkuṟippiyum uṇarvuk kuṟippiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi.

3. anta aṭippaṭi ki.mu.3000kku muṟpaṭṭatu.

4. ‘jemṭeṭ nasar’ nakaril kāṇappaṭṭa paḻiya cumēriyar eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷi (ki.mu.3500) mikavum ottuḷḷaṉa; paḻiya ēlattiya eḻuttukaḷiyum cumēriyar eḻuttukaḷiyum ottuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, ivi aṉittum ki.mu.4000kku muṉṉarē oru potu eḻuttu muṟiyiliruntu pirintaṉavātal vēṇṭum.

5. ciṟcila eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ ekiptiyak kuṟikaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa.

6. kirīṭ tīvil kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷum ivi pōṉṟaviyē. eṉavē, mikap paḻiya kālattil cittira eḻuttukaḷi mūlamākak kerṇṭa oru potu moḻiyi liruntē ivi aṉittum pirintaṉavātal vēṇṭum.

7. capiya eḻuttukaḷ, cipras eḻuttukaḷ, poṉiṣiya eḻuttukaḷ ākiya mūṉṟilum uḷḷa oru pakuti eḻuttukaḷ intac cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntē piṟantaṉavātal vēṇṭum. appaṇṭikkālattil cintuveḷi makkaḷ arapikkaṭal, ceṅkaṭal, matya tarikkaṭal ākiya im mūṉṟaṉ vaḻiyāka mēṟkup puṟanāṭukaḷōṭu vāṇipam ceytu vantaṉarātaliṉ, avarkaḷiṭamiruntu mēṟcoṉṉa mūṉṟiṭattu makkaḷum eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟik kaṭaṉ peṟṟirukkalām.[5]

pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ

acōkaṉ cācaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ ki.mu.mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntavi. avi iḻantuviṭṭa intiyā eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu sar aleksāṇṭar kaṉṉiṅhām neṭunāṭkaḷukku muṉ karutiṉār. avar karutiyatu cari eṉpati iṉṟu pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ uṇarntār; ‘cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntē pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ tōṉṟiṉavātal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu pala kāraṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭi meyppittuḷḷār. ‘cintu veḷi nākarikam ki.mu.2500kku muṟpaṭṭatu. pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ ki.mu.300kkuc cariyāṉa kālattavi. ivi iraṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa eḻuttu. vaḷarccikkuriya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ illāta pōtu,[6] avaṟṟiliruntu ivi vantaṉa eṉal eṅṅaṉam poruntum? eṉṟu cilar kēṭkalām. cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyar varukiyōṭu aḻintu viṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟak kūṭiya cāṉṟu itukāṟum kiṭittilatu. cintuveḷic camayanili iṉṟaḷavum intiyāvil iruntu varukiyil, eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ maṭṭum maṟintuviṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟutal poruttamaṟṟa vātamākum. mēlum, moheñco-tarō nakara maṇ mēṭṭiṉ mītu ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta peḷattarkaḷ stupam kaṭṭi vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. cintu pañcāp maṇṭilaṅkaḷil naṉṟākap paravi irunta paṇpu muṉṉarē viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, peḷattarkaḷ kālam vari cintu veḷi nākarikam toṭarntu vantateṉakkōṭal tavaṟākātu. mēlum, putitāka vanta āriyar, nīṇṭa nāḷāka nāṭṭil nilipeṟṟirunta paṇṭik kuṭikaḷiṉ nākarikattiyō piṟavaṟṟiyō piramātamāka māṟṟiviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟavum itukāṟum cāṉṟu kiṭittilatu. āriyar teyvaṅkaḷāṉa intiraṉ, akni mutaliyaṉavum avartam matattiṉ uyir nāṭiyāṉa vēḷvi iyaṟṟalum irunta iṭam teriyāmal maṟintuviṭṭaṉa. iṉṟaḷavum intumatattil ucca niliyil iruppaṉa cintuveḷitteyvaṅkaḷē ākum; kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa intukkaḷiṭam iruppavi cintuveḷi makkaḷ kiyāṇṭa teyva vaṇakkamē yākum. ātaliṉ, āriyar varukiyāl, cintu veḷi nākarikattiṟku māṟākap periya puraṭci oṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭuviṭavilli. pōtiya eḻuttuc cātaṉaṅkaḷ iṉmiyāl, acōkaṉukku muṟpaṭṭavarkaḷ kaṟkampaṅkaḷil eḻuttukaḷ poṟikkavilli. poṟikka vēṇṭiya tēvi ēṟpaṭavum illi. ākavē, acōkaṉukku muṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷ namakkuk kiṭittila. oruvēḷi, avi maṇṇuḷ maṟippuṇṭu iruppiṉum, irukkalām; nāḷaṭivil veḷippaṭalām. avi eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, ‘acōkaṉuṭiya pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatil aiyamē. illi’ eṉṟu pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ karutukiṟār. ‘avaratu karuttuc cariyē’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭarum aṟivikkiṉṟār.

eḻuttukaḷ

cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷic cōtikkiyil, teḷivāṉa vēṟupaṭṭa 234 eḻuttukkuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pirāmiyil a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, ē, ō, eṉṉum eṭṭu uyir eḻuttukaḷum, 33 mey eḻuttukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl uṇṭāṉa uyirmey eḻuttukaḷ (33 X 8=) 264 ākum. ivaṟṟil 50 eḻuttukaḷukku uriya kuṟikaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷil teḷivākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āciya-āsṭrēliya (muṇṭā) moḻikaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cila cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cila pirāhuyi moḻi eḻuttukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil muṇṭā moḻikaḷē tirāviṭattukku muṟpaṭṭavi. ātaliṉ avi tirāviṭattilum ōraḷavu kalantirukkalām.

cintu veḷi makkaḷ moḻi oraciyuṭiya (monosyllabic) coṟkaḷiyē peritum koṇṭatākum. ammoḻi camaskirutamaṉṟu; cemiṭṭiya moḻiyum aṉṟu eṉpatu uṇmi.[7]

cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷākak karutappaṭupavi mīṉ, kaṭṭaṅkaḷ, nāy, kōḻi, vāttu, vaṇṭu, maṉita uruvam, vēṟupala vaḷivukaḷ mutaliyaṉavām. ivi cittira eḻuttukaḷ ākum. cila kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ 2 mutal 21 kaṭṭaṅkaḷ variyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi aṉittum taṉippaṭṭa pakutikaḷākavum coṟkaḷākavum iṇippuṇṭa coṟkaḷākavum iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. inta uru eḻuttukaḷ oli eḻuttukaḷāka māṟuvataṟku nīṇṭa kālam ākiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli. muttirikaḷiṉ mēl eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa katikaḷ iṉṉavi eṉpatu terinta piṉṉarē, cintuveḷi makkaḷuṭiya nākarikattippaṟṟiya cuvi payakkum ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟi aṟintu iṉpuṟalām.[8]

“inta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷic cōtittāl, pala poruḷkaḷik kuṟikka orē cittiram payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭati uṇaralām. utāraṇamāka, ‘veḷiccam viḷakku, cūriyaṉ, oḷi, cuṭar eṉṉum palavaṟṟik kuṟikka ēṟakkuṟiya orē orē aṭiyāḷam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āriyar ki.mu.1200kku muṉ intiyāviṟ pukavilli. avarkaṭku muṉ cintuveḷiyil iruntavar tirāviṭarākalām. palucistāṉattil uḷḷa prāhuyi moḻiyiṟ pēraḷavu tirāviṭak kalappu iruttalum, prāhuyi makkaḷ maṇṭi oṭukaḷi ottavi moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittiruttalum, cintuveḷi moḻi prāhayiyip pōla oracic col uṭiyatāka iruttalum nōkkat tirāviṭam vaṭa intiyāvil āriyar varukikku muṉ iruntatu eṉṉalām. tirāviṭarē taṅkaḷ kalikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum āriyariṭam oppaṭittavarātal vēṇṭum”[9]

“cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ paḻiya tirāviṭa mūla eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntu piṟantaṉavātal vēṇṭum. ittakiya eḻuttukaḷ lipiyāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. paḻiya lipiya eḻuttukaḷiliruntu aipīriyaṉ, eṭraskaṉ, lipiyaṉ, miṉōvaṉ, paḻiya ekiptiya eḻuttukaḷ mutaliyaṉa tōṟṟa meṭuttaṉavākum” cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiliruntu piṟantavi:(1) cumēriyar eḻuttukkuṟikaḷ. ivaṟṟiliruntu piṟantaṉavē pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷum piṟkāla aciriya eḻuttukaḷumākum, (2) paḻiya ēlattu eḻuttukaḷ. ivi iṉṟaḷavum vācikkak kūṭavilli. (3) paḻiya cīṉa eḻuttukaḷ, (4) teṉ arēpiyāvil uḷḷa ‘capiyaṉ’ eḻuttukaḷ.

“vaṭa pirāmi eḻuttukaḷum teṉ pirāmi eḻuttukaḷum cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiliruntu vaḷarcci peṟṟaviyē ākum. teṉ pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiliruntu tirāviṭarāl vaḷarkkappaṭṭa nērāṉa vaḷarcciyuṭi eḻuttukaḷ ākum. avaṟṟiṉ orumippāṭṭit tirunelvēliyiṟ kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītuḷḷa eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiyum. nīlakiriyil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷiyum, hitarāpāt camātikaḷiṟ kiṭitta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiyum koṇṭu naṉkuṇaralām. vaṭapirāmi eḻuttukaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntu nērāṉa vaḷarcci peṟṟavi alla. avi eḻutat teriyāmal intiyāvi aṭinta āriyarāl ēṟṟu vaḷarkkappaṭṭavi; avar tam vaṭamoḻikkēṟpa nāḷaṭivil māṟṟappeṟṟavi. acōkaṉuṭiya pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ āriyarāl vaḷarkkap peṟṟa cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉ piṟkālat tōṟṟam ākum. ikkāraṇattāṟṟāṉ, vaṭa pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ, teṉ pirāmi eḻuttukaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; vaṭapirāmi eḻuttukaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

“cinruvaḷi eḻuttukaḷik koṇṭa moḻi paḻiya tirāviṭamākum. attirāviṭattiṉ perumpālāṉa coṟkaḷ tamiḻilēyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, cila coṟkaḷ kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷuvam mutaliya piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. prāhuyi moḻiyil uḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷum cintu, paluci, pāracīka moḻikaḷāl tam uṇmiyāṉa uccarippi iḻantuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, cintu veḷit tirāviṭa moḻi iṉṟu pēcappaṭukiṉṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pōṉṟataṉṟu; ivaṟṟiṉ tāymoḻi eṉal poruntum. atu paḻiya kaṉṉaṭattiyum caṅkat tamiḻiyum oruvāṟu ottatākum”. “cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭitta cila eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ taḷataḷa, mukil (nīr aṟṟa mēkam), kārmukil (nīr uṟṟa mēkam), maḻi mūṉ(ṟu) kaṇ, mūṉ(ṟu) mīṉ kaṇ, pērāḷ (pērāṉ) eṇṇāḷ (eṇṇāṉ), nāyvēl, naṇṭur, vēlūr, kuraṅkar, mīṉavar mutaliya coṟkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa”. oru muttiriyil muṉutayatu eṉṉum coṟṟoṭar kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ poruḷ ‘mūṉṟu kampaḷi pōrttuk koḷḷattakka kuḷiriyuṭiya kālam’ eṉpatām. ivvaḻakku iṉṟum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭuk keḷaṭariṭam iruntu varukiṉṟatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. pērāḷ,[10] eṇṇāḷ,[11] mukkaṇ,[12] eṉpaṉa civaṉik kuṟippaṉa.

“itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, ‘cintu veḷi makkaḷ’ lipiyāviliruntu vantavarkaḷ, avarkaḷ neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē intiyāvi aṭintu, āsṭrēliya makkaḷuṭaṉ kalappuṇṭaṉar; nam veṇmi niṟattiyum uruva amippiyum iḻantaṉar; āsṭrēliya moḻiyil ōraḷavu koṇṭaṉar; taṅkaḷ cittira eḻuttukaḷi mēṉmiyuṟa vaḷarttu vantaṉar. avarkaḷē piṟkālattavarāl ‘tirāviṭar’ eṉap paṭṭaṉar. avartam eḻuttukaḷ ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa pala eḻuttukaḷ piṟappataṟku mūlamāyiṉa. anta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷik koṇṭē vaṭapirāmi vaḷarntatu” eṉpaṉavum piṟavum naṉkaṟiyalām.[13]

cintuveḷiyiṟ kāṇappeṟṟa eḻuttuk kuṟi ovvoṉṟum oru poruḷik kuṟippatākum. atu nāḷaṭivil antap poruḷiṉ peyariyē kuṟikka māṟi iruttal vēṇṭum. atuvē oli eḻuttākum... eḻuttukaḷ tōṉṟum vintiyi oraḷavu aṟital iṅku avaciyamākum:” maṉitaṉ pēcamuṭiyāta niliyil irunta kālam oṉṟuṇṭu. avaṉ piṟaku (1) tāṉ virumpiya poruḷiṉ uruvattic cittirittuk kāṭṭiṉāṉ: (2) piṉṉar ataṉ kuṇa vicēṭattit taṉ ceykiyāl uṇarttip peṟṟu vantāṉ; (3) piṟaku apporuḷi uṇartta or eḻuttip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ, (4) iṟutiyil pēccu vakiyāl coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kuṟikka aṭiyāḷak kuṟikaḷi iṭṭuvantāṉ. maṉitaṉatu moḻi iṅṅaṉamē paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcci peṟṟu vantatu” eṉa moḻi nūl eḻutap pukunta āṭṭō jespersaṉ eṉpār aṟintuḷḷār. innāṉkum tamiḻil muṟiyē (1) uru eḻuttu, (2) taṉmi eḻuttu, (3) uṇarveḻuttu, (4) oli eḻuttu eṉappaṭum. ivi viḷakkamāka naṉṉūl mayilinātar uriyil (eḻuttiyal īṟṟuc cūttira vuriyiṟ) kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyiṉ kaṭicic cūttira vuriyilum ivvaki eḻuttukaḷip paṟṟiya ilakkaṇam viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ avvuriyil, “makaṭūu ā piṭi, kumari, kaṉṉi, piṉavu, muṭuval eṉṟiṉṉa cila eḻuttum tēr, patam mutaliya nālvaki eḻuttum, cāti mutaliya taṉmi eḻuttum, uccāṭaṉam mutaliya ukkira eḻuttum, cittirakārūṭam mutaliya muttiṟa eḻuttum, pākiyal mutaliya nālvaki eḻuttum, puttēḷ mutaliya nāṟkati eḻuttum, tātu mutaliya yōṉi eḻuttum, mātamatiyam mutaliya caṅkēta eḻuttum, kavi mutaliya caṅkēta eḻuttum, pārppāṉ vaḻakkākiya patiṉmūṉṟeḻuttum eṉṟu ittoṭakkattaṉavum.... kaṭṭuri eḻuttum, vacciram mutaliya vaṭiveḻuttum.... maṟṟum palavakiyāṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭa ellā eḻuttum vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka” eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal nōkkattakkatu. mēlum, cilappatikārttil, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattut tuṟimukattil iṟakkumatiyāṉa mūṭṭikaṭkum paṇṭam ēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaṭkum kṇ eḻuttukaḷ iṭappaṭṭiruntaṉavām.

“vampa mākkaḷ tampeyar poṟitta

kaṇṇeḻuttup paṭutta eṇṇup palpoti”,



“irupatiṉāyiram kaṇṇeḻuttup paṭuttaṉa.

kipuṉi cakaṭamum”[14]





“ivaṟṟāl kaṭic caṅka kālattil (ki.mu.300 ki.pi.200) tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ eḻuttukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa.” eṉpati aṟiyalām, kaṇṇeḻuttu - cittira eḻuttu; kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar-cittirakārikaḷ; nōkkiṉār kaṇṇiṭattē tam toḻili niṟuttuvōr’ eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri” cuvittaṟkuriyatu. eṉavē, ‘kaṇ eḻuttu’ eṉpatu, pārppavar kaṇṇuḷ toḻiṟṟiṟami kāṭṭi niṟkum eḻuttu’ eṉap poruḷpaṭum. karanteḻuttu eṉṉum oruvaki eḻuttum vaḻakkil irunta uṇmi cintāmaṇiyāl aṟiyattakum (ce1767). iṅṅaṉam pala tiṟappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatit tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ kūṟic celvatāl, ivi tamiḻakattil peruvaḻakku uṭiyaṉavāka oru kālattil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivām. mēlum, yāpparuṅkala virutti yuriyiṟ kāṇappeṟum taṉmi mutaliya eḻuttu vakikaḷip paṟṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ yāpparuṅkala viruttiyāḷar kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum; iṉṟu tamiḻakattuḷ kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻc cāsaṉaṅkaḷil attakiya eḻuttukaḷ kāṇap peṟāmiyālum, ac cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ paḻiya kālam ki.pi. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākalāṉum, ki.pi.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa kālattiṟṟāṉ avvaki eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kōṭal poruttamāṉatu.

“ki.mu.3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉa eṉṟu karutat takum teṉ pirāmi cāsaṉaṅkaḷil uḷḷa moḻi pirākirutamoḻi kalanta tamiḻiṉ citivākat terikiṉṟatu. accāsaṉaṅkaḷ, tamiḻ makkaḷ taṉiyē vaḻaṅkiya lipikaḷum pirāmi lipikaḷum kalanta eḻuttamitiyum, pākatamum tamiḻum kalanta pāṣi amitiyum uṭiyaṉavākavē tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ātit tamiḻar tamakkeṉṟu aminta eḻuttamiti uṭiyavar eṉpatu teḷivām ākavē, kaṭiccaṅkattār kālattiṉum (ki.mu.400-ki.pi.200) ataṟku veku kālattiṟku muṉṉum vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukaḷ ellām cittira caṅkēta lipikaḷ (Hieroglyph) pōṉṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum... yāpparuṅkāla uriyāl, cīṉar ekiptiyar vaḻaṅkiyavi pōṉṟa eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻakattil aṟavaṟṟiruntaṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭ cila yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuṭiyār kālattum (ki.pi.11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa eṉṟum nām koḷḷalām.... ‘tamiḻāciriyaṉ oruvaṉ, nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum ittakiya eḻuttu vakikaḷi ellām uṇarntavaṉātal vēṇṭum eṉa muṉṉōr niyamittiruttalāl, paḻiya ‘vaṭivu’ mutaliya caṅkēta eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil peruvaḻakkup peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉṟum, avi yāvum muṉṉōrāl muṟiyākak kaṟkavum kaṟpikkavum peṟṟuvantaṉa eṉṟum nām naṉkaṟiyak kūṭum. ik kuṟi eḻuttukkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiya ulakattup paṇṭi makkaḷil tamiḻarum vilakkappaṭṭavar allar”.[15]

“iṅṅaṉam eḻuttukaḷ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ niliyi aṭivataṟku muntiya kālaṅkaḷil peṟṟirunta uruvaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ tamiḻit tavira vēṟu intiya moḻikaḷil uḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli.[16] ivaṟṟāl, cittiraṅkaḷālum vēṟu kuṟikaḷālum maṉitarkaḷ oru kālattil eḻuti vanta paḻakkattit tamiḻ makkaḷum aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉpati eṇṇalām. ‘eḻuttu’ eṉṉum col tamiḻil payilappaṭuvati nōkkiṉālum itu veḷippaṭum. ‘eḻuttu’ eṉṉum tamiḻc collukku akṣaram, likitam, kalvi, oviyam, patumi eṉappala poruḷkaḷ uḷa. paḻiya tamiḻnūlkaḷil, eḻuttu, eḻututal eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil,

“iṉṉapalapala eḻuttu nili maṇṭapam” (paripāṭal, 19)

“teyvak kuṭavari eḻutiya.. pāvi” (kuṟun 39) eṉpaṉapōlac cittiram, pāvi mutaliyavaṟṟi nirumittal ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟippataṟkup payilappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ cittirattiyum eḻuttiṉ vakiyākavē koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷutal kūṭum.[17]

“...nām, pirāmiyip paṟṟiya inta āṟu viṣayaṅkaḷiyum kavaṉittāl, pirāmiya lipi mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟpaṭavilli eṉṟum, uyir eḻuttukaḷuḷ a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappi aḷippatum meyyeḻuttukaḷuḷ varkka eḻuttukaḷik koḷḷātatumāṉa oru pāṣikkeṉa amikkappaṭṭup piṉṉāl vaṭamoḻikku upayōkappaṭumpaṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum eṇṇavēṇṭi irukkiṟatu. intiyāvil uḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ ivvitam uḷḷatu ‘tamiḻ’ oṉṟutāṉ. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, pirāmi mutalil tamiḻukkeṉa aminta lipiyāka iruttal kūṭumā? iḵtu ārāytaṟkuriyatu. harappā, moheñco-tarō ivaṟṟiṟ kiṭitta kuṟikaṭkum pirāmikkum mattiya niliyil uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum kuṟikaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa cāsaṉam oṉṟu mattiya intiyāvil vikramakōl eṉṉum iṭattil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (ināṉṭ. 62 āvatu vālyūm). moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta kuṟikaḷip pōṉṟavaṟṟik koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ hitarāpāt camastāṉattilum, nāṇayaṅkaḷ tirunelvēli jillāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivvitamāṉa kuṟikaḷ intiyāviṉ piṟa pākaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭikkavilli”[18]

muṭivuri

itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ, (1) oraciyic ciṟappākak koṇṭa moḻiyic cērntavi: (2) avaṟṟiliruntē pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ tōṉṟi vaḷarcci peṟṟaṉa; (3) pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻ moḻikkeṉṟē amikkap paṭṭuppiṉṉar vaṭamoḻikkum payaṉpaṭumpaṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa; 4) cintu veḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ tirunelvēli nāṇayaṅkaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; (3) intiya moḻikaḷil tamiḻ oṉṟilētāṉ, eḻuttukaḷ neṭuṅkaṇakku niliyi aṭivataṟku muṉṉiya kālaṅkaḷiṟ peṟṟirunta uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu... eṉṉum cuvi payakkum ceytikaḷi aṟiyalām. eṉavē, cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ, hīrās pātiriyār kūṟuvatupōla paṇṭittirāviṭa makkaḷ tōṟṟuvittaṉavē avi tirāviṭa moḻikku aminta paṇṭi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷē eṉṟu muṭipu kūṟal oruvāṟu poruntum.
